Explanatory Remarks:

1. This text includes only Nāradapurāṇa chapters 2.44-2.82, as the remaining sections are already available in the Gretil.

Text:

<2.44>

athacatuścatvāriṃśo'dhyāyaḥ
vasiṣṭha uvāca -
tatasstu mohinī bhūpa śrutvā māhātmyamuttamam
gaṅgāyāḥ pāpanāśinyāḥ punaḥ prāha purohitam || 1 ||
mohinyuvāca -
tvayā cānugṛhītāsmi bhagavannanukampayā
yaduktaṃ puṇyamākhyānaṃ gaṅgāyāḥ pāpaśodhanam || 2 ||
gayātīrthaṃ tu vikhyātaṃ kathaṃ loke dvijottama
tadahaṃ śrotumicchāmi kṛpāṃ kṛtvādhunā vada || 3 ||
vasuruvāca -
pitṛtīrthaṃ gayānāma sarvatīrthavaraṃ smṛtam
yatrāste devadeveśaḥ svayameva pitāmahaḥ || 4 ||
yatraiṣā pitṛbhirgītā gāthā yogamabhīpsubhiḥ
eṣṭavyā bahavaḥ putrā yadyeko'pi gayāṃ vrajet || 5 ||
yajeta vāśvamedhena nīlaṃ vā vṛṣamutsṛjet
sārātsārataraṃ devi gayāmāhātmyamuttamam || 6 ||
pravakṣyāmi samāsena bhuktimuktipradaṃ śṛṇu
gayāsuro'bhavatpūrvaṃ vīryavānparamaḥ sa ca || 7 ||
tapaścakre mahāghoraṃ sarvabhūtopatāpanam
tattapastāpitā devāstadvadhārthaṃ hariṃ gatāḥ || 8 ||
śaraṇaṃ harirūce tān bhavitavyaṃ śivātmabhiḥ
pātitasya mahāndehe tathetyūcuḥ surā harim || 9 ||
kadācicchivapūjārthaṃ kṣīrābdheḥ kamalāni ca
ānīya nikaṭe deśe śayanaṃ cākaroddhareḥ || 10 ||
viṣṇumāyāvimūḍho'sau gadayā viṣṇunā hataḥ
tato gadādharo viṣṇurgayāyāṃ muktidaḥ smṛtaḥ || 11 ||
tasya dehe liṅgarūpī sthitaḥ śuddhaḥ pitāmahaḥ
viṣṇuvāhārthamaryādāṃ puṇkṣetraṃ bhaviṣyati || 12 ||
yajñaṃ śrāddhaṃ piṇḍadānaṃ snānādi kurute naraḥ
saḥ svargaṃ brahmalokaṃ vā gacchenna narakaṃ naraḥ || 13 ||
gayātīrthaṃ paraṃ jñātvā yogaṃ cakre pitāmahaḥ
brāhmaṇānpūjayāmāsa ṛṣīṃśca samupāgatān || 14 ||
nadīṃ sarasvatīṃ sṛṣṭvā sthito vyāptadigantaraḥ
bhakṣyabhojyaphalādīṃśca kāmadhenūstathāsṛjat || 15 ||
pañcakrośaṃ gayātīrthaṃ brāhmaṇebhyo dhanaṃ dadau
dharmayāge tu lobhādvai pratigṛhya dhanādikam || 16 ||
sthitā viprāstadā śaptā gayāyāṃ brahmaṇā tataḥ
mā bhūttripuruṣī vidyā mābhūttripuruṣaṃ dhanam || 17 ||
yuṣmākaṃ syāddhi virasā nadī pāṣāṇaparvataḥ
sa taistu prārthito brahmā tīrthāni kṛtavānprabhuḥ || 18 ||
lokāḥ puṇyā gayāyāṃ vai śrāddhena brahmalokagāḥ
yuṣmānye pūjayiṣanti tairahaṃ pūjitaḥ sadā || 19 ||
brahmajñānaṃ gayāśrāddhaṃ gogṛhe maraṇaṃ tathā
vāsaḥ puṃsāṃ kurukṣetre muktireṣā caturvidhā || 20 ||
samudrāḥ saritaḥ sarve vāpīkūpahradāstathā
snātukāmā gayātīrthaṃ devi yāṃti na saṃśayaḥ || 21 ||
brahmahatyā surāpānaṃ steyaṃ gurvaṅganāgamaḥ
pāpaṃ tatsaṅgajaṃ sarvaṃ gayāśrāddhādvinaśyati || 22 ||
aṃsaṃskṛtā mṛtā ye ca paśubhiḥ prahatāśca ye
sarpadaṣṭā gayāśrāddhānmuktāḥ svargaṃ vrajanti te || 23 ||
gayāyāṃ piṇḍadānena yatphalaṃ labhate naraḥ
na tacchakyaṃ mayā vaktuṃ kalpakoṭiśatairapi || 24 ||
atraiva śrūyate devi itihāsaḥ purātanaḥ
taṃ pravakṣyāmi subhage śṛṇuṣvaikāgramānasā || 25 ||
traitāyuge vai nṛpatirbabhūva viśālanāmā sa purīṃ viśālām
uvāsa dhanyo dhṛtimānaputraḥ svayaṃ viśālādhipatirdvijāgryān || 26 ||
papraccha putrārthamamitrahantā taṃ brāhmaṇāḥ procuradīnasatvāḥ
rājan pitṝṃstarpaya putrahetorgatvā gayāyāṃ vidhivattu piṇḍaiḥ || 27 ||
dhruvaṃ tataste bhavitā tu vīra sahasradātā sakalakṣitīśaḥ
itīrito vipragaṇaiḥ sa thṛṣṭo rājā viśālādhipatiḥ prayatnāt || 28 ||
samastatīrthapravarāṃ dvijena gayāmiyāttadgatamānasaḥ san
āgatya tīrthapravaraṃ sutārthī gayāśiro yāgaparaḥ pitṝṇām || 29 ||
piṇḍapradānaṃ vidhinā prayacchattāvadviyatyuttamamūrtiyuktān
paśyan sa puṃsaḥ sitaraktakṛṣṇānuvāca rājā kimidaṃ bhavaṃtaḥ || 30 ||
sammuhyate śaṃsata sarvametatkutūhalaṃ me manasi pravṛttam
sita uvāca -
ahaṃ sitaste janako'smi rājannāmnā ca varṇena ca karmaṇā ca || 31 ||
ayaṃ ca me janako raktavarṇo nṛśaṃsakṛdbrahmahā pāpakārī
ataḥ paraṃ śṛṇu prapitāmahaśca kṛṣṇo nāmnā karmaṇā varṇataśca || 32 ||
etena kṛṣṇena hatā purā vai janmanyanekā ṛṣayaḥ purāṇāḥ
etau smṛtau dvāvapi pitṛputro avīcisaṃjñaṃ narakaṃ praviṣṭau || 33 ||
ataḥ paro'ya janakaḥ paro'sya tatkṛṣṇavaktrāvapi dīrghakālam
ahaṃ ca śuddhena nijena karmaṇā śakrāsanaṃ prāpya sudurlabhaṃ tat || 34 ||
tvayā punarmantravidā gayāyāṃ piṇḍapradānena balādimau ca
mokṣāyitau tīrthavaraprabhāvādavīcisaṃjñaṃ narakaṃ gatau tau || 35 ||
pitṝn pitāmahāṃścaiva tathaiva prapitāmahān
prīṇayāmīti yattoyaṃ tvayā dattamarindama || 36 ||
tenāsmadyugapadyogo jāto vākyena sattama
tīrthaprabhāvādgacchāmaḥ pitṛlokaṃ na saṃśayaḥ || 37 ||
tatra piṇḍapradānena etau tava pitāmahau
tvadgatāvapi saṃsiddhau pāpādvikṛtaliṅgakau || 38 ||
etasmātkāraṇātputra ahametau pragṛhya tu
āgato'smi bhavantaṃ vai dra ṣṭuṃ yāsyāmi sāṃpratam || 39 ||
tīrthaprabhāvādyatnena brahmaghnasyāpi vai pituḥ
gayāyāṃ piṇḍadānena kuryāduddharaṇaṃ sutaḥ || 40 ||
ityevamuktvā tu pitā sito'sya sārddhaṃ ca tābhyāṃ hi pitāmahābhyām
jagāma sadyo hi sutaṃ viśālaṃ saṃyojya cāśīrbhirapi svalokam || 41 ||
sakṛdgayābhigamanaṃ sakṛtpiṃḍaprapātanam
durlabhaṃ kiṃ punarnityamasminneva vyavasthitiḥ || 42 ||
kriyate patitānāṃ tu gate saṃvatsare kvacit
deśakālapramāṇatvādgayākūpe svabandhubhiḥ || 43 ||
pretarājo'tha vaṇijaṃ kañcitprāha svamuktaye
gayātīrthaṃ tu dṛṣṭvā tvaṃ snātvā śaucasamanvitaḥ || 44 ||
mama nāma samuddiśya piṇḍanirvapaṇaṃ kuru
tatra piṇḍapradānena pretabhāvādahaṃ sukham || 45 ||
muktastu sarvadātṝṇāṃ prāpsyāmi śubhalokatām
ityevamuktvā vaṇijaṃ pretarājo'nugaiḥ saha || 46 ||
svanāmāni yathānyāyaṃ samyagākhyātavān rahaḥ
upārjayitvā prayayau gayāśīrṣamanuttamam || 47 ||
pāṃśunirvapaṇaṃ cakre pretānāmanupūrvaśaḥ
cakāra vasudānaṃ ca pitṝnkṛtvā puraḥsarān || 48 ||
ātmano'sau mahābuddhirvidhināpi tilairvinā
piṇḍanirvapaṇaṃ cakre tathānyānapi gotrajān || 49 ||
evaṃ datte tu vai piṇḍe vaṇijā pretabhāvataḥ
vimuktā dvijatāṃ prāpya brahmalokaṃ tato gatāḥ || 50 ||
pāyasaṃ khaḍgamāṃsaṃ ca putrairdattaṃ pitṛkṣayaṃ
kṛṣṇo lohastathā chāga ānantyāya prakalpate || 51 ||
gayāyāmakṣayaṃ śrāddhaṃ japahomatapāṃsi ca
pitṛkṣaye hi tatputraiḥ kṛtamānantyatāṃ vrajet || 52 ||
kāṃkṣaṃti pitaraḥ putrānnarakasya bhayārdditāḥ
gayāṃ yāsyati yaḥ putraḥ so'smānsantārayiṣyati || 53 ||
gayāyāṃ dharmapṛṣṭhe ca sadasi brahmaṇastathā
gayāśīrṣe'kṣayavaṭe pitṝṇāṃ dattamakṣayam || 54 ||
brahmāraṇyaṃ dharmapṛṣṭhaṃ dhenukāraṇyameva ca
dṛṣṭvaitāni pitṝṃścārcya vaṃśyānviṃśatimuddharet || 55 ||
mahākalpakṛtaṃ pāpaṃ gayāṃ prāpya vinaśyati
gavi gṛdhravaṭe caiva śrāddhaṃ dattaṃ mahāphalam || 56 ||
mataṅgasya padaṃ tatra dṛśyate sarvamānuṣaiḥ
khyāpitaṃ dharmasarvasvaṃ lokasyaiva nidarśanāt || 57 ||
tatpaṅkajavanaṃ puṇyaṃ puṇyavadbhirniṣevitam
yasmin pāṇḍurviśatyeva tīrthaṃ sarvanidarśanam || 58 ||
tṛtīyāṃ tathā pāde nikṣīrāyāśca maṇḍale
mahāhrade ca kauśikyāṃ dattaṃ śrāddhaṃ mahāphalam || 59 ||
muṇḍapṛṣṭhe padaṃ nyastaṃ mahādevena dhīmatā
bahuvarṣaśataṃ taptaṃ tapastīrtheṣu duṣkaram || 60 ||
alpenāpyatra kālena naro dharmaparāyaṇaḥ
pāpmānamutsṛjatyāśu jīrṇāṃ tvacamivoragaḥ || 61 ||
nāmnā kanakanandeti tīrthaṃ tatraiva viśrutam
udīcyāṃ muṇḍapṛṣṭhasya brahmarṣigaṇasevitam || 62 ||
tatra snātvā divaṃ yāṃti svaśarīreṇa mānavāḥ
dattaṃ tatra sadā śrāddhamakṣayaṃ samudāhṛtam || 63 ||
snātvā dinatrayaṃ tatra niḥkṣīrāyāṃ sulocane
mānase sarasi snātvā śrāddhaṃ tatra samācaret || 64 ||
uttaraṃ mānasaṃ gatvā siddhiṃ prāpnotyanuttamām
yastatra nirvapecchrāddhaṃ yathāśakti yathābalam || 65 ||
kāmānsaṃlabhate divyānmokṣopāyāṃśca kṛtsnaśaḥ
tato brahmasaro gacchedbrahmāvaśyopaśobhitam || 66 ||
brahmalokamavāpnoti prabhātāmeva śarvarīm
brahmaṇā tatra sarasi yūpaḥ puṇyaḥ prakalpitaḥ || 67 ||
yūpaṃ pradakṣiṇīkṛtya vājapeyaphalaṃ labhet
tato gacchettu subhage dhenukaṃ lokaviśrutam || 68 ||
ekarātroṣito yatra prayacchettiladhenukām
sarvapāpavinirmuktaḥ somalokaṃ vrajeddhruvam || 69 ||
tatra cihnaṃ mahābhāge adyāpi mahadadbhutam
kapilā saha vatsena parvate vicaratyuta || 70 ||
padāni tatra dṛśyante savatsāyāśca mohini
savatsāyāḥ pradṛṣṭeṣu padeṣu narapuṅgavaiḥ || 71 ||
yatkiñcidaśubhaṃ karma teṣāṃ tannaśyati kṣaṇāt
tato gṛdhravaṭaṃ gacchetsthānaṃ devasya dhīmataḥ || 72 ||
srāyīta bhasmanā tatra abhigamya vṛṣadhvajam
brāhmaṇānāṃ bhaveddevi vrataṃ dvādaśavārṣikam || 73 ||
itareṣāṃ tu varṇānāṃ sarvaṃ pāpaṃ praṇaśyati
udyantaṃ ca tato gacchetparvataṃ gītanāditam || 74 ||
sāvitryāstu padaṃ yatra dṛśyate puṇyadaṃ mahat
tatra sandhyāmupāsīta brāhmaṇaḥ śaṃsitavrataḥ || 75 ||
upāsitā bhavetsandhyā tena dvādaśavārṣikī
yonidvāraṃ ca tatraiva vidyate vidhinandini || 76 ||
tatrādhigamya mucyeta puruṣo yonisaṅkaṭāt
śuklakṛṣṇāvubhau pakṣau gayāyāṃ yo vasennṛpaḥ || 77 ||
punātyāsaptamaṃ caiva kulānyatra na saṃśayaḥ
tato gacchecca subhage dharmapṛṣṭhaṃ mahāphalam || 78 ||
yatra dharmaḥ sthitaḥ sākṣātpitṛlokasya pālakaḥ
abhigamya tatastatra vājimedhaphalaṃ labhet || 79 ||
tato gaccheta manujo brahmaṇastīrthamuttamam
tatrādhigamya brahmāṇaṃ rājasūyaphalaṃ labhet || 80 ||
phalgutīrthaṃ ca vikhyātaṃ bahumūlaphalānvitam
kauśikī ca nadī yatra śrāddhaṃ tatrākṣayaṃ smṛtam || 81 ||
tato mahīdharaṃ gaccheddharmajñenābhirakṣitam
rājarṣiṇā puṇyakṛtā gayenānupabhujyate || 82 ||
saro gayaśiro yatra puṇyā caiva mahānadī
ṛṣijuṣṭaṃ mahāpuṇyaṃ tīrthaṃ brahmasarovaram || 83 ||
agastyo bhagavānyatra gato vaivasvataṃ prati
uvāsa satataṃ yatra dharmarājaḥ sanātanaḥ || 84 ||
sarvāsāṃ saritāṃ yatra samudbhedo hi dṛśyate
yatra sannihito nityaṃ mahādevaḥ pinākadhṛk || 85 ||
yatrākṣayo vaṭo nāma vartate lokaviśrutaḥ
gayena yajamānena tatreṣṭaṃ kratunā purā || 86 ||
āsthitā tu saricchreṣṭhā gayayajñeṣu rakṣitā
muṇḍapṛṣṭhaṃ gayāṃ caiva raivataṃ devaparvatam || 87 ||
tṛtīyaṃ krauñcapādaṃ ca dṛṣṭvā pāpātpramucyate
śivanadyāṃ śivakaraṃ gayāyāṃ ca gadādharam || 88 ||
sarvatra paramātmānaṃ dṛṣṭvā mucyedaghavrajāt
vārāṇasyāṃ viśālākṣī prayāge lalitā tathā || 89 ||
gayāyāṃ maṅgalā nāma kṛtaśauce tu saiṃhikā
yaddadāti gayāsthastatsarvamānantyamaśnute || 90 ||
nandaṃti pitarastasya suprakṛṣṭena karmaṇā
yadgayāstho dadātyannaṃ pitarastena putriṇaḥ || 91 ||
iti śrībṛhannāradīyapurāṇottarabhāge vasumohinīsaṃvāde gayāmāhatmyaṃ nāma catuścatvāriṃśo'dhyāyaḥ || 44 ||

<2.45>

athapañcacatvāriṃśo'dhyāyaḥ
vasuruvāca -
śṛṇu mohini vakṣyāmi puṇyaṃ pretaśilābhavam
māhātmyaṃ yatra datvā tu piṇḍānpitṝnsamuddharet || 1 ||
ācchāditaśilāpādaḥ prabhāsenātriṇā tataḥ
prabhāso munibhistuṣṭaḥ śilāṅguṣṭhādvinirgataḥ || 2 ||
aṅguṣṭhasthita īśo'pi prabhāseśaḥ prakīrtitaḥ
śilāṅguṣṭhaikadeśo yaḥ sā ca pretaśilā sthitā || 3 ||
piṇḍadānādyatastasmātpretatvānmucyate naraḥ
mahānadī prabhāsātryoḥ saṅgame snānakṛnnaraḥ || 4 ||
vāmadevaḥ svayaṃ bhūyādvāmatīrthaṃ tataḥ smṛtam
prārthito'tha mahānadyāṃ rāmasnāto'bhavadyadā || 5 ||
rāmatīrthaṃ tvatrajātaṃ sarvalokasupāvanam
janmāntarasahasraistu yatkṛtaṃ pātakaṃ naraiḥ || 6 ||
tatsarvaṃ vilayaṃ yāti rāmatīrthābhiṣecanāt
mantreṇānena yaḥ snātvā śrāddhaṃ kurvīta mānavaḥ || 7 ||
rāmatīrthe piṇḍadastu viṣṇuloke mahīyate
rāmarāma mahābāho devānāmabhayaṅkara || 8 ||
tvāṃ namasye tu deveśa mama naśyatu pātakam
namaskṛtya prabhāseśaṃ bhāsamānaṃ śivaṃ vrajet || 9 ||
taṃ ca śambhuṃ namaskṛtya kuryādyāmyabali tataḥ
āpastvamasi deveśaṃ jyotiṣāṃ patireva ca || 10 ||
pāpaṃ nāśaya me śīghraṃ manovākkāyakarmmajam aṃ
śilāyā jaghanaṃ bhūyaḥ samākrāntaṃ yamena ca || 11 ||
dharmarājenādri rukto na gaccheti nagaḥ smṛtaḥ
yamarājadharmarājau niścalāvaha saṃsthitau || 12 ||
tābhyāṃ balimakṛtvā syādgayāśrāddhamapārthakam
śvānau dvau śyāmaśabalau vaivasvatakulodbhavau || 13 ||
tābhyāṃ piṇḍaṃ pradāsyāmi syātāmetāvahiṃsakau
tīrthe pretaśilādau ca caruṇā saghṛtena ca || 14 ||
pitṝnāvāhya tebhyaśca mantraiḥ piṇḍāṃstu nirvapet
kṛtvā dhyānaṃ pitṝṇāṃ tu prayataḥ pretaparvate || 15 ||
prācīnāvītiko bhūyāddakṣiṇābhimukhaḥ smaran
kavyavālo'nalaḥ somo yamaścaivāryamā tathā || 16 ||
agniṣvāttā barhiṣadaḥ somapāḥ pitṛdevatāḥ
āgacchantu mahābhāgā yuṣmābhī rakṣitāstviha || 17 ||
madīyāḥ pitaro ye ca kule jātāḥ sanābhayaḥ
teṣāṃ piṇḍapradānārthamāgato'smi gayāmimām || 18 ||
te sarve tṛptimāyāntu śrāddhenānena śāśvatīm
ācamyoktvātha pañcāṅgaṃ prāṇānāyamya yatnataḥ || 19 ||
punarāvṛttirahitabrahmalokāptihetave
evaṃ saṅkalpya vidhivacchrāddhaṃ kuryādyathākramam || 20 ||
pitṝnāvāhya cābhyarcya mantraiḥ piṇḍaprado bhavet
prajvālya pūrvaṃ tatsthānaṃ pañcagavyaiḥ pṛthak pṛthak || 21 ||
datvā śrāddhaṃ sapiṇḍānāṃ teṣāṃ dakṣiṇabhāgataḥ
kuśairāstīrya teṣāṃ tu sakṛddatvā tilodakam || 22 ||
gṛhītvāñjalinā tebhyaḥ pitṛtīrthena yatnataḥ
saktunā muṣṭimātreṇa dadyādakṣayyapiṇḍakam || 23 ||
tilājyadadhimadhvādi piṇḍadra vyeṣu yojayet
sambadhinastilādyaiśca kuśeṣvāvāhayettataḥ || 24 ||
etāṃstu mantrāṃstrīñchrāddhe strīliṅgānvai samuccaret
piṇḍāndadyādyathā pūrvaṃ pitṝnāvāhya pūrvavat || 25 ||
svagotre vā vigotre vā dampatyoḥ piṇḍapātane
apṛthaṅniṣphalaṃ śrāddhaṃ piṇḍaṃ codakatarpaṇam || 26 ||
piṇḍapātre tilāndatvā pūrayitvā śubhodakaiḥ
mantreṇānena piṇḍāṃstānpradakṣiṇakaraṃ yathā || 27 ||
pariṣiñcettridhā sarvānpraṇipatya kṣamāpayet
pitṝnvisṛjya cācamya sākṣiṇaḥ śrāvayetsurān || 28 ||
sarvasthāneṣu caivaṃ syātpiṃḍadānaṃ tu mohini
gayāyāṃ piṇḍadāne tu na ca kālaṃ vicintayet || 29 ||
adhimāse janidine hyaste ca guruśukrayoḥ
na tyajettu gayāśrāddhaṃ siṃhasthe ca bṛhaspatau || 30 ||
daṇḍaṃ pradarśayedbhikṣurgayāṃ gatvā na piṇḍadaḥ
nyasya viṣṇupade daṇḍaṃ mucyate pitṛbhiḥ saha || 31 ||
pāyasena gayāyāṃ ca saktunā piṣṭakena vā
caruṇā taṇḍulādyairvā piṇḍadānaṃ vidhīyate || 32 ||
gayāṃ dṛṣṭvā tu subhage mahāpāpo'pi pātakī
pūtaḥ kṛtyādhikārī ca śrāddhakṛdbrahmalokabhāk || 33 ||
aśvamedhasahasrāṇāṃ sahasraṃ yaḥ samācaret
nāsau tatphalamāpnoti phalgutīrthe yadāpnuyāt || 34 ||
gayāṃ prāpyārpayetpiṃḍānpitṝṇāṃ cātivallabhān
vilambo naiva kartavyo naiva vighnaṃ samācaret || 35 ||
pitā pitāmahaścaiva tathaiva prapitāmahaḥ
mātā pitāmahī caiva tathaiva prapitā mahī || 36 ||
mātāmahastatpitā ca pramātāmahakādayaḥ
teṣāṃ piṇḍo mayā datto hyakṣayyamupatiṣṭhatām || 37 ||
asmatkule mṛtā ye ca gatiryeṣāṃ na vidyate
teṣāmuddharaṇārthāya imaṃ piṇḍaṃ dadāmyaham || 38 ||
bandhuvarge kule ye ca gatiryeṣāṃ na vidyate
teṣāmuddharaṇārthāya imaṃ piṇḍaṃ dadāmyaham || 39 ||
ajātadantā ye kecidye ca garbhe prapīḍitāḥ
teṣāmuddharaṇārthāya imaṃ piṇḍaṃ dadāmyaham || 40 ||
agnidagdhāśca ye kecinnāgnidagdhāstathā pare
vidyuccaurahatā ye ca tebhyaḥ piṇḍaṃ dadāmyaham || 41 ||
dāvadāhe mṛtā ye ca siṃhavyāghrahatāśca ye
daṃṣṭribhiḥ śṛṅgibhirvāpi tebhyaḥ piṇḍaṃ dadāmyaham || 42 ||
udbandhanamṛtā ye ca viṣaśastrahatāśca ye
ātmano ghātino ye ca tebhyaḥ piṇḍaṃ dadāmyaham || 43 ||
araṇye vartmani vane kṣudhayā tṛṣayā hatāḥ
bhūtapretapiśācaiśca tebhyaḥ piṇḍaṃ dadāmyaham || 44 ||
raurave ye ca tāmisre kālasūtre ca ye sthitāḥ
teṣāmuddharaṇārthāya imaṃ piṇḍaṃ dadāmyaham || 45 ||
anekayātanāsaṃsthāḥ pretalokaṃ ca ye gatāḥ
teṣāmuddharaṇārthāya imaṃ piṇḍaṃ dadāmyaham || 46 ||
durgatiṃ samanuprāpya abhiśāpādinā hatāḥ
teṣāmuddharaṇārthāya imaṃ piṇḍaṃ dadāmyaham || 47 ||
narakeṣu samasteṣu yamadūtavaśaṃ gatāḥ
teṣāmuddharaṇārthāya imaṃ piṇḍaṃ dadāmyaham || 48 ||
paśuyonigatā ye ca pakṣikīṭasarīsṛpāḥ
athavā vṛkṣayonisthāstebhyaḥ piṇḍaṃ dadāmyaham || 49 ||
jātyantarasahasreṣu ye bhramanti svakarmaṇā
mānuṣyaṃ durlabhaṃ yeṣāṃ tebhyaḥ piṇḍaṃ dadāmyaham || 50 ||
divyantarikṣabhūmiṣṭhāḥ pitaro bāndhavādayaḥ
asaṃskṛtamṛtā ye ca tebhyaḥ piṇḍaṃ dadāmyaham || 51 ||
ye kacitpretarūpeṇa vartaṃte pitaro mama
te sarve tṛptimāyāntu piṇḍenānena sarvadā || 52 ||
ye bāndhavābāndhavā vā ye'nyajanmani bāndhavāḥ
teṣāṃ piṇḍo mayā datto hyakṣayyamupatiṣṭhatām || 53 ||
pitṛvaṃśe mṛtā ye va mātṛvaṃśe ca ye mṛtāḥ
guruśvaśurabandhūnāṃ ye cānye bāndhavā mṛtāḥ || 54 ||
ye me kule luptapiṇḍāḥ putradāravivarjitāḥ
kriyālopagatā ye ca jātyandhāḥ paṅgavaśca ye || 55 ||
virūpā āmagarbhāśca jñātājñātāḥ kule mama
teṣāṃ piṇḍo mayā datto hyakṣayyamupatiṣṭhatām || 56 ||
ā brahmaṇo ye pitṛvaṃśajātā mātustathā vaṃśabhavā madīyāḥ
kuladvaye ye mama saṅgatāśca tebhyaḥ svadhā piṇḍamahaṃ dadāmi || 57 ||
sākṣiṇaḥ santu me devā brahmeśānādayastathā
mayā gayāṃ samāsādya pitṝṇāṃ niṣkṛtiḥ kṛtā || 58 ||
āgato'smi gayāṃ deva pitṛkārye gadādhara
tvameva sākṣī bhagavānanṛṇo'hamṛṇatrayāt || 59 ||
apare'hni śucirbhūtvā gacchettu pretaparvatam
brahmakuṇḍe tataḥ snātvā devādīṃstarpayetsudhīḥ || 60 ||
kṛtvāhvānaṃ pitṝṇāṃ tu prayataḥ pretaparvate
pūrvavaccaiva saṅkalpya tataḥ piṇḍānpradāpayet || 61 ||
svamantrairatha sampūjya paramāḥ pitṛdevatāḥ
yāvantastu tilāḥ puṃbhirgṛhītāḥ pitṛkarmaṇi || 62 ||
gacchaṃti bhītā asurāstāvaṃto garuḍāhivat
pūrvavatsakalaṃ karma kuryāttatrāpi mohini || 63 ||
tilamiśrāṃstathā saktūnniḥkṣipetpretaparvate
ye kecitpretarūpeṇa vartaṃte pitaro mama || 64 ||
te sarve tṛptimāyāntu saktubhistilamiśritaiḥ
ābrahmastambaparyantaṃ yatkiñcitsacarācaram || 65 ||
mayā dattena piṇḍena tṛptimāyāntu sarvaśaḥ
ādau tu pañcatīrtheṣu cottare mānase vidhiḥ || 66 ||
ācamya kuśahastena śiraścābhyukṣya vāriṇā
uttaraṃ mānasaṃ gatvā mantreṇa snānamācaret || 67 ||
uttare mānase snānaṃ karomyātmaviśuddhaye
sūryalokādisaṃprāptisiddhaye pitṛmuktaye || 68 ||
snātvātha tarpaṇaṃ kuryāddevādīnāṃ yathāvidhi
ābrahmastambaparyantaṃ devarṣipitṛmānavāḥ || 69 ||
tṛpyantu pitaraḥ sarve mātṛmātāmahādayaḥ
śrārddhe sapiṇḍakaṃ kuryātsvasūtroktavidhānataḥ || 70 ||
aṣṭakāsu ca vṛddhau ca gayāyāṃ ca kṣaye'hani
mātuḥ śrāddhaṃ pṛthakkuryādanyatra svāminā saha || 71 ||
oṃnamo'stu bhānave bhartre somabhaumajñarūpiṇe
jīvabhārgavaśanaiścararāhuketusvarūpiṇe || 72 ||
sūryaṃ natvārcayitvā ca sūryalokaṃ nayetpitṛn
mānasaṃ hi saro hyatra tasmāduttaramānasam || 73 ||
uttarānmānasānmaunī vrajeddakṣiṇamānasam
udīcītīrthamityuktaṃ tatodīcyāṃ vimuktidam || 74 ||
udīcyāṃ muṇḍapṛṣṭhasya devarṣipitṛtarpaṇam
madhye kanakhalaṃ tīrthaṃ pitṝṇāṃ gatidāyakam || 75 ||
snātaḥ kanakavadbhāti naro yāti pavitratām
ataḥ kanakhalaṃ loke khyātaṃ tīrthamanuttamam || 76 ||
tasmāddakṣiṇabhāge tu tīrthaṃ dakṣiṇamānasam
dakṣiṇe mānase caiva tīrthatrayamudāthṛtam || 77 ||
snātvā teṣu vidhānena kuryācchrāddhaṃ pṛthak pṛthak
divākara karomīha snānaṃ dakṣiṇamānase || 78 ||
brahmahatyādipāpaughaghātanāya vimuktaye
anena snānapūjādi kuryācchrāddhaṃ sapiṇḍakam || 79 ||
namāmi sūryaṃ tṛptyarthaṃ pitṝṇāṃ tāraṇāya ca
putrapautradhanaiśvaryaāyurārogyavṛddhaye || 80 ||
dṛṣṭvā sampūjya maunārkamimaṃ mantramudīrayet
kavyavāḍādayo ye ca pitṝṇāṃ devatāstathā || 81 ||
madīyaiḥ pitṛbhiḥ sārddhaṃ tarpitāḥstha svadhābhujaḥ
phalgutīrthaṃ vrajettasmātsarvatīrthottamottamam || 82 ||
muktirbhavati kartṝṇāṃ pitṝṇāṃ śrāddhataḥ sadā
brahmaṇā prārthito viṣṇuḥ phalguko hyabhavatpurā || 83 ||
dakṣiṇāgnau kṛtaṃ nūnaṃ tadbhavaṃ phalgutīrthakam
yasminphalati phalgvāṃ gauḥ kāmadhenurjalaṃ mahī || 84 ||
sṛṣṭerantargataṃ yasmātphalgutīrthaṃ na niṣphalam
tīrthāni yāni sarvāṇi bhuvaneṣvakhileṣu ca || 85 ||
tāni snātuṃ samāyānti phalgutīrthaṃ na saṃśayaḥ
gaṅgā pādodakaṃ viṣṇoḥ phalguścādigadādharaḥ || 86 ||
himaṃ ca dra varūpeṇa tasmādgaṅgādhikaṃ viduḥ
aśvamedhasahasrāṇāṃ phalaṃ phalgujalāplavāt || 87 ||
phalgutīrthe viṣṇujale karomi snānamadya vai
pitṝṇāṃ viṣṇulokāya bhuktimuktiprasiddhaye || 88 ||
phalgutīrthe naraḥ snātvā tarpaṇaṃ śrāddhamācaret
sapiṇḍakaṃ svasūtroktaṃ namedatha pitāmaham || 89 ||
namaḥ śivāya devāya īśānapuruṣāya ca
aghoravāmadevāya sadyojātāya śambhave || 90 ||
natvā pitāmahaṃ devaṃ mantreṇānena pūjayet
phalgutīrthe naraḥ snātvā dṛṣṭvā devaṃ gadādharam || 91 ||
ānamya pitṛbhiḥ sārddhaṃ svaṃ nayedvaiṣṇavaṃ padam
oṃ namo vāsudevāya namaḥ saṅkarṣaṇāya ca || 92 ||
pradyumnāyāniruddhāya śrīdharāya ca viṣṇave
pañcatīrthyāṃ naraḥ snātvā brahmaloke nayetpitṝn || 93 ||
amṛtaiḥ pañcabhiḥ snātaṃ puṣpavastrādyalaṃkṛtam
na kuryādyo gadāpāṇiṃ tasya śrāddhamapārthakam || 94 ||
nāgakūṭādgṛdhrakūṭādviṣṇoścottaramānasāt
etadgayāśiraḥ proktaṃ phalgutīrthaṃ taducyate || 95 ||
muṇḍapṛṣṭhanagādhastātphalgutīrthamanuttamam
atra śrāddhādinā sarve pitaro mokṣamāpnuyuḥ || 96 ||
śamīpatrapramāṇena piṇḍaṃ dadyādgayāśire
yannāmnā pātayetpiṃḍaṃ taṃ nayedbrahma śāśvatam || 97 ||
avyaktarūpī yo devo muṇḍapṛṣṭhādri rūpataḥ
phalgutīrthādirūpeṇa namasyati gadādharam || 98 ||
śilāparvataphalgvādirūpeṇāvyaktamāsthitaḥ
gadādharādirūpeṇa vyaktamādidharastathā || 99 ||
dharmāraṇyaṃ tato gaccheddharmo yatra vyavasthitaḥ
mataṅgavāpyāṃ snātvā tu tarpaṇaṃ śrāddhamācaret || 100 ||
gatvā natvā mataṅgeśamimaṃ mantramudīrayet
pramāṇaṃ devatāḥ śambhurlokapālāśca sākṣiṇaḥ || 101 ||
mayāgatya mataṅge'sminpitṝṇāṃ niṣkṛtiḥ kṛtā
pūrvaṃ tu brahmatīrthe ca kūpe śrāddhādi kārayet || 102 ||
tatkūpayūpayormadhye kurvaṃstu trāyate pitṝn
dharmaṃ dharmeśvaraṃ natvā mahābodhitaruṃ namet || 103 ||
dvitīyadivase kṛtyaṃ mayā te samudāhṛtam
snānatarpaṇapiṇḍārcānatyādyaiḥ pitṛsaukhyadam || 104 ||
iti śrībṛhannāradīyapurāṇottarabhāge vasumohinīsaṃvāde gayāmāhātmye piṇḍadānavidhirnāma pañcacatvāriṃśattamo'dhyāyaḥ || 45 ||

<2.46>

athaṣaṭcatvāriṃśattamo'dhyāyaḥ
vasuruvāca -
atha te sampravakṣyāmi bhuktimuktipradāyakam
tṛtīyadivase kṛtyaṃ gayāsaṅgaphalapradam || 1 ||
snātvā tu brahmasarasi śrāddhaṃ kuryātsapiṇḍakam
snānaṃ karomi tīrthe'sminnṛṇatrayavimuktaye || 2 ||
śrāddhāya piṇḍadānāya tarpaṇāyārthasiddhaye
tatkūpayūpayormadhye kurvaṃstārayate pitṝn || 3 ||
snānaṃ kṛtvocchrito yūpo brahmaṇo yūpa ityuta
kṛtvā brahmasaraḥśrāddhaṃ brahmalokaṃ nayetpitṝn || 4 ||
gopracārasamīpasthā āmrā brahmaprakalpitāḥ
teṣāṃ secanamātreṇa pitaro mokṣagāminaḥ || 5 ||
āmraṃ brahmasarodbhūtaṃ sarvadevamayaṃ vibhum
viṣṇurūpaṃ prasiñcāmi pitṝṇāṃ caiva muktaye || 6 ||
eko muniḥ kumbhakuśāgrahasta āmrasya mūle salilaṃ dadāti
āmrāśca siktāḥ pitaraśca tṛptā ekā kriyā dvyarthakarī prasiddhā
ācamya mūle salilaṃ dadāno nopekṣaṇīyo vibudhairmanuṣyaḥ || 7 ||
yūpaṃ pradakṣiṇīkṛtya vājapeyaphalaṃ labhet
brahmāṇaṃ ca namaskṛtya pitṝn brahmapuraṃ nayet || 8 ||
oṃ namo brahmaṇe'jāya jagajjanmādikāriṇe
bhaktānāṃ ca pitṝṇāṃ ca tārakāya namo namaḥ || 9 ||
tato yamabali kṣiptvā mantreṇānena saṃyataḥ
yamarājadharmarājau niścalārthāviti sthitau || 10 ||
tābhyāṃ bali prayacchāmi pitṝṇāṃ muktihetave
tataḥ śvānabali kṛtvā pūrvamantreṇa mohini || 11 ||
tataḥ kākabali kuryānmantreṇānena saṃyataḥ
endra vāruṇavāyavyā yāmyā vai nairṛtāstathā || 12 ||
vāyasāḥ pratigṛhṇantu bhūmau piṇḍaṃ mayārpitam
tataḥ snānaṃ prakurvīta brahmatīrthe kuśānvitaḥ || 13 ||
evaṃ tṛtīyadivase samāpya niyamaṃ sudhīḥ
natvā gadādharaṃ devaṃ brahmacaryaparo bhavet || 14 ||
phalgutīrthe caturthe ca snānādikamathācaret
gayāśirasyatho śrāddhaṃ pade kuryātsapiṇḍakam || 15 ||
sākṣādgayāśirastatra phalgutīrthāśrayaṃ kṛtam
krauñcapādātphalgutīrthaṃ yāvatsākṣādgayāśiraḥ || 16 ||
gayāśire nagādyāśca sākṣāttatphalgutīrthakam
mukhaṃ gayāsurasyaitatsnātvā śrāddhaṃ samācaret || 17 ||
ādyo gadādharo devo vyaktāvyaktārthamāsthitaḥ
viṣṇvādipadarūpeṇa pitṝṇāṃ muktihetave || 18 ||
tatra viṣṇupadaṃ divyaṃ darśanātpāpanāśanam
sparśanātpūjanāccāpi pitṝṇāṃ mokṣadāyakam || 19 ||
śrāddhaṃ sapiṇḍakaṃ kṛtvā sahasrakulamātmanaḥ
viṣṇulokaṃ samuddhṛtya nayedviṣṇupade naraḥ || 20 ||
śrāddhaṃ kṛtvā rudra pade nayetkulaśataṃ naraḥ
sahātmanā śivapuraṃ tathā brahmapade śubhe || 21 ||
dakṣiṇāgripade śrāddhī vājapeyaphalaṃ labhet
gārhapatyapade śrāddhī rājasūyaphalaṃ labhet || 22 ||
śrāddhaṃ kṛtvā candra pade vājimedhaphalaṃ labhet
śrāddhaṃ kṛtvā satyapade jyotiṣṭomaphalaṃ labhet || 23 ||
āvasathyapade śrāddhī somalokamavāpnuyāt
śrāddhaṃ kṛtvā candra pade śakralokaṃ nayetpitṝn || 24 ||
anyeṣāṃ ca pade śrāddhī pitṝnbrahmapade nayet
śrāddhī sūryapade yaśca pāpino'kapuraṃ nayet || 25 ||
kārtikeyapade śrāddhī śivaloke nayetpitṝn
śrāddhaṃ kṛtvāgastyapade brahmalokaṃ nayetpitṝn || 26 ||
sarveṣāṃ kāśyapaṃ śreṣṭaṃ viṣṇo rudra sya vai padam
brahmaṇaśca padaṃ tatra sarvaśreṣṭhamudāhṛtam || 27 ||
prārambhe ca samāptau ca teṣāmanyatamaṃ smṛtam
śreyaskaraṃ bhavettatra śrāddhakartuśca mohini || 28 ||
kaśyapasya pade divyo bhāradvājo muniḥ purā
śrāddhaṃ hi codyato dātuṃ pitrādibhyaśca piṇḍakam || 29 ||
śuklakṛṣṇau tadā hastau padamudbhidya niṣkṛtau
dṛṣṭvā hastadvayaṃ tatra pitṛsaṃśayamāgataḥ || 30 ||
tataḥ svamātaraṃ śāntāṃ bhāradvājastu pṛṣṭavān
kaśyapasya pade kasmiñchukle kṛṣṇe pade punaḥ || 31 ||
piṇḍo deyo mayā mātarjānāsi pitaraṃ vada
tacchrutvā vacanaṃ tasya bhāradvājasya dhīmataḥ || 32 ||
śāntovāca prasannāsyā putraṃ śrāddhapradāyinam
bhāradvāja mahāprājña piṇḍaṃ kṛṣṇāya dehi bhoḥ || 33 ||
bhāradvājastataḥ piṇḍaṃ dātuṃ kṛṣṇāya codyataḥ
śveto dṛśyo'bravītputra dehi putro mamaurasaḥ || 34 ||
kṛṣṇo'bravīt kṣetrajastvaṃ tato me dehi piṇḍakam
śuklo'bravītsvauriṇīyaṃ yato'tastvaṃ mamaurasaḥ || 35 ||
svairiṇījo dadau cādau kṣetriṇe bījine tataḥ
tato bhaktyā mahābhāge datvā piṇḍānmahāmatiḥ || 36 ||
kṛtakṛtyaṃ nijātmānaṃ mene pratyakṣabhāṣaṇāt
bhīṣmo viṣṇupade śrāddhaṃ āhūya tu pitṝnsvakān || 37 ||
śrāddhaṃ kṛtvā vidhānena piṇḍadānāya codyataḥ
piturvinirgatau hastau gayāśirasi śantanoḥ || 38 ||
bhīṣmaḥ piṇḍaṃ dadau bhūmau nādhikāraḥ kare yataḥ
śaṃntanuḥ prāha santuṣṭaḥ śāstrārthe niścalo bhavān || 39 ||
trikāladarśī bhava ca viṣṇuścānte gatistava
svecchayā maraṇaṃ cāstu ityuktvā muktimāgataḥ || 40 ||
rāmo rudra padaṃ ramye piṇḍārpaṇakṛtodyamaḥ
pitā daśarathaḥ svargātprasārya karamāgataḥ || 41 ||
nādātpiṃḍaṃ kare rāmo dadau rudra pade tataḥ
śāstrārthātikramādbhīto rāmaṃ daśaratho'bravīt || 42 ||
tārito'ha tvayā putra rudra loko hyabhūnmama
pade piṇḍapradānena haste tu svargatirnahi || 43 ||
tvaṃ ca rājyaṃ ciraṃ kṛtvā pālayitvā nijāḥ prajāḥ
yajñānsadakṣiṇānkṛtvā viṣṇulokaṃ gamiṣyasi || 44 ||
sahāyodhyājanaiḥ sarvaiḥ kṛmikīṭādibhiḥ saha
ityuktvā sa nṛpo rāmaṃ rudra lokaṃ paraṃ yayau || 45 ||
kanakeśaṃ ca kedāraṃ nārasiṃhaṃ ca vāmanam
rathamārge samabhyarcya pitṝnsarvāṃśca tārayet || 46 ||
gayāśirasi yaḥ piṇḍaṃ yeṣāṃ nāmnā tu nirvapet
narakasthā divaṃ yāṃti svargasthā mokṣagāminaḥ || 47 ||
gayāśirasi yaḥ piḍaṃ śamīpatrapramāṇataḥ
kandamūlaphalādyairvā dadyātsvargaṃ nayetpitṝn || 48 ||
padāni yatra dṛśyante viṣṇvādīnāṃ tadagrataḥ
śrāddhaṃ kṛtvā pade yeṣāṃ teṣāṃ lokānnayetpitṝn || 49 ||
sarvatra muṇḍapṛṣṭhādriḥ padairebhiḥ sa lakṣitaḥ
prayāṃti pitarastatra pūjitā brahmaṇaḥ padam || 50 ||
gayāsurasya tu śiro gadayā yaddvidhā kṛtam
yataḥ prakṣālitā tīrthe gadālolastadā smṛtaḥ || 51 ||
krauñcarūpeṇa hi munirmuṇḍapṛṣṭhe tapo'karot
tasya pādāṅkako yasmātkrauñcapādaḥ smṛtastataḥ || 52 ||
viṣṇvādīnāṃ padānyatra liṅgarūpasthitāni ca
devāditarpaṇaṃ kṛtvā śrāddhaṃ rudra padāditaḥ || 53 ||
caturthadivase kṛtyametatkṛtvā tu mohini
pūtaḥ karmādhikārī syācchrāddhakṛdbrahmalokabhāk || 54 ||
śilāsthiteṣu tīrtheṣu snātvā kṛtvātha tarpaṇam
śrāddhaṃ sapiṇḍakaṃ yeṣāṃ brahmalokaṃ prayāṃti te || 55 ||
sthāsanti ca ramiṣanti yāvadābhūtasamplavam
dehaṃ tyaktvā śilāpṛṣṭhe svedajāṇḍajarāyujāḥ || 56 ||
gacchaṃti viṣṇusāyujyaṃ kulaiḥ saptaśataiḥ saha || 57 ||
iti śrībṛhannāradīyapurāṇottarabhāge vasumohinīsaṃvāde gayāmāhātmye viṣṇvādipade piṇḍadānamāhatmyakathanaṃ nāma ṣaṭcatcāriṃśattamo'dhyāyaḥ || 46 ||

<2.47>

athasaptacatvāriṃśattamo'dhyāyaḥ
vasuruvāca -
pañcame'hni gadālole kṛtvā snānādi pūrvavat
śrāddhaṃ sapiṇḍakaṃ kuryāttato'kṣayavaṭe naraḥ || 1 ||
tatra śrāddhādikaṃ kṛtvā pitṝnbrahmapuraṃ nayet
brahmaprakalpitān viprānbhojayetpūjayedatha || 2 ||
kṛtaśrāddho'kṣayavaṭe anenaiva prayatnataḥ
dṛṣṭvā natvātha sampūjya vaṭeśaṃ ca samāhitaḥ || 3 ||
pitṝnnayetad brahmapuramakṣayaṃ tu sanātanam
gadālole mahātīrthe gadāprakṣālane vare || 4 ||
snānaṃ karomi śurddhythamakṣayyāya svarāptaye
ekāntare vaṭasyāgre yaḥ śete yoganidra yā || 5 ||
bālarūpadharastasmai namaste yogaśāyine
saṃsāravṛkṣaśastrāyāśeṣapāpakṣayāya ca || 6 ||
akṣayyabrahmadātre ca namo'kṣayyavaṭāya vai
kalau māheśvarā lokā yena tasmād gadādharaḥ || 7 ||
liṅgarūpo'bhavattaṃ ca vande tvāṃ prapitāmaham
nayetpitṝn rudra padaṃ natvā taṃ prapitāmaham || 8 ||
hetiṃ hatvāsuraṃ tasya śiraścaiva dvidhā kṛtam
gadayā sā gadā yatra kṣālitā prabhuṇā'bhavat || 9 ||
gadālolamiti khyātaṃ tattīrthapravaraṃ hyabhūt
hetī rakṣo brahmaputrastapastepe'dbhutaṃ mahat || 10 ||
brahmādīṃstapasā tuṣṭānvaraṃ vavre varapradān
daityādibhiśca śastrādyairvividhairmanujairapi || 11 ||
kṛṣṇeśānādicakrādyravadhyaḥ syāṃ mahābalaḥ
tathetyuktvāntarhitāste hetirdevānathājayat || 12 ||
indra tvamakaroddhetistadā brahmaharādayaḥ
devā hariṃ prapannāstamūcurhetiṃ jahīti ca || 13 ||
ūce hariravadhyo'ya hetirdevāḥ surāsuraiḥ
brahmāstraṃ me prayacchaddhvaṃ hetiṃ hanyāṃ hi yena tam || 14 ||
ityuktāste tato devā viṣṇave tāṃ gadāṃ daduḥ
upendra tvaṃ jahītyeva hetiṃ procurajādayaḥ || 15 ||
dadhāra tāṃ gadāmādau devairukto gadādharaḥ
gadayā hetimāhatya devebhyastridivaṃ dadau || 16 ||
upoṣito'tha gāyatrītīrthe mahānadīsthite
gāyatryāḥ purataḥ snātastataḥ sandhyāṃ samācaret || 17 ||
śrāddhaṃ sapiṇḍakaṃ kṛtvā nayedbrāhmaṇatāṃ kulam
tīrthe samudyate snātvā sāvitryāḥ purato naraḥ || 18 ||
sandhyāmupāsya madhyāhne nayetpitṝnvidhikṣayam
prācīsarasvatīsnātaḥ sarasvatyāstato'grataḥ || 19 ||
sandhyāmupāsya sāyāhne nayetsarvajñatāṃ kulam
bahujanmakṛtātsandhyālopapāpādviśuddhyti || 20 ||
viśālāyāṃ lilehāne tīrthe ca bharatāśrame
padāṅkite muṇḍapṛṣṭhe gadādharasamīpataḥ || 21 ||
tīrtha ākāśagaṅgāyāṃ girikarṇamukheṣu ca
śrāddhadaḥ piṇḍado brahmalokaṃ pitṛśataṃ nayet || 22 ||
snāto godāvaitaraṇyāṃ triḥsaptakulamuddharet
devanadyāṃ gopracāre tathā mānasake pade || 23 ||
puṣkariṇyāṃ gadālole tīrthe cāmarake tathā
koṭitīrthe rukmakuṇḍe piṇḍadaḥ svarnayetpitṝn || 24 ||
mārkaṇḍeyeśakoṭīśau natvā syātpitṛtārakaḥ
tathā pāṇḍuśilāyāstu puṇyadāyāḥ sulocane || 25 ||
dṛṣṭimātreṇa sampūtānnarakasthāndivaṃ nayet
ityuktvā prayayau pāṇḍuḥ śāśvataṃ padamavyayam || 26 ||
ghṛtakulyā madhukulyā devikā ca mahānadī
śilāyāṃ saṅgatā tatra madhusravā prakīrtitā || 27 ||
ayutaṃ hyaśvamedhānāṃ snānena labhate naraḥ
tarpayityā pitṛgaṇaṃ śrāddhaṃ kṛtvā sapiṇḍakam || 28 ||
sahasrakulamuddhṛtya nayedviṣṇupuraṃ naraḥ
udbhijjāḥ svedajā vāpi hyaṇḍajā ye jarāyujāḥ || 29 ||
madhusravāṃ samāsādya mṛtā viṣṇupadaṃ yayuḥ
daśāśvamedhike haṃsatīrthe śrāddhāddivaṃ vrajet || 30 ||
daśāśvamedhahaṃsau ca natvā śivapuraṃ vrajet
mataṅgasya pade śrāddhakartā brahmapure vaset || 31 ||
nirmathyāgnīnśamīgarbhe vidhirviṣṇvādibhiḥ saha
manthokuṇḍaṃ hi tattīrthaṃ pitṝṇāṃ muktikārakam || 32 ||
tarpaṇātpiṃḍadānācca snānakṛnmuktimāpnuyāt
pitṝnsvargaṃ nayennatvā rāmeśakarakeśvarau || 33 ||
gayākūpe piṇḍadānādaśvamedhaphalaṃ labhet
bhasmakūṭe bhasmanā'tha snānāttārayate pitṝn || 34 ||
dhautapāpo'tha niḥkṣīrāsaṅgame snānakṛnnaraḥ
śrāddhī rāmapuṣkariṇyāṃ brahmalokaṃ nayetpitṝn || 35 ||
suṣumnāyāṃ mahānadyāṃ triḥsaptakulamuddharet
snāto natvā vasiṣṭheśaṃ tasya tīrthe'śvamedhabhāk || 36 ||
piṇḍado dhenukāraṇye kāmadhenupadeṣu ca
snāto natvā tu taṃ devaṃ brahmalokaṃ nayetpitṝn || 37 ||
kardamāle gayānābhau muṇḍapṛṣṭhasamīpataḥ
snātvā śrāddhī nayetsvargaṃ pitṝnnatvā ca caṇḍikām || 38 ||
phalgucaṇḍīśanāmānaṃ saṅgamādhīśamarcya ca
gayāgajo gayādityo gāyatrī ca gadādharaḥ || 39 ||
gayā gayāśiraścaiva ṣaḍgayā muktidāyikāḥ
gayāyāṃ tu vṛṣotsargāttriḥsaptakulamuddharet || 40 ||
yatra tatra sthito vipragadito vijitendri yaḥ
ādyaṃ gadādharaṃ dhyāyan śrāddhapiṃḍāni kārayet || 41 ||
kulānāṃ śatamuddhṛtya brahmalokaṃ nayeddhruvam
tato dadhyodanenaiva datvā naivedyamuttamam || 42 ||
janārdanāya devāya samabhyarcya yathāvidhi
dadyānnikṣipya piṇḍāṃstu taccheṣeṇaiva jīvati || 43 ||
daityasya muṇḍapṛṣṭhe tu yasmātsā saṃsthitā śilā
tasmādvai muṇḍapṛṣṭhādriḥ pitṝṇāṃ brahmalokadaḥ || 44 ||
rāme vanaṃ gate śailamāruhya bharataḥ sthitaḥ
pitre piṇḍādikaṃ datvā rāmeśaṃ sthāpya tatra ca || 45 ||
snātvā natvā ca rāmeśaṃ rāmaṃ sītāṃ samāhitaḥ
śrāddhaṃ piṇḍapradānaṃ ca kṛtvāviṣṇupuraṃ vrajet || 46 ||
pitṛbhiḥ saha dharmātmā kulānāṃ ca śataiḥ saha
śilādakṣiṇahaste ca sthāpitaḥ kuṇḍapṛṣṭhataḥ || 47 ||
tatra śrāddhādinā sarvānpitṝnbrahmapuraṃ nayet
kuṇḍenātha tapastaptaṃ sītādre rdakṣiṇe nage || 48 ||
mataṅgasya pade puṇye piṇḍadaḥ svarnayetpitṝn
vāmahaste śilāyāśca hyantako vidhṛto giriḥ || 49 ||
udayādri rihānīto hyagastyena mahātmanā
sthāpitaḥ piṇḍadastatra pitṝnbrahmapuraṃ nayet || 50 ||
kuṇḍamudyantakaṃ tatra svātmanastapase kṛtam
brahmā tatra ca sāvitrī kumārābhyāṃ sthitastviha || 51 ||
hāhāhuhūprabhṛtayo gītaṃ vādyaṃ pracakramuḥ
snāto'gastye ca madhyāhne sāvitrīṃ samupāsya ca || 52 ||
koṭijanma bhavedvipro dhanāḍhyo vedadapāragaḥ
agastyasya pade snātaḥ piṇḍadaḥ svarnayetpitṝn || 53 ||
brahmayoniṃ praviśyātha nirgacchedyastu mānavaḥ
paraṃ brahma sa yātīha vimukto yonisaṅkaṭāt || 54 ||
natvā gayākumāraṃ ca brāhmaṇyaṃ labhate naraḥ
somakuṇḍābhiṣekādyaiḥ somalokaṃ nayetpitṝn || 55 ||
bali kākaśilāyāṃ tu kākebhyaḥ kṣaṇamokṣadaḥ
svargadvāreśvaraṃ natvā svargādbrahmapuraṃ nayet || 56 ||
piṇḍado vyomagaṅgāyāṃ nirmalaḥ svarnayetpitṝn
śilāyā dakṣiṇe haste bhasmakūṭamadhārayat || 57 ||
dharmmo'tastatra ca harastannāma samakārayat
yatrāsau bhasmakūṭādri rbhasmanāmā tu mohini || 58 ||
vaṭo vareśvarastatra sthitaśca prapitāmahaḥ
tadagre rukmiṇīkuṇḍaṃ paścime kapilā nadī || 59 ||
kapileśo nadītīre umāsomasamāgamaḥ
kapilāyāṃ naraḥ snātvā kapileśaṃ namedyajet || 60 ||
śrāddhadaḥ svargabhāgī syānmaheśīkuṇḍa eva ca
gaurī ca maṅgalā tatra sarvasaubhāgyadārcitatā || 61 ||
janārdano bhasmakūṭe tasya haste tu piṇḍadaḥ
mantreṇa cātmano'nyeṣāṃ savyenāpi tilairvinā || 62 ||
piṇḍaṃ ca dadhisaṃmiśraṃ sarve te viṣṇulokagāḥ
eṣa piṇḍo mayā dattastava haste janārdana || 63 ||
gayāśrāddhe tvayā deyo mahyaṃ piṇḍo mṛte mayi
tubhyaṃ piṇḍo mayā datto yamuddiśya janārdana || 64 ||
dehi deva gayāśīrṣe tasmai tasmai mṛte tataḥ
janārdana namastubhyaṃ namaste pitṛrūpiṇe || 65 ||
pitṛpātra namastubhyaṃ namaste muktihetave
gayāyāṃ pitṛrūpeṇa svayameva janārdanaḥ || 66 ||
taṃ dṛṣṭvā puṃḍarīkākṣaṃ mucyate ca ṛṇatrayāt
namaste puṃḍarīkākṣa ṛṇatrayavimocana || 67 ||
lakṣmīkānta namaste'stu namaste pitṛmokṣada
puṇḍarīkākṣamabhyarcya svargagaḥ syājjanārdanam || 68 ||
vāmajānuṃ tu saṃpātya natvā bhūmiṃ janārdanam
śrāddhaṃ sapiṇḍakaṃ kṛtvā bhrātṛbhirviṣṇulokabhāk || 69 ||
śilāyā vāmapāde tu pretakūṭo girirdhṛtaḥ
dharmarājena pāpāḍhyo giriḥ pretaśilāmayaḥ || 70 ||
pādena dūre nikṣiptaḥ śilāyāḥ pādabhārataḥ
pretā dhānuṣkarūpeṇa karagrahaṇakārakāḥ || 71 ||
pṛthak sthitāśca bahavo vighnakāriṇa eva te
śrāddhādikāriṇaṃ nṝṇāṃ tīrthe pitṛvimuktaye || 72 ||
gataḥ śilādri samparkātpretakūṭaḥ pavitratām
pretakuṇḍaṃ tu tatrāste devāstatra padaiḥ sthitāḥ || 73 ||
śrāddhapiṇḍādikṛtsnātaḥ pretatvānmocayetpitṝn
kīkaṭeṣu gayā puṇyā puṇyaṃ rājagṛhaṃ vanam || 74 ||
cyavanasyāśramaḥ puṇyo nadī puṇyā punaḥ punā
vaikuṇṭho lohadaṇḍaśca girikūṭaśca śoṇagaḥ || 75 ||
śrāddhapiṇḍādikṛttatra pitṝnbrahmapuraṃ nayet
śilādakṣiṇapāde tu gṛdhrakūṭo girirdhṛtaḥ || 76 ||
dharmarājena svasthairyakaraṇāyāśu pāvanaḥ
gṛdhrarūpeṇa saṃsiddhāstapaḥ kṛtvā maharṣayaḥ || 77 ||
ato girirgṛdhrakūṭastatra gṛdhreśvaraḥ śivaḥ
dṛṣṭvā gṛdhreśvaraṃ snātvā yāti śambhoḥ puraṃ naraḥ || 78 ||
tatra gṛdhrapuraṃ gatvā prāptakālo divaṃ vrajet
ṛṇamokṣaṃ pāpamokṣaṃ śivaṃ dṛṣṭvā śivaṃ vrajet || 79 ||
ādipādena giriṇā samākrāntaṃ śilodakam
tatrāste gajarūpeṇa vighneśo vighnanāśanaḥ || 80 ||
taṃ dṛṣṭvā mucyate vighnaiḥ pitṝñchivapuraṃ nayet
gāyatrīṃ ca gayādityaṃ snāto dṛṣṭvā divaṃ vrajet || 81 ||
brāhmaṇaṃ cādipādasthaṃ dṛṣṭvā syātpitṛtārakaḥ
nābhau ca piṇḍado yastu pitṝnbrahmapuraṃ nayet || 82 ||
śobhārthe muṇḍapṛṣṭhasya aravindavaraṃ tvabhūt
muṇḍapṛṣṭhāravinde ca dṛṣṭvā pāpairvimucyate || 83 ||
śṛṅgibhirdaṃṣṭribhirvyālairviṣavahnistriyā jalaiḥ
sudūrātparihartavyaḥ kurvan krīḍāṃ mṛtastu yaḥ || 84 ||
nāgānāṃ vipriyaṃ kurvanhataścāpyatha vidyutaḥ
nigṛhītaḥ svayaṃ rājñā cauryadoṣeṇa ca kvacit || 85 ||
paradārān ramantaśca dveṣāttatpatibhirhatāḥ
asamānaiśca saṅkīrṇaiścāṇḍālādyaiśca vigraham || 86 ||
kṛtvā tairnihatāstāṃśca cāṃḍālādīnsamāśritāḥ
gavāgniviṣadāścaiva pākhaṇḍāḥ krūrabuddhayaḥ || 87 ||
krodhātprāyo viṣaṃ vahniṃ śastramudbandhanaṃ jalam
girivṛkṣātprapātaṃ ca ye kurvaṃti narādhamāḥ || 88 ||
kuśilpajīvino ye ca pañcasūnādhikāriṇaḥ
makhe sabhāsu ye keciddīnaprāyā napuṃsakāḥ || 89 ||
brahmadaṇḍahatā ye tu ye cāpi brāhmaṇairhatāḥ
mahāpātakino ye ca patitāste prakīrtitāḥ || 90 ||
snānena śuddhimāyāṃti gayākūpasya bhasmanā
iti te kathitaṃ devi gayāmāhātmyamuttamam || 91 ||
sarvapāpapraśamanaṃ pitṝṇāṃ muktidāyakam
yaḥ śṛṇoti naro bhaktyā śrāddhe parvaṇi vānvaham || 92 ||
śrāvayedvā varārohe so'pi syādbrahmalokabhāk
idaṃ svastyayanaṃ puṇyaṃ dhanyaṃ svargatidaṃ nṛṇām || 93 ||
yaśasyamapi cāyuṣyaṃ putrapautravivarddhanam || 94 ||
iti śrībṛhannāradīyapurāṇottarabhāge mohinīvasusaṃvāde gayāmāhātmyaṃ nāma saptacatvāriṃśattamo'dhyāyaḥ || 47 ||

<2.48>

athāṣṭacatvāriṃśattamo'dhyāyaḥ
māndhātovāca -
bhagavansamyagākhyātaṃ sarvajñena kṛpālunā
mohinīcaritaṃ puṇyaṃ mahāpātakanāśanam || 1 ||
patiṃ putraṃ sapatnīṃ ca yā prasahya bhavārṇavāt
mocayāmāsa dharmasya rakṣaṇe piturājñayā || 2 ||
sā brahmaputrī sarvajñā sarvalokahite ratā
purodhasaṃ ca saṃprāptā śaraṇaṃ prabhumātmanaḥ || 3 ||
śrutvā gayāyā māhātmyaṃ pitṝṇāṃ gatidaṃ param
bhūyaḥ papraccha kiṃ vipraṃ vasuṃ vedavidāṃvaram || 4 ||
vasiṣṭha uvāca -
śṛṇu bhūpa pravakṣyāmi yadapṛcchatpunarvasum
mohinī mohamāpannā tīrthasevanakāmukā || 5 ||
mohinyuvāca -
sādhu sādhu dvijaśreṣṭha lokoddharaṇatatpara
tvayā hyanugṛhītāhamadhunā karuṇātmanā || 6 ||
śrutaṃ puṇyaṃ mayā brahman gayāmahātmyamuttamam
gopyaṃ pitṝṇāṃ gatidaṃ dharmākhyānaṃ sukhāvaham || 7 ||
adhunā vada viprenda kāśīmāhātmyamuttamam
mayā pūrvaṃ śrutaṃ brahman kiñcitsandhyāvalīmukhāt || 8 ||
tena me smṛtimāpannaṃ vistarādvada sāmpratam
vasiṣṭha uvāca -
tacchrutvā mohinī vākyaṃ vasustasyāḥ purohitaḥ || 9 ||
vedavedāṅgatattvajñaḥ prāha tāṃ śrūyatāmiti
vasuruvāca -
śubhā kāśīpurī dhanyā dhanyo devo maheśvaraḥ || 10 ||
yaḥ sevate'niśaṃ kāśīṃ muktidāṃ vaiṣṇavīṃ purīm
yācayitvā hareḥ kṣetraṃ sthito devaḥ sanātanaḥ || 11 ||
pūjayaṃstaṃ hṛṣīkeśaṃ pūjyamānaḥ surādibhiḥ || 12 ||
vārāṇasī tu bhuvanatrayasārabhūtā
ramyā nṛṇāṃ sugatidā kila sevyamānā
atrāgatā vividhaduṣkṛtakāriṇo'pi
pāpakṣaye virajasaḥ sumanaḥprakāśāḥ || 13 ||
idaṃ guhyatamaṃ kṣetraṃ sarvaprāṇisukhāvaham
mokṣadaṃ sarvajantūnāṃ vaiṣṇavaṃ śaivameva ca || 14 ||
brahmaghnagoghnagurutalpagamitradhruk ca
nyāsāpahārakuśidādiniṣiddhavṛttiḥ
saṃsārabhūtadṛḍhapāśavimuktadeho
vārāṇasīṃ śivapurīṃ samupaiti martyaḥ || 15 ||
kṣetraṃ tathedaṃ surasiddhajuṣṭaṃ
samprāpya martyaḥ sukṛtaprabhāvāt
khyāto bhavetsarvasurāsurāṇāṃ
mṛtaśca yāyātparamaṃ padaṃ saḥ || 16 ||
kṣetre'sminnivasaṃti ye sukṛtino bhaktā harau vā hare
paśyanto'nvahamādareṇa śucayaḥ santaḥ samāḥ śambhunā
te martyā bhayaduḥkhapāparahitāḥ saṃśuddhakarmakriyāḥ
bhitvā sambhavabandhajālagahanaṃ vindaṃti mokṣaṃ param || 17 ||
dviyojanamathārddhaṃ ca pūrvapaścimataḥ sthitam
arddhayojanavistīrṇaṃ dakṣiṇottarataḥ smṛtam || 18 ||
varaṇāsirnadī yāvadasiḥ śuṣkanadī śubhe
eṣa kṣetrasya vistāraḥ prokto devena śaṃbhunā || 19 ||
ayanaṃ tūttaraṃ jñeyaṃ timicaṇḍeśvaraṃ tataḥ
dakṣiṇaṃ śaṅkukarṇaṃ tu oṃkāre tadanantaram || 20 ||
piṅgalā nāma yattīrthaṃ āgneyī sā prakīrtitā
śuṣkā saricca sā jñeyā lolārko yatra tiṣṭhati || 21 ||
iḍānāmnī tu yā nāḍī sā saumyā samprakīrtitā
varaṇā nāma sā jñeyā keśavo yatra saṃsthitaḥ || 22 ||
ābhyāṃ madhye tu yā nāḍī suṣumṇā sā prakīrtitā
matsyodarī ca sā jñeyā visvaraṃ tatprakīrtitam || 23 ||
vimuktaṃ na kadā yasmānmokṣyate na kadācana
mahākṣetramidaṃ tasmādavimuktamidaṃ smṛtam || 24 ||
prayāgādapi tīrthāderadhikaṃ dustarācchubhe
anāyāsena vai yatra mokṣaprāptiḥ prajāyate || 25 ||
nānāvarṇā vikarṇāśca cāṇḍālā ye jugupsitāḥ
kilbiṣaiḥ pūrṇadehāśca prakṛṣṭaiḥ pātakaistathā || 26 ||
bheṣajaṃ paramaṃ teṣāmavimuktaṃ vidurbudhāḥ
duṣṭāndhān dīnakṛpaṇānpāpānduṣkṛtakāriṇaḥ || 27 ||
haro'nukampayā sarvānnayatyāśu parāṃ gatim
kṣetramadhyādyadā gaṅgā saṅgatā saritāṃ patim || 28 ||
tataḥ prabhṛti sā puṇyā purī jātā śubhānane
puṇyā codaṅmukhī gaṅgā prācī caiva sarasvatī || 29 ||
tatra muktaṃ kapālaṃ tu śivena sumahātmanā
tasmiṃstīrthe tu ye gatvā piṇḍadānena vai pitṝn || 30 ||
śrāddheṣu prīṇayiṣyanti teṣāṃ lokāstu bhāsvarāḥ
brahmahā yo'bhigacchettu avimuktaṃ kadācana || 31 ||
tasya kṣetrasya māhātmyādbrahmahatyā nivartate
avimuktaṃ gatā ye vai mahāpuṇyakṛto narāḥ || 32 ||
akṣayyā hyajarāścaiva videhāśca bhavaṃti te
ājñānājjñānato vāpi striyā vā puruṣeṇa vā || 33 ||
yatkiñcidaśubhaṃ karma kṛtaṃ caiva kubuddhinā
avimuktaṃ praviṣṭasya tatsarvaṃ bhasmasādbhavet || 34 ||
sadā yajati yajñena sadā dānaṃ prayacchati
sadā tapasvī bhavati hyavimukte sthito naraḥ || 35 ||
na sā gatiḥ kurukṣetre gaṅgādvāre na puṣkare
yā gatirvihitā puṃsāmavimuktanivāsinām || 36 ||
sarvātmanā tapaḥ satyaṃ prāṇināṃ nātra saṃśayaḥ
avimukte vasedyastu sa tu sākṣānmaheśvaraḥ || 37 ||
avimuktaṃ na sevaṃte ye mūḍhāstāmasā narāḥ
viṇmūtrarajasāṃ madhye te vasaṃti punaḥ punaḥ || 38 ||
avimukte sthitā nityaṃ pāṃśubhirvāyuneritaiḥ
spṛṣṭā duṣkṛtakarmāṇo yāṃti vai paramāṃ gatim || 39 ||
yastatra nivasenmartyaḥ saṃyatātmā samāhitaḥ
trailokyamapi bhuñjāno vāyubhakṣasamaḥ smṛtaḥ || 40 ||
tatra māsaṃ vasedyastu labdhāhāro jitendri yaḥ
samyaktena vrataṃ cīrṇaṃ mahāpāśupataṃ bhavet || 41 ||
janmamṛtyubhayaṃ jitvā sa yāti paramāṃ gatim
niḥśreyasagatiṃ puṇyāṃ tathā yogagatiṃ labhet || 42 ||
nahi yogagatirlabhyā janmāntaraśatairapi
prāpyate kṣetramāhātmyātprabhāvācchaṅkarasya ca || 43 ||
ekāhārastu yastiṣṭhenmāsaṃ tatra śubhānane
yāvajjīvakṛtaṃ pāpaṃ māsenaikena naśyati || 44 ||
ādehapātādyo martyo'vimuktaṃ naiva muñcati
brahmacaryeṇa saṃyuktaḥ sa sākṣācchaṃkaro bhavet || 45 ||
vighnairāhanyamāno'pi yo'vimuktaṃ na ca tyajet
sa muñcati jarāmṛtyuṃ janma caitacca naśvaram || 46 ||
ādehapatanādye tu sevaṃte hyavimuktakam
te mṛtā haṃsayānena divyān lokānprayāṃti hi || 47 ||
viṣayāsaktacitto'pi tyaktabhaktimatirnaraḥ
iha kṣetre mṛtaḥ so'pi saṃsāraṃ na punarviśet || 48 ||
svargāpavargayorheturetattīrthavaraṃ bhuvi
yastatra pañcatāṃ yāti tasya muktirna saṃśayaḥ || 49 ||
janmāntarasahasreṇa yogī yatpadamāpnuyāt
tadihaiva paraṃ mokṣaṃ maraṇādadhigacchati || 50 ||
brāhmaṇāḥ kṣattriyā vaiśyāḥ śūdrā vai varṇasaṅkarāḥ
krimayaścaiva ye mlecchāḥ saṅkīrṇāḥ pāpayonayaḥ || 51 ||
kīṭāḥ pipīlikāścaiva ye cānye mṛgapakṣiṇaḥ
kālena nidhanaṃ prāptāste'pi deveśvarāḥ smṛtāḥ || 52 ||
candrā rddhamaulayaḥ sarve lalāṭākṣā bṛṣadhvajāḥ
prāṇāṃstyajanti ye tatra prāṇinastattvataḥ śubhe || 53 ||
rudra tvaṃ te tu samprāpya modaṃte śivasannidhau
akāmo vā sakāmo vā tiryagyonigato'pi vā || 54 ||
avimukte tyajanprāṇānmuktibhāksyānna saṃśayaḥ
śivabhaktiparā nityaṃ nānyabhaktāśca ye narāḥ || 55 ||
taccittāstadgataprāṇā jīvanmuktā na saṃśayaḥ
agnipraveśaṃ ye kuryuravimukte vicārataḥ || 56 ||
kālāgnirudra sāyujyaṃ te prayānti ca mohini
kurvantyanaśanaṃ ye tu śivabhaktāḥ suniścitāḥ || 57 ||
na teṣāṃ punarāvṛttiḥ kalpakoṭiśatairapi
avimukte mṛtyukāle bhūtānāmīśvaraḥ svayam || 58 ||
karmabhiḥ preryamāṇānāṃ karṇajāpyaṃ prayacchati
svayaṃ rāmeṇa cāpyuktaṃ śivāya śivakāriṇe || 59 ||
atiprasannacittena avimuktanivāsine
mumūrṣordakṣiṇe karṇe yasya kasyāpi vā svayam || 60 ||
upadekṣyasi manmantraṃ sa mukto bhavitā śiva
antakāle manuṣyāṇāṃ chidyamāneṣu karmasu || 61 ||
vāyunā preryamāṇānāṃ smṛtirnaivopajāyate
ye'vimukte sthitā rudrā bhaktaprītipradāyakāḥ || 62 ||
karṇajāpyaṃ prayacchanti ḍimicaṇḍeśvarādayaḥ
nāvimukte mṛtaḥ kaścinnarakaṃ yāti kilbiṣī || 63 ||
īśvarānugṛhītā hi sarve yāṃti parāṃ gatim
uddeśamātrātkathitā avimuktaguṇāstava || 64 ||
samudra syaiva ratnānāmavimuktasya vistaraḥ
jñānavijñānaniṣṭhānāṃ paramānandamicchatām || 65 ||
yā matirvihitā nūnaṃ svarnnīte tu mṛtasya sā
prāṇāniha narastyaktvā na punarjāyate kvacit || 66 ||
anantā sā gatistasya yogināmeva yā smṛtā
yogapīṭhaṃ śmaśānākhyaṃ yattīrthaṃ maṇikarṇikā || 67 ||
teṣu muktiḥ samuddiṣṭā patitānāṃ svakarmaṇā
tatrāpi sarvatīrthānāmuttamā maṇikarṇikā || 68 ||
yatra nityaṃ varārohe sānnidhyaṃ dhūrjaṭeḥ smṛtam
daśānāmaśvamedhānāṃ yajñānāṃ yatphalaṃ smṛtam || 69 ||
tadavāpnoti dharmātmā tatra snātvā varānane
svasvamapyatra yo dadyād brāhmaṇe vedapārage || 70 ||
śubhāṃ gatimavāpnoti hutāśa iva dīpyate
upavāsaṃ tu yaḥ kṛtvā viprānsantarpayennaraḥ || 71 ||
sa sautrāmaṇiyajñasya phalamāpnoti niścitam
tatra dīpapradānena jñānavatsphuratīndri yam || 72 ||
prāpnoti dhūpadānena sthānaṃ rudra niṣevitam
vṛṣabhaṃ taruṇaṃ saumyaṃ caturvatsatarīyutam || 73 ||
yo'ṅkayitvā mocayati sa yāti paramāṃ gatim
pitṛbhiḥ sahito mokṣaṃ gacchatyeva na saṃśayaḥ || 74 ||
kimatra bahunoktena dharmādīṃstu prakurvataḥ
yacchivaṃ tu samuddiśya tadanantaphalaṃ bhavet || 75 ||
daśāśvamedhikaṃ puṇyaṃ puṣpadāne prakīrtitam
agnihotraphalaṃ dhūpe gandhe bhūdānajaṃ phalam || 76 ||
mārjane gopradānasya phalamatra prakīrtitam
anulepe daśaguṇaṃ mālye daśaguṇaṃ smṛtam || 77 ||
gīte sahasraguṇitaṃ vādye lakṣaguṇaṃ smṛtam
avimukte mahādevamarcanti stuvaṃti vai || 78 ||
sarvapāpavimuktāste svastiṣṭhaṃtyajarāmarāḥ
avimuktaṃ samāsādya liṅgamarcayate naraḥ || 79 ||
kalpakoṭiśataiścāpi tasya nāsti punarbhavaḥ
ajaro hyamaraścaiva krīḍetsa bhavasannidhau || 80 ||
ye tu dhyānaṃ samāsādya muktātmānaḥ samāhitāḥ
sanniyamyendri yagrāmaṃ japaṃti śatarudri yam || 81 ||
avimukte sthitā nityaṃ kṛtārthāste dvijottamāḥ
ekāhamupavāsaṃ yaḥ kariṣyati yaśasvini || 82 ||
phalaṃ varṣaśatasyeha labhate nātra saṃśayaḥ
ataḥ paraṃ tu sāyujyaṃ gaṅgāvaruṇasaṅgamam || 83 ||
śravaṇadvādaśīyogo budhavāre yadā bhavet
tadā tasminnaraḥ snātvā sannihatyāphalaṃ labhet || 84 ||
śrāddhaṃ karoti yastatra tasminkāle śubhānane
tārayitvā pitṝnsarvānviṣṇulokaṃ sa gacchati || 85 ||
varaṇāsyostu jāhnavyāḥ saṅgame lokaviśrute
datvāśvaṃ ca vidhānena sa bhūyo'pi na jāyate || 86 ||
yastatra saṅgameśānamarcayedbhaktimānnaraḥ
sa sākṣāddevadeveśo nigrahānugrahe kṣamaḥ || 87 ||
deveśvarasya pūrveṇa svayaṃ tiṣṭhati keśavaḥ
keśavasya ca pūrveṇa viśrutaḥ saṅgameśvaraḥ || 88 ||
iti śrībṛhannāradīyapurāṇottarabhāge vasumohinīsaṃvāde kāśīmāhātmyaṃ nāmāṣṭacatvāriṃśattamo'dhyāyaḥ || 48 ||

<2.49>

athaikonapañcāśattamo'dhyāyaḥ
vasuruvāca -
vāyavye tu diśo bhāge tasya pīṭhasya sundari
liṅgaṃ prasthāpitaṃ tatra sagareṇa caturmukham || 1 ||
sāgarādvāyukoṇe tu bhadra dehaṃ saraḥ smṛtam
gavāṃ kṣīreṇa sañjātaṃ sarvapātakanāśanam || 2 ||
kapilānāṃ sahasrasya samyagdattasya yatphalam
tatphalaṃ labhate martyaḥ snānamātreṇa mohini || 3 ||
pūrvābhādra padāyuktā paurṇamāsī yadā bhavet
tadā puṇyatamaḥ kālo hyaśvamedhaphalapradaḥ || 4 ||
yatra sā dṛśyate devī vikhyātā bhīṣmacaṇḍikā
śmaśāne tāṃ samabhyarcya na naro durgatiṃ vrajet || 5 ||
antakeśvarapūrveṇa dakṣe sarveśvarasya ca
mātalīśvarasaumye tu kṛttivāseśvaraḥ smṛtaḥ || 6 ||
kṛttivāseśvaraṃ dṛṣṭvā taṃ sampūjya parāṃ gatim
ekena janmanā devi kṛttivāse tu labhyate
pūrvajanmakṛtaṃ pāpaṃ tapasāpi na śuddhyti || 7 ||
tatkṣaṇānnaśyate pāpaṃ tasya liṅgasya darśanāt
kṛte tu tryambakaṃ pūrvaṃ tretāyāṃ kṛttivāsasam || 8 ||
maheśvaraṃ tu devasya dvāpare nāma gīyate
hastipāleśvaraṃ nāma kalau siddhaistu gīyate || 9 ||
kṛttivāseśvaro devo dra ṣṭavyaśca punaḥ punaḥ
yadīhettārakaṃ jñānaṃ śāśvataṃ cāmṛtapradam || 10 ||
darśanāddevadevasya brahmahāpi pramucyate
sparśane pūjane caiva sarvayajñaphalaṃ labhet || 11 ||
śraddhayā parayā devaṃ ye'canti sanātanam
phālgunasya caturdaśyāṃ kṛṣṇapakṣe samāhitāḥ || 12 ||
puṣpaiḥ phalaistathā patrairbhakṣyairuccāvacaistathā
kṣīreṇa madhunā caiva toyena saha sarpiṣā || 13 ||
tarpanti paraṃ liṅgamarcanti śubhaṃ śivam
ḍuṇḍukāranamaskārairnṛtyagītaistathaiva ca || 14 ||
mukhavādyairanekaiśca stotrairmatraistathaiva ca
upoṣya rajanīmekāṃ bhaktyā paramayā haram || 15 ||
te yāṃti paramaṃ sthānaṃ pūjayitvā ca mohini
bhūtāyāṃ caitramāsasya yo'cayetparameśvaram || 16 ||
sa ca vitteśvaraṃ prāpya krīḍate yakṣarāḍiva
vaiśākhasya caturddaśyāṃ yo'cayetprayataḥ śivam || 17 ||
vaiśākhalokamāsādya tasyaivānucaro bhavet
jyeṣṭhamāse caturdaśyāṃ yo'cayecchraddhayā haram || 18 ||
svargalokamavāpnoti yāvadābhūtasamplavam
caturddaśyāṃ śucau bhadre yo'cayetprayataḥ śivam || 19 ||
sūryalokaṃ samāsādya krīḍate yāvadīpsitam
śrāvaṇasya caturddaśyāṃ kāmaliṅgaṃ samutthitam || 20 ||
dadāti vāruṇaṃ lokaṃ krīḍate cāpsaronvitaḥ
māsi bhādra pade yuktamarcayitvā tu śaṅkaram || 21 ||
puṣpaiḥ phalaiśca vividhairindra syaiti salokatām
pitṛpakṣe caturddaśyāṃ pūjayitvā yatheśvaram || 22 ||
prāpnoti pitṛlokaṃ tu krīḍate pūjitaśca taiḥ
prabodhamāse deveśamarcayitvā maheśvaram || 23 ||
candra loke samāsādya krīḍate yāvadīpsitam
bahule mārgaśīrṣasya pūjayitvā pinākinam || 24 ||
piṣṇulokamavāpnoti krīḍate kālamakṣayam
arcayitvā tathā pauṣe sthāṇuṃ hṛṣṭena cetasā || 25 ||
prāpnoti nairṛtaṃ sthānaṃ tenaiva saha modate
māghe samarcayitvā vai puṣpamūlaphalaiḥ śubhaiḥ || 26 ||
prāpnoti śivalokaṃ tu tyaktvā saṃsārasāgaram
kṛttivāseśvaraṃ devamarcayettu prayatnataḥ || 27 ||
avimukte vaseccaiva yadīcchecchāṅkaraṃ padam
ghaṇṭākarṇo hradastatra vyāseśasya tu paścime || 28 ||
snānaṃ kṛtvā hrade tasminvyāseśasya ca darśanāt
yatra tatra mṛto devi vārāṇasyāṃ mṛto bhavet || 29 ||
daṇḍakhāte naraḥ snātvā tarpayitvā svakānpitṝn
narakasthāstu ye devi pitṛlokaṃ vrajaṃti te || 30 ||
piśācatvaṃ gatā devi ye narāḥ pāpakarmiṇaḥ
teṣāṃ piṇḍapradānena dehasyoddharaṇaṃ smṛtam || 31 ||
darśanāttasya khātasya kṛtakṛtyo'bhijāyate
tatraiva lalitā devī vartate lokaśarmadā || 32 ||
ye cā tāṃ pūjayiṣanti tasminsthāne sthitāḥ svayam
teṣāṃ sā vividhānbhogānsampradāsyati mānade || 33 ||
jāgaraṃ ye tu tasyāśca puraḥ kurvaṃti dīpakaiḥ
teṣāṃ sā hyakṣayān lokān vitariṣyati mohini || 34 ||
ālayaṃ ye prakurvaṃti bhūmiṃ sammārjayanti ca
teṣāmaṣṭasahasrasya suvarṇasya phalaṃ bhavet || 35 ||
tāmuddiśya tu yo devi brāhmaṇānvedapāragān
bhojayiṣyati miṣṭhānnaistasya puṇyaphalaṃ śṛṇu || 36 ||
durgāloke vasetkalpamihaivāgacchate punaḥ
naro vā yadi vā nārī sarvabhogasamanvitau || 37 ||
dhanadhānyasamāyuktau jāyete mahatāṃ kule
subhagau darśanīyau ca rūpayauvanagarvitau || 38 ||
bhavetāmīdṛśau devi sarvasaukhyasya bhājanau
mānuṣyaṃ durlabhaṃ prāpya vidyutsampātacañcalam || 39 ||
yena sā lalitā dṛṣṭā tasya janmabhayaṃ kutaḥ
pṛthvīpradakṣiṇāṃ kṛtvā yatphalaṃ labhate naraḥ || 40 ||
tatphalaṃ lalitāyāśca vārāṇasyāṃ pradarśanāt
māsi māsi caturthyāṃ tu tasminkāla upoṣitaḥ || 41 ||
arcayitvā tu tāṃ devīṃ jāgaraṃ tatra kārayet
tasyārddhiḥ sakalā devi trailokyasyāpi pūjitam || 42 ||
nalakūbarakeśānaṃ taṃ ca sampūjya mohini
sarvasiddhipradātāraṃ kṛtakṛtyo naro bhavet || 43 ||
tasyaiva dakṣiṇe devi maṇikarṇīti ca śrutam
tasya cāgre mahattīrthaṃ sarvapāpapraṇāśanam || 44 ||
maṇikarṇīśvaraṃ devaṃ kuṇḍamadhye vyavasthitam
dṛṣṭvā natvā samabhyarcya na bhūyo jaṭhare vaset || 45 ||
tasya dakṣiṇapārśve tu gaṅgāyāṃ sthāpitaṃ param
gaṅgeśvaraṃ samabhyarcya suralokamavāpnuyāt || 46 ||
anyadāyatanaṃ vakṣye vārāṇasyāṃ sumohini
yatra vai devadevasya ruciraṃ sthānamīpsitam || 47 ||
nīyamānaṃ purā liṅgaṃ subhage śaśimaulinaḥ
rākṣasairantarikṣarsthvrajamānaiśca satvaram || 48 ||
asmindeśe yadā prāptaṃ tadā devena ciṃtitam
avimuktaviyogastu kathaṃ me na bhavediti || 49 ||
imamarthaṃ tu deveśo yāvaccintayate śubhe
tāvatkukkuṭaśabdastu tasminsthāne babhūva ha || 50 ||
śabdaṃ śrutvā tu taṃ devi rākṣasāstrastacetasaḥ
liṅgamutsṛjya tatraiva prabhātasamaye gatāḥ || 51 ||
gateṣu rākṣaseṣvevaṃ liṅgaṃ tatraiva saṃsthitam
sthāne'tirucire śubhre devadevaḥ svayaṃ prabhuḥ || 52 ||
avimukte tatra madhye avimuktataraṃ smṛtam || 53 ||
tadā vimukteti surairharasya nāma smṛtaṃ puṇyatamākṣarāḍhyam
mokṣapradaṃ sthāvarajaṅgamānāṃ ye prāṇinaḥ pañcatāṃ yāṃti tatra || 54 ||
kukkuṭāścāpi subhage tasminsthāne sthitāḥ sadā
adyāpi tatra dṛśyante pūjyamānāḥ śubhātmabhiḥ || 55 ||
avimuktaṃ sadā devi yaṃ śrayedīkṣayā naraḥ
na tasya punarāvṛttiḥ kalpakoṭiśatairapi || 56 ||
devasya dakṣiṇe bhāge vāpī tiṣṭhati śobhanā
tasyāstathodakaṃ pītvā nāvṛttiḥ punaratra ca || 57 ||
trīṇi liṅgāni vartante hṛdaye puruṣasya tu
tathā yaistajjalaṃ pītaṃ te kṛtārthāstu mānavāḥ || 58 ||
teṣāṃ tu tārakaṃ jñānamastyeveti na saṃśayaḥ
vāpījale naraḥ snātvā dṛṣṭvā daṇḍakanāmakam || 59 ||
avimuktaṃ tato dṛṣṭvā kaivalyaṃ labhate kṣaṇāt
tatra sandhyāmupāsitvā brāhmaṇaḥ sakṛdeva tu || 60 ||
pañcaṣaṣṭisamāḥ saṃdhyā tena copāsitā bhavet
purīṃ vārāṇasīṃ tāṃ tu śmaśānaṃ cāvimuktakam || 61 ||
avimukteśvaraṃ caiva dṛṣṭvā gaṇapatirbhavet
avimukteśvaraṃ liṅgaṃ tatra dṛṣṭvaiva mānavaḥ || 62 ||
sadyaḥ pāpaistathā rogaiḥ paśupāśairvimucyate
avimuktasya cāgre tu liṅgaṃ paścānmukhaṃ sthitam || 63 ||
avimuktaṃ ca te bhadre nāmnā vai lakṣaṇeśvaram
tena vai dṛṣṭamātreṇa jñānavān jāyate naraḥ || 64 ||
tasya cottarato devi liṅgaṃ caiva caturmukham
caturtheśvaranāmedaṃ pāpabhīmocanaṃ param || 65 ||
kṣetraṃ vārāṇasīnāma muktidaṃ prāṇināṃ bhuvi
avimukteśvaraṃ tatra jīvanmuktaṃ prakīrtitam || 66 ||
yatra tatra sthitasyāpi gāṇapatyaṃ vidhīyate
prāṇāṃstu tatra santyajya muktimātyantikīṃ vrajet || 67 ||
etadabhyantare kṣetre prathamāvaraṇaṃ smṛtam
tathā dvitīyāvaraṇe prācyāṃ tu maṇikarṇikā || 68 ||
saptakoṭyastu liṅgāni tatra sthāne sthitāni hi
teṣāṃ darśanamātreṇa yajñānāṃ phalamāpnuyāt || 69 ||
etāni siddhaliṅgāni kūpāḥ puṇyāstasthā hradāḥ
vāpyo nadyo'tha kaṃṇḍāni tathā te'pi prakīrtitāḥ || 70 ||
eteṣu caiva yaḥ snānaṃ kariṣyati samāhitaḥ
liṅgāni sparśayitvā ca saṃsāre na viśetpunaḥ || 71 ||
pṛthivyāṃ yāni tīrthāni hyantarikṣe ca yāni tu
teṣāṃ madhye tu mukhyāni kīrtitāni mayā hi te || 72 ||
tīrthayātrā varārohe kathitā pāpanāśinī
yena caiṣā kṛtā dṛṣṭā so'pi vai muktibhāgbhavet || 73 ||
avimuktaṃ tu suśroṇi madhyamāvaraṇaṃ śubham
etattu kaṇṭakaṃ nāma mṛtyukāle'mṛtapradam || 74 ||
iti śrībṛhannāradīyapurāṇottarabhāge vasumohinīsaṃvāde kāśīmāhātmye tīrthayātrāvarṇanaṃ nāmaikonapañcāśattamo'dhyāyaḥ || 49 ||

<2.50>

athapañcāśattamo'dhyāyaḥ
vasuruvāca -
ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi yātrākālaṃ tu mohini
devādyaistu kṛtā yā tu yathāyogyaphalāptidā || 1 ||
caitramāse tu divijairyātreyaṃ vihitā purā
tatrasthaiḥ kāmakuṇḍe tu snānapūjanatatparaiḥ || 2 ||
jyeṣṭhamāse tu vai siddhaiḥ kṛtā yātrā śubhānane
rudrā vāsasya kuṇḍe tu snānapūjāparāyaṇaiḥ || 3 ||
āṣāḍhe cāpi gandharvairyātreyaṃ vihitā śubhaiḥ
priyādevyāstu kuṇḍe vai snānapūjanakārakaiḥ || 4 ||
vidyādharaistu yātreyaṃ śrāvaṇe māsi mohini
lakṣmīkuṇḍasthitaiścīrṇā snānārcanaparāyaṇaiḥ || 5 ||
mārkaṇḍeyahradasthaistu snānapūjanatatparaiḥ
kṛtā yakṣaistu yātreyamiṣamāse varānane || 6 ||
pannagaiścaiva yātreyaṃ mārgamāse tu mohini
koṭitīrthasthitaiścīrṇā snānapūjāvidhāyakaiḥ || 7 ||
kapālamocanasthaistu guhyakaiḥ śubhalocane
pauṣe māsi kṛtā yātrā snānadhyānārcanānvitaiḥ || 8 ||
kāleśvarākhyakuṇḍasthaiḥ phālgune māsi śobhane
piśācaistu kṛtā yātrā snānapūjāditatparaiḥ || 9 ||
phālgune tu śubhe māse site yā tu caturdaśī
tena sā procyate devi piśācīnāma viśrutā || 10 ||
atha te sampravakṣyāmi yātrākṛtyaṃ śubhānane
kṛtena yena manujo yātrāphalamavāpnuyāt || 11 ||
udakumbhāstu dātavyā miṣṭānnena samanvitāḥ
phalapuṣpasamopetā vastraiḥ sañchāditāḥ śubhāḥ || 12 ||
caitrasya śuklapakṣe tu tṛtīyā yā mahāphalā
tatra gaurī tu dra ṣṭavyā bhaktibhāvena mānavaiḥ || 13 ||
snānaṃ kṛtvā tu gantavyaṃ goprekṣe tu varānane
svardvāri kālikādevī arcitavyā prayatnataḥ || 14 ||
anyā cāpi parā proktā saṃvartā lalitā śubhā
dra ṣṭavyā caiva sā bhaktyā sarvakāmaphalapradā || 15 ||
tatastu bhojayedviprāñchivabhaktāñchucivratān
vāsobhirddakṣiṇābhiśca puṣkalābhiryathārhataḥ || 16 ||
pañcagaurīḥ samuddiśya rasān gandhāndvije'payet
uttamaṃ śreya āpnoti saubhāgyena samanvitaḥ || 17 ||
vināyakānpravakṣyāmi kṣetrāvāse tu vighnadān
yānsaṃpūjya naro devi nirvighnena phalaṃ labhet || 18 ||
ḍhuṇḍhiṃ tu prathamaṃ dṛṣṭvā tathā kilavināyakam
devyā vināyakaṃ caiva goprekṣaṃ hastihastinaṃm || 19 ||
vināyakaṃ tathaivānyaṃ sindūryaṃ nāma viśrutam
caturthyāṃ devi dra ṣṭavyā evaṃ caiva vināyakāḥ || 20 ||
laḍḍukāśca pradātavyā etānuddiśya vāḍave
etena caiva kṛtyena siddhimāñjāyate naraḥ || 21 ||
ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi caṇḍikāḥ kṣetrarakṣikāḥ
dakṣiṇe rakṣate durgā nairṛte cāntareśvarī || 22 ||
aṅgāreśī paścime tu vāyavye bhadra kālikā
uttare bhīmacaṇḍā ca mahāmattā tathaiśake || 23 ||
ūrddhvakeśī samāyuktā śāṅkarī pūrvataḥ smṛtā
adhaḥkeśī tathāgneyyāṃ citraghaṇṭā ca madhyataḥ || 24 ||
etāstu caṇḍikādevīryo vai paśyati mānavaḥ
tasya tuṣṭāśca tāḥ sarvāḥ kṣetraṃ rakṣaṃti tatparāḥ || 25 ||
vighnaṃ kurvaṃti satataṃ pāpināṃ devi sarvadā
tasmāddevyaḥ sadā pūjyā rakṣārthe savināyakāḥ || 26 ||
yadīcchetparamāṃ siddhiṃ santatiṃ vibhavaṃ sukham
tato bhaktyā gandhapuṣpanaivedyādīnsamarppayet || 27 ||
anyacca te pravakṣyāmi tasminsthāne sulocane
tisro nadyastu tatrasthā vanhati ca śubhodakāḥ || 28 ||
tāsāṃ darśanamātreṇa brahmahatyā nivartate
ekā tu tatra trisrotā tathā mandākinī parā || 29 ||
matsyodarī tṛtīyā ca etāstisrastu puṇyadāḥ
maṃdākinī tatra puṇyā madhyameśvarasaṃsthitā || 30 ||
saṃsthitā trisrotikā ca avimukteti puṇyadā
matsyodarī tu oṃkāre puṇyadā sarvadaiva hi || 31 ||
tasminsthāne yadā gaṅgā āgamiṣyati mohinī
tadā puṇyatamaḥ kālo devānāmapi durlabhaḥ || 32 ||
varaṇāsiktasalile jāhnavījalaviplute
tatra nādeśvare puṇye snātaḥ kimanuśocati || 33 ||
matsyodarīsamāyuktā yadā gaṅgā babhūva ha
tasminkāle śivaḥ snānātkapālaṃ muktavāñchubhe || 34 ||
kapālamocanaṃ nāma tatraiva sumahatsaraḥ
pāvanaṃ sarvasattvānāṃ puṇyadaṃ parikīrtitam || 35 ||
matsyodarījale gaṅgā oṃkāreśvarasannidhau
tadā tasmiñjale snātvā dṛṣṭvā coṅkāramīśvaram || 36 ||
śokaṃ jarāṃ mṛtyubandhaṃ tato na spṛśate naraḥ
tasminsnātaḥ śivaḥ sākṣādoṅkāreśvarasaṃjñitaḥ || 37 ||
etadra hasyamākhyātaṃ tava snehādvarānane
akāraṃ cāpyukaraṃ ca makāraṃ ca prakīrtitam || 38 ||
akārastatra vijñeyo viṣṇulokagatipradaḥ
tasya dakṣiṇapārśve tu ukāraḥ parikīrtitaḥ || 39 ||
tatra siddhiṃ parāṃ prāpto devācāryo bṛhaspatiḥ
oṃkāraṃ tatra vijñeyaṃ brahmaṇaḥ padamavyayam || 40 ||
tayostathottare bhāge makāraṃ viṣṇusaṃjñitam
tasmim̐ lliṅge tu saṃsiddhaḥ kapilarṣirmahāmuniḥ || 41 ||
vārāṇasīmabhyupetya pañcāyatanamuttamam
ārādhyamāno deveśaṃ bhīṣmastatra sthito'bhavat || 42 ||
tasminsthāne tu subhage svayamāvirabhūcchivaḥ
goprekṣaka iti khyātaḥ saṃstutaḥ sarvadaivataiḥ || 43 ||
goprekṣeśvaramāgatya dṛṣṭvābhyarcya ca mānavaḥ
na durgatimavāpnoti kalmaṣaiśca vimucyate || 44 ||
vanasthā dahyamānāstu surabhyo dāvavahninā
bhramantyo'sminhrade'bhyetya śāntāstoyaṃ papustadā || 45 ||
kapilāhrada ityevaṃ tataḥ prabhṛti kathyate
tatrāpi sa śivaḥ sākṣādvṛṣadhvaja iti smṛtaḥ || 46 ||
sānnidhyaṃ kṛtavāndevo dṛśyamānaḥ sadā sthitaḥ
kapilāhradatīrthe'sminsnātvā saṃyatamānasaḥ || 47 ||
vṛṣadhvajaṃ śivaṃ dṛṣṭvā sarvayajñaphalaṃ labhet
svarlokatāṃ mṛtastatra pūjayitvā śivo bhavet || 48 ||
labhate dehabhedena gaṇatvaṃ cātidurlabham
asminneva pradeśe tu gāvo vai brahmaṇā svayam || 49 ||
śāntyarthaṃ sarvalokānāṃ sarvānpāvayituṃ dhruvam
bhadra dohaṃ sarastatra puṇyaṃ pāpaharaṃ śubham || 50 ||
tasminsthāne naraḥ snātaḥ sākṣādvāgīśvaro bhavet
śivastatra samānīya sthāpitaḥ parameṣṭhinā || 51 ||
brahmaṇaścāpi saṅgṛhya viṣṇunā sthāpitaḥ punaḥ
hiraṇyagarbha ityevaṃ nāmnā tatra sthitaḥ śivaḥ || 52 ||
punaścāpi tato brahmā svarlokeśvarasaṃjñakam
sthāpayāmāsa vai liṅgaṃ svarlīlaṃ kāraṇe kvacit || 53 ||
dṛṣṭvā vai taṃ tu deveśaṃ śivaloke mahīyate
prāṇāniha punastyaktvā na punarjāyate kvacit || 54 ||
anantā sā gatistasya yogināmeva yā smṛtā
asminneva mahīdeśe daityo daivatakaṇṭakaḥ || 55 ||
vyāghrarūpaṃ samāsthāya nihato darpito balī
vyāghreśvara iti khyāto nityaṃ tatra samāsthitaḥ || 56 ||
na punardurgatiṃ yāti dṛṣṭvainamamareśvaram
himavatsthāpitaṃ liṅgaṃ śaileśvaramiti sthitam || 57 ||
dṛṣṭvaitanmanujo bhadre na durgatimavāpnuyāt
utpalo vidalaścaiva yau daityau brahmaṇo varāt || 58 ||
strīlaulyāddarpitau dṛṣṭvā pārvatyā nihatāvubhau
sāraṅgaṃ kantukenātra tasyedaṃ cihnamāsthitam || 59 ||
dṛṣṭvaitanmanujo liṅgaṃ jyeṣṭhasthānaṃ samāśritam
na śocati punarbhadre siddho janmani janmani || 60 ||
samantāttasya devaistu liṅgāni sthāpitāni ca
dṛṣṭvā ca tāni vai martyo dehabhede gaṇo bhavet || 61 ||
nadī vārāṇasī ceyaṃ puṇyā pāpapraṇāśinī
kṣetrametadalaṅkṛtya jāhnavyā saha saṅgatā || 62 ||
sthāpitaṃ saṅgame cāsminbrahmaṇā liṅgamuttamam
saṅgameśvaramityeva khyātaṃ jagati dṛśyatām || 63 ||
saṅgame devanadyośca yaḥ snātvā manujaḥ śubhe
arcayetsaṅgameśānaṃ tasya janmabhayaṃ kutaḥ || 64 ||
sthāpitaṃ liṅgametacca śukreṇa bhṛgusūnunā
nāmnā śukreśvaraṃ bhadre sarvasiddhāmarārcitam || 65 ||
dṛṣṭvaitanmānavaḥ sadyo muktaḥ syātsarvakilbiṣaiḥ
mṛtaśca na punarjanma saṃsāre labhate naraḥ || 66 ||
jambuko'tra hato daityo mahādevena mohini
talliṅgaṃ tu naro dṛṣṭvā sarvānkāmānavāpnuyāt || 67 ||
devaiḥ śakrapurogaiśca etāni sthāpitāni hi
jānīhi puṇyaliṅgāni sarvakāmapradāni ca || 68 ||
evametāni sarvāṇi śivaliṅgāni mohini
kathitāni mayā tubhyaṃ kṣetre'sminnavimuktake || 69 ||
iti śrībṛhannāradīyapurāṇottarabhāge mohinīvasusaṃvāde kāśīmāhātmyaṃ nāma pañcāśattamo'dhyāyaḥ || 50 ||

<2.51>

athaikapañcāśattamo'dhyāyaḥ
vasuruvāca -
athānyatte pravakṣyāmi gaṅgāmāhātmyamuttamam
vārāṇāsīsthitaṃ bhadre bhuktimuktiphalapradam || 1 ||
avimukte kṛtaṃ yattu tadevākṣayatāṃ vrajet
avimuktagataḥ kaścinnarakaṃ naiti kilbiṣī || 2 ||
avimuktakṛtaṃ yattu pāpaṃ vajraṃ bhavecchubhe
trailokye yāni tīrthāni mokṣadāni ca kṛtsnaśaḥ || 3 ||
sevaṃte satataṃ gaṅgāṃ kāśyāmuttaravāhinīm
daśāśvamedhe yaḥ snātvā dṛṣṭvā viśveśvaraṃ śivam || 4 ||
sadyo niṣpātako bhūtvā mucyate bhavabandhanāt
gaṅgā hi sarvataḥ puṇyā brahmahatyāpahāriṇī || 5 ||
vārāṇasyāṃ viśeṣeṇa yatra cottaravāhinī
varaṇāyāstathāsyāśca jāhnavyāḥ saṅgame naraḥ || 6 ||
snānamātreṇa sarvebhyaḥ pātakebhyaḥ pramucyate
kāśyāmuttaravāhinyāṃ gaṅgāyāṃ kārtike tathā || 7 ||
snātvā māghe ca mucyante mahāpāpādipātakaiḥ
sarvalokeṣu tīrthāni yāni khyātāni tāni ca || 8 ||
sarvāṇyetāni subhage kāśyāmāyāṃti jāhnavīm
nityaṃ parvasu sarveṣu puṇyaiścāyatanaiḥ saha || 9 ||
uttarābhimukhīṃ gaṅgāṃ kāśyāmāyāṃti cānvaham
mahāpātakadoṣādiduṣṭānāṃ sparśanodbhavam || 10 ||
vyapohituṃ svapāpaṃ ca jantupāpavimuktaye
janmāntaraśatenāpi satkarmaniratasya ca || 11 ||
anyatra sudhiyā bhadre mokṣo labhyeta vā na vā
ekena janmanā tvatra gaṅgāyāṃ maraṇena ca || 12 ||
mokṣastu labhyate kāśyāṃ nareṇāvalitātmanā
khyāto dharmanado nāma hradastatraiva sundari || 13 ||
dharma eva svarūpeṇa mahāpātakanāśanaḥ
dhūlī ca dhūtapāpā sā sarvatīrthamayī śubhā || 14 ||
harenmahāpāpasaṅghānkūlajāniva pādapān
kiraṇā dhūtapāpā ca puṇyatoyā sarasvatī || 15 ||
gaṅgā ca yamunā caiva pañca nadyaḥ prakīrtitāḥ
ataḥ pañcanadaṃ nāma tīrthaṃ trailokyaviśrutam || 16 ||
tatrāpluto na gṛhṇīyāddehitāṃ pāñcabhautikīm
asminpañcanadīnāṃ tu saṅgame'ghaughabhedane || 17 ||
snānamātrānnaro yāti bhitvā brahmāṇḍamaṇḍapam
prayāge māghamāse tu samyak snānasya yatphalam || 18 ||
tatphalaṃ syāddinaikena kāśyāṃ pañcanade dhruvam
snātvā pañcanade tīrthe kṛtvā ca pitṛtarpaṇam || 19 ||
viṣṇuṃ mādhavamabhyarcya na bhūyo janmabhāgbhavet
yāvatsaṅkhyāstilā dattāḥ pitṛbhyo jalatarpaṇe || 20 ||
puṇye pañcanade tīrthe tṛptiḥ syāttāvadābdikī
śraddhayā yaiḥ kṛtaṃ śrāddhaṃ tīrthe pañcanade śubhe || 21 ||
teṣāṃ pitāmahā muktā nānāyonigatā api
yamaloke pitṛgaṇairgātheyaṃ parigīyate || 22 ||
mahimānaṃ pāñcanadaṃ dṛṣṭvā śrāddhavidhānataḥ
asmākamapi vaṃśyo'tra kaścicchrāddhaṃ kariṣyati || 23 ||
kāśyāṃ pañcanadaṃ prāpya yena mucyāmahe vayam
tatra pañcanade tīrthe yakiñciddīyate vasu || 24 ||
kalpakṣaye'pi na bhavettasya puṇyasya saṃkṣayaḥ
vandhyāpi varṣaparyantaṃ snātvā pañcanade hrade || 25 ||
samarcya maṅgalāṃ gaurīṃ putraṃ janayati dhruvam
jalaiḥ pāñcanadaiḥ puṇyairvāsasā pariśodhitaiḥ || 26 ||
mahāphalamavāpnoti snāpayitveha dikśrutām
pañcāmṛtānāṃ kalaśairaṣṭottaraśatonmitaiḥ || 27 ||
tulito'dhikatāṃ prāpto binduḥ pāñcanadastu saḥ
pañcakūrcena pītena yātrāśuddhirudāhṛtā || 28 ||
sā śuddhiḥ śraddhayā prāśya binduṃ pañcanadāmbhasām
bhavedatha hradasnānādrā jasūyāśvamedhayoḥ || 29 ||
yatphalaṃ tacchataguṇaṃ smṛtaṃ pañcanadāmbunā
rājasūyāśvamedhau ca bhavetāṃ svargasādhane || 30 ||
ābrahmapaṭṭikādvandvānmuktiḥ pañcanadāmbubhiḥ
svarganadyabhiṣeko'pi na tathā sammataḥ satām || 31 ||
abhiṣekaḥ pāñcanado yathānanyo varapradaḥ
śataṃ samāstapastaptvā kṛte yatprāpyate phalam || 32 ||
tatkārtike pañcanade sakṛtsnānena labhyate
iṣṭāpūrteṣu dharmeṣu yāvajjanmakṛteṣu yat || 33 ||
anyatra syātphalaṃ tasyādhikaṃ pañcanadāmbubhiḥ
na dhūtapāpasadṛśaṃ tīrthaṃ kvāpi mahītale || 34 ||
yadekasnānato naśyedaghaṃ janmatrayārjitam
kṛte dharmanadaṃ nāma tretāyāṃ dhūtapātakam || 35 ||
dvāpare bindutīrthaṃ ca kalau pañcanadaṃ smṛtam
bidutīrthe naro datvā kāñcanaṃ kṛṣṇalonmitam || 36 ||
na daridro bhavetkvāpi na sukhena viyujyate
gobhūtilahiraṇyāśvavāso'nnasthānabhūṣaṇam || 37 ||
yatkiṃñcidbindutīrthe'tra datvākṣayamavāpnuyāt
ekāmapyāhutiṃ kṛtvā samiddhe'gnau vidhānataḥ || 38 ||
puṇye dharmanadītīrthe koṭihomaphalaṃ labhet
na pañcanadatīrthasya mahimānamanantakam || 39 ||
ko'pi varṇayituṃ śaktaścaturvargaśubhaukasaḥ
iti te kathitaṃ bhadre kāśīmāhātmyamuttamam || 40 ||
sukhadaṃ mokṣadaṃ nṝṇāṃ mahāpātakanāśanam
brahmaghno madhupaḥ svarṇasteyī ca gurutalpagaḥ || 41 ||
mahāpātakayukto'pi saṃyukto'pyupapātakaiḥ
avimuktasya māhātmyaśravaṇācchuddhimāpnuyāt || 42 ||
brāhmaṇo vedavidvānsyātkṣattriyo vijayī raṇe
vaiśyo dhanapatiḥ śūdro viṣṇubhaktasamāgamī || 43 ||
śravaṇādasya subhage bhūyātpaṭhanato'pi vā
sarvayajñeṣu yatpuṇyaṃ sarvatīrtheṣu yatphalam || 44 ||
tatsarvaṃ samavāpnoti paṭhanācchravaṇādapi
vidyārthī labhate vidyāṃ dhanārthī labhate dhanam || 45 ||
bhāryārthī labhate bhāryāṃ sutārthī putramāpnuyāt
avimuktasya māhātmyaṃ mayā te parikīrtitam || 46 ||
viṣṇubhaktāya dātavyaṃ śivabhaktiratāya ca
jagajjananibhaktāya sūryaherambasevine || 47 ||
guruśuśrūṣave datvā tīrthasnānaphalaṃ labhet
śaṭhāya nindakāyāpi goviprasuravidviṣe
gurudruhe'sūyakāya datvā mṛtyumavāpnuyāt || 48 ||
iti śrībṛhannāradīyapurāṇottarabhāge vasumohinīsaṃvāde kāśīmāhātmye ekapañcāśattamo'dhyāyaḥ || 51 ||

<2.52>

athadvipañcāśattamo'dhyāyaḥ
mohinyuvāca -
dhanyo'si vipravarya tvaṃ kṛpāluḥ sarvadehiṣu
yacchrutaṃ te mukhāmbhojātkāśīmāhātmyamuttamam || 1 ||
adhunāhaṃ kṛtārthāsmi tvayā hi pratibodhitā
kṛpālunā nipatitābhyuddhṛtā bhavasāgarāt || 2 ||
adhunā śrotumicchāmi hareḥ kṣetrasya mānada
māhātmyaṃ yatra gamanātkṛtārtho jāyate naraḥ || 3 ||
puruṣottamaviṣṇostu kṣetraṃ muktividhāyakam
śrūyate hi purāṇeṣu varṇitaṃ munibhirdvijaiḥ || 4 ||
tatkathyatāṃ mahābhāga śiṣyāhaṃ yadi te priyā
sādhavaḥ sarvalokasya satatopakṛtau sthitāḥ || 5 ||
vasuruvāca -
śṛṇu devi pravakṣyāmi tubhyaṃ māhātmyamuttamam
puruṣottamanāmnastu kṣetrasya brahmaṇoditam || 6 ||
pṛthivyāṃ bhārataṃ varṣaṃ karmabhūmirudāhṛtā
tatrāste bhārate varṣe dakṣiṇodadhitīragaḥ || 7 ||
utkaleti samākhyātaḥ svargamokṣapradāyakaḥ
samudrā duttaraṃ tāvadyāvadvirajamaṇḍalam || 8 ||
deśo'sau puṇyaśīlānāṃ guṇaiḥ sarvairalaṅkṛtaḥ
sarvatīrthāni puṇyāni puṇyānyāyatanāni ca || 9 ||
utkale tu viśālākṣi veditavyāni tāni tu
samudra syottare tīre tasmindeśe'khilottame || 10 ||
āste guhyaṃ paraṃ kṣetraṃ muktidaṃ pāpanāśanam
sarvatra vālukākīrṇe pavitraṃ dharmakāmadam || 11 ||
daśayojanavistīrṇaṃ kṣetraṃ paramadurlabham
nakṣatrāṇāṃ yathā somaḥ sarasāṃ sāgaro yathā || 12 ||
tathā samastatīrthānāṃ variṣṭhaṃ puruṣottamam
vasūnāṃ pāvako yadvadrudrā ṇāṃ śaṅkaro yathā || 13 ||
tathā śreṣṭhaṃ hi tīrthānāṃ sarveṣāṃ puruṣottamam
varṇānāṃ brāhmaṇo yadvadvainateyaśca pakṣiṇām || 14 ||
tathā samastatīrthānāṃ variṣṭhaṃ puruṣottamam
senānīnāṃ yathā skandaḥ siddhānāṃ kapilo yathā || 15 ||
erāvato gajendrā ṇāṃ maharṣīṇāṃ bhṛguryathā
meruḥ śikhariṇāṃ yadvannāgānāṃ ca himālayaḥ || 16 ||
uccaiḥśravā yathāśvānāṃ kavīnāmuśanā yathā
munīnāṃ ca yathā vyāsaḥ kubero yakṣarakṣasām || 17 ||
indri yāṇāṃ mano yadvadbhūtānāmavanī yathā
aśvatthaḥ sarvavṛkṣāṇāṃ pavanaḥ pavatāṃ yathā || 18 ||
arundhatī yathā strīṇāṃ śastrāṇāṃ kuliśaṃ yathā
akāraḥ sarvavarṇānāṃ gāyatrī chandasāṃ yathā || 19 ||
sarvāṅgebhyo yathā śreṣṭhamuttamāṅgaṃ vidhātṛje
yathā samastavidyānāṃ mokṣavidyā parā smṛtā || 20 ||
manuṣyāṇāṃ yathā rājā dhenūnāṃ kāmadhugyathā
suvarṇaṃ sarvadhātūnāṃ sarpāṇāṃ vāsukiryathā || 21 ||
prahlādaḥ sarvadaityānāṃ rāmaḥ śastrabhṛtāṃ yathā
jhaṣāṇāṃ makaro yadvanmṛgāṇāṃ mṛgarāḍ yathā || 22 ||
varuṇo yādasāṃ yadvadyamaḥ saṃyamināṃ yathā
kṣīrodaḥ sāgarāṇāṃ ca devarṣīṇāṃ ca nāradaḥ || 23 ||
purodhasāṃ yathā jīvaḥ kālaḥ kalayatāṃ yathā
grahāṇāṃ bhāskaro yadvanmantrāṇāṃ praṇavo yathā || 24 ||
kṛtyānāṃ dharmakāryaṃ ca tadvacchrīpuruṣottamam
puruṣākhyaṃ sakṛddṛṣṭvā sāgarāntaḥ sakṛnmataḥ || 25 ||
brahmavidyāṃ sakṛjjñātvā garbhavāso na vidyate
evaṃ sarvaguṇopetaṃ kṣetraṃ paramadurlabham || 26 ||
āste yatra varārohe vikhyātaṃ puruṣottamam
jagadvyāpī sa viśvātmā deveśaḥ puruṣottamaḥ || 27 ||
jagadyonirjagannāthastatra sarvaṃ pratiṣṭhitam
ajaḥ śakraśca rudra śca devāścāgnipurogamāḥ || 28 ||
nivasanti mahābhāge tasmindeśe sadaiva hi
gandharvāpsarasaḥ siddhāḥ pitaro devamānuṣāḥ || 29 ||
yakṣā vidyādharāścaiva munayaḥ śaṃsitavratāḥ
ṛṣayo vālakhilyādyāḥ kaśyapādyāḥ prajeśvarāḥ || 30 ||
suparṇāḥ kinnarā nāgāstathānye svargavāsinaḥ
sāṅgā vedāśca catvāro śāstrāṇi vividhāni ca || 31 ||
itihāsapurāṇāni yajñāśca bahudakṣiṇāḥ
nadyaśca vividhāḥ puṇyāstīrthānyāyatanāni ca || 32 ||
sāgarāśca tathā śailāstasmindeśe vyavasthitāḥ
evaṃ puṇyatame deśe devarṣipitṛsevite || 33 ||
sarvopabhogasahite vāsaḥ kasya na rocate
śreṣṭhatvaṃ tasya devasya kiṃ cānyadadhikaṃ tataḥ || 34 ||
āste yatra jagaddevo muktidaḥ puruṣottamaḥ
dhanyāste vibudhaprakhyā ye vasantyutkale narāḥ || 35 ||
tīrtharājajale snātvā paśyanti puruṣottamam
svarge vasanti te martyā na tu te rājasālaye || 36 ||
ye vasaṃtyutkale kṣetre puṇye śrīpuruṣottame
saphalaṃ jīvitaṃ teṣāmautkalānāṃ sumedhasām || 37 ||
ye paśyanti sutāmrauṣṭhaprasannāyatalocanam
cārubhrūkeśamukuṭaṃ cārukarṇalatāñcitam || 38 ||
cārusmitaṃ cārudantaṃ cārukuṇḍalamaṇḍitam
sunāsaṃ sukapolaṃ ca sulalāṭaṃ sulakṣaṇam || 39 ||
trailokyānandajananaṃ kṛṣṇasya mukhapaṅkajam
purā kṛtayuge devi śakratulyaparākramaḥ || 40 ||
babhūva nṛpatiḥ śrīmānindra dyumna iti śrutaḥ
satyavādī śucirdakṣaḥ sarvaśastrabhṛtāṃ varaḥ || 41 ||
rūpavānsubhagaḥ śūro dātā bhoktā priyaṃvadaḥ
yaṣṭā samastayajñānāṃ brahmaṇyaḥ satyasaṅgaraḥ || 42 ||
dhanurvede ca vede ca śāstre ca nipuṇaḥ kṛtī
vallabho naranārīṇāṃ paurṇamāsyāṃ yathā śaśī || 43 ||
āditya iva duṣprekṣyo madhuraścandra mā iva
vaiṣṇavaḥ satyasampanno jitakrodho jitendri yaḥ || 44 ||
adhyātmavidyānirato yuyutsurdharmatatparaḥ
evaṃ sa pālayetpṛthvadyaṃ rājā sarvaguṇākaraḥ || 45 ||
tasya buddhiḥ samutpannā viṣṇorārādhanaṃ prati
kathamārādhayiṣyāmi devadevaṃ janārdanam || 46 ||
kasminkṣetre'thavā tīrthe nadītīre tathāśrame
evaṃ cintāparaḥ so'tha nirīkṣya manasā mahīm || 47 ||
ālokya sarvatīrthāni yāni pāpaharāṇi ca
tāni sarvāṇi sañcintya jagāma manasā punaḥ || 48 ||
vikhyātaṃ paramaṃ kṣetraṃ muktidaṃ puruṣottamam
sa gatvā nṛpatistatra samṛddhabalavāhanaḥ || 49 ||
ayajaccāśvamedhena vidhivadbhūridakṣiṇaḥ
kārayitvā mahotsedhaṃ prāsādaṃ bhūridakṣiṇam || 50 ||
tatra saṅkarṣaṇaṃ kṛṣṇaṃ subhadrāṃ sthāpya vīryavān
pañcatīrthaṃ ca vidhivatkṛtvā tatra mahīpatiḥ || 51 ||
snānaṃ dānaṃ japaṃ homaṃ devatāprekṣaṇaṃ tathā
bhaktyā cārādhya vidhivatpratyahaṃ puruṣottamam
prasādāddevadevasya tato mokṣamavāptavān || 52 ||
mohinyuvāca -
tasmin kṣetre vare puṇye vaiṣṇave puruṣottame || 53 ||
kiṃ tatra pratimā pūrvaṃ susthitā vaiṣṇavī prabho
yenāsau nṛpatistatra gatvā sabalavāhanaḥ || 54 ||
sthāpayāmāsa kṛṣṇaṃ ca rāmaṃ bhadrāṃ śubhapradām
saṃśayo'sti mahāṃstatra vismayaśca dvijottama || 55 ||
śrotumicchāmi tatsarvaṃ brūhi tatkāraṇaṃ ca yat
vasuruvāca -
śṛṇuṣva pūrvavṛttāntaṃ kathāṃ pāpapraṇāśinīm || 56 ||
pravakṣyāmi samāsena śriyā pṛṣṭaṃ ca yatpurā
sumeroḥ kāñcane śṛṅge sarvāścaryasamanvite || 57 ||
tatra sthitaṃ jagannāthaṃ jagatsraṣṭāramavyayam
praṇamya śirasā devī lokānāṃ hitakāmyayā || 58 ||
papracchedaṃ mahāpraśnaṃ bhūmau sthānamanuttamam
śrīruvāca -
brūhi tvaṃ sarvalokeśa saṃśayaṃ me hṛdi sthitam || 59 ||
martyaloke mahāścarye bhūmau karmasudurlabhe
lobhamohamahāgrāhe kāmakrodhamahārṇave || 60 ||
yena mucyeta ātmeśa durgasaṃsārasāgarāt
tvāmṛte nāsti loke'sminvaktā saṃśayanirṇaye || 61 ||
śrutvaivaṃ vacanaṃ tasyā devadevo janārdanaḥ
provāca parayā prītyā paraṃ sārāmṛtopamam
sukhopāyaṃ susādhyaṃ ca nirāyāsaṃ mahāphalam || 62 ||
śrībhagavānuvāca -
āste tīrthavaraṃ devi vikhyātaṃ puruṣottamam || 63 ||
na tena sadṛśaṃ kiñcittriṣu lokeṣu vidyate
kīrtanādyasya deveśi mucyate sarvapātakaiḥ || 64 ||
na vijñāto naraiḥ sarvairna daityairna ca dānavaiḥ
marīcyādyairmunivarairdarśito'ya varānane || 65 ||
dakṣiṇasyodadhestīre nyagrodho yatra tiṣṭhati
yastu kalpe samutpanne mahadulkānibarhaṇe || 66 ||
vināśaṃ naiva cābhyeti svayaṃ tatraiva saṃsthitaḥ
dṛṣṭamātre vaṭe tasmiñchāyāmāśritya cāsakṛt || 67 ||
brahmahatyā pramucyeta pāpeṣvanyeṣu kā kathā
pradakṣiṇaṃ kṛtaṃ yaistu namaskāraistu jantubhiḥ || 68 ||
sarve vidhūtapāpāste gatā vai keśavālayam
nyagrodhasyottare kiñciddakṣiṇe keśavasya tu || 69 ||
prāsāde tatra tiṣṭhettu padaṃ dharmamayaṃ hi tat
pratimāṃ tatra tāṃ dṛṣṭvā svayaṃ devena nirmitām || 70 ||
anāyāsena vai yānti bhavanaṃ me tato narāḥ
gacchanneva tu taṃ dṛṣṭvā ekadā dharmarāṭ svayam || 71 ||
madantikamanuprāpya praṇamya śirasābravīt
namaste bhagavandeva lokanāthāya tejase || 72 ||
kṣīrodavāsinaṃ devaṃ śeṣabhogoruśāyinam
varaṃ vareṇyaṃ varadaṃ kartāraṃ hyakṣayaṃ prabhum || 73 ||
viśveśvaramajaṃ viṣṇuṃ sarvajñamaparājitam
nīlotpaladalaśyāmaṃ puṃḍarīkanibhekṣaṇam || 74 ||
sarvagaṃ nirguṇaṃ śāntaṃ jagaddhātāramavyam
sarvalokavidhātāraṃ lokanāthaṃ sukhāvaham || 75 ||
purāṇapuruṣaṃ vedyaṃ vyaktāvyaktaṃ sanātanam
purā purāṇaṃ sraṣṭāraṃ lokatīrthaṃ jagadgurum || 76 ||
śrīvatsavakṣasā yuktaṃ vanamālāvibhūṣitam
pītavastraṃ caturbāhuṃ śaṅkhacakragadādharam || 77 ||
hārakeyūrasaṃyuktaṃ mukuṭāṅgadadhāriṇam
sarvalakṣaṇasaṃyuktaṃ sarvendri yavivarjitam || 78 ||
kūṭasthamacalaṃ sūkṣmaṃ jyotīrūpaṃ sanātanam
bhāvābhāvavinirmuktaṃ vyāpinaṃ prakṛteḥ param || 79 ||
taṃ namasye jagannāthamīśvaraṃ sukhadaṃ prabhum
ityevaṃ dharmarājastu purā nyagrodhasannidhau || 80 ||
stutvā nānāvidhaiḥ stotraiḥ praṇāmamakarottadā
taṃ dṛṣṭvā ca mahābhāge praṇataṃ prāñjali sthitam || 81 ||
stotrasya kāraṇaṃ devi pṛṣṭavānahamantakam
vaivasvata mahābāho sarvadevamayo hyasi || 82 ||
kimarthaṃ stutavānitthaṃ saṃkṣepād brūhi tanmama || 83 ||
yama uvāca -
asminnāyatane puṇye vikhyāte puruṣottame
indra nīlamayī sṛṣṭā pratimā sārvakāmikī || 84 ||
tāṃ dṛṣṭvā puṇḍarīkākṣa bhāvenaikena śraddhayā
śvetākhyaṃ bhuvanaṃ yānti niṣkāmāścaiva mānavāḥ || 85 ||
ataścaivaṃ na śaknomi vyāpāramarisūdana
prasīda tvaṃ mahādeva saṃhara pratimāṃ vibho || 86 ||
śrutvā vaivasvatasyaitadvākyaṃ tamahamuktavān
yamaitāṃ gopayiṣyāmi sikatābhiḥ samantataḥ || 87 ||
tataḥ sā pratimā devi vallībhirgopitā tathā
yathā tatra na paśyanti manujāḥ svargakāṃkṣiṇaḥ || 88 ||
pracchādya vallikairdaivi jātarūpaparicchadaiḥ
yamaṃ prasthāpayāmāsa tāṃ purīṃ dakṣiṇāṃ diśam || 89 ||
guptāyāṃ pratimāyāṃ tu indra nīlasya vai tadā
tasminkṣetravare puṇye vikhyāte puruṣottame || 90 ||
yatkṛtaṃ tatra vṛttānte devadevo janārdanaḥ
tatsarvaṃ kathayāmāsa sa tasmai bhagavānpurā || 91 ||
indra dyumnasya gamanaṃ kṣetrasandarśanaṃ tathā
kṣetrasya varṇanaṃ caiva vyuṣṭiṃ tasya ca mohini || 92 ||
darśanaṃ baladevasya kṛṣṇasya ca viśeṣataḥ
subhadrā yāśca tatraiva māhātmyaṃ caiva sarvaśaḥ || 93 ||
darśanaṃ narasiṃhasya vyuṣṭisaṅkīrtanaṃ tathā
anantavāsudevasya darśanaṃ guṇakīrtanam || 94 ||
śvetamādhavamāhātmyaṃ svargadvārasya varṇanam
udadherdarśanaṃ caiva snānaṃ tarpaṇameva ca || 95 ||
samudra snānamāhātmyamindra dyumnasya cāpi vai
pañcatīrthaphalaṃ caiva mahājyaiṣṭyāṃ tathaiva ca || 96 ||
snānaṃ kṛṣṇasya halinaḥ sarvayātrāphalaṃ tathā
varṇanaṃ viṣṇulokasya kṣetrasya ca punaḥ svayam
pūrvaṃ kathitavāṃstathyaṃ tasyai sa puruṣottamaḥ || 97 ||
iti śrībṛhannāradīyapurāṇottarabhāge vasumohinīsaṃvāde puruṣottamamāhātmye dvipañcāśattamo'dhyāyaḥ || 52 ||

<2.53>

atha tripañcāśattamo'dhyāyaḥ
mohinyuvāca -
brūhi me muniśārdūla yatpṛcchāmi purātanam
yathā tāḥ pratimāḥ pūrvamindra dyumnena nirmitāḥ || 1 ||
kena caiva prakāreṇa tuṣṭastasmai sa mādhavaḥ
tatsarvaṃ vada me vipra śrotuṃ kautūhalaṃ mama || 2 ||
vasuruvāca -
śṛṇuṣva cārunayane purāṇaṃ vedasammitam
kathayāmi purāvṛttaṃ pratimānāṃ ca saṃbhavam || 3 ||
pravṛtte ca mahāyajñe prāsāde caiva nirmite
cintā tasya babhūvātha pratimārthamaharniśam || 4 ||
kenopāyena deveśaṃ sarvalokavibhāvanam
sargasthityantakarttāraṃ paśyāmi puruṣottamam || 5 ||
cintāviṣṭo narendra stu naivaṃ śete divāniśam
na bhuṅkte vividhānbhogānna ca snānaṃ prasādhanam || 6 ||
śailajā dārujā vāpi dhātujā vā mahītale
viṣṇoryogyāstu pratimāḥ sarvalakṣaṇalakṣitāḥ || 7 ||
etaireva trayāṇāṃ tu dayitaṃ yatsurārcitam
sthāpite prītimabhyeti iti cintāparo'bhavat || 8 ||
pañcarātravidhānena sampūjya puruṣottamam
cintāviṣṭo mahīpālaḥ saṃstotumupacakrame || 9 ||
indra dyumna uvāca -
vāsudeva namaste'stu namaste mokṣakāraṇa
trāhi māṃ sarvalokeśa janmasaṃsārasāgarāt || 10 ||
nirmalāṃśukasaṅkāśa namaste puruṣottama
saṅkarṣaṇa namaste'stu trāhi māṃ dharaṇīdhara || 11 ||
namaste hemagarbhāya namaste makaradhvaja
ratikānta namastubhyaṃ trāhi māṃ śambarāntaka || 12 ||
namaste meghasaṅkāśa namaste bhaktavatsala
aniruddha namaste'stu trāhi māṃ varado bhava || 13 ||
namaste vibudhāvāsa namaste vibudhapriya
nārāyaṇa namaste'stu trāhi māṃ śaraṇāgatam || 14 ||
namaste balināṃ śreṣṭhaṃ namaste lāṅgalāyudha
namaste vibudhaśreṣṭha namaste kamalodbhava || 15 ||
caturbhuja jagaddhātastrāhi māṃ prapitāmaha
namaste nīlameghābha namaste tridaśārcita || 16 ||
trāhi viṣṇo jagannātha magnaṃ māṃ bhavasāgare
pralayānalasaṅkāśa namaste ditijāntaka || 17 ||
narasiṃha mahāvīrya trāhi māṃ dīptalocana
yathā rasātalāccorvī jale magnoddhṛtā purā || 18 ||
tathā mahāvarāha tvaṃ trāhi māṃ duḥkhasāgarāt
tavāṅgamūrtayaḥ kṛṣṇa varadāḥ saṃstutā mayā || 19 ||
tvaṃ ceme baladevādyāḥ pṛthagrūpeṇa saṃsthitāḥ
aṅgāni tava deveśa garuḍādyāstathā prabho || 20 ||
dikpālāḥ sāyudhāścaiva vāsavādyāstathācyuta
ye cānye tava deveśa bhedāḥ proktā manīṣibhiḥ || 21 ||
te'pi sarve jagannātha prasannāyatalocana
ye vārcitāḥ stutāḥ sarve tathā yūyaṃ namaskṛtāḥ || 22 ||
prayacchata varaṃ mahyaṃ dharmakāmārthamokṣadam
bhedāste kīrtitā ye tu hare saṅkarṣaṇādayaḥ || 23 ||
tava pūjārthasambaddhāstatastvayi samāśritāḥ
na bhedastava deveśa vidyate paramārthataḥ || 24 ||
vividhaṃ tava yadrū paṃ yuktaṃ tadupacārataḥ
advaitaṃ tvāṃ kathaṃ dvaitaṃ vaktuṃ śaknoti mānavaḥ || 25 ||
ekastvaṃ hi hare vyāpī citsvabhāvo nirañjanaḥ
paramaṃ tava yadrū paṃ bhāvābhāvavivarjitam || 26 ||
nirlepaṃ nirmalaṃ sūkṣmaṃ kūṭasthamacalaṃ dhruvam
sarvopādhivinirmuktaṃ sattāmātraṃ vyavasthitam || 27 ||
taddevo'pi na jānāti kathaṃ jānāmyahaṃ prabho
aparaṃ tava yadrū paṃ pītavastraṃ caturbhujam || 28 ||
śaṅkhacakragadāpāṇiṃ mukuṭāṅgadadhāriṇam
śrīvatsavakṣasā yuktaṃ vanamālāvibhūṣitam || 29 ||
tadarcayanti vibudhā ye cānye tvatsamāśrayāḥ
deva sarvasuraśreṣṭha bhaktānāmabhayaprada || 30 ||
trāhi māṃ cārupadmākṣa magnaṃ viṣayasāgare
nānyaṃ paśyāmi lokeśa yasyāhaṃ śaraṇaṃ vraje || 31 ||
tvāmṛte kamalākānta prasīda madhusūdana
jarāvyādhiśatairyukto nānāduḥkhairnipīḍitaḥ || 32 ||
harṣaśokānvito mūḍhaḥ karmapāśaiḥ suyaṃtritaḥ
patito'ha mahāraudre ghore saṃsārasāgare || 33 ||
viṣayodakaduṣpāre rāgadveṣasamākule
indri yāvartagambhīre tṛṣṇāśokormisaṅkule || 34 ||
nirāśraye nirālambe niḥsāre'tyantacañcale
māyayā mohitastatra bhramāmi suciraṃ prabho || 35 ||
nānājātisahasreṣu jāyamānaḥ punaḥ punaḥ
mayā janmānyanekāni sahasrāṇyayutāni ca || 36 ||
vividhānyanubhūtāni saṃsāre'smiñjanārdana
vedāḥ sāṅgā mayādhītāḥ śāstrāṇi vividhāni ca || 37 ||
itihāsapurāṇāni tathā śilpānyanekaśaḥ
asantoṣāśca santoṣāḥ sañcayā bahavo vyayāḥ || 38 ||
mayā prāptā jagannātha kṣayavṛddhyduyetarāḥ
bhāryāmitrasvabandhūnāṃ viyogāḥ saṅgamāstathā || 39 ||
pitaro vividhā dṛṣṭā mātaraśca tathā mayā
duḥkhāni cānubhūtāni mayā saukhyānyanekaśaḥ || 40 ||
prāptāśca bāndhavāḥ svasṛbhrātaro jñātayastathā
mayoṣitaṃ tathā strīṇāṃ koṣṭhe viṇmūtrapicchile || 41 ||
garbhavāse mahāduḥkhamanubhūtaṃ tathā prabho
duḥkhāni yānyanekāni bālye yauvanagarvite || 42 ||
vārddhakye ca hṛṣīkeśa tāni prāptāni vai mayā
maraṇe yāni duḥkhāni guptamārge yamālaye || 43 ||
mayā tānyanubhūtāni narake yātanākule
krimikīṭadrumāṇāṃ ca hastyaśvamṛgapakṣiṇām || 44 ||
mahiṣāṇāṃ gavāṃ caiva tathānyeṣāṃ vanaukasām
dvijātīnāṃ ca sarveṣāṃ śūdrā ṇāṃ caiva yoniṣu || 45 ||
dhanināṃ kṣatriyāṇāṃ ca daridrā ṇāṃ tapasvinām
nṛpāṇāṃ nṛpabhṛtyānāṃ tathānyeṣāṃ ca dehinām || 46 ||
gṛhe teṣāṃ samutpanno deva cāhaṃ punaḥ punaḥ
gato'tmi dāsatāṃ nātha bhṛtyānāṃ bahuśo nṛṇām || 47 ||
