daridra tvaṃ ceśvaratvaṃ svāmitvaṃ ca tathā gataḥ
hatā mayā hataścānyairhataṃ me ghātitā mayā || 48 ||
dattaṃ me'nyairathānyebhyo mayā dattamanekaśaḥ
pitṛmātṛsuhṛdvargakalatrāṇāṃ kṛtena ca || 49 ||
ahaṃ hṛṣṭo'sakṛddainyaiścāśrudhautānano gataḥ
devatiryaṅmanuṣyeṣu careṣu sthāvareṣu ca || 50 ||
na vidyate ca tatsthānaṃ yatrāhaṃ na gataḥ prabho
kadā me narake vāsaḥ kadā svarge jagatpate || 51 ||
kadā manuṣyalokeṣu kadā tiryaggateṣu ca
jalayantre tathā cakre kadā'vaṭanibandhane || 52 ||
pātito'dhastathorddhvaṃ ca kadā madhye sthitastvaham
tathā cāhaṃ suraśreṣṭha karmmavallīṃ samāśritaḥ || 53 ||
evaṃ saṃsāracakre'sminbhairave lomaharṣaṇe
bhramāmi suciraṃ kālaṃ nāntaṃ paśyāmi karhicit || 54 ||
na jāne kiṃ karomyeṣa hare vyākulitendri yaḥ
śokatṛṣṇābhibhūtaśca kāṃdiśīko vicetanaḥ || 55 ||
idānīṃ tvamāhaṃ deva vikalaḥ śaraṇaṃ gataḥ
trāhi māṃ duḥkhitaṃ kṛṣṇa magnaṃ saṃsārasāgare || 56 ||
kṛpāṃ kuru jagannātha bhakto'ha yadi manyase
tvāmṛte nāsti me baṃdhuryo'sau cintāṃ kariṣyati || 57 ||
deva tvāṃ nāthamāsādya na bhayaṃ me'sti kutracit
jīvite maraṇe caiva yogakṣeme tathā prabho || 58 ||
ye tu tvāṃ vidhivaddeva nārcayanti narādhamāḥ
sugatistu kathaṃ teṣāṃ bhavetsaṃsārabandhanāt || 59 ||
kiṃ teṣāṃ kulaśīlena vidyayā jīvitena ca
yeṣāṃ na jāyate bhaktirjagaddhātari keśave || 60 ||
prakṛtiṃ tvāsurīṃ prāpya ye tvāṃ niṃndanti mohitāḥ
patanti narake ghore jāyamānāḥ punaḥ punaḥ || 61 ||
na teṣāṃ niṣkṛtistasmādvidyate narakārṇavāt
ye dūṣayanti durvṛttāste deva puruṣādhamāḥ || 62 ||
yatra yatra bhavejjanma mama karmanibandhanāt
tatra tatra hare bhaktistvayi syādakṣatā sadā || 63 ||
ārādhya tvāṃ paraṃ daityā narāścānye'pi saṅgatāḥ
avāpuḥ paramāṃ siddhiṃ kastvāṃ deva na pūjayet || 64 ||
na śaknuvanti brahmādyāḥ stotuṃ tvāṃ prakṛteḥ param
yathā cājñānabhāvena saṃstuto'si mayā prabho || 65 ||
tatkṣamasvāparādhānme yadi te'sti dayā mayi
kṛtāparādhe'pi hare kṣamāṃ kurvaṃti sādhavaḥ || 66 ||
tasmātprasīda deveśa bhaktyā snehaṃ samāśritaḥ
stuto'si yanmayā deva bhaktibhāvena cetasā || 67 ||
sāṅgaṃ bhavatu tatsarvaṃ vāsudeva namo'stu te || 68 ||
iti śrībṛhannāradīyapurāṇottarabhāge vasumohinīsaṃvāde puruṣottamamāhātmye tripañcāśattamo'dhyāyaḥ || 53 ||

<2.54>

athacatuṣpañcāśattamo'dhyāyaḥ
vasuruvāca -
itthaṃ stutastadā tena prasanno garuḍadhvajaḥ
dadau tasmai tu subhage sakalaṃ manasepsitam || 1 ||
yaḥ sampūjya jagannāthaṃ pratyahaṃ stauti mānavaḥ
stotreṇānena matimānsa mokṣaṃ labhate dhruvam || 2 ||
trisaṃdhyaṃ yo japedvidvānimaṃ stotravaraṃ śuciḥ
dharmaṃ cārthaṃ ca kāmaṃ ca mokṣaṃ ca labhate naraḥ || 3 ||
yaḥ paṭhecchṛṇuyādvāpi śrāvayedvā samāhitaḥ
sa lokaṃ śāśvataṃ viṣṇoryāti nirddhūtakalmaṣaḥ || 4 ||
dhanyaṃ pāpaharaṃ cedaṃ bhuktimuktipradaṃ śivam
guhyaṃ sudurlabhaṃ puṇyaṃ na deyaṃ yasya kasya cit || 5 ||
na nāstikāya mūrkhāya na kṛtaghnāya mānine
na duṣṭamataye dadyānnābhaktāya kadācana || 6 ||
dātavyaṃ bhaktiyuktāya guṇaśīlānvitāya ca
viṣṇubhaktāya śāntāya śraddhānuṣṭhānaśīline || 7 ||
idaṃ samastāghavināśahetuṃ kāruṇyasaṃjñaṃ sukhamokṣadaṃ ca
aśeṣavāñchāphaladaṃ variṣṭhaṃ stotraṃ mayoktaṃ puruṣottamasya || 8 ||
ye taṃ susūkṣmaṃ vimalāmbarāmaṃ dhyāyanti nityaṃ puruṣaṃ purāṇam
te muktibhājaḥ praviśanti viṣṇuṃ mantrairyathājyaṃ hutamadhvarāgnau || 9 ||
ekaḥ sa devo bhavaduḥkhahantā paraḥ pareṣāṃ na tato'sti cānyaḥ
sraṣṭā sa pātā sakalāntakartā viṣṇuḥ samaścākhilasārabhūtaḥ || 10 ||
kiṃ vidyayā kiṃ suguṇaiśca teṣāṃ yajñaiśca dānaiśca tapobhirugraiḥ
yeṣāṃ na bhaktirbhavatīha kṛṣṇe jagadgurau mokṣasukhaprade ca || 11 ||
loke sa dhanyaḥ sa śuciḥ sa vidvānsa eva vaktā sa ca dharmaśīlaḥ
jñātā sa dātā sa ca satyavaktā yasyāsti bhaktiḥ puruṣottamākhye || 12 ||
stutvaivaṃ brahmatanayaḥ praṇamya ca sanātanam
vāsudevaṃ jagannāthaṃ sarvakāmaphalapradam || 13 ||
cintāviṣṭo mahīpālaḥ kuśānāstīrya bhūtale
vastraṃ ca tanmanā bhūtvā suṣvāpa dharaṇītale || 14 ||
kathaṃ pratyakṣamabhyeti devadevo janārdanaḥ
mama cintāharo devaḥ kadāsāviti cintayan || 15 ||
suptasya tasya nūpatervāsudevo jagadguruḥ
ātmānaṃ darśayāmāsa svapne tasmai ca cakradhṛk || 16 ||
sa dadarśa ca taṃ svapne devadevaṃ jagatpatim
śaṅkhacakradharaṃ śāntaṃ gadāpadmāgrapāṇinam || 17 ||
śārṅgabāṇāsiyuktaṃ ca jvalattejogramaṇḍalam
yugāntādityavarṇābhaṃ nīlavaiḍūryasannibham || 18 ||
suparṇapṛṣṭhamāsīnaṃ ṣoḍaśārddhabhujaṃ vibhum
sa cāsau prābravīdvīkṣya sādhu rājanmahāmate || 19 ||
kratunānena divyena tathā bhaktyā ca śraddhayā
tuṣṭo'smi te mahīpāla vṛthā kimanuśocasi || 20 ||
yadatra pratimā rājan rājapūjyā sanātanī
yathā tāṃ prāpnuyā bhūpa tadupāyaṃ bravīmi te || 21 ||
gatāyāmadya śarvaryāṃ nirmale bhāskarodaye
sāgarasya jalasyānte nānādrumavibhūṣite || 22 ||
jalaṃ tathaiva velāyāṃ dṛśyate yatra vai mahat
lavaṇasyodadhe rājaṃstaraṅgaiḥ samabhiplutam || 23 ||
kūlālambī mahāvṛkṣaḥ sthitaḥ sthalajaleṣu ca
velābhirhanyamānaśca na cāsau kampate dhruvaḥ || 24 ||
hastena parśumādāya ūrmerantastato vraja
ekākī viharan rājanyaṃ tvaṃ paśyasi pādapam || 25 ||
idaṃ cihnaṃ samālokya cchedaya tvamaśaṅkitaḥ
śātyamānaṃ tu taṃ vṛkṣaṃ prāṃśumadbhutadarśanam || 26 ||
dṛṣṭvā tenaiva sañcintya tadā bhūpāla darśanam
kuru tatpratimāṃ divyāṃ jahi cintāṃ vimohinīm || 27 ||
evamuktvā mahābhāgo jagāmādarśanaṃ hariḥ
sa cāpi svapnamālakṣya paraṃ vismayamāgataḥ || 28 ||
tāṃ niśāṃ samudīkṣansa tatastadgatamānasaḥ
vyāharanvaiṣṇavānmantrānsūktaṃ caiva tadātmakam || 29 ||
prabhātāyāṃ rajanyāṃ tu tudgato'nanyamānasaḥ
sa snātvā sāgare samyagyathāvadvidhinā tataḥ || 30 ||
datvā dānaṃ tu viprebhyo grāmāṃśca nagarāṇi ca
kṛtvā paurvāhṇikaṃ karma jagāma sa nṛpottamaḥ || 31 ||
na ratho na padātiśca na gajo na ca sārathiḥ
ekākī sa mahāvelāṃ praviveśa mahīpatiḥ || 32 ||
taṃ dadarśa mahāvṛkṣaṃ tejasvantaṃ mahādrumam
mahāntakaṃ mahārohaṃ puṇyaṃ viphalameva ca || 33 ||
mahotsavaṃ mahākāyaṃ prasuptaṃ ca jalāntike
sāndra māñjiṣṭhavarṇābhaṃ nāmajātivivarjitam || 34 ||
naranāthastathā viṣṇordrumaṃ dṛṣṭvā mudānvitaḥ
parśunā śātayāmāsa śitena ca dṛḍhena ca || 35 ||
dvaidhībhūtā matistatra babhūvendra sakhasya ca
nirīkṣamāṇe kāṣṭhe tu babhūvādbhutadarśanam || 36 ||
viśvakarmā ca viṣṇuśca viprarūpadharāvubhau
ājagmaturmahātmānau tathā tulyāgrajanmanau || 37 ||
jvalamānau sutejobhirdivyasraggandhalepanau
atha tau taṃ samāsādya nṛpamindra sakhaṃ tadā || 38 ||
tāvabrūtāṃ mahārāja tvaṃ kimatra kariṣyasi
kimayaṃ te mahābāho śātitaśca vanaspatiḥ || 39 ||
asahāyo mahādurge nirjane gahane vane
mahāsindhutaṭe caiva śātito vai mahādrumaḥ || 40 ||
tayoḥ śrutvā vacaḥ subhru sa tu rājā mudānvitaḥ
babhāṣe vacanānyābhyāṃ mṛdūni madhurāṇi ca || 41 ||
dṛṣṭvā tau brāhmaṇau tatra candra sūryāvivāgatau
namaskṛtya jagannāthāvavāṅmukhamavasthitaḥ || 42 ||
deva devamanādyantamameyaṃ jagataḥ patim
ārādhituṃ vai pratimāṃ karomīti matirmama || 43 ||
ahaṃ sa devadevena parameṇa mahātmanā
svapnānte ca samuddiṣṭo bhavadbhya śrāvito mayā || 44 ||
yajñastadvacanaṃ śrutvā devendra pratimasya ca
prahasya tasmai viśveśastuṣṭo vacanamabravīt || 45 ||
sādhu sādhu mahīpāla yadetanmana uttamam
saṃsārasāgare ghore kadalīdalasannibhe || 46 ||
niḥsāre duḥkhabahule kāmakrodhasamākule
indri yāvartakalile dustare lomaharṣaṇe || 47 ||
nānāvyādhiśatāvarte calabudbudasannibhe
yataste matirutpannā viṣṇorārādhanāya vai || 48 ||
dhanyastvaṃ nṛpaśārdūla guṇaiḥ sarvairalaṅkṛtaḥ
saprajā dharaṇī dhanyā saśailavanapattanā || 49 ||
sapuragrāmanagarā caturvarṇairalaṅkṛtā
yatra tvaṃ nṛpaśārdūla prajāpālayitā prabhuḥ || 50 ||
ehyehi tvaṃ mahābhāga drume'sminsukhaśītale
āvābhyāṃ saha tiṣṭha tvaṃ kathābhirddharmasaṃśritaḥ || 51 ||
ayaṃ tava sahāyārthamāgataḥ śilpināṃ varaḥ
viśvakarmasamaḥ sākṣānnipuṇaḥ sarvakarmasu || 52 ||
mayoddiṣṭāṃ tu pratimāṃ karotyeṣa taṭaṃ tyaja
śrutvaivaṃ vacanaṃ tasya tadā rājā dvijanmanaḥ || 53 ||
sāgarasya taṭaṃ tyaktvā gatvā tasya samīpataḥ
tasthau sa nṛpatiśreṣṭho vṛkṣacchāyāṃ suśītalām || 54 ||
tatastasmai sa viśvātmā tadākārāṃ tadākṛtim
śilpimukhyāya vidhije kuruṣvetyabhyabhāṣata || 55 ||
kṛṣṇarūpaṃ paraṃ śāntaṃ padmapatrāyatekṣaṇam
śrīvatsakaustubhadharaṃ śaṅkhacakragadādharam || 56 ||
gauraṃ gokṣīravarṇābhaṃ dvitīyaṃ svastikāṅkitam
lāṅgalāstradharaṃ devamanantākhyaṃ mahābalam || 57 ||
devadānavagandharvayakṣavidyādharoragaiḥ
na vijñāto hi tasyāntastenānanta iti smṛtaḥ || 58 ||
bhaginīṃ vāsudevasya rukmavarṇāṃ suśobhanām
tṛtīyāṃ vai subhadrāṃ ca sarvalakṣaṇalakṣitām || 59 ||
śrutvaitadvacanaṃ tasya viśvakarmā sukarmakṛt
tatkṣaṇātkārayāmāsa pratimāḥ śubhalakṣaṇāḥ || 60 ||
kuṇḍalābhyāṃ vicitrābhyāṃ karṇābhyāṃ suvirājitāḥ
cakralāṅgalavinyāsahatābhyāṃ bhānusammatāḥ || 61 ||
prathamaṃ śuklavarṇānāṃ śāradendusamaprabham
suraktākṣaṃ mahākāyaṃ phaṭāvikaṭamastakam || 62 ||
nīlāmbaradharaṃ cograṃ balamadbhutakuṇḍalam
mahāhaladharaṃ divyaṃ mahāmusaladhāriṇam || 63 ||
dvitīyaṃ puṇḍarīkākṣaṃ nīlajīmūtasannibham
atasīpuṣpasaṅkāśaṃ padmapatrāyatekṣaṇam || 64 ||
śrīvatsavakṣasaṃ bhrājatpītavāsasamacyutam
cakrakambukaraṃ divyaṃ sarvapāpaharaṃ harim || 65 ||
tṛtīyāṃ svarṇavarṇābhāṃ padmapatrāyatekṣaṇām
vicitravastrasañchannāṃ hārakeyūrabhūṣitām || 66 ||
vicitrābharaṇopetāṃ ratnamālāvalambitām
pīnonnatakucāṃ ramyāṃ viśvakarmā vinirmame || 67 ||
sa tu rājādbhutaṃ dṛṣṭvā kṣaṇenaikena nirmitāḥ
divyavastrayugācchannā nānāratnairalaṅkṛtāḥ || 68 ||
sarvalakṣaṇasampannāḥ pratimāḥ sumanoharāḥ
vismayaṃ paramaṃ gatvā idaṃ vacanamabravīt || 69 ||
kiṃ devau samanuprāptau dvijarūpadharāvubhau
dvāvapyadbhutakarmāṇau devavṛttau tvamānuṣau || 70 ||
devau vā mānuṣau vāpi yakṣavidyādharau varau
kiṃ vā brahmathṛṣīkeśau vasurudrā vutāśvinau || 71 ||
na vedmi satyavadbhāvau māyārūpeṇa saṃsthitau
yuvāṃ gato'smi śaraṇamātmānaṃ vadataṃ mama || 72 ||
dvija uvāca -
nāhaṃ devo na yakṣo vā na daityo na ca devarāṭ
na brahmā na ca rudro 'ha viddhi māṃ puruṣottamam || 73 ||
ārtighnaṃ sarvalokānāmanantabalapauruṣam
arcanīyo hi bhūtānāmanto yasya na vidyate || 74 ||
paṭhyate sarvaśāstreṣu vedānteṣu nigadyate
yadukta dhyānagamyaṃ ca vāsudeveti yogibhiḥ || 75 ||
ahameva svayaṃ brahmā ahaṃ viṣṇuḥ śivo hyaham
idro 'ha devarājaśca jagatsaṃyamano yamaḥ || 76 ||
pṛthivyādīni bhūtāni tretāgnirhutabhugyathā
varuṇo'pā patiścāhaṃ dharitrī ca mahīdharāḥ || 77 ||
yatkiñcidvāṅmayaṃ loke jagatsthāvarajaṅgamam
viśvarūpaṃ ca māṃ viddhi matto'nyannāsti kiñcana || 78 ||
prīto'ha te nṛpaśreṣṭha varaṃ varaya suvrata
yadiṣṭaṃ tatprayacchāmi hṛdi yatte vyavasthitam || 79 ||
maddarśanamapuṇyānāṃ svapnānte'pi na jāyate
tvaṃ punardṛḍhabhaktitvātpratyakṣaṃ dṛṣṭavānasi || 80 ||
śrutvaivaṃ vāsudevasya vacanaṃ tasya mohini
romāñcitatanurbhūtvā idaṃ stotraṃ jagau nṛpaḥ || 81 ||
rājovāca -
śriyaḥkānta namaste'stu śrīpate pītavāsase
śrīda śrīśa śrīnivāsa namaste śrīniketana || 82 ||
ādyaṃ puruṣamīśānaṃ sarveśaṃ sarvatomukham
niṣkalaṃ paramaṃ devaṃ praṇato'smi sanātanam || 83 ||
śabdātītaṃ guṇātītaṃ bhāvābhāvavivarjitam
nirlepaṃ nirguṇaṃ sūkṣmaṃ sarvajñaṃ sarvabhāvanam || 84 ||
śaṅkhacakradharaṃ devaṃ gadāmusaladhāriṇam
namasye varadaṃ devaṃ nīlotpaladalacchavim || 85 ||
nāgaparyaṅkaśayanaṃ kṣīrodārṇavavāsinam
namasye'ha hṛṣīkeśaṃ sarvapāpaharaṃ harim || 86 ||
punastvāṃ devadeveśa namasye varadaṃ vibhum
sarvalokeśvaraṃ viṣṇuṃ mokṣakāraṇamavyayam || 87 ||
evaṃ stutvā tu taṃ devaṃ praṇipatya kṛtāñjali
uvāca praṇato bhūtvā nipatya vasudhātale || 88 ||
prīto'si yadi me nātha vṛṇomi varamuttamam
devāḥ surāḥ sagandharvā yakṣarakṣomahoragāḥ || 89 ||
siddhavidyādharāḥ sādhyāḥ kinnarā guhyakāstathā
ṛṣayo ye mahābhāgā nānāśāstraviśāradāḥ || 90 ||
pravrajyāyogayuktāśca vedatattvānucintakāḥ
mokṣamārgavido ye'nye dhyāyanti param padam || 91 ||
nirmalaṃ nirguṇaṃ śāntaṃ yatpaśyanti manīṣiṇaḥ
tatpadaṃ gantumicchāmi prasādātte sudurlabham || 92 ||
śrībhagavānuvāca -
sarvaṃ bhavatu bhadraṃ te yatheṣṭaṃ sarvamāpnuhi
bhaviṣyati yathākāmaṃ matprasādānna saṃśayaḥ || 93 ||
daśa varṣasahasrāṇi tathā nava śatāni ca
avicchinnaṃ mahārājyaṃ kuru tvaṃ nṛpasattama || 94 ||
prapadya paramaṃ divyaṃ durlabhaṃ yatsurāsuraiḥ
pūrṇaṃ manorathaṃ śāntaṃ guhyamavyaktamavyayam || 95 ||
parātparataraṃ sūkṣmaṃ nirlepaṃ nirguṇaṃ dhruvam
cintāśokavinirmuktaṃ kriyākāraṇavarjitam || 96 ||
tadahaṃ darśayiṣyāmi vijñeyākhyaṃ paraṃ padam
samprāpya paramānandaṃ prāpsyase paramāṃ gatim || 97 ||
kīrtiśca tava rājendra bhavatvatra mahītale
yāvaddharā nabho yāvadyāvaccandrā rkatārakāḥ || 98 ||
yāvatsamudrāḥ saptaiva yāvanmervvādiparvatāḥ
tiṣṭhanti divi devāśca yāvatsarvatra cāvyayāḥ || 99 ||
indra dyumnasaro nāma tīrthaṃ yajñājyasambhavam
yatra snātvā sakṛllokaḥ śakralokamavāpsyati || 100 ||
dāpayiṣyati yaḥ piṇḍaṃ taṭe'sminnambudheḥ śubhe
kulaikaviṃśamuddhṛtya śakralokaṃ gamiṣyati || 101 ||
pūjyamāno'psarobhiśca gandharvairgītaniḥsvanaiḥ
vimānena carettatra yāvadindrā ścaturdaśa || 102 ||
saraso dakṣiṇe bhāge nairṛtyāṃ tu samāśrite
nyagrodho'sti drumastatra tatsamīpe tu maṇḍapaḥ || 103 ||
ketakīvanasañchanno nānāpādapasaṅkulaḥ
āṣāḍhasya site pakṣe pañcamyāṃ pitṛdaivate || 104 ||
ṛkṣe neṣyanti nastatra nītvā sapta dināni vai
maṇḍape sthāpayiṣyanti suveśyābhiḥ suśobhanaiḥ || 105 ||
krīḍāviśeṣabahulairnṛtyagītamanoharaiḥ
cāmaraiḥ svarṇadaṇḍaiśca vyajanai ratnabhūṣaṇaiḥ || 106 ||
vyañjayantyastadāsmabhyaṃ sthāsyanti paramāṅganāḥ
brahmacārī yatiścaiva snātakāśca dvijottamāḥ || 107 ||
vānaprasthā gṛhasthāśca siddhāścānye ca vai dvijāḥ
nānāvarṇapadaiḥ stotrairṛgyajuḥsāmaniḥsvanaiḥ || 108 ||
kariṣyanti stutiṃ rājan rāmakeśavayoḥ punaḥ
tataḥ stutvā ca dṛṣṭvā ca sampraṇamya ca bhaktitaḥ || 109 ||
naro varṣāyutaṃ divyaṃ śrīmaddharipure vaset
pūjyamāno'psarobhiśca gandharvairgītaniḥsvanaiḥ || 110 ||
hareranucarastatra krīḍate keśavena vai || 111 ||
vimānenārkavarṇena ratnahāreṇa rājate
sarve kāmā mahābhāge tiṣṭhanti bhavanottame || 112 ||
tapaḥkṣayādihāgatya manuṣyo brāhmaṇo bhavet
koṭīdhanapatiḥ śrīmāṃścaturvedo bhaveddhruvam || 113 ||
evaṃ tasmai varaṃ dattvā kṛtvā ca samayaṃ hariḥ
jagāmādarśanaṃ bhadre sahito viśvakarmaṇā || 114 ||
sa tu rājā tadā hṛṣṭo romāñcitatanūruhaḥ
kṛtakṛtyamivātmānaṃ mene sandarśanāddhareḥ || 115 ||
tataḥ kṛṣṇaṃ ca rāmaṃ ca subhadrāṃ ca varapradām
rathairvimānasaṅkāśairmaṇikāñcanacitritaiḥ || 116 ||
saṃvāhya tāṃstato rājā jayamaṅgalaniḥsvanaiḥ
ānayāmāsa matimānsāmātyaḥ sapurohitaḥ || 117 ||
nānāvāditranirghoṣairnānāvedasvanaiḥ śubhaiḥ
saṃsthāpya ca śubhe deśe pavitre sumanohare || 118 ||
tataḥ śubhe tithau svarkṣe kāle ca śubhalakṣaṇe
pratiṣṭhāṃ kārayāmāsa sumuhūrte dvijaiḥ saha || 119 ||
yathoktena vidhānena vidhidṛṣṭena karmaṇā
ācāryānumatenaiva sarvaṃ kṛtvā mahīpatiḥ || 120 ||
ācāryāya tadā dattvā dakṣiṇāṃ vidhivatprabhuḥ
ṛtvigbhyaḥ savidhānena tathānyebhyo dhanaṃ dadau || 121 ||
kṛtvā pratiṣṭhāṃ vidhivatprāsāde bhavanottame
sthāpayāmāsa tānsarvānviśvakarmavinirmitān || 122 ||
tataḥ sampūjya vidhinā nānāpuṣpaiḥ sugandhibhiḥ
suvarṇamaṇimuktādyairnānāvastraiḥ suśobhanaiḥ || 123 ||
dadau dvijebhyo viṣayānpurāṇi nagarāṇi ca
evaṃ bahuvidhāndattvā rājyaṃ kṛtvā yathocitam || 124 ||
iṣṭvā ca vividhairyajñairdattvā dānānyanekaśaḥ
kṛtakṛtyastato rājā tyaktasarvaparigrahaḥ
jagāma paramaṃ sthānaṃ tadviṣṇoḥ paramaṃ padam || 125 ||
iti śrībṛhannāradīyapurāṇottarabhāge mohinīvasusaṃvāde śrīpuruṣottamamāhātmye catuṣpañcāśattamo'dhyāyaḥ || 54 ||

<2.55>

atha pañcapañcāśattamo'dhyāyaḥ
mohinyuvāca -
kasmin kāle dvijaśreṣṭha gantavyaṃ puruṣottame
vidhinā kena kartavyā pañcatīrthyapi mānada || 1 ||
ekaikasya ca tīrthasya snāne dāne ca yatphalam
devatāprekṣaṇe caiva brūhi sarvaṃ pṛthak pṛthak || 2 ||
vasuruvāca -
nirāhāraḥ kurukṣetre pādenaikena yastapet
jitendri yo jitakrodhaḥ saptasaṃvatsarāyutam || 3 ||
dṛṣṭvā sakṛjjyeṣṭhaśukladvādaśyāṃ puruṣottamam
kṛtopavāsaḥ prāpnoti tato'dhikataraṃ phalam || 4 ||
tasmājjyeṣṭhe tu subhage prayatnena susaṃyataiḥ
svargalokepsubhirmartyairdra ṣṭavyaḥ puruṣottamaḥ || 5 ||
pañcatīrthīṃ ca vidhivatkṛtvā jyeṣṭhe narottamaḥ
dvādaśyāṃ śuklapakṣasya paśyettaṃ puruṣottamam || 6 ||
ye paśyantyavyayaṃ devaṃ dvādaśyāṃ puruṣottamam
te viṣṇulokamāsādya na cyavante kadācana || 7 ||
tasmājjyeṣṭhe prayatnena gantavyaṃ vidhinandini
kṛtvā samyakpañcatīrthīṃ dra ṣṭavyaḥ puruṣottamaḥ || 8 ||
sudūrastho'pi prītātmā kīrtayetpuruṣottamam
ahanyahani śuddhātmā so'pi viṣṇupuraṃ vrajet || 9 ||
yātrāṃ karoti kṛṣṇasya śraddhayā yaḥ samāhitaḥ
sarvapāpavinirmukto viṣṇulokaṃ vrajennaraḥ || 10 ||
cakraṃ dṛṣṭvā harerdūrātprāsādopari saṃsthitam
sahasā mucyate pāpānnaro bhaktyā praṇamya tam || 11 ||
pañcatīrthīvidhiṃ vakṣye śṛṇu mohini sāmpaṃtam
yasyāṃ kṛtāyāṃ manujo mādhavasya priyo bhavet || 12 ||
mārkaṇḍeyahradaṃ gatvā snātvā codaṅmukhaḥ śuciḥ
nimajjettatra trīnvārānimaṃ mantramudīrayet || 13 ||
saṃsārasāgare magnaṃ pāpagrastamacetanam
trāhi māṃ bhaganetraghna tripurāre namo'stu te || 14 ||
namaḥ śivāya śāntāya sarvapāpaharāya ca
snānaṃ karomi deveśa mama naśyatu pātakam || 15 ||
nābhimātre jale sthitvā vidhivaddevatā ṛṣīn
tilodakena matimānpitṝnanyāṃśca tarpayet || 16 ||
snātvaivaṃ ca tathācamya tato gacchecchivālayam
praviśya devatāgāraṃ kṛtvā taṃ triḥ pradakṣiṇam || 17 ||
mūlamantreṇa sampūjya mārkaṇḍeyeśamādarāt
aghoreṇa tu mantreṇa praṇipatya kṣamāpayet || 18 ||
trilocana namaste'stu namaste śaśibhūṣaṇa
trāhi māṃ puṇḍarīkākṣa mahādeva namo'stu te || 19 ||
mārkaṇḍeyahrade tvevaṃ snātvā dṛṣṭvā ca śaṅkaram
daśānāmaśvamedhānāṃ phalaṃ prāpnoti mānavaḥ || 20 ||
pāpaiḥ sarvairvinirmuktaḥ śivalokaṃ sa gacchati
tatra bhuktvā varānbhogānyāvadābhūtasamplavam || 21 ||
iha loke samāsādya bhavedvipro bahuśrutaḥ
śāṅkaraṃ yogamāsādya tato mokṣamavāpnuyāt || 22 ||
kalpavṛkṣaṃ tato gatvā kṛtvā taṃ triḥ pradakṣiṇam
pūjayetparayā bhaktyā mantreṇānena taṃ vaṭam || 23 ||
oṃ namo'vyaktarūpāya mahate natapāline
mahodakopaviṣṭāya nyagrodhāya namo'stu te || 24 ||
avasastvaṃ sadā kalpe hareścāyatane vaṭe
nyagrodha hara me pāpaṃ kalpavṛkṣa namo'stu te || 25 ||
bhaktyā pradakṣiṇaṃ kṛtvā gatvā kalpavaṭaṃ naraḥ
sahasojjhati pāpaughaṃ jīrṇāṃ tvacamivoragaḥ || 26 ||
chāyāṃ tasya samākramya kalpavṛkṣasya mohini
brahmahatyāṃ naro jahyātpāpeṣvanyeṣu kā kathā || 27 ||
dṛṣṭvā kṛṣṇāṅgasambhūtaṃ brahmatejomayaṃ param
nyagrodhākṛtikaṃ viṣṇuṃ praṇipatya ca vaidhasi || 28 ||
rājasūyāśvayedhābhyāṃ phalaṃ prāpnoti cādhikam
tathā kulaṃ samuddhṛtya viṣṇulokaṃ sa gacchati || 29 ||
vainateyaṃ namaskṛtya kṛṣṇasya purataḥ sthitam
sarvapāpavinirmuktastato viṣṇupuraṃ vrajet || 30 ||
dṛṣṭvā vaṭaṃ vainateyaṃ yaḥ paśyetpuruṣottamam
saṅkarṣaṇaṃ subhadrāṃ ca sa yāti paramāṃ gatim || 31 ||
praviśyāyatanaṃ viṣṇoḥ kṛtvā taṃ triḥ pradakṣiṇam
saṅkarṣaṇaṃ subhadrāṃ ca bhaktyā pūjya prasādayet || 32 ||
namaste haladhṛṅnāmne namaste musalāyudha
namaste revatīkānta namaste bhaktavatsala || 33 ||
namaste balināṃ śreṣṭha namaste dharaṇīdhara
pralambāre namaste'stu trāhi māṃ kṛṣṇapūrvaja || 34 ||
evaṃ prasādya cānantamajeyaṃ tridaśārcitam
kailāsaśikharākāraṃ candra kāntavarānanam || 35 ||
nīlavastradharaṃ devaṃ phaṇāvikaṭamastakam
mahābalaṃ haladharaṃ kuṇḍalaikavibhūṣitam || 36 ||
rauhiṇeyaṃ naro bhaktyā labhedabhimataṃ phalam
sarvapāpairvinirmukto viṣṇulokaṃ ca gacchati || 37 ||
ābhūtasamplavaṃ yāvadbhuktvā tatra svayaṃ budhaḥ
puṇyakṣayādihāgatya pravaro yogināṃ kule || 38 ||
brāhmaṇapravaro bhūtvā sarvaśāstrārthapāragaḥ
jñānaṃ tatra samāsādya muktiṃ prāpnoti durlabhām || 39 ||
evamabhyarcya halinaṃ tataḥ kṛṣṇaṃ vicakṣaṇaḥ
dvādaśākṣaramantreṇa pūjayetsusamāhitaḥ || 40 ||
dviṣaṭvarṇakamantreṇa bhaktyā ye puruṣottamam
pūjayanti sadā dhīrāste mokṣaṃ prāpnuvanti vai || 41 ||
na tāṃ gatiṃ surā yānti yogino naiva somapāḥ
yāṃ gatiṃ yānti vidhije dvādaśākṣaratatparāḥ || 42 ||
tasmāttenaiva mantreṇa bhaktyā kṛṣṇaṃ jagadgurum
sampūjya gandhapuṣpādyaiḥ praṇipatya prasādayet || 43 ||
jaya kṛṣṇa jagannātha jaya sarvāghanāśana
jaya cāṇūrakeśighna jaya kaṃsaniṣūdana || 44 ||
jaya padmapalāśākṣa jaya cakragadādhara
jaya nīlāmbudaśyāma jaya sarvasukhaprada || 45 ||
jaya deva jagatpūjya jaya saṃsāranāśana
jaya lokapate nātha jaya vāñchāphalaprada || 46 ||
saṃsārasāgare ghore niḥsāre duḥkhaphenile
krodhagrāhakule raudre viṣayodakasamplave || 47 ||
nānārogormikalile mohāvartasudustare
nimagno'ha suraśreṣṭha trāhi māṃ puruṣottama || 48 ||
evaṃ prasādya deveśaṃ varadaṃ bhaktavatsalam
sarvapāpaharaṃ devaṃ sarvakāmaphalapradam || 49 ||
pīnāṃsaṃ dvibhujaṃ kṛṣṇaṃ padmapatrāyatekṣaṇam
mahoraskaṃ mahābāhuṃ pītavastraṃ śubhānanam || 50 ||
śaṅkhacakragadāpāṇiṃ mukuṭāṅgadabhūṣaṇam
sarvalakṣaṇasaṃyuktaṃ vanamālāvibhūṣitam || 51 ||
dṛṣṭvā naro'ñjali kṛtvā daṇḍavatpraṇipatya ca
aśvamedhasahasrāṇāṃ phalaṃ prāpnoti mohini || 52 ||
yatphalaṃ sarvatīrtheṣu snāne dāne prakīrtitam
narastatphalamāpnoti dṛṣṭvā kṛṣṇaṃ praṇamya ca || 53 ||
yatphalaṃ sarvavedeṣu sarvayajñeṣu yatphalam
tatphalaṃ samavāpnoti naraḥ kṛṣṇaṃ praṇamya ca || 54 ||
yatphalaṃ sarvadāneṣu vrateṣu niyameṣu ca
narastatphalamāpnoti dṛṣṭvā kṛṣṇaṃ praṇamya ca || 55 ||
yatphalaṃ brahmacaryeṇa samyak cīrṇena kīrtitam
narastatphalamāpnoti dṛṣṭvā kṛṣṇaṃ praṇamya ca || 56 ||
gārhasthyena yathoktena yatphalaṃ samudāhṛtam
narastatphalamāpnoti dṛṣṭvā kṛṣṇaṃ praṇamya ca || 57 ||
yatphalaṃ vanavāsena vānaprasthasya kīrtitam
narastatphalamāpnoti dṛṣṭvā kṛṣṇaṃ praṇamya ca || 58 ||
sanyāsena yathoktena yatphalaṃ samudāhṛtam
narastatphalamāpnoti dṛṣṭvā kṛṣṇaṃ praṇamya ca || 59 ||
kiṃ cātra bahunoktena māhātmyaṃ tasya bhāmini
dṛṣṭvā kṛṣṇaṃ naro bhaktyā mokṣaṃ prāpnoti durlabham || 60 ||
pāpairvimuktaḥ śuddhātmā kalpakoṭisamudbhavaiḥ
śriyā paramayā yuktaḥ sarvaiḥ samudito guṇaiḥ || 61 ||
sarvakāmasamṛddhena vimānena suvarcasā
triḥsaptakulamuddhṛtya naro viṣṇupuraṃ vrajet || 62 ||
tataḥ kalpaśataṃ yāvadbhuktvā bhogānmanoramān
gandharvāpsarasaiḥ sārdhaṃ yathā viṣṇuścaturbhujaḥ || 63 ||
cyutastasmādihāyāto viprāṇāṃ pravare kule
sarvajñaḥ sarvavedī ca jāyate gatamatsaraḥ || 64 ||
svadharmanirataḥ śānto dātā bhūtahite rataḥ
āsādya vaiṣṇavaṃ jñānaṃ tato muktimavāpnuyāt || 65 ||
tataḥ sampūjya mantreṇa subhadrāṃ bhaktavatsalām
prasādayecca vidhije praṇipatya kṛtāñjali || 66 ||
namaste sarvage devi namaste śubhasaukhyade
trāhi māṃ padmapatrākṣi kātyāyani namo'stu te || 67 ||
evaṃ prasādyatāṃ devīṃ jagaddhātrīṃ jagaddhitām
baledavasya bhaginīṃ subhadrāṃ varadāṃ śivām || 68 ||
kāmagena vimānena naro viṣṇupuraṃ vrajet
ābhūtasamplavaṃ yāvatkīḍitvā tatra devavat || 69 ||
iha mānuṣatāṃ prāpto brāhmaṇo vedavidbhavet
prāpya yogaṃ harestatra mokṣaṃ ca labhate dhruvam || 70 ||
niṣkramya devatāgārātkṛtakṛtyo bhavennaraḥ
praṇamyāyatane paścādvrajettatra samāhitaḥ || 71 ||
indra nīlamayo viṣṇuryatrāste bālukāvṛtaḥ
antardhāne'pi taṃ natvā tato viṣṇupuraṃ vrajet || 72 ||
sarvadevamayo devo hiraṇyakaśipūddharaḥ
yatrāste nityadā devi siṃhārddhakṛtavigrahaḥ || 73 ||
bhaktyā dṛṣṭvā tu taṃ devaṃ praṇamya nṛhariṃ śubhe
mucyate pātakairmartyaḥ samastairnātra saṃśayaḥ || 74 ||
narasiṃhasya ye bhaktā bhavanti bhuvi mānavāḥ
na teṣāṃ duṣkṛtaṃ kiñcitphalaṃ ca syādyadīpsitam || 75 ||
tasmātsarvaprayatnena narasiṃhaṃ samāśrayet
dharmārthakāmamokṣāṇāṃ phalaṃ yasmātprayacchati || 76 ||
tasmāttaṃ brahmatanaye bhaktyā sampūjayetsadā
mṛgarājaṃ mahāvīryaṃ sarvakāmaphalapradam || 77 ||
brāhmaṇāḥ kṣatriyā vaiśyāḥ striyaḥ śūdrā ntyajādayaḥ
sampūjya tu suraśreṣṭhaṃ bhaktāḥ siṃhavapurddharam || 78 ||
mucyante cāśubhādduḥkhājjanmakoṭisamudbhavāt
sampūjya taṃ suraśreṣṭhaṃ prāpnuvantyabhivāñchitam || 79 ||
devatvamamareśatvaṃ dhaneśatvaṃ ca bhāmini
yakṣavidyādharatvaṃ ca tathānyacca prayacchati || 80 ||
śṛṇuṣva narasiṃhasya prabhāvaṃ vidhinandini
ajitasyāprameyasya bhuktimuktipradasya ca || 81 ||
kaḥ śaknoti guṇānvaktuṃ samastāṃstasya suvrate
siṃhārddhakṛtadehasya pravakṣyāmi samāsataḥ || 82 ||
yāḥ kāścitsiddhayaścātra śrūyante daivamānuṣāḥ
prasādāttasya tāḥ sarvāḥ siddhynte nātra saṃśayaḥ || 83 ||
svarge martye ca pātāle divi toye sure nage
prasādāttasya devasya bhavatyavyāhatā gatiḥ || 84 ||
asādhyaṃ tasya devasya nāstyatra sacarācare
narasiṃhasya subhage sadā bhaktānukampinaḥ || 85 ||
vidhānaṃ tasya vakṣyāmi bhaktānāmupakārakam
yena prasīdate cāsau siṃhārddhakṛtavigrahaḥ || 86 ||
yattattvaṃ tasya devasya tadajñātaṃ surāsuraiḥ
śākayāvakamūlaistu phalapiṇyākasaktubhiḥ || 87 ||
payobhakṣyeṇa vā bhadre vartate sādhakeśvaraḥ
kāsakaupīnavāsāśca dhyānayukto jitendri yaḥ || 88 ||
araṇye vijane deśe nadīsaṅgamaparvate
siddhakṣetre coṣare ca narasiṃhāśrame tathā || 89 ||
pratiṣṭhāpya svayaṃ cāpi pūjāṃ kṛtvā vidhānataḥ
upapātakavāndevi mahāpātakavānapi || 90 ||
mukto bhavetpātakebhyaḥ sādhako nātra saṃśayaḥ
kṛtvā pradakṣiṇaṃ tatra narasiṃhaṃ prapūjayet || 91 ||
gandhapuṣpādibhirdhūpaiḥ praṇamya śirasā prabhum
karpūracandanāktāni jātīpuṣpāṇi mastake || 92 ||
pradadyānnarasiṃhasya tataḥ siddhiḥ prajāyate
bhagavānsarvakāryeṣu na kvacitpratidūyate || 93 ||
na śaktāstaṃ samākrāntuṃ brahmarudrā dayaḥ surāḥ
kiṃ punardānavā loke siddhagandharvamānuṣāḥ || 94 ||
vidyādharāḥ yakṣagaṇāḥ sakinnaramahoragāḥ
te sarve pralayaṃ yānti divyā divyāgnitejasā || 95 ||
sakṛjjaptvāgniśikhayā hanyātsarvānupadra vān
trirjaptvā kavacaṃ divyaṃ saṃrakṣeddaityadānavāt || 96 ||
bhūtātpiśācādra kṣobhyo ye cānye paripaṃthinaḥ
trirjaptaṃ kavacaṃ divyamabhedyaṃ ca surāsuraiḥ || 97 ||
yojanadvādaśāntastu devo rakṣati sarvadā
narasiṃho mahābhāge mahābalaparākramaḥ || 98 ||
tato gatvā biladvāramupoṣya rajanītrayam
palāśakāṣṭhaiḥ prajvālya bhagavajjātavedasam || 99 ||
pālāśasamidhaṃ tatra juhuyāttrimadhuplutām
dve'yute kañjanayane śakaṭaścaiva sādhakaḥ || 100 ||
tataḥ kavallīvivaraṃ prakaṭaṃ jāyate kṣaṇāt
tato viśettu niḥśaṅkaḥ kavallīvivaraṃ budhaḥ || 101 ||
gacchataḥ śakaṭasyātha tamo mohaśca naśyati
rājamārgastu vistīrṇo dṛśyate tatra mohini || 102 ||
narasiṃhaṃ smaraṃstatra pātālaṃ viśate tadā
gatvā tatra japecchuddho narasiṃhaṃ tamavyayam || 103 ||
tataḥ strīṇāṃ sahasrāṇi vīṇācāmarakarmaṇām
nirgacchanti purāccaiva svāgataṃ tā vadanti ca || 104 ||
praveśayanti taṃ haste gṛhītvā sādhakeśvaram
tato rasāyanaṃ divyaṃ pāyayanti sulocane || 105 ||
pītamātre divyadeho jāyate sumahābalaḥ
krīḍate divyakanyābhiryāvadābhūtasamplavam || 106 ||
bhinnadeho vāsudevaṃ nīyate nātra saṃśayaḥ
yadā na rocayantyetāstato nirgacchate punaḥ || 107 ||
paṭṭaṃ śūlaṃ ca khaḍgaṃ ca rocanāṃ ca maṇiṃ tathā
rasaṃ rasāyanaṃ caiva pādukāñjanameva ca || 108 ||
kṛṣṇāñjali ca subhage guṭikāṃ ca manaḥ śilām
muṇḍalāṃ cākṣasūtraṃ ca ṣaṣṭhīṃ sañjīvanīṃ tathā || 109 ||
siddhāṃ vidyāṃ ca śāstrāṇi gṛhītvā sādhakottamaḥ
jvaladvahnisphuliṅgormiveṣṭitaṃ tridaśaṃ hṛdi || 110 ||
sakṛnnyastaṃ dahetsarvaṃ vṛjinaṃ janmakoṭijam
viṣe nyastaṃ viṣaṃ hanyātkuṣṭhaṃ hanyāttanau sthitam || 111 ||
sudehabhrūṇahatyādi kṛtvā divyena śudhyati
mahāgrahagṛhīteṣu jvalamānaṃ vicintayet || 112 ||
rudanti vai tataḥ śīghraṃ naśyeyurdāruṇā grahāḥ
bālānāṃ kaṇṭhake baddhvā rakṣā bhavati nityaśaḥ || 113 ||
gaṇḍapiṇḍakakṛtyānāṃ nāśanaṃ kurute dhruvam
vyādhighāte samidbhiśca ghṛtaṃ kṣīreṇa homayet || 114 ||
trisandhyaṃ māsamekaṃ tu sarvarogānvināśayet
asādhyaṃ nāsya paśyāmi tattvasya sacarācare || 115 ||
yāṃ yāṃ kāmayate siddhiṃ tāṃ tāṃ prāpnotyapi dhruvam
aṣṭottaraśataṃ tvekaṃ pūjayitvā mṛgādhipam || 116 ||
mṛttikāṃ sapta valmīke śmaśāne ca catuṣpathe
raktacandanasammiśrāṃ gavāṃ kṣīreṇa lepayet || 117 ||
siṃhasya pratimāṃ kṛtvā pramāṇena ṣaṅgulām
bhūrjapatre viśeṣeṇa likhedro canayā tathā || 118 ||
narasiṃhasya kaṇṭhe tu baddhvā caiva samantravat
japetsaṅkhyāvihīnaṃ tu pūjayitvā jalāśaye || 119 ||
yāvanmantraṃ tu japati saptāhaṃ saṃyatendri yaḥ
jalākīrṇā muhūrtena jāyate sarvabhedinī || 120 ||
athavā śuddhavṛkṣāgre narasiṃhaṃ tu pūjayet
japtvā cāṣṭaśataṃ tattvaṃ varṣaṃ tadvinivārayet || 121 ||
tameva piñjake baddhvā bhrāmayetsādhakottamaḥ
mahāvāto muhūrtena āgacchennātra saṃśayaḥ || 122 ||
punaścāvārayansiktāṃ saptajaptena vāriṇā
atha tāṃ pratimāṃ dvāre nikhanedyasya sādhakaḥ || 123 ||
gotrotsādo bhavettasya uddhṛte caiva śāntidaḥ
tasmādvai brahmatanaye pūjayedbhaktitaḥ sadā || 124 ||
mṛgarājaṃ mahāvīryaṃ sarvakāmaphalapradam
dṛṣṭvā stutvā namaskṛtya sampūjya nṛhariṃ śubhe || 125 ||
prāpnuvanti narā rājyaṃ svargaṃ mokṣaṃ ca durlabham
narasiṃhaṃ naro dṛṣṭvā labhedabhimataṃ phalam || 126 ||
vimuktaḥ sarvapāpebhyo viṣṇulokaṃ ca gacchati
sakṛd dṛṣṭvā tu taṃ devaṃ bhaktyā siṃhavapurddharam || 127 ||
mucyate pātakaiḥ sarvaiḥ kāyavākcittasambhavaiḥ
saṅgrāme saṅkaṭe durge cauravyāghrādipīḍite || 128 ||
kāntāre prāṇasandehe viṣavahnijaleṣu ca
rājādibhīṣu saṅgrāme graharogādipīḍite || 129 ||
smṛtvā taṃ yo hi puruṣaḥ saṅkaṭairvipramucyate
sūryodaye yathā nāśaṃ tamo'bhyeti mahattaram || 130 ||
tathā sandarśane tasya vināśaṃ yāntyupadra vāḥ
guṭikāṃ janapātāle pādaleparasāyanam || 131 ||
narasiṃhe prasanne tu prāpnotyanyāṃśca vāñchitān
yānyānkāmānabhidhyāyanbhajeta nṛhariṃ naraḥ || 132 ||
tāṃstānkāmānavāpnoti naro nāstyatra saṃśayaḥ || 133 ||
iti śrībṛhannāradīyapurāṇottarabhāge vasumohinisaṃvāde puruṣottamamāhātmye pañcapañcāśattamo'dhyāyaḥ || 55 ||

<2.56>

atha ṣaṭpañcāśattamo'dhyāyaḥ
vasuruvāca -
anyacchṛṇu mahābhāge tasmiñchrīpuruṣottame
tīrthavrajaṃ mahatpuṇyaṃ darśanātpāpanāśanam || 1 ||
anantākhyaṃ vāsudevaṃ dṛṣṭvā bhaktyā praṇamya ca
sarvapāpavinirmukto naro yāti paraṃ padam || 2 ||
śvetagaṅgāṃ naraḥ snātvā yaḥ paśyecchvetamādhavam
matsyākhyaṃ mādhavaṃ caiva śvetadvīpaṃ sa gacchati || 3 ||
tuṣārapratimaṃ śuddhaṃ śaṅkhacakragadādharam
sarvalakṣaṇasaṃyuktaṃ puṇḍarīkāyatekṣaṇam || 4 ||
śrīvatsavakṣasā yuktaṃ suprasannaṃ caturbhujam
vanamālāvṛtoraskaṃ mukuṭāṅgadadhāriṇam || 5 ||
pītavastraṃ supīnāṃsaṃ kuṇḍalābhyāmalaṃkṛtam
kuśāgreṇāpi rājendra śvetagāṅgeyameva ca || 6 ||
spṛṣṭvā svargaṃ gamiṣyanti viṣṇubhaktāḥ samāhitāḥ
yastvimāṃ pratimāṃ paśyenmādhavākhyāṃ śaśiprabhām || 7 ||
śaṅkhagokṣīrasaṅkāśāmaśeṣāghavināśinīm
tāṃ praṇamya sakṛdbhaktyā puṇḍarīkanibhekṣaṇām || 8 ||
vihāya sarvakāmānvai viṣṇuloke mahīyate
manvantarāṇi tatraiva devakanyābhirāvṛtaḥ || 9 ||
gīyamānaśca gandharvaiḥ siddhavidyādharārcitaḥ
bhunakti vipulānbhogānyatheṣṭaṃ daivataiḥ saha || 10 ||
cyutastasmādihāgatya mānuṣye brāhmaṇo bhavet
vedavedāṅgaviddhīmān bhogavāṃścirajīvitaḥ || 11 ||
gajāśvarathayānāḍhyo dhanadhānyavṛtaḥ śuciḥ
rūpavānbahubhāgyaśca putrapautrasamanvitaḥ || 12 ||
puruṣottamaṃ punaḥ prāpya vaṭamūle'tha sāgare
tyaktvā dehaṃ hariṃ smṛtvā tataḥ śāntaṃ padaṃ vrajet || 13 ||
śvetamādhavamālokya samīpe matsyamādhavam
ekārṇave jale pūrvaṃ rūpaṃ rohitamāsthitaḥ || 14 ||
vedānāṃ haraṇārthāya rasātalatale sthitaḥ
cintayitvā kṣitiṃ matsyaṃ tasminsthāne vyavasthitam || 15 ||
ādhāya taruṇaṃ rūpaṃ mādhavaṃ matsyamādhavam
praṇamya prayato bhūtvā sarvānkaṣṭānvimuñcati || 16 ||
prayāti paramaṃ sthānaṃ yatra devo hariḥ svayam
kāle punarihāyāto rājā syātpṛthivītale || 17 ||
matsyamādhavamāsādya durādharṣo bhavennaraḥ
dātā bhoktā bhavedyoddhā vaiṣṇavaḥ satyasaṅgaraḥ || 18 ||
yogaṃ prāpya hareḥ paścāttato mokṣamavāpnuyāt
matsyamādhavamāhātmyaṃ mayā te parikīrtitam || 19 ||
yaṃ dṛṣṭvā brahmatanaye sarvānkāmānavāpnuyāt
mārjanaṃ tatra vakṣyāmi mārkaṇḍeyahrade śubhe || 20 ||
bhaktyā tu tanmanā bhūtvā purāṇaṃ puṇyamuktidam
mārkaṃḍeyahrade snānaṃ sarvakālaṃ praśasyate || 21 ||
caturdaśyāṃ viśeṣeṇa sarvapāpapraṇāśanam
tadvatsnānaṃ samudra sya sarvakālaṃ praśasyate || 22 ||
paurṇamāsyāṃ viśeṣeṇa hayamedhaphalaṃ labhet
pūrṇimā jyeṣṭhamāsasya jyeṣṭhā ṛkṣaṃ yadā bhavet || 23 ||
tadā gacchedviśeṣeṇa tīrtharājaṃ paraṃ śubham
kāyavāṅmānasaiḥ śuddhasadbhāvo'nanyamānasaḥ || 24 ||
sarvadvandvavinirmukto vītarāgo vimatsaraḥ
kṛlpavṛkṣaṃ vaṭaṃ ramyaṃ yatra sākṣājjanārdanaḥ || 25 ||
pradakṣiṇaṃ prakurvīta trīnvārānsusamāhitaḥ
dṛṣṭvā naśyati yatpāpaṃ saptajanmasamudbhavam || 26 ||
puṇyaṃ prāpnoti vipulaṃ gatimiṣṭāṃ ca mohini
tasya nāmāni vakṣyāmi sapramāṇaṃ yuge yuge || 27 ||
vaṭaṃ vaṭeśvaraṃ śāntaṃ purāṇapuruṣaṃ viduḥ
vaṭasyaitāni nāmāni kīrtitāni kṛtādiṣu || 28 ||
yojanaṃ pādahīnaṃ ca yojanārddhaṃ tadarddhakam
pramāṇaṃ kalpavṛkṣasya kṛtādiṣu yathākramam || 29 ||
pūrvoktena tu mantreṇa namaskṛtvā ca taṃ vaṭam
dakṣiṇābhimukho gaccheddhanvantaraśatatrayam || 30 ||
yatrāsau dṛśyate cihnaṃ svargadvāraṃ manoramam
sāgarāntaḥ samākṛṣṭaṃ kāṣṭhaṃ sarvaguṇānvitam || 31 ||
praṇipatya tatastiṣṭhetparipūjya tataḥ punaḥ
mucyate sarvapāpaughaistathā pāpagrahādibhiḥ || 32 ||
ugrasenaḥ purā dṛṣṭvā svargadvāreṇa sāgaram
gatvā'camya śucistatra dhyātvā nārāyaṇaṃ param || 33 ||
nyasedaṣṭākṣaraṃ mantraṃ paścāddhastaśarīrayoḥ
oṃ namo nārāyaṇāyeti yaṃ vadanti manīṣiṇaḥ || 34 ||
kiṃ kāryaṃ bahubhirmantrairmanovibhavakārakaiḥ
namo nārāyaṇāyeti mantraḥ sarvārthasādhakaḥ || 35 ||
āpo narasya sūnutvānnārā iti ha kīrtitāḥ
viṣṇostāstvālayaṃ pūrvaṃ tena nārāyaṇaḥ smṛtaḥ || 36 ||
nārāyaṇaparā vedā nārāyaṇaparā dvijāḥ
nārāyaṇaparaṃ jñānaṃ nārāyaṇaparā kriyā || 37 ||
nārāyaṇaparo dharmo nārāyaṇaparaṃ tapaḥ
nārāyaṇaparaṃ dānaṃ nārāyaṇaparaṃ vratam || 38 ||
nārāyaṇaparāḥ lokā nārāyaparāḥ surāḥ
nārāyaṇaparaṃ nityaṃ nārāyaṇaparaṃ padam || 39 ||
nārāyaṇaparā pṛthvī nārāyaṇaparaṃ jalam
nārāyaṇaparo vahnirnārāyaṇaparaṃ nabhaḥ || 40 ||
nārāyaṇaparo vāyurnārāyaṇaparaṃ manaḥ
ahaṅkāraśca buddhiśca ubhe nārāyaṇātmake || 41 ||
bhūtaṃ bhavyaṃ bhaviṣyacca yatkiñcijjīvasaṃjñitam
sthūlaṃ sūkṣmaṃ paraṃ caiva sarvaṃ nārāyaṇātmakam || 42 ||
nārāyaṇātparaṃ kiñcinneha paśyāmi mohini
tena vyāptamidaṃ sarvaṃ dṛśyādṛśyaṃ carācaram || 43 ||
āpo hyāyatanaṃ viṣṇoḥ sa cāsāvambhasāṃpatiḥ
tasmādapsu sa ityevaṃ nārāyaṇamaghāpaham || 44 ||
snānakāle viśeṣeṇa copasthāya jale śuciḥ
smarennārāyaṇaṃ dhyāyeddhaste kāye ca vinyaset || 45 ||
oṃkāraṃ vāmakaṭyā tu nākāraṃ dakṣiṇe tathā
rākāraṃ nābhideśe tu yakāraṃ vāmabāhuke || 46 ||
ṇākāraṃ dakṣiṇe nyasya yakāraṃ mūrdhni vinyaset
adhaścorddhvaṃ ca hṛdaye pārśvataḥ pṛṣṭhato'grataḥ || 47 ||
dhyātvā nārāyaṇaṃ paścādārabhetkavacaṃ budhaḥ
pūrve māṃ pātu govindo dakṣiṇe madhusūdanaḥ || 48 ||
paścime śrīdharo devaḥ keśavastu tathottare
pātu viṣṇustathāgneye nairṛte mādhavo'vyayaḥ || 49 ||
vāyavye tu hṛṣīkeśastatheśāne ca vāmanaḥ
bhūtale pātu vārāhastathorddhve ca trivikramaḥ || 50 ||
kṛtvaivaṃ kavacaṃ paścādātmānaṃ cintayettataḥ
ahaṃ nārāyaṇo devaḥ śaṅkhacakragadādharaḥ || 51 ||
evaṃ dhyātvā tadātmānamimaṃ mantramudīrayet
tvamagnirdvipadāṃ nātha retodhāḥ kāmadīpanaḥ || 52 ||
pradhānaḥ sarvabhūtānāṃ jīvānāṃ prabhuravyayaḥ
amṛtasyāraṇistvaṃ hi devayonirapāṃpate || 53 ||
vṛjinaṃ hara me sarvaṃ tīrtharāja namo'stu te
evamuccārya vidhivattataḥ snānaṃ samācaret || 54 ||
anyathā brahmatanaye snānaṃ tatra na śasyate
kṛtvā cābdaivatairmatrairabhiṣekaṃ ca mārjanam || 55 ||
antarjale japanpaścāttrirāvṛtyāghamarṣaṇam
hayamedho yathā devi sarvapāpaharaḥ kratuḥ || 56 ||
tathāghamarṣaṇaṃ cātra sūktaṃ sarvāghamarṣaṇam
uttīrya vāsasī dhaute nirmale paridhāya ca || 57 ||
prāṇānāyamya cācamya sandhyāṃ copāsya bhāskaram
upātiṣṭhettataścorddhvaṃ kṣiptvā puṣpajalāñjalim || 58 ||
upasthāyorddhvabāhuśca talliṅgairbhāskaraṃ tataḥ
gāyatrīṃ pāvanīṃ devīṃ japedaṣṭottaraṃ śatam || 59 ||
anyāṃśca sauramantrānhi japtvā tiṣṭhansamāhitaḥ
kṛtvā pradakṣiṇaṃ sūryaṃ namaskṛtyopaviśya ca || 60 ||
svādhyāyaṃ prāṅmukhaḥ kṛtvā tarpayeddevamānavān
ṛṣīnpitṝnhi svīyāṃśca vidhivannāmagotravit || 61 ||
toyena tilamiśreṇa vidhivatsusamāhitaḥ
śrāddhe havanakāle ca pāṇinaikena nirvapet || 62 ||
tarpaṇe tūbhayaṃ kuryādeṣa eva vidhiḥ sadā
anvārabdhena savyena pāṇinā dakṣiṇena tu || 63 ||
tṛpyatāmiti suvyaktaṃ nāmagotreṇa vāgyataḥ
kāyasthairyastilairmohātkaroti pitṛtarpaṇam || 64 ||
tarpitāstena pitarastvaṅmāṃsarudhirāsthibhiḥ
jale sthitvā sthale dattaṃ sthale sthitvā jale'pitam || 65 ||
nopatiṣṭhati tattoyaṃ yadbhūmyāṃ na pradīyate
pitṝṇāmakṣayaṃ sthānaṃ mahī dattā virañcinā || 66 ||
tasmāttatraiva dātavyaṃ pitṝṇāṃ prītimicchatā
bhūmistena samutpannā bhūmyāṃ caiva tu saṃsthitam || 67 ||
bhūmyāṃ caiva layaṃ yānti bhūmau dadyāttato jalam
āstīrya ca kuśānsāgrānāvāhya svasvamantrataḥ
prācīnāgreṣu vai devānyāmyāgreṣu tathā pitṝn || 68 ||
iti śrībṛhannāradīyapurāṇottarabhāge vasumohinīsaṃvāde puruṣottamamāhātmye ṣaṭpañcāśattamo'dhyāyaḥ || 56 ||

<2.57>

atha saptapañcāśattamodhyāyaḥ
vasuruvāca -
devān ṛṣīnpitṝṃścānyānsantarpyācamya vāgyataḥ
hastamātraṃ catuṣkoṇaṃ caturdvāraṃ suśobhanam || 1 ||
puraṃ vilikhya vidhije tīre tasya mahodadheḥ
madhye tatra likhetpadmamaṣṭapatraṃ sakarṇikam || 2 ||
evaṃ maṇḍalamālikhya pūjayettatra mohini
aṣṭākṣaravidhānena nārāyaṇamajaṃ vibhum || 3 ||
atha te sampravakṣyāmi kāyaśodhanamuttamam
kṣakāraṃ hṛdaye cintyaṃ raktaṃ rephasamanvitam || 4 ||
jvalantaṃ triśikhaṃ caiva dahantaṃ pāpasañcayam
candra maṇḍalamadhyasthamekāraṃ mūrdhni cintayet || 5 ||
śuklavarṇaṃ pravarṣantamamṛtaṃ plāvayanmahīm
evaṃ nirddhūtapāpastu divyadehastato bhavet || 6 ||
aṣṭākṣaraṃ tato mantraṃ nyaseddehātmanorbudhaḥ
vāmapādaṃ samārabhya kramaśaścaiva vinyaset || 7 ||
pañcāṅgaṃ vaiṣṇavaṃ caiva caturvyūhaṃ tathaiva ca
karaśuddhiṃ prakurvīta mūlamantreṇa sādhakaḥ || 8 ||
ekaikaṃ caiva varṇaṃ tu aṅgulīṣu pṛthak pṛthak
oṃkāraṃ pṛthivī śuklaṃ vāmapāde tu vinyaset || 9 ||
nakārastu bhuvaḥ śyāmo dakṣiṇe tu vyavasthitaḥ
mokāraṃ kālamevāhurvāmakaṭyāṃ nidhāpayet || 10 ||
nākāraṃ pūrvabījaṃ tu dakṣiṇasyāṃ vyavasthitam
rākārasteja ityāhurnābhideśe vyavasthitaḥ || 11 ||
vāyavyo'ya yakārastu vāmaskandhe samāśritaḥ
ṇākāraḥ sarvadā jñeyo dakṣiṇāṃse vyavasthitaḥ || 12 ||
yakāro'ya śirasthaśca yatra lokā vyavasthitāḥ
oṃkāraṃ hṛdaye nyasya vikāraṃ vā śirasyatha || 13 ||
ṣṇakāraṃ vai śikhāyāṃ tu vekāraṃ kavace nyaset
nakāraṃ netrayostu syānmakāraṃ cāstramīritam || 14 ||
lalāṭe vāsudevastu śuklavarṇaḥ samāsthitaḥ
raktaḥ saṅkarṣaṇaścaiva mukhe vahnyarkasannibhaḥ || 15 ||
pradyumno hṛdaye pīto'niruddho mehane sthitaḥ
sarvāṅge sarvaśaktiśca caturvyūhārcito hariḥ || 16 ||
mamāgre'vasthito viṣṇuḥ pṛṣṭhataścāpi keśavaḥ
govindo dakṣiṇe pārśve vāme tu madhusūdanaḥ || 17 ||
upariṣṭāttu vaikuṇṭho vārāhaḥ pṛthivītale
avāntaradiśo yāstu tāsu sarvāsu mādhavaḥ || 18 ||
gacchatastiṣṭhato vāpi jāgrataḥ svapato'pi vā
narasiṃhakṛtā guptirvāsudevamayo hyaham || 19 ||
evaṃ viṣṇumayo bhūtvā tataḥ karma samārabhet
yathā dehe tathā deve sarvatattvāni yojayet || 20 ||
phakārāntaṃ samuddiṣṭaṃ sarvavighnaharaṃ śubham
tatrārkacandra vahnīnāṃ maṇḍalāni vicintayet || 21 ||
padmamadhye nyasedviṣṇuṃ bhuvanasyāntarasya tu
tato vicintya thṛdaye praṇavaṃ jyotiruttamam || 22 ||
karṇikāyāṃ samāsīnaṃ jyotīrūpaṃ sanātanam
aṣṭākṣaraṃ tato mantraṃ nyaseccaiva yathākramam || 23 ||
tena vyastasamastena pūjanaṃ paramaṃ smṛtam
dvādaśākṣaramantreṇa yajeddevaṃ sanātanam || 24 ||
tato'vadhārya hṛdaye karṇikāyāṃ bahirnyaset
caturbhujaṃ mahāsatvaṃ sūryakoṭisamaprabham || 25 ||
cintayitvā mahāyogaṃ tataścāvāhayetkramāt
mīnarūpāvahaścaiva narasiṃhaśca vāmanaḥ || 26 ||
āyāntu devā varadā mama nārāyaṇāgrataḥ
sumeruḥ pādapīṭhaṃ te padmakalpitamāsanam || 27 ||
sarvatattvahitārthāya tiṣṭha tvaṃ madhusūdana
pādyaṃ te pādayordeva padmanābha sanātana || 28 ||
viṣṇo kamalapatrākṣa gṛhāṇa madhusūdana
madhuparkaṃ mahādeva brahmādyaiḥ kalpitaṃ mayā || 29 ||
niveditaṃ ca bhaktyārghaṃ gṛhāṇa puruṣottama
mandākinyāstato vāri sarvapāpaharaṃ śivam || 30 ||
gṛhāṇācamanīyaṃ tvaṃ mayā bhaktyā niveditam
tvamāpaḥ pṛthivī caiva jyotistvaṃ vāyureva ca || 31 ||
lokasandhṛtimātreṇa vāriṇā snāpayāmyaham
devatantusamāyukte yajñavarṇasamanvite || 32 ||
svarṇavarṇaprabhe deva vāsasī pratigṛhyatām
śarīraṃ ca na jānāmi ceṣṭāṃ ca tava keśava || 33 ||
mayā niveditaṃ gandhaṃ pratigṛhya vilipyatām
ṛgyajuḥsāmamantreṇa trivṛtaṃ padmayoninā || 34 ||
sāvitrīgranthisaṃyuktamupavītaṃ tavārpyate
divyaratnasamāyuktā vahnibhānusamaprabhāḥ || 35 ||
gātrāṇi śobhayiṣyanti alaṅkārāstu mādhava
sūryācandra masorjyotirvidyudagnyostathaiva ca || 36 ||
tvameva jyotiṣāṃ deva dīpo'ya pratigṛhyatām
vanaspatiraso divyo gandhāḍhyaḥ surabhiśca te || 37 ||
mayā nivedito bhaktyā dhūpo'ya pratigṛhyatām
annaṃ caturvidhaṃ svādu rasaiḥ ṣaḍbhiḥ samanvitam || 38 ||
mayā niveditaṃ bhaktyā naivedyaṃ tava keśava
pūrve dale vāsudevaṃ yāmye saṅkarṣaṇaṃ nyaset || 39 ||
pradyumnaṃ paścime kuryādaniruddhaṃ tathottare
vārāhaṃ ca tathāgneye narasiṃhaṃ ca nairṛte || 40 ||
vāyavyāṃ mādhavaṃ caiva tathaiśāne trivikramam
tathāṣṭākṣaradevasya garuḍaṃ parito nyaset || 41 ||
vāmapārśve tathā cakraṃ śaṅkhaṃ dakṣiṇato nyaset
tathā mahāgadāṃ caiva nyaseddevasya dakṣiṇe || 42 ||
tataḥ śārṅgadhanurvidvānnyaseddevasya vāmataḥ
dakṣiṇe ceṣudhī divye khaḍgaṃ vāme ca vinyaset || 43 ||
śriyaṃ dakṣiṇataḥ sthāpya puṣṭimuttarato nyaset
vanamālāṃ ca puratastataḥ śrīvatsakaustubhau || 44 ||
vinyaseddhṛdayādīni pūrvādiṣu caturṣvapi
tato'stra devadevasya koṇe caiva tu vinyaset || 45 ||
indra magniṃ yamaṃ caiva nirṛtiṃ varuṇaṃ tathā
vāyuṃ dhanadamīśānamanantaṃ brahmaṇā saha || 46 ||
pūjayettānsvakairmantrairadhaścordhvaṃ tathaiva ca
evaṃ sampūjya deveśaṃ maṇḍalasthaṃ janārdanam || 47 ||
labhedabhimatānkāmānnaro nāstyatra saṃśayaḥ
anenaiva vidhānena maṇḍalasthaṃ janārdanam || 48 ||
pūjitaṃ yastu paśyetsa praviśedviṣṇumavyayam
sakṛdapyarcito yena vidhinānena keśavaḥ || 49 ||
janmamṛtyujarāstīrtvā viṣṇoḥ padamavāpnuyāt
yaḥ smaretsatataṃ bhaktyā nārāyaṇamatandri taḥ || 50 ||
anvahaṃ tasya vāsāya śvetadvīpaḥ prakīrtitaḥ
oṃkārādisamāyuktaṃ namaskāraṃ tadīyakam || 51 ||
sanāmasarvatattvānāṃ maṃtra ityabhidhīyate
anenaina vidhānena gandhapuṣpaṃ nivedayet || 52 ||
ekaikasya prakurvīta yathoddiṣṭaṃ krameṇa tu
mudrā stato nibadhnīyādyathoktikramaveditam || 53 ||
japaṃ caiva prakurvīta mūlamantreṇa tattvavit
aṣṭāviṃśatimaṣṭau vā śatamaṣṭottaraṃ tathā || 54 ||
kāmyeṣu ca yathoktaṃ syādyathāśakti samāhitaḥ
padmaṃ śaṅkhaṃ ca śrīvatsaṃ gadāṃ garuḍameva ca || 55 ||
cakraṃ khaḍgaṃ ca śārṅgaṃ ca aṣṭau mudrāḥ prakīrtitāḥ
gaccha gaccha paraṃ sthānaṃ purāṇapuruṣottama || 56 ||
yanna brahmādayo devā vindanti paramaṃ padam
arcanaṃ ye na jānanti harermantrairyathoditam || 57 ||
te tvatra mūlamantreṇa pūjayantyacyutaṃ śubhe || 58 ||
iti śrībṛhannāradīyapurāṇottarabhāge vasumohinīsaṃvāde puruṣottamamāhātmye saptapañcāśattamo'dhyāyaḥ || 57 ||

<2.58>

athāṣṭapañcāśattamo'dhyāyaḥ
vasuruvāca -
evaṃ sampūjya vidhivadbhaktyā taṃ puruṣottamam
praṇamya śirasā paścātsāgaraṃ ca prasādayet || 1 ||
prāṇastvaṃ sarvabhūtātāṃ viśvasminsaritāṃ pate
tīrtharāja namaste'stu trāhi māmacyutapriya || 2 ||
snātvaivaṃ sāgare samyak tasmin kṣetravare śubhe
tīre cābhyarcya vidhivannārāyaṇamanāmayam || 3 ||
rāmaṃ kṛṣṇaṃ subhadrāṃ ca praṇipatya ca sāgaram
śatānāmaśvamedhānāṃ phalaṃ prāpnoti mānavaḥ || 4 ||
sarvapāpavinirmktaḥ sarvaduḥkhavivarjitaḥ
vṛndārakahariḥ śrīmān rūpayauvanagarvitaḥ || 5 ||
vimānenārkavarṇena divyagandharvanādinā
kulaikaviṃśatiṃ dhṛtvā viṣṇulokaṃ ca gacchati || 6 ||
bhuktvā tatra varānbhogānkrīḍitvā ca suraissaha
cyutastasmādihāyāto brāhmaṇo brahmavittamaḥ || 7 ||
yaśasvī matimāñchrīmānsatyavādī jitendri yaḥ
vedaśāstrārthavidvipro bhavetpaścāttu vaiṣṇavaḥ || 8 ||
yogaṃ ca vaiṣṇavaṃ prāpya tato mokṣamavāpnuyāt
grahoparāge saṅkrāntyāmayane viṣuve tathā || 9 ||
yugādiṣu ca manvādau vyatīpāte dinakṣaye
āṣāḍhyāṃ caiva kārtikyāṃ māghyāṃ vānyaśubhe tithau || 10 ||
ye tvatradānaṃ viprebhyaḥ prayacchanti sumedhasaḥ
phalaṃ sahasraguṇitamanyatīrthāllabhanti te || 11 ||
pitṝṇāṃ ye prayacchanti piṇḍaṃ tatra vidhānataḥ
akṣayāṃ pitarasteṣāṃ tṛptiṃ samprāpnuvanti vai || 12 ||
evaṃ snānaphalaṃ samyak sāgarasya mayeritam
dānasya ca phalaṃ devi piṇḍadānasya caiva hi || 13 ||
dharmārthamokṣaphaladamāyuḥkīrtiyaśaskaram
bhuktimuktipradaṃ nṝṇāṃ dhanyaṃ duḥsvapnanāśanam || 14 ||
sarvapāpaharaṃ puṇyaṃ sarvakāmaphalapradam
nāstikāya na vaktavyaṃ śaṭhāya kṛpaṇāya ca || 15 ||
tāvadgarjjaṃti tīrthāni māhātmyaiḥ svaiḥ pṛthak pṛthak
yāvanna tīrtharājasya māhātmyaṃ varṇyate dvijaiḥ || 16 ||
puṣkarādīni tīrthāni prayacchanti svakaṃ phalam
tīrtharājaḥ samudra stu sarvatīrthaphalapradaḥ || 17 ||
bhūtale yāni tīrthāni saritaśca sarāṃsi ca
viśanti sāgare tāni tena vai śreṣṭhatāṃ gataḥ || 18 ||
rājā samastatīrthānāṃ sāgaraḥ saritāṃ patiḥ
tasmātsamastatīrthebhyaḥ śreṣṭho'sau sarvakāmadaḥ || 19 ||
tamo nāśaṃ yathābhyeti bhāskare'bhyudite sati
koṭyo navanavatyastu yatra tīrthāni santi vai || 20 ||
tasmātsnānaṃ ca dānaṃ ca homaṃ japyaṃ surārcanam
yatkiñcitkriyate tatra tadakṣayamitīritam || 21 ||
mohinyuvāca -
sarveṣu tu samudre ṣu kṣāro'ya saritāṃ patiḥ
kathaṃ jāto guro brūhi sarvajño'si yato dvija || 22 ||
vasuruvāca -
śṛṇu vakṣyāmi subhage kṣāratvaṃ cāsya vāridheḥ
yathā prāptaḥ purāsīcca mātrāsya jagatāmapi || 23 ||
purā sṛṣṭikrame jātāḥ samudrāḥ sapta mohini
rādhikāgarbhasambhūtā divyadehāḥ pṛthagvidhāḥ || 24 ||
ekadā rādhikānāthaḥ kāntayā saha saṅgataḥ
āste vṛndāvane sākṣādgopagopīgavāṃpatiḥ || 25 ||
rāsamaṇḍalamadhye tu sudīpte maṇimaṇḍape
susnigdhayā samāyuktaḥ śṛṅgāre kāntayā tayā || 26 ||
te sapta sāgarā bālāḥ stanyapānakṛtakṣaṇāḥ
tataste sarvato dṛṣṭvā mātaraṃ tāṃ jagatprasūm || 27 ||
kṣudhārtāśca rudantastu āsedurmaṇimaṇḍapam
tatra jagmustu te sarve stanyapānakṛtekṣaṇāḥ || 28 ||
sarve nivāritāścāpi dvārasthairvallavīgaṇaiḥ
viviśuśca bhṛśaṃ kruddhā bālakāste stanārthinaḥ || 29 ||
upekṣitā gopikābhirmāturutsaṅgavartinaḥ
tatastu prarudanto vai te gatvā maṇimaṇḍapam || 30 ||
uccaiḥ pracukruśurdevi mātaḥ kvāsītivādinaḥ
yadā nāhvayate mātā bālakāṃstānstanārthinaḥ || 31 ||
tadā kaniṣṭhaḥ sarveṣāṃ viveśāyaṃ ratisthalam
taṃ dṛṣṭvā svasutaṃ rādhā mugdhaṃ śṛṅgārabhaṅgadam || 32 ||
śaśāpa kṣubhitā bhadre bhūrlokaṃ yāta mā ciram
yataḥ śṛṅgārabhaṅgaṃ tu mama kartuṃ samudyatāḥ || 33 ||
tato yūyaṃ bhuvaṃ gatvā sthāsyathaikākinaḥ sutāḥ
tacchrutvā vacanaṃ māturjagaddhātryā virañcije || 34 ||
atyuccai ruruduḥ sarve viyogabhayakātarāḥ
tataḥ prasanno bhagavāñchrīkṛṣṇaḥ praṇatārtihā || 35 ||
mā bhaiṣṭa putrāstiṣṭhāmi samīpe bhavatāmaham
dra varūpā bhavantastu pṛthagrūpacarāḥ sadā || 36 ||
vartadhvaṃ kṣāratāṃ yātu kaniṣṭho'bhyantare sthitaḥ
evamuktvā jagannātho bālakānvisasarja ha || 37 ||
teṣāṃ tu sāntvanārthāya samīpasthaḥ sadābhavat
yaḥ praviṣṭo ratigṛhaṃ sa kṣārodo babhūva ha
anye tu dra varūpā vai kṣīrodādyāḥ pṛthak sthitāḥ || 38 ||
mohinyuvāca -
kā rādhā bhavatā proktā guro lokaprasūḥ satī
tasyāstattvaṃ samākhyāhi śrotuṃ kautūhalaṃ mama || 39 ||
purāṇeṣu rahasyaṃ tu rādhāmādhavavarṇanam
yataḥ sarvaṃ bhavānvetti yāthātathyena suvrata || 40 ||
vasiṣṭha uvāca -
tacchrutvā mohinīvākyaṃ bhūpate sa vasurmahān
atīvabhakto govinde nimagnahṛdayo'bhavat || 41 ||
pulakāṅkitasarvāṅgaḥ prathṛṣṭahṛdayo mudā
uvāca mohamāpanno mohinīṃ dvijasattamaḥ || 42 ||
vasuruvāca -
śṛṇu devi pravakṣyāmi rahasyātirahasyakam
sugopyaṃ kṛṣṇacaritaṃ brahmaikatvavidhāyakam || 43 ||
prakṛteḥ puruṣasyāpi niyantāraṃ vidhervidhim
saṃhartāraṃ ca saṃharturbhagavantaṃ nato'smyaham || 44 ||
devi sarve'vatārāstu brahmaṇaḥ kṛṣṇarūpiṇaḥ
avatārī svayaṃ kṛṣṇaḥ saguṇo nirguṇaḥ svayam || 45 ||
sa eva rāmaḥ kṛṣṇaśca vastuto guṇataḥ pṛthak
sarve prākṛtikā lokā goloko nirguṇaḥ svayam || 46 ||
gāvastejaoṃ'śavo bhadre vedavidbhirnirūpitāḥ
brahmaviṣṇuśivādyāstu prākṛtā guṇanirmitāḥ || 47 ||
tattejaḥ sarvadā devi nirguṇaṃ guṇakṛnmatam
guṇāstadaṃśavo bhadre sarve vyākṛtarūpiṇaḥ || 48 ||
vyākṛtotpādakā jñeyā rajaḥsattvatamobhidhāḥ
avyākṛtasya puṃso hi guṇā vijñāpakāḥ śubhe || 49 ||
dehabhūtāḥ smṛtāstasya tacchaktiḥ prakṛtirmatā
pradhānaprakṛtiṃ prāhuḥ kāryakāraṇarūpiṇīm || 50 ||
sākṣiṇaṃ puruṣaṃ prāhurnirguṇaṃ tu sanātanam
puruṣo vīryamādhatta prakṛtyāṃ ca tato guṇāḥ || 51 ||
sattvādyā hyabhavaṃstebhyo mahattattvaṃ samudgatam
puruṣasyecchayā tattu vyākṛtaṃ samabhūdaham || 52 ||
tattridhā samabhūdbhadre dra vyajñānakriyātmakam
vaikārikastaijasaśca tāmasaścetyahaṃ tridhā || 53 ||
vaikārikānmano jajñe devā vaikārikā daśa || 54 ||
digvātārkapracetośvibrahmendro pendra mitrakāḥ
taijasānīndri yāṇyāhurjñānakarmamayāni ca || 55 ||
śrotratvagghrāṇadṛgjihvāvijñānendri yarūpakāḥ
karmendri yāṇi subhage vāgdormeḍhrāṅighrapāyavaḥ || 56 ||
śabdastu tāmasājjajñe tasmādākāśa eva ca
ākāśādabhavatsparśastasmādvāyurabhūtsati || 57 ||
vāyorabhūttato rūpaṃ tasmāttejo vyajāyata
tejasastu rasastasmādāpaḥ samabhavansati || 58 ||
adbhyo gandhaḥ samutpanno gandhātkṣitirajāyata
carācarasya niṣṭhā tu bhūmāveva pradṛśyate || 59 ||
ākāśādiṣu tattveṣu ekadvitricaturguṇāḥ
bhūmau pañca guṇāḥ proktā viśeṣastu tataḥ kṣiteḥ || 60 ||
kālamāyāṃśaliṅgebhya etebhyo'ṇḍamacetanam
samabhūccetanaṃ jātaṃ dareṇa viśatā sati || 61 ||
tasmādaṇḍādvirāḍ jajñe so'śayiṣṭa jalāntare
mukhādīnyasya jātāni virājo'vayavā api || 62 ||
vacanādeśca sirddhythaṃ salilasthasya bhāmini
tasya nābhyāmabhūtpadmaṃ sahasrārkorudīdhitiḥ || 63 ||
tasminsvayambhūḥ samabhūllokānāṃ prapitāmahaḥ
tena taptvā tapastīvraṃ puṃso'nujñāmavāpya ca || 64 ||
lokāśca lokapālāśca kalpitā brahmaṇā sati
kaṭyādibhiradhaḥ sapta saptorddhvaṃ jaghanādibhiḥ || 65 ||
caturdaśabhirebhistu lokairbrahmāṇḍamīritam
tasmin sasarja bhūtāni sthāvarāṇi carāṇi ca || 66 ||
brahmaṇo manaso jātāśvatvāraḥ sanakādayaḥ
dehādbhāvādayo devi yairidaṃ varddhitaṃ jagat || 67 ||
iti śrībṛhannāradīyapurāṇottarabhāge vasumohinīsaṃvāde puruṣottamamāhātmye brahmāṇḍotpattivarṇanaṃ nāmāṣṭapañcāśattamo'dhyāyaḥ || 58 ||

<2.59>

athaikonaṣaṣṭitamo'dhyāyaḥ
vasuruvāca -
yo'sau nirañjano devaścitsvarūpī janārdanaḥ
jyotīrūpo mahābhāge kṛṣṇastallakṣaṇaṃ śṛṇu || 1 ||
goloke sa vibhurnityaṃ jyotirabhyantare sthitaḥ
eka eva paraṃ brahma dṛśyādṛśyasvarūpadhṛk || 2 ||
tasmim̐ lloke tu gāvo hi gopā gopyaśca mohini
vṛndāvanaṃ pūrvataśca śataśṛṅgastathā sarit || 3 ||
virajā nāma vṛkṣāśca pakṣiṇaśca pṛthagvidhāḥ
yāvatkālaṃ tu prakṛtirjāgarti vidhinandini || 4 ||
tāvatkālaṃ tu goloke dṛśya eva vibhuḥ sthitaḥ
laye suptā gavādyāstu na jānanti vibhuṃ param || 5 ||
jyotiḥsamūhāntarataḥ kamanīyavapurddharaḥ
kiśoro jaladaśyāmaḥ pītakauśāmbarāvṛtaḥ || 6 ||
dvibhujo muralīhastaḥ kirīṭādivibhūṣitaḥ
āste kaivalyanāthastu rādhāvakṣasthalojjvalaḥ || 7 ||
prāṇādhikapriyatamā sā rādhārādhito yayā
suvarṇavarṇā devī sā cidrū pā prakṛteḥ parā || 8 ||
tayordehasthayornāsti bhedo nityasvarūpayoḥ
dhāvalyadugdhayoryadvatpṛthivīgandhayoryathā || 9 ||
tatkāraṇaṃ kāraṇānāṃ nirddeṣṭuṃ naiva śakyate
vedānirvacanīyaṃ yattadvaktuṃ naiva śakyate || 10 ||
jyotirantarataḥ proktaṃ yadrū paṃ śyāmasundaram
śivena dṛṣṭaṃ tadrū paṃ kadāciddhynāgocaram || 11 ||
tataḥ prabhṛti jānanti golokākhyānamīpsitam
nāradādyā vidhisute sanakādyāśca yoginaḥ || 12 ||
śrutaṃ dhyāyanti taṃ sarve na tairdṛṣṭaṃ kadācana
sākṣāddra ṣṭuṃ tu tapate śivo'dyāpi sanātanaḥ || 13 ||
naiva paśyati tadrū paṃ dhyāyati dhyānagocaram
kadācitkrīḍatordevi rādhāmādhavayorvapuḥ || 14 ||
dvidhābhūtamabhūttatra vāmāṅgaṃ tu caturbhujam
samānarūpāvayavaṃ samānāmbarabhūṣaṇam || 15 ||
tadvadrā dhāsvarūpaṃ ca dvidhārūpamabhūtsati
tābhyāṃ dṛṣṭaṃ tatsvarūpaṃ sākṣāttāvapi tatsamau || 16 ||
caturbhujaṃ tu yadrū paṃ lakṣmīkāntaṃ manoharam
taddṛṣṭaṃ tu śivādyaiśca bhaktavṛndairanekaśaḥ || 17 ||
sakṛttu brahmaṇā dṛṣṭaṃ devi rūpaṃ caturbhujam
sṛṣṭikāryapramugdhena darśitaṃ kṛpayā svayam || 18 ||
lakṣmyā sanatkumārāya varṇitaṃ vidhinandini
viṣvaksenāya tūddiṣṭaṃ svarūpaṃ tattvamūrtaye || 19 ||
nārāyaṇena vidhije tato dhyāyanti sarvaśaḥ
dharmaputreṇa deveśi nāradāya samīritam || 20 ||
golokavarṇanaṃ sarvaṃ rādhākṛṣṇamayaṃ tathā
yā tu rādhā vidhisute devī devavarārcitā || 21 ||
sā svayaṃ śivarūpābhūtkautukena varānane
taddṛṣṭvā sahasāścaryaṃ kṛṣṇo yogeśvareśvaraḥ || 22 ||
mūlaprakṛtirūpaṃ tu dadhre tatsamayocitam
viparītaṃ vapurdhṛtvā vāmadevo mudānvitaḥ || 23 ||
dhyāyedaharniśaṃ devaṃ durgārūpadharaṃ harim
yā rādhā saiva lakṣmīstu sāvitrī ca sarasvatī || 24 ||
gaṅgā ca brahmatanaye naiva bhedo'sti vastutaḥ
pañcadhā sā sthitā vidyā kāmadhenusvarūpiṇī || 25 ||
yaḥ kṛṣṇo rādhikānāthaḥ sa lakṣmīśaḥ prakīrtitaḥ
sa eva brahmarūpaśca dharmo nārāyaṇastathā || 26 ||
evaṃ tu pañcadhā rūpamāsthito bhagavānajaḥ
kāryakāraṇarūpo'sau dhyāyanti jagatītale || 27 ||
tena vai premasambaddho viṣayī yaḥ śivaḥ sa tu
rādheśaṃ rādhikārūpaṃ svayaṃ saccitsukhātmakam || 28 ||
devatejaḥsamudbhūtā mūlaprakṛtirīśvarī
kṛṣṇarūpā mahābhāge daityasaṃhārakāṇī || 29 ||
satī dakṣasutā bhūtvā viṣayeśaṃ śivaṃ śritā
bharturvinindanaṃ śrutvā satī tyaktvā kalevaram || 30 ||
jajñe himavataḥ kṣetre menāyāṃ punareva ca
tatastaptvā tapo bhadre śivaṃ prāpa śivapradā || 31 ||
vastutaḥ kṛṣṇarādhāsau śivamohanatatparā
jagadambāsvarūpā ca yato māyā svayaṃ vibhuḥ || 32 ||
ata eva brahmasute skando gaṇapatistathā
svayaṃ kṛṣṇo gaṇapatiḥ svayaṃ skandaḥ śivo'bhavat || 33 ||
śivamevaṃ vadantyeke rādhārūpaṃ samāśritam
kṛṣṇavakṣaḥsthalasthānaṃ tayorbhedo na lakṣyate || 34 ||
kṛṣṇo vā mūlaprakṛtiḥ śivo vā rādhikā svayam
ekaṃ vā mithunaṃ vāpi na kenāpīti niścitam || 35 ||
anirdeśyaṃ tu yadvastu tannirdeṣṭuṃ na ca kṣamam
upalakṣaṇametaddhi yannideśanamaiśvaram || 36 ||
śāstraṃ vedāśca subhage varṇayaṃnti yadīśvaram
tatsarvaṃ prākṛtaṃ viddhi nirdeṣṭuṃ śakyameva ca || 37 ||
anirdeśyaṃ tu yaddevi tannetīti niṣidhyate
niṣedhaśeṣaḥ sa vibhuḥ kīrtitaḥ śaraṇāgataiḥ || 38 ||
śāstraṃ niyāmakaṃ bhadre sarveṣāṃ karmaṇāṃ bhavet
karmī tu jīvaḥ kathita īśvarāṃśo vibhuḥ svayam || 39 ||
prakṛtestu paro nityo māyayā mohitaḥ śubhe
yastu sākṣī svayaṃ pūrṇaḥ sahānuśayitā sthitaḥ || 40 ||
na vetti taṃ cānuśayī vedānuśayinaṃ sa tu
śaṅkhacakragadāpadmairalaṅkṛtabhujadvayāḥ || 41 ||
prapannāste tu vijñeyāḥ dvividhā vidhinandani
ārtadṛptavibhedana tatrārtā asahā matāḥ || 42 ||
dṛptā janmāntarasahā nirbhayāḥ sadasajjanāḥ
ye prapannā mahālakṣmyāṃ sakhibhāvaṃ samāśritāḥ || 43 ||
teṣāṃ mantraṃ pravakṣyāmi prayānti vidhibodhitam
gopījanapadasyānte vallabheti samuccaret || 44 ||
caraṇañccharaṇaṃ paścātprapadye padamīrayet
ṣoḍaśārṇo mantrarājaḥ sākṣāllakṣmyā prakāśitaḥ || 45 ||
pūrvaṃ sanatkumārāya śambhave tadanantaram
sakhibhāvaṃ samāśritya gopikāvṛndamadhyagam || 46 ||
ātmānaṃ cintayedbhadre rādhāmādhavasaṃjñakam
guruṣvīśvarabhāvena vartteta praṇataḥ sadā || 47 ||
vaiṣṇaveṣu ca satkṛtya tathā samatayānyataḥ
divāniśaṃ cintanaṃ ca svāminoḥ premabandhanāt
kuryātparvasvapi sadā yātrāparvamahotsavān || 48 ||
iti śrībṛhannāradīyapurāṇottarabhāge vasumohinīsaṃvāde puruṣottamamāhātmyaṃ nāma ekonaṣaṣṭitamo'dhyāyaḥ || 59 ||

<2.60>

ṣaṣṭitamo'dhyāyaḥ
vasuruvāca -
tato gacchedvidhisute tīrthaṃ yajñāṅgasambhavam
indra dyumnasaro nāma yatrāste pāvanaṃ śubham || 1 ||
tatra gatvā śucirvidvānācamya manasā harim
dhyātvopasthāya ca vibhuṃ mantramenamudīrayet || 2 ||
aśvamedhāṅgasambhūta tīrtha sarvāghanāśana
snānaṃ tvayi karomyadya pāpaṃ hara namo'stu te || 3 ||
evamuccārya vidhivatsnātvā devān ṛṣīnpitṝn
tilodakena vānyāṃśca santarpyācamya vāgyataḥ || 4 ||
datvā pitṝṇāṃ piṇḍāṃśca sampūjya puruṣottamam
daśāśvamedhikaṃ samyakphalaṃ prāpnoti mānavaḥ || 5 ||
saptāvarānsapta parānvaṃśānuddhṛtya devavat
kāmagena vimānena viṣṇulokaṃ sa gacchati || 6 ||
bhuktvā tatra varānbhogānyāvaccandrā rkatārakam
cyutastasmādihāyāto mokṣaṃ ca labhate dhruvam || 7 ||
evaṃ kṛtvā pañcatīrthīmekādaśyāmupoṣitaḥ
jyeṣṭhaśukle pañcadaśyāṃ yaḥ paśyetpuruṣottamam || 8 ||
sa pūrvoktaphalaṃ prāpya krīḍitvā cācyutālaye
prayāti paramaṃ sthānaṃ yasmānnāvartate punaḥ || 9 ||
pṛthivyāṃ yāni tīrthāni saritaśca sarāṃsi ca
puṣkariṇyastaḍāgāni vāpyaḥ kūpāstathā hradāḥ || 10 ||
nānā nadyaḥ samudrā śca saptāhaṃ puruṣottame
jyeṣṭhaśukladaśamyādi pratyakṣaṃ yānti sarvadā || 11 ||
snānadānādikaṃ yasmāddevatāprekṣaṇe sati
nṛbhiryatkriyate tatra tatsarvaṃ cākṣayaṃ bhavet || 12 ||
jyeṣṭhamāse tu daśamī śuklapakṣasya mohini
harate daśa pāpāni tasmāddaśaharā smṛtā || 13 ||
yastasyāṃ halinaṃ kṛṣṇaṃ paśyedbhadrāṃ ca suvrataḥ
sarvapāpavinirmukto viṣṇulokaṃ vrajennaraḥ || 14 ||
naro dolāgataṃ dṛṣṭvā govindaṃ puruṣottamam
phālgunyāṃ saṃyato bhūtvā govindasya puraṃ vrajet || 15 ||
viṣuve divase prāpte pañcatīrthavidhānataḥ
dṛṣṭvā saṅkarṣaṇaṃ kṛṣṇāṃ subhadrāṃ ca sulocane || 16 ||
naraḥ samastayajñānāṃ phalaṃ prāpnoti durlabham
vimuktaḥ sarvapāpebhyo viṣṇulokaṃ ca gacchati || 17 ||
yaḥ paśyanti tṛtīyāyāṃ kṛṣṇaṃ candanarūṣitam
vaiśākhasya site pakṣe sa yātyacyutamandiram || 18 ||
yadā bhavenmahājyaiṣṭhī rāśinakṣatrayogataḥ
prayatnena tadā martyairgantavyaṃ puruṣottamam || 19 ||
kṛṣṇaṃ dṛṣṭvā mahājyeṣṭhyāṃ rāmaṃ bhadrāṃ ca mohini
naro dvādaśayātrāṇāṃ phalaṃ prāpnoti cādhikam || 20 ||
prayāge ca kurukṣetraṃ naimiṣe puṣkare gaye
gaṅgādvāre ca kubjāmragaṅgāsāgarasaṅgame || 21 ||
kokāmukhe śūkare ca mathurāyāṃ marusthale
śālagrāme vāyutīrthe mandare sindhusāgare || 22 ||
piṇḍārake citrakūṭe prabhāse kanakhale tathā
śaṅkhoddhāre dvārakāyāṃ tathā badarikāśrame || 23 ||
lohakūṭe cāśvatīrthe sarvapāpapramocane
karddamāle koṭitīrthe tathā cāmarakaṇṭake || 24 ||
lolārke jambumārge ca somatīrthe pṛthūdake
utpalāvartake caiva pṛthutuṅge sakubjake || 25 ||
ekāmrake ca kedāre kāśyāṃ vā viraje sati
kālañjare ca gokarṇe śrīśaile gandhamādane || 26 ||
mahendre malaye vindhye pāriyātre himāhvaye
sahye ca śuktimati ca gomante cārbude tathā || 27 ||
gaṅgāyāṃ ca mahābhāge yatpuṇyaṃ yāmuneṣu ca
sārasvateṣu gomatyāṃ brahmaputreṣu saptasu || 28 ||
godāvarī bhīmarathī tuṅgabhadrā ca narmadā
tāpī payoṣṇī kāverī śiprā carmaṇvatī tathā || 29 ||
vitastā candra bhāgā ca śatadrurbāhudā tathā
ṛṣikulyā marudvṛdhā vipāśā ca dṛṣadvatī || 30 ||
sarayūrnākagaṅgā ca gaṇḍakī ca mahānadī
kauśikī karatoyā ca trisrotā madhuvāhinī || 31 ||
mahānadī vaitaraṇī yāścānyā nānukīrtitāḥ
tāssarvā na samāḥ proktāḥ kṛṣṇasandarśanasya ca || 32 ||
yatphalaṃ snānadānena rāhugraste divākare
tatphalaṃ kṛṣṇamālokya mahājyeṣṭhyāṃ labhennaraḥ || 33 ||
tasmātsarvaprayatnena gantavyaṃ puruṣottamam
mahājyaiṣṭhyāṃ vidhisute sarvakāmaphalepsubhiḥ || 34 ||
dṛṣṭvā rāmaṃ mahājyaiṣṭhyāṃ kṛṣṇaṃ cāpi subhadra yā
viṣṇulokaṃ naro yāti samuddhṛtya kulaṃ śatam || 35 ||
bhuktvā tatra varānbhogānyāvadābhūtasamplavam
puṇyakṣayādihāgatya caturvedī dvijo bhavet || 36 ||
svadharmanirataḥ śāntaḥ kṛṣṇabhakto jitendri yaḥ
vaiṣṇavaṃ yogamāsthāya tato mokṣamavāpnuyāt || 37 ||
māsi jyeṣṭhe ca samprāpte nakṣatre cendra daivate
paurṇamāsyāṃ tadā snānaṃ praśastaṃ sāgarāmbhasi || 38 ||
sarvatīrthamayaḥ kūpastadāste sajalaḥ śuciḥ
tathā bhogavatī tatra pratyakṣā bhavati dhruvam || 39 ||
tasmājjyaiṣṭhyāṃ samuddhṛtya haimādikalaśairjalam
kṛṣṇarāmābhiṣekārthaṃ subhadrā yāśca mohini || 40 ||
kṛtvā suśobhanaṃ mañcaṃ patākābhiralaṅkṛtam
sudṛḍhaṃ sukhasañcāraṃ vastraiḥ puṣpairalaṅkṛtam || 41 ||
vistīrṇaṃ dhūpitaṃ dhūpaiḥ snānārthaṃ rāmakṛṣṇayoḥ
pītavastraparicchannaṃ muktāhārāvalambitam || 42 ||
tatra nānāvidhairvādyaiḥ kṛṣṇaṃ nīlāmbaraṃ sati
mañce saṃsthāpya bhadrāṃ ca jayamaṅgalaniḥsvanaiḥ || 43 ||
brāhmaṇaiḥ kṣatriyairvaiśyaiḥ śūdrai ścānyaiśca mohini
anekaśatasāhasrairvṛtaṃ strīpuruṣaistathā || 44 ||
gṛhasthāḥ snātakāścaiva yatayo brahmacāriṇaḥ
snāpayanti tadā kṛṣṇaṃ mañcasthaṃ sahalāyudham || 45 ||
tathā samastatīrthāni pūrvoktāni ca sundari
modakaiḥ puṣpamiśraiśca snāpayanti pṛthak pṛthak || 46 ||
paścātpaṭahaṃsaghoṣairbherīmurajaniḥsvanaiḥ
kāhalaistālaśabdaiśca mṛdaṅgairjharjharaistathā || 47 ||
anyaiśca vividhairvādyairghaṇṭāsvanavimiśritaiḥ
strīṇāṃ maṅgalaśabdaiśca stutiśabdairmanoramaiḥ || 48 ||
jayaśabdaistathā stotrairvīṇāveṇunināditaiḥ
śrūyate sumahāñchabdaḥ sāgarasyeva garjataḥ || 49 ||
munīnāṃ vedaghoṣaiśca mantraghoṣaistathāparaiḥ
nānāstotraravaiḥ puṇyaiḥ sāmagānopabṛṃhitaiḥ || 50 ||
śyāmāvadātaveśyābhiḥ pītaraktāṃśukaistathā
cāmarai ratnadaṇḍaiśca vījyete rāmakeśavau || 51 ||
yakṣavidyādharaiḥ siddhairdevagandharvacāraṇaiḥ
ādityā vasavo rudrāḥ sādhyā viśve marudgaṇāḥ || 52 ||
lokapālāstathānye ca stuvanti puruṣottamam
namaste devadeveśa purāṇapuruṣottama || 53 ||
sargasthityantakṛddeva lokanātha jagatpate
trailokyaśaraṇaṃ devaṃ brahmaṇyaṃ mokṣakāraṇam || 54 ||
taṃ namasyāmahe bhaktyā sarvakāmaphalapradam
stutvaivaṃ vibudhāḥ kṛṣṇaṃ rāmaṃ caiva mahābalam || 55 ||
subhadrāṃ cāpi vidhije tadākāśe vyavasthitāḥ
gāyanti devagandharvā nṛtyantyapsarasastathā || 56 ||
devatūryāṇi vādyante vātā vānti suśītalāḥ
puṣpamiśraṃ tadā meghā varṣantyākāśagocarāḥ || 57 ||
jayaśabdaṃ ca kurvaṃti munayaḥ siddhacāraṇāḥ
śakrādyā vibudhāḥ sarve ṛṣayaḥ pitarastathā || 58 ||
prajānāṃ patayo nāgā ye cānye svargavāsinaḥ
tato maṅgalasambhārairvidhimantrapuraskṛtam || 59 ||
ābhiṣecanikaṃ dra vyaṃ gṛhītvā devatāgaṇāḥ
indra viṣṇū mahāvīryau sūryācandra masau tathā || 60 ||
dhātā caiva vidhātā ca tathā caivānilānalau
pūṣā bhago'yamā tvaṣṭā vivasvānaṃśumāṃstathā || 61 ||
rūdrā śvisahito dhīmānmitreṇa varuṇena ca
rudrai rvasubhirādityairvālakhilyairmarīcijaiḥ || 62 ||
bhṛgubhiścāṅgirobhiśca sarvavidyāsu niṣṭhitaiḥ
pitāmahaḥ pulastyaśca pulahaśca mahātapāḥ || 63 ||
aṅgirāḥ kaśyapo'triśca marīrcibhṛgureva ca
kraturharaḥ pracetāśca manurdakṣastathaiva ca || 64 ||
ṛtavaśca grahāścaiva jyotīṃṣi ca dvijottamāḥ
mūrtimatyaśca sarito devāścaiva sanātanāḥ || 65 ||
subhadrā śca hradāścaiva tīrthāni vividhāni ca
pṛthivī dyaurdiśaścaiva pādapāśca dvijottamāḥ || 66 ||
aditirdevamātā ca hrīḥ śrīḥ svāhā sarasvatī
umā śacī sinīvālī tathā cānumatiḥ kuhūḥ || 67 ||
rākā ca dhiṣaṇā caiva patnyaścānyā divaukasām
śreṣṭho girīṇāṃ himavānnāgarājaśca vāmanaḥ
pāriyātraśca vindhyaśca meruścānekaśṛṅgavān || 68 ||
erāvataḥ sānucaraḥ kalāḥ kāṣṭhāstathaiva ca
māsārddhamāsā ṛtavastathā rātryahanī samāḥ || 69 ||
uccaiḥśravā hayaśreṣṭho nāgarājaśca vāmanaḥ
aruṇo garuḍaścaiva latāścauṣadhibhiḥ saha || 70 ||
dharmaśca bhagavāndevaḥ samāgacchanti sarvataḥ
kālo yamaśca mṛtyuśca yamasyānucarāśca ye || 71 ||
bahulatvācca noktā ye vividhā devatāgaṇāḥ
te devasyābhiṣekārthaṃ samāyānti tatastataḥ || 72 ||
divyasambhārasaṃyuktaiḥ kalaśaiḥ kāñcanaistathā
sārasvatībhiḥ puṇyābhirdivyatoyābhireva ca || 73 ||
toyenākāśagaṅgāyāḥ kṛṣṇaṃ rāmeṇa saṅgatam
supuṣpaiḥ kumbhasalilaiḥ snāpayantyambare sthitāḥ || 74 ||
sañcaranti vimānāni devānāmambare tathā
uccāvacāni divyāni kāmagāni sthirāṇi ca || 75 ||
divyaratnavicitrāṇi sevitānyapsarogaṇaiḥ
gītairvādyaiḥ patākābhiḥ śobhitāni samantataḥ || 76 ||
iti śrībṛhannāradīyapurāṇottarabhāge vasumohinīsaṃvāde puruṣottamamāhātmye'bhiṣeko nāma ṣaṣṭitamo'dhyāyaḥ || 60 ||

<2.61>

athaikaṣaṣṭitamo'dhyāyaḥ
vasuruvāca -
evaṃ tadā vidhisute kṛṣṇaṃ rāmeṇa saṅgatam
subhadrāṃ ca mahābhāgāḥ saṃstuvati mudānvitāḥ || 1 ||
devā ūcuḥ
jaya jaya lokapāla jaya padmanābha bhūdharaṇa
jaya jaya cādideva bahukāraṇa || 2 ||
jaya jaya vāsudeva jaya saccaraṇa satkaraṇa
jaya jaya divyamīna jaya tridaśavara || 3 ||
jaya jaya jaladhiśayana jaya jaya yogeśa jaya vedadhara
jaya jaya viśvamūrte jaya cakradhara || 4 ||
jaya jaya bhūtanātha jaya śrīnivāsa jaya jaya yogivara
jaya jaya sūryanetra jaya deva varāha || 5 ||
jaya jaya kaiṭabhāre jaya jaya vedavara
jaya jaya kūrmarūpa jaya yajñavara || 6 ||
jaya jaya kamalanābha jaya śailadhara
jaya jaya yogeśa jaya vegadhara || 7 ||
jaya jaya viśvamūrte jaya cakradhara
jaya jaya bhūtanātha jaya dharaṇīdhara || 8 ||
jaya jaya śeṣaśāyiñjaya pītavāsa
jaya somakānta jaya yogavāsa jaya jaya || 9 ||
dahanacakra jaya dharmavāsa jaya jaya
guṇanidhāna jaya śrīnivāsa jaya jaya || 10 ||
garuḍāsana jaya sukhanivāsa jaya jaya
jaya jaya dharmakānta jaya jaya matinivāsa || 11 ||
jaya jaya gahanagehanivāsa
jaya jaya yogigamya jaya makhanivāsa || 12 ||
jaya jaya vedavedya jaya śāntikara
jaya jaya yogicintya jaya puṣṭikara || 13 ||
jaya jaya jñānamūrte jaya kamalākara
jaya jaya bhāvavedya jaya muktikara || 14 ||
jaya vimaladeha jaya jaya sattvanilaya
jaya jaya guṇasamūha jaya jaya yajñakara || 15 ||
jaya jaya guṇavihīna jaya jaya mokṣakara
jaya jaya bhūhiraṇya jaya jaya kāntiyuta || 16 ||
jaya lokaśaraṇa jaya jaya lakṣmīpate
jaya paṅkajākṣa jaya jaya sṛṣṭikara || 17 ||
jaya yogayuta jayātasīkusumaśyāmadeha
jaya jaya subhadrā viṣṭadeha jaya lakṣmīpaṅkajabhogadeha || 18 ||
jaya jaya bhaktibhāvana lokageya
jaya lokakānta jaya paramaśānta || 19 ||
jaya jaya paramasāra jayacakradhara
jaya bhogiyuta jaya nīlāmbara || 20 ||
jaya sāṅkhyanuta jaya kaluṣahara
jaya kṛṣṇa jagannātha jaya saṅkarṣaṇānuja || 21 ||
jaya jaya padmapalāśākṣa jaya vāñchitaphalaprada
jaya mālāvṛtoraska jaya cakragadādhara || 22 ||
jaya padmālayākānta jaya viṣṇo namo'stu te
evaṃ stutvā tadā devāḥ śakrādyā thṛṣṭamānasāḥ || 23 ||
siddhacāraṇagandharvā ye cānye svargavāsinaḥ
dṛṣṭvā stutvā namaskṛtya tadgatenāntarātmanā || 24 ||
kṛṣṇaṃ rāmaṃ subhadrāṃ ca yānti svaṃ svaṃ niveśanam
kapilāśatadānena yatphalaṃ puṣkare smṛtam || 25 ||
tatphalaṃ kṛṣṇamālokya mañcasthaṃ sahalāyudham
kanyāśatapradānena yatphalaṃ samudāhṛtam || 26 ||
tatphalaṃ kṛṣṇamālokya mañcasthaṃ labhate naraḥ
śataniṣkasuvarṇena bhūmidānena yatphalam || 27 ||
tatphalaṃ cānnadānena sarvātithyena yatphalam
vṛṣotsargeṇa vidhivad grīṣme toyapradānataḥ || 28 ||
tiladhenupradānena gajāśvarathadānataḥ
cāndrā yaṇena cīrṇena tathā māsopavāsataḥ || 29 ||
yatphalaṃ sarvatīrtheṣu vratairdānaiśca yatphalam
sasubhadrau rāmakṛṣṇau tallabhedvīkṣya mañcagau || 30 ||
tasmānnaro'thavā nārī paśyettaṃ puruṣottamam
snānaśeṣeṇa kṛṣṇasya toyena yadi mohini || 31 ||
vandhyā mṛtaprajā vāpi durbhagā grahapīḍitāḥ
rākṣasādyairgṛhītā vā tathā rogaiśca saṃhatāḥ || 32 ||
sadyastāḥ śuddhimāyānti vidhinā hyabhiṣecitāḥ
prāpnuvantīpsitānkāmānyānyānvāñchanti suprabhe || 33 ||
puṇyāni yāni toyāni santyanyāni dharātale
tāni snātāvaśeṣasya kalāṃ nārhanti ṣoḍaśīm || 34 ||
tasmātsnānāvaśeṣeṇa jalena jalaśāyinaḥ
abhyukṣetsarvagātrāṇi sarvakāmapradena ca || 35 ||
snātaṃ paśyanti ye kṛṣṇaṃ vrajantaṃ dakṣiṇāmukham
brahmahatyādibhiḥ pāpairmucyante te na saṃśayaḥ || 36 ||
śāstreṣu yatphalaṃ proktaṃ triḥ pradakṣiṇayā bhuvaḥ
dṛṣṭvā naro labhetkṛṣṇaṃ tatphalaṃ dakṣiṇāmukham || 37 ||
tīrthayātrāphalaṃ yattu pṛthivyāṃ samudāhṛtam
dṛṣṭvā yāntaṃ labhetkṛṣṇaṃ tatphalaṃ dakṣiṇāmukham || 38 ||
gaṅgādvāre ca kubjāmre kurukṣetre'kaparvaṇi
puṣkarādiṣu cānyeṣu yatphalaṃ snānataḥ smṛtam || 39 ||
tatphalaṃ dakṣiṇāsyaṃ tu kṛṣṇaṃ yāntaṃ nirīkṣya ca
atha kiṃ bahunoktena yatphalaṃ puṇyakarmaṇaḥ || 40 ||
vedaśāstrapurāṇeṣu bhārate saṃhitādiṣu
tatphalaṃ vīkṣya dakṣāsyau subhadrau balakeśavau || 41 ||
guṇḍicāmaṇḍapaṃ yāntaṃ ye paśyanti rathe sthitam
sabhadraṃ sabalaṃ kṛṣṇaṃ te yānti bhavanaṃ hareḥ || 42 ||
guṇḍicāyānasamaye phālgunyāṃ viṣuve tathā
sakṛdyātrāṃ naraḥ kṛtvā viṣṇulokaṃ pragacchati || 43 ||
yāvatīḥ kurute mattaryo yātrāḥ śrīpuruṣottame
tāvatkalpaṃ viṣṇuloke vasediti viniścayaḥ || 44 ||
yātrā dvādaśa sampūrṇā yadā syurvidhinandini
tadā pratiṣṭhāṃ kurvīta vidhinā pāpanāśini || 45 ||
jyeṣṭhamāsi site pakṣe hyekādaśyāṃ samāhitaḥ
gatvā jalāśayaṃ puṇyamācamya prayataḥ śuciḥ || 46 ||
āvāhya sarvatīrthāni dhyātvā nārāyaṇaṃ tathā
tataḥ snānaṃ prakurvīta vidhibodhitavartmanā || 47 ||
snātvā samyaktato devānṛṣīṃścāpi pitṝnsvakān
santarpayettathānyāṃśca nāmagotroktipūrvakam || 48 ||
uttīrya vāsasī dhaute nirmale paridhāya ca
upaspṛśya vidhānena tathopasthāya bhāskaram || 49 ||
vedamātaramāvartya puṇyāmaṣṭottaraṃ śatam
sauramantrāṃstathā cānyāṃstriḥ parītya namedra vim || 50 ||
vedoktaṃ triṣu varṇeṣu snānaṃ japyamudāthṛtam || 51 ||
strīśūdra yorvarārohe vedoktavidhivarjitam
tato vrajenmandirasthaṃ bhaktyā śrīpuruṣottamam || 52 ||
prakṣālya hastau pādau ca upaspṛśya yathāvidhi
ghṛtena snāpayeddevaṃ kṣīreṇa tadanantaram || 53 ||
madhugandhodakenāpi tīrthacandanavāriṇā
tato vastrayugaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ bhaktyā taṃ paridhāpayet || 54 ||
candanāgurukarpūraiḥ kuṅkumena vilepayet
pūjayetparayā bhaktyā padmaiśca puruṣottamam || 55 ||
sampūjyaivaṃ jagannāthaṃ bhuktimuktipradaṃ harim
dhūpaṃ cāgurusaṃyuktaṃ dehe devasya cāgrataḥ || 56 ||
guggulaṃ ca suniṣpūtaṃ dahedghṛtasamanvitam
dīpaṃ prajvālayedbhaktyā yathāśakti ghṛtena vai || 57 ||
anyām̐ śca dīpakāndadyāddvādaśaiva samāhitaḥ
goghṛtena tu deveśi tilatailena vā punaḥ || 58 ||
naivedyaṃ pāyasāpūpaśaṣkulīveṣṭakāni ca
modakaṃ phāṇikaṃ cānyatphalāni ca nivedayet || 59 ||
evaṃ pañcopacāreṇa sampūjya puruṣottamam
oṃnamaḥ puruṣottamāyeti japedaṣṭottaraṃ śatam || 60 ||
tataḥ prasādayeddevaṃ daṇḍavatpraṇipatya ca
tato'cayedguruṃ bhaktyā puṣpavastrānulepanaiḥ || 61 ||
nānayorantaraṃ yasmādvidyate vidhinandini
devasyopari kurvīta maṇḍapaṃ susamāhitaḥ || 62 ||
nānāpuṣpaiḥ suviśadaṃ vicitraṃ maṇḍalaṃ puraḥ
kṛtvāvadhāraṇaṃ paścājjāgaraṃ kārayenniśi || 63 ||
kathāṃ ca vāsudevasya gītikāṃ cāpi kārayet
dhyāyanpaṭhanstuvandevaṃ praṇayedra janīṃ budhaḥ || 64 ||
tataḥ prabhāte vimale dvādaśyāṃ dvādaśaiva tu
nimantrayedvratasnātānbrāhmaṇānvedapāragān || 65 ||
itihāsapurāṇajñāñchrotriyānsaṃyatendri yān
snātvā samyagvidhānena dhautavāsā jitendri yaḥ || 66 ||
snāpayetpūrvavadbhaktyā tatrasthaṃ puruṣottamam
gandhaiḥ puṣpaistūpahārairnaivedyairdīpakaistathā || 67 ||
upacārairbahuvidhaiḥ praṇipātaiḥ pradakṣiṇaiḥ
jāpyastutinamaskārairgītavādyairmanoharaiḥ || 68 ||
sampūjyaivaṃ jagannāthaṃ brāhmaṇānpūjayettataḥ
dvādaśaiva tu gāstebhyo dattvā kanakameva ca || 69 ||
chatropānadyugaṃ cārpya kāṃsyapātraṃ ca bhaktitaḥ
tatastānbhojayedviprānbhojyaṃ pāyasapūrvakam || 70 ||
pakvānnaṃ bhakṣyabhojyaṃ ca saguḍaṃ śarkarānvitam
tato bhuktvā susantṛptānbrāhmaṇānsusthamānasān || 71 ||
dvādaśaivodakumbhāṃśca grāhayettānsamodakān
dakṣiṇāṃ ca yathāśakti viṣṇutulyaṃ viriṃcije || 72 ||
suvarṇavastragodhānyairdra vyaiścānyairvarairbudhaḥ
sampūjya tānnamaskṛtya imaṃ mantramudīrayet || 73 ||
sarvavyāpī jagannāthaḥ śaṅkhacakragadādharaḥ
anādinidhano devaḥ prīyatāṃ puruṣottamaḥ || 74 ||
ityuccārya tato viprāṃstriḥ parikramya sādaram
praṇamya śirasā bhaktyā sācāryāṃstānvisarjayet || 75 ||
tatastānbrāhmaṇānbhaktyā cāsīmāntamanuvrajet
anuvrajya tu tānviprānnamaskṛtya nivartya ca || 76 ||
bāndhavaiḥ svajanairyuktastato bhuñjīta vāgyataḥ
evaṃ kṛtvā naraḥ samyaṅ nārī vā labhate phalam || 77 ||
aśvamedhasahasrasya rājasūyaśatasya ca
atītaṃ śatamuddhṛtya puruṣāṇāṃ narottamaḥ || 78 ||
bhaviṣyacca śataṃ devi divyarūpadharo'vyayaḥ
sarvalakṣaṇasampannaḥ sarvālaṅkārabhūṣitaḥ || 79 ||
sarvakāmasamṛddhātmā guṇarūpavayo'nvitaḥ
stūyamāno'tha gandharvairapsarobhiḥ samantataḥ || 80 ||
vimānenārkavarṇena kāmagena sthireṇa ca
patākādhvajayuktena śatasūryaprabheṇa ca || 81 ||
udyotayandiśaḥ sarvā ākāśe vigataklamaḥ
yuvā mahābalo dhīmānviṣṇulokaṃ sa gacchati || 82 ||
tatra kalpaśataṃ yāvadbhuṅkte bhogānyathepsitān
stuto munivarairdevi tiṣṭhecca vigatajvaraḥ || 83 ||
yathādevo jagannāthaḥ śaṅkhacakragadādharaḥ
tathā'sau mudito devi dhṛtvā rūpaṃ caturbhujam || 84 ||
bhuktvā tatra varānbhogānkrīḍitvā suciraṃ sati
tadante brahmasadanamāyātyakhilakāmadam || 85 ||
siddhavidyādharaiścāpi śobhitaṃ surakinnaraiḥ
kālaṃ navatikalpaṃ tu tatra bhuktvā sukhaṃ naraḥ || 86 ||
rudra lokaṃ samāyāti sukhadaṃ sevitaṃ suraiḥ
siddhavidyādharairyakṣairbhūṣitaṃ daityadānavaiḥ || 87 ||
aśītikalpakālaṃ tu tatra bhuktvā sukhaṃ naraḥ
tadante yāti golokaṃ sarvabhogasamanvitam || 88 ||
surasiddhāpsarobhiśca śobhitaṃ sumanoharam
tatra saptatikalpaṃ tu bhuktvā bhogānabhīpsitān || 89 ||
paścādāyāti vai lokaṃ prājāpatyamanuttamam
ṣaṣṭikalpaṃ sukhaṃ tatra bhuktvā nānāvidhaṃ mudā || 90 ||
tadante śakrabhavanaṃ nānāścaryaṃ samāvrajet
āgatya tatra pañcāśatkalpaṃ bhuktvā sukhaṃ naraḥ || 91 ||
prapadyate'maragṛhānvimānaiḥ samalaṅkṛtān
catvāriṃśatkalpakālaṃ bhogānbhuktvā sudurlabhān || 92 ||
nākṣatraṃ lokamāyāti nānāsaukhyasamanvitam
tatra bhuktvā varānbhogāṃstriṃśatkalpaṃ yathepsitān || 93 ||
tasmādgacchati taṃ lokaṃ śaśāṅkasya viriṃñcije
yatra tiṣṭhati somo'sau sarvadevairalaṅkṛtaḥ || 94 ||
tatra viṃśatikalpaṃ tu bhuktvā bhogaṃ sudurlabham
ādityasya tato lokamāyāti surapūjitam || 95 ||
tatra bhuktvā śubhānbhogandaśa kalpaṃ sunirvṛtaḥ
tasmādāyāti bhavanaṃ gandharvāṇāṃ sudurlabham || 96 ||
tatra bhogānsamastāṃśca kalpamekaṃ yathāsukham
bhuktvā cāyāti medinyāṃ rājā bhavati dhārmikaḥ || 97 ||
cakravartī mahāvīryo guṇaiḥ sarvairalaṅkṛtaḥ
kṛtvā rājyaṃ tu dharmeṇa yajñairiṣṭvā sadakṣiṇaiḥ || 98 ||
tadante yogināṃ lokaṃ gatvā mokṣapradaṃ śivam
tatra bhuktvā varānbhogānyāvadābhūtasamplavam || 99 ||
tasmātpunarihāyāto jāyate yogināṃ kule || 100 ||
pravare vaiṣṇave bhadre durlabhe sādhusammate
caturvedī vipravaro brahmayajñaparaḥ sati || 101 ||
vaiṣṇavaṃ yogamāsādya kaivalyaṃ mokṣamāpnuyāt
evaṃ yātrāphalaṃ tubhyaṃ proktaṃ śrīpuruṣottame || 102 ||
bhuktimuktipradaṃ nṝṇāṃ kimanyacchrotumicchasi || 103 ||
iti śrībṛhannāradīyapurāṇottarabhāge vasumohinīsaṃvāde puruṣottamakṣetramāhātmye puruṣottamakṣetrayātrāphalavarṇanaṃ nāmaikaṣaṣṭitamo'dhyāyaḥ || 61 ||

<2.62>

atha dviṣaṣṭitamo'dhyāyaḥ
vasiṣṭha uvāca -
etacchrutvā tu bhūpāla mohinī vidhinandinī
puruṣottamamāhātmyaṃ bhuktimuktipradāyakam || 1 ||
punaḥ papraccha taṃ vipraṃ vasuṃ svasya purohitam
mohinyuvāca -
śrutamatyadbhutaṃ vipra puruṣottamasambhavam || 2 ||
māhātmyaṃ cādhunā brūhi prayāgasyāpi suvrata
tīrtharājaḥ prayāgākhyaḥ śrutaḥ pūrvaṃ mayā guro || 3 ||
tanmāhātmyaṃ mamākhyāhi tīrthayātrāvidhānayuk
sa mānyānāṃ viśeṣāṇāṃ tīrthānāṃ gamane dvija || 4 ||
yatkarttavyaṃ ca vidhinā nṛbhirddharmaparāyaṇaiḥ
tacchrutvā sa dvijo rājanmohinyā bhāṣitaṃ vacaḥ || 5 ||
sāmānyavidhipūrvaṃ tatprayāgākhyānamabravīt
vasuruvāca -
śṛṇu bhadre pravakṣyāmi tīrthābhigamane vidhim || 6 ||
yaṃ samāśritya manujo yathoktaṃ phalamāpnuyāt
tīrthābhigamanaṃ puṇyaṃ yajñairapi viśiṣyate || 7 ||
anupoṣya trirātrāṇi tīrthānyapyabhigamya ca
adattvā kāñcanaṃ gāśca daridro jāyate naraḥ || 8 ||
agniṣṭomādibhiryajñairiṣṭvā vipuladakṣiṇaiḥ
na tatphalamavāpnoti tīrthābhigamanena yat || 9 ||
ajñānenāpi yasyeha tīrthābhigamanaṃ bhavet
sarvakāmasamṛddhaḥ sa svargaloke mahīyate || 10 ||
sthānaṃ ca labhate nityaṃ dhanadhānyasamākulam
eśvaryajñānasampūrṇaḥ sadā bhavati bhogavān || 11 ||
tāritāḥ pitarastena narakātprapitāmahāḥ
yasya hastau ca pādau ca manaścaiva susaṃyatam || 12 ||
vidyā tapaśca kīrtiśca sa tīrthaphalamaśnute
pratigrahādapāvṛttaḥ santuṣṭo yena kenacit || 13 ||
ahaṅkāravimuktaśca sa tīrthaphalamāpnuyāt
akalpako nirārambho laghvāhāro jitendri yaḥ || 14 ||
vimuktaḥ sarvasaṅgaistu sa tīrthaphalabhāgbhavet
tīrthānyanusarandhīraḥ śraddadhānaḥ samāhitaḥ || 15 ||
kṛtapāpo viśudhyettu kiṃ punaḥ śuddhakarmakṛt
aśraddadhānaḥ pāpārto nāstiko'cchinnasaṃśayaḥ || 16 ||
hetuniṣṭhaśca pañcaite na tīrthaphalabhāginaḥ
nṛṇāṃ pāpakṛtāṃ tīrthe pāpasya śamanaṃ bhavet || 17 ||
yathoktaphaladaṃ tīrthaṃ bhavecchuddhātmanāṃ nṛṇām
kāmaṃ krodhaṃ ca lobhaṃ ca yo jitvā tīrthamāviśet
na yena kiñcidaprāptaṃ tīrthābhigamanādbhavet
tīrthāni ca yathoktena vidhinā sañcaranti ye
sarvadvandvasahā dhīrāste narāḥ svargagāminaḥ || 18 ||
gaṅgāditīrtheṣu vasanti matsyā devālaye pakṣigaṇāśca santi
bhāvojjhitāste na phalaṃ labhante tīrthācca devāyatanācca mukhyāt || 19 ||
bhāvaṃ tato hṛtkamale nidhāya tīrthāni seveta samāhitātmā
yā tīrthayātrā kathitā munīndraiḥ kṛtā prayuktā hyanumoditā ca || 20 ||
tāṃ brahmacārī vidhivatkaroti susaṃyato guruṇā saṃniyuktaḥ
sarvasvanāśe'pyatha vālpapakṣe sa brāhmaṇānagrata eva kṛtvā || 21 ||
yajñādhikāre'pyathavā nivṛtte viprastu tīrthāni paribhramecca
tīrtheṣvalaṃ yajñaphalaṃ hi yasmātproktaṃ munīndrai ramalasvabhāvaiḥ || 22 ||
yasyeṣṭiyajñeṣvadhikāritāsti varaṃ gṛhaṃ gṛhadharmāśca sarve
evaṃ gṛhasthāśramasaṃsthitasya tīrthe gatiḥ pūrvatarairniṣiddhā
savāṇi tīrthānyapi cāgnihotratulyāni naiveti vadanti kecit || 23 ||
yo yaḥ kaścittīrthayātrāṃ tu gacchetsusaṃyataḥ sa ca pūrvaṃ gṛheṣu
kṛtāvāsaḥ śucirapramattaḥ sampūjayedbhaktinamro gaṇeśam || 24 ||
devānpitṝnbrāhmaṇāṃścaiva sādhūndhīmānvipro vittaśaktyā prayatnāt
pratyāgataścāpi punastathaiva devānpitṝnbrāhmaṇānpūjayecca || 25 ||
evaṃ kurvatastasya tīrthādyaduktaṃ phalaṃ tatsyānnātra sandehaleśaḥ || 26 ||
gacchandeśāntaraṃ yastu śrāddhaṃ kuryātsasarṣiṣā
yātrārthamiti tatproktaṃ praveśe ca na saṃśayaḥ || 27 ||
prayāge tīrthayātrāyāṃ pitṛmātṛviyogataḥ
kacānāṃ vapanaṃ kuryād vṛthā na vikaco bhavet || 28 ||
udyataścedgayāṃ gantuṃ śrāddhaṃ kṛtvā vidhānataḥ
vidhāya kārpaṭīveṣaṃ kṛtvā grāmapradakṣiṇām || 29 ||
tato grāmāntaraṃ gatvā śrāddhaśeṣasya bhojanam
tataḥ pratidinaṃ gacchetpratigrahavivarjitaḥ || 30 ||
padepade'śvamedhasya syātphalaṃ gacchato gayām
balīvardasamārūḍhastīrthaṃ yo yāti suvrate || 31 ||
narake vasate ghore gavāṃ krodho hi dāruṇaḥ
salilaṃ ca na gṛhṇanti pitarastasya dehinaḥ || 32 ||
eśvaryāllobhamohādvā gacchedyānena yo naraḥ
niṣphalaṃ tasya tattīrthaṃ tasmādyānaṃ vivarjayet || 33 ||
goyāne govadhaḥ prokto hayayāne tu niṣphalam
narayāne tadarddhaṃ syātpadbhyāṃ tacca caturguṇam || 34 ||
varṣātapādike chatrī daṇḍī śarkarakaṇṭake
śarīratrāṇakāmo'sau sopānatkaḥ sadā vrajet || 35 ||
tīrthaṃ prāpyānuṣaṅgeṇa snānaṃ tīrthe samācaran
snānajaṃ phalamāpnoti tīrthayātrāphalaṃ na tu || 36 ||
ṣoḍaśāṃśaṃ sa labhate yaḥ parārthena gacchati
arddhaṃ tīrthaphalaṃ tasya yaḥ prasaṅgena gacchati || 37 ||
tīrtheṣu brāhmaṇaṃ naiva parīkṣeta kadācana
atrārthinamanuprāptaṃ bhojyaṃ taṃ manurabravīt || 38 ||
saktubhiḥ piṇḍadānaṃ ca saṃyāvaiḥ pāyasena vā
badarāmalakairvāpi piṇyākairvā sulocane || 39 ||
śrāddhaṃ tu tatra kartavyamarccāvāhanavarjitam
śvadhvāṃkṣagṛdhrapāpānāṃ naiva dṛṣṭihataṃ ca yat || 40 ||
śrāddhaṃ tu tairthikaṃ proktaṃ pitṝṇāṃ tṛptikārakam
akāle'pyathavā kāle tīrthaśrāddhaṃ tathā naraiḥ || 41 ||
prāptaireva sadā tatra kartavyaṃ pitṛtarpaṇam
vilambo naiva kartavyo naiva vighnaṃ samācaret || 42 ||
pratikṛtiṃ kuśamayīṃ tīrthavāriṇi majjayet
yamuddiśya viśālākṣi so'ṣṭamāṃśaṃ phalaṃ labhet || 43 ||
kuśo'si kuśaputro'si brahmaṇā nirmitaḥ purā
tvayi snāte tu sa snāto yasyedaṃ granthibandhanam || 44 ||
muṇḍanaṃ copavāsaśca sarvatīrtheṣvayaṃ vidhiḥ
varjayitvā kurukṣetraṃ viśālāṃ virajāṃ gayām || 45 ||
bhaumānāmatha tīrthānāṃ puṇyatve kāraṇaṃ śṛṇu
yathā śarīrasyoddeśāḥ kecinmukhyatamāḥ smṛtāḥ || 46 ||
prabhāvādadbhutādbhūmeḥ salilasya ca tejasaḥ
parigrahānmunīnāṃ ca tīrthānāṃ puṇyatā smṛtā || 47 ||
gaṅgāṃ samprāpya yo devi muṇḍanaṃ naiva kārayet
kriyā tasyākriyā sarvā tīrthadro hī bhavettathā || 48 ||
gaṅgāyāṃ bhāskarakṣetre muṇḍanaṃ yo na kārayet
sa koṭikulasaṃyukta ākalpaṃ rauravaṃ vrajet || 49 ||
gaṅgāṃ prāpya saricchreṣṭhāṃ kalpāntapāpasañcayāḥ
keśānāśritya tiṣṭhanti tasmāttānparivarjayet || 50 ||
yāvanti nakhalomāni gaṅgātoye patanti vai
tāvadvarṣasahasrāṇi svargaloke mahīyate || 51 ||
prayāgavyatireke tu gaṅgāyāṃ muṇḍanaṃ na hi
yo'nyathā kurute mohātsa mahārauravaṃ viśet || 52 ||
sa jīvatpitṛko yastu tīrthaṃ prāpya vidhānavit
kṣauraṃ samācarennaiva śmaśrūṇāṃ vapanaṃ sati || 53 ||
gayādāvapi deveśi śmaśrūṇāṃ vapanaṃ vinā
na kṣauraṃ munibhiḥ sarvairniṣiddhaṃ ceti kīrtitam || 54 ||
saśmaśrukeśavapanaṃ muḍanaṃ tadvidurbudhāḥ
na kṣauraṃ muṇḍanaṃ subhru kīrtitaṃ vedavedibhiḥ || 55 ||
iti śrībṛhannāradīyapurāṇe bṛhadupākhyāne uttarabhāge vasumohinīsaṃvāde prayāgarājamāhātmye tīrthavidhirnāma dviṣaṣṭitamo'dhyāyaḥ || 62 ||

<2.63>

atha triṣaṣṭitamo'dhyāyaḥ
vasuruvāca -
śṛṇu mohini vakṣyāmi māhātmyaṃ vedasammatam
prayāgasya vidhānena snātvā yatra viśudhyati || 1 ||
kurukṣetrasamā gaṅgā yatra tatrāvagāhitā
tasmāddaśaguṇā proktā yatra vindhyena saṅgatā || 2 ||
tasmācchataguṇā proktā kāśyāmuttaravāhinī
kāśyāḥ śataguṇā proktā gaṅgā yatrārkajānvitā || 3 ||
sahasraguṇitā sāpi bhavetpaścimavāhinī
sā devi darśanādeva brahmahatyādihāriṇī || 4 ||
paścimābhimukhī gaṅgā kālindyā saha saṅgatā
hanti kalpaśataṃ pāpaṃ sā māghe devi durlabhā || 5 ||
amṛtaṃ kathyate bhadre sā veṇī bhuvi saṅgatā
yasyāṃ māghe muhūrtaṃ tu devānāmapi durlabham || 6 ||
pṛthivyāṃ yāni tīrthāni puryaḥ puṇyāstathā sati
snātumāyānti tā veṇyāṃ māghe makarabhāskare || 7 ||
brahmaviṣṇumahādevā rudrā dityamarudgaṇāḥ
gandharvā lokapālāśca yakṣakinnaraguhyakāḥ || 8 ||
aṇimādiguṇopetā ye cānye tattvadarśinaḥ
brahmāṇī pārvatī lakṣmīḥ śacī medhā'ditī ratiḥ || 9 ||
sarvāstā devapatnyaśca tathā nāgāṅganāḥ śubhe
ghṛtācī menakā rambhāpyurvaśī ca tilottamā || 10 ||
gaṇāścāpsarasāṃ sarve pitṝṇāṃ ca gaṇāstathā
snātumāyānti te sarve māghe veṇyāṃ virañcije || 11 ||
kṛte yuge svarūpeṇa kalau pracchannarūpiṇaḥ
sarvatīrthāni kṛṣṇāni pāpināṃ saṅgadoṣataḥ || 12 ||
bhavanti śuklavarṇāni prayāge māghamajjanāt
makarasthe ravau māghe govindācyuta mādhava || 13 ||
snānenānena me deva yathoktaphalado bhava
imaṃ mantraṃ samuccārya snāyānmaunaṃ samāśritaḥ || 14 ||
vāsudevaṃ hariṃ kṛṣṇaṃ mādhavaṃ ca smaretpunaḥ
taptena vāriṇā snānaṃ yadgṛhe kriyate naraiḥ || 15 ||
ṣaṣṭyabdena phalaṃ taddhi makarasthe divākare
bahiḥ snānaṃ tu vāpyādau dvādaśābdaphalaṃ smṛtam || 16 ||
taḍāge dviguṇaṃ taddhi nadyādau taccaturguṇam
daśadhā devakhāte ca mahānadyāṃ ca tacchatam || 17 ||
caturyugaśataṃ tacca mahānadyostu saṅgame
sahasraguṇitaṃ sarvaṃ tatphalaṃ makare ravau || 18 ||
gaṅgāyāṃ snānamātreṇa prayāge tatprakīrtitam
