gaṅgāṃ ye cāvagāhanti māghe māsi sulocane || 19 ||
caturyugasahasraṃ te na patanti surālayāt
śatena guṇitaṃ māghe sahasraṃ vidhinandini || 20 ||
nirdiṣṭamṛṣibhiḥ snānaṃ gaṅgāyamunasaṅgame
pāpaughairbhuvi bhārasya dāhāyemaṃ prajāpatiḥ || 21 ||
prayāgaṃ vidadhe devi prajānāṃ hitakāmyayā
snānasthānamidaṃ samyak sitāsitajalaṃ kila || 22 ||
pāparūpapaśūnāṃ hi brahmaṇā nirmitaṃ purā
sitāsitā tu yā dhārā sarasvatyā vidarbhitā || 23 ||
taṃ mārgaṃ brahmalokasya sṛṣṭikarttā sasarja vai
jñānado mānase māgho na tu mokṣaphalapradaḥ || 24 ||
himavatpṛṣṭhatīrtheṣu sarvapāpapraṇāśanaḥ
vedavidbhirvinirddiṣṭa indra lokaprado hi saḥ
sarvamāsottamo māgho mokṣado badarīvane || 25 ||
pāpahā duḥkhahārī ca sarvakāmaphalapradaḥ
rudra lokaprado māgho nārmade parikīrtitaḥ || 26 ||
sārasvataughavidhvaṃsī sarvalokasukhapradaḥ
viśālaphalado māgho viśālāyāṃ prakīrtitaḥ || 27 ||
pāpendhanadavāgniśca garbhavāsavināśanaḥ
viṣṇulokāya mokṣāya jāhnavaḥ parikīrtitaḥ || 28 ||
sarayūrgaṇḍakī sindhuścadra bhāgā ca kauśikī
tāpī godāvarī bhīmā payoṣṇī kṛṣṇaveṇikā || 29 ||
kāverī tuṅgabhadrā ca yāstathānyāḥ samudra gāḥ
tāsu snāyī naro yāti svargalokaṃ vikalmaṣaḥ || 30 ||
naimiṣe viṣṇusārūpyaṃ puṣkare brahmaṇo'ntikam
ākhaṇḍalasya loko hi kurukṣetre ca māghataḥ || 31 ||
māgho devahrade devi yogasiddhiphalapradaḥ
prabhāse makarāditye snātvā rudra gaṇo bhavet || 32 ||
devikāyāṃ devadeho naro bhavati māghataḥ
māghasnānena vidhije gomatyāṃ na punarbhavaḥ || 33 ||
hemakūṭe mahākāle oṃkāre hyapare tathā
nīlakaṇṭhārbude māgho rudra lokaprado mataḥ || 34 ||
sarvāsāṃ saritāṃ devi sampūro mākare ravau
snānena sarvakāmānāṃ prāptyai jñeyo vicakṣaṇaiḥ || 35 ||
māghastu prāpyate dhanyaiḥ prayāge vidhinandini
apunarbhavadaṃ tatra sitāsitajalaṃ yataḥ || 36 ||
gāyanti devāḥ satataṃ diviṣṭhā māghaḥ prayāge kila no bhaviṣyati
snātā narā yatra na garbhavedānāṃ paśyanti tiṣṭhanti ca viṣṇusannidhau || 37 ||
tīrthairvratairdānatapobhiradhvaraiḥ sārddhaṃ vidhātrā tulayā dhṛtaṃ purā
māghaḥ prayāgaśca tayordvayorabhūnmāgho garīyāṃścaturānanātmaje || 38 ||
vātāmbuparṇāśanadehaśoṣaṇaistapobhirugraiścirakālasañcitaiḥ
yogaiśca saṃyānti narāstu yāṃ gatiṃ snānātprayāgasya hi yānti tāṃ gatim || 39 ||
snātā hi ye mākarabhāskarodaye tīrthe prayāge surasindhusaṅgame
teṣāṃ gṛhadvāramalaṅkaroti bhṛṅgāvalī kuñjarakarṇatāḍitā || 40 ||
yo rājasūyākhyasamādhvarasya snānātphalaṃ saṃpradadāti cākhilam
pāpāni sarvāṇi nihatya līlayā nūnaṃ prayāgaḥ sa kathaṃ na varṇyate || 41 ||
caturvediṣu yatpuṇyaṃ satyavādiṣu caiva hi
snāta eva tadāpnoti gaṅgākālindisaṅgame || 42 ||
tatrābhiṣekaṃ kurvīta saṅgame śaṃsitavrataḥ
tulyaṃ phalamavāpnoti rājasūyāśvamedhayoḥ || 43 ||
pañcayojanavistīrṇaṃ prayāgasya tu maṇḍalam
praveśādasya bhūmau tu aśvamedhaḥ pade pade || 44 ||
trīṇi kuṇḍāni subhage teṣāṃ madhye tu jāhnavī
prayāgasya praveśena pāpaṃ naśyati tatkṣaṇāt || 45 ||
māsamekaṃ naraḥ snātvā prayāge niyatendri yaḥ
mucyate sarvapāpebhyo yathā dṛṣṭaṃ svayambhuvā || 46 ||
śucistu prayato bhūtvā'hisakaḥ śraddhayānvitaḥ
snātvā mucyeta pāpebhyo gacchecca paramaṃ padam || 47 ||
naimiṣaṃ puṣkaraṃ caiva gotīrthaṃ sindhusāgaram
gayā ca dhenukaṃ caiva gaṅgāsāgarasaṅgamaḥ || 48 ||
ete cānye ca bahavo ye ca puṇyāḥ śiloccayāḥ
daśa tīrthasahasrāṇi triṃśatkoṭyastathā parāḥ || 49 ||
prayāge saṃsthitā nityamedhamānā manīṣiṇaḥ
trīṇi yānyagnikuṇḍāni teṣāṃ madhye tu jāhnavī || 50 ||
prayāgāddhi viniṣkrāntā sarvatīrthapuraskṛtā
tapanasya sutā devī triṣu lokeṣu viśrutā || 51 ||
yamunā gaṅgayā sārddhaṃ saṅgatā lokapāvanī
gaṅgāyamunayormadhye pṛthivyāṃ yatparaṃ smṛtam || 52 ||
prayāgasya tu tīrthasya kalāṃ nārhaṃti ṣoḍaśīm
tisraḥ koṭyo'ddhakoṭī ca tīrthānāṃ vāyurabravīt || 53 ||
divi bhuvyantarikṣe ca jāhnavyāṃ tāni santi ca
prayāgaṃ samadhidhaṣṭhāya kambalāśvatarāvubhau || 54 ||
bhogavatyathavā caiṣā vedī vedyā prajāpateḥ
tatra vedāśca yajñāśca mūrtimantaḥ samāsthitāḥ || 55 ||
prajāpatimupāsante ṛṣayaśca tapodhanāḥ
yajanti kratubhirdevāstathā cakradharāḥ sati || 56 ||
tataḥ puṇyatamo nāsti triṣu lokeṣu sundari
prabhāvātsarvatīrthebhyaḥ prabhavatyadhikastathā || 57 ||
tatra dṛṣṭvā tu tattīrthaṃ prayāgaṃ paramaṃ padam
mucyante sarvapāpebhyaḥ śaśāṅka iva rāhuṇā || 58 ||
tato gatvā prayāgaṃ tu sarvadevābhirikṣitam
brahmacārī vasanmāsaṃ pitṝndevāṃśca tarpayan || 59 ||
īpsitām̐ llabhate kāmānyatra tatrābhisaṅgataḥ
sitāsite tu yo majjedapi pāpaśatāvṛtaḥ || 60 ||
makarasthe ravau māghe na sa bhūyastu garbhagaḥ
durjayā vaiṣṇavī māyā devairapi sudustyajā || 61 ||
prayāge dahyate sā tu māghe māsi virañcije
teṣu teṣu ca lokeṣu bhuktvā bhogānanekaśaḥ || 62 ||
paścāccakriṇi līyante prayāge māghamajjinaḥ
upaspṛśati yo māghe makarārke sitāsite || 63 ||
tasya puṇyasya saṅkhyāṃ no citragupto'pi vettyalam
rājasūyasahasrasya vājapeyaśatasya ca
phalaṃ sitāsite māghe snātānāṃ bhavati dhruvam || 64 ||
ākalpajanmabhiḥ pāpaṃ sañcitaṃ manujaistu yat
tadbhavedbhasmasānmāghe snātānāṃ tu sitāsite || 65 ||
gaṅgāyamunayoścaiva saṅgamo lokaviśrutaḥ
sa eva kāmikaṃ tīrthaṃ tatra snānena bhaktitaḥ || 66 ||
yasya yasya ca yaḥ kāmastasya tasya bhaveddhi saḥ
bhogakāmasya bhogāḥ syuḥ syādrā jyaṃ rājyakāminaḥ || 67 ||
svargaḥ syātsvargakāmasya mokṣaḥ syānmokṣakāminaḥ
kāmapradāni tīrthāni trailokye yāni kāni ca || 68 ||
tāni sarvāṇi sevante prayāgaṃ makare ravau
haridvāre prayāge ca gaṅgāsāgarasaṅgame || 69 ||
snātvaiva brahmaṇo viṣṇoḥ śivasya ca puraṃ vrajet
sitāsite tu yatsnānaṃ māghamāse sulocane || 70 ||
na datte punarāvṛttiṃ kalpakoṭiśatairapi
satyavādī jitakrodho hyahiṃsāṃ paramāṃ śritaḥ || 71 ||
dharmānusārī tattvajño gobrāhmaṇahite rataḥ
gaṅgāyamunayormadhye snāto mucyeta kilbiṣāt || 72 ||
manasā cintitānkāmāṃstatra prāpnoti puṣkalān
svarṇabhārasahasreṇa kurukṣetre ravigrahe || 73 ||
yatphalaṃ labhate māghe veṇyāṃ tattu dine dine
gavāṃ śatasahasrasya samyagdattasya yatphalam || 74 ||
prayāge māghamāse tu tryahaṃ snātasya tatphalam
yogābhyāsena yatpuṇyaṃ saṃvatsaraśatatraye || 75 ||
prayāge māghamāse tu tryahaṃ snānena yatphalam
nāśvamedhasahasreṇa tatphalaṃ labhate sati || 76 ||
tryahasnānaphalaṃ māghe purā kāñcanamālinī
rākṣasāya dadau prītyā tena muktaḥ sa pāpakṛt || 77 ||
tryahātpāpakṣayo jātaḥ saptaviṃśatibhirdinaiḥ
snānena yadabhūtpuṇyaṃ tena devatvamāgatā || 78 ||
ramamāṇā tu kailāse girijāyāḥ priyā sakhī
jātismarā tathā jātā prayāgasya prasādataḥ || 79 ||
avantīviṣaye rājā vāsarājo'bhavatpurā
narmadātīrthamāsādya rājasūyaṃ cakāra saḥ || 80 ||
aśvaiḥ ṣoḍaśabhistatra svarṇayūpavirājitaiḥ
svarṇabhūṣaṇabhūṣāḍhyai reje so'pi yathāvidhi || 81 ||
pradadau dhānyarāśiṃ ca dvijebhyaḥ parvatopamam
śraddhāvāndevatābhakto gopradaśca suvarṇadaḥ || 82 ||
brāhmaṇo bhadra ko nāma mūrkho hīnakulastathā
kṛṣīvalo'dhamācāraḥ sarvadharmabahiṣkṛtaḥ || 83 ||
sīrakarmasamudvigno bandhubhiśca sa vañcitaḥ
itastataḥ parikramya nirgato'dṛṣṭapīḍitaḥ || 84 ||
daivato jñānamāśritya prayāgaṃ samupāgataḥ
mahāmāghīṃ puraskṛtya sasnau tatra dinatrayam || 85 ||
anaghaḥ snānamātreṇa samabhūtsa dvijottamaḥ
prayāgāccalitastasmādyayau yasmātsamāgataḥ || 86 ||
sa rājā so'pi vai vipro vipannāvekadā tadā
tayorgatiḥ samā dṛṣṭā devarājasya sannidhau || 87 ||
tejo rūpaṃ balaṃ straiṇaṃ devayānaṃ vibhūṣaṇam
mālā ca pārijātasya nṛtyaṃ gītaṃ samaṃ tayoḥ || 88 ||
iti dṛṣṭvā hi māhātmyaṃ kṣetrasya kathamucyate
māghaḥ sitāsite bhadre rājasūyasamo na ca || 89 ||
dhanurviṃśativistīrṇe sitanīlāmbusaṅgame
māghādapunarāvṛttī rājasūyātpunarbhavet || 90 ||
kambalāśvatarau nāgau vipule yamunātaṭe
tatra snātvā ca pītvā ca sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate || 91 ||
tatra gatvā ca saṃsthāne mahādevasya dhīmataḥ
narastārayate puṃso daśa pūrvāndaśāvarān || 92 ||
kūpaṃ caiva tu tatrāsti pratiṣṭhāne'tiviśrutam
tatra snātvā pitṝndevānsantarpya yatamānasaḥ || 93 ||
brahmacārī jitakodhastrirātraṃ yo'tra tiṣṭhati
sarvapāpaviśuddhātmā so'śvamedhaphalaṃ labhet || 94 ||
uttareṇa pratiṣṭhānādbhāgīrathyāśca pūrvataḥ
haṃsapratapanaṃ nāma tīrthaṃ lokeṣu viśrutam || 95 ||
aśvamedhaphalaṃ tatra snānamātreṇa labhyate
yāvaccandra śca sūryaśca tāvatsvarge mahīyate || 96 ||
tato bhogavatīṃ gatvā vāsukeruttareṇa ca
daśāśvamedhikaṃ nāma tattīrthaṃ paramaṃ smṛtam || 97 ||
tatra kṛtvābhiṣekaṃ tu vājimedhaphalaṃ labhet
dhanāḍhyo rūpavāndakṣo dātā bhavati dhārmikaḥ || 98 ||
caturvediṣu yatpuṇyaṃ satyavādiṣu yatphalam
ahiṃsāyāṃ tu yo dharmo gamanāttasya tatphalam || 99 ||
pāyateścottare kūle prayāgasya tu dakṣiṇe
ṛṇamocanakaṃ nāma tīrthaṃ tu paramaṃ smṛtam || 100 ||
ekarātroṣitaḥ snātvā ṛṇaiḥ sarvaiḥ pramucyate
svargalokamavāpnoti hyamaraśca tathā bhavet || 101 ||
trikālamekasnāyī cāhāramuktiṃ ya ācaret
viśvāsaghātapāpāttu tribhirmāsaiḥ sa śuddhyti || 102 ||
kīrtanāllabhate puṇyaṃ dṛṣṭvā bhadrā ṇi paśyati
avagāhya ca pītvā ca punātyāsaptamaṃ kulam || 103 ||
makarasthe ravau māghe na snātyanudite ravau
kathaṃ pāpaiḥ pramucyeta kathaṃ vā tridivaṃ vrajet || 104 ||
prayāge vapanaṃ kuryādgaṅgāyāṃ piṇḍapātanam
dānaṃ dadyātkurukṣetre vārāṇasyāṃ tanuṃ tyajet || 105 ||
kiṃ gayāpiṇḍadānena kāśyāṃ vā maraṇena kim
kiṃ kurukṣetradānena prayāge muṇḍanaṃ yadi || 106 ||
saṃvatsaraṃ dvimāsonaṃ punastīrthaṃ vrajedyadi
muṇḍanaṃ copavāsaṃ ca tato yatnena kārayet || 107 ||
prayāgaprāptanārīṇāṃ muṇḍanaṃ tvevamīrayet
sarvānkeśānsamuddhṛtya chedayedaṅguladvayam || 108 ||
keśamūlānyupāśritya sarvapāpāni dehinām
tiṣṭhanti tīrthasnānena tasmāttānyatra vāpayet || 109 ||
amārkapātaśravaṇairyuktā cetpauṣamāghayoḥ
arddhodayaḥ sa vijñeyaḥ sūryaparvaśatādhikaḥ || 110 ||
kiñcinnyūne tu vidhije mahodaya iti smṛtaḥ
aruṇodayavelāyāṃ śuklā māghasya saptamī || 111 ||
prayāge yadi labhyeta sahasrārkagrahaiḥ samā
ayane koṭipuṇyaṃ syāllakṣaṃ tu viṣuve phalam || 112 ||
ṣaḍaśītyāṃ sahasraṃ tu tathā viṣṇupadīṣu ca
dānaṃ prayāge kartavyaṃ yathāvibhavavistaram || 113 ||
tena tīrthaphalaṃ caiva vardhate vidhinandini
gaṅgāyamunayormadhye yastu gāṃ vai prayacchati || 114 ||
suvarṇaṃ maṇimuktāṃ vā yadi vānyaṃ pratigraham
pāṭalāṃ kapilāṃ bhadre yastu tatra prayacchati || 115 ||
svarṇaśṛṅgīṃ raupyakhurāṃ cailakaṇṭhīṃ payasvinīm
savatsāṃ śrotriyaṃ sādhuṃ grāhayitvā yathāvidhi || 116 ||
śuklāmbaradharaṃ śāntaṃ dharmajñaṃ vedapāragam
sā ca gaustasya dātavyā gaṅgāyamunasaṅgame || 117 ||
vāsāṃsi ca mahārhāṇi ratnāni vividhāni ca
yāvanto romakūpāḥ syustasyā gorvatsakasya ca || 118 ||
tāvadvarṣasahasrāṇi svargaloke mahīyate
yatrāsau labhate janma sā gaustatrābhijāyate || 119 ||
na ca paśyanti narakaṃ dātārastena karmaṇā
uttarāṃśca kurūnprāpya modante kālamakṣayam || 120 ||
gavāṃ śatasahasrebhyo dadyādekāṃ payasvinīm
putrāndārāṃstathā bhṛtyān gaurekā pratitārayet || 121 ||
tasmātsarveṣu dāneṣu godānaṃ tu viśiṣyate
durgame viṣame ghore mahā pātakasaṅkrame || 122 ||
gaureva rakṣāṃ kurute tasmāddeyā dvijottame
tīrthe na pratigṛhṇīyātpuṇyeṣvāyataneṣu ca || 123 ||
nimitteṣu ca sarveṣu hyapramatto bhaveddvijaḥ
svakārye pitṛkārye vā devatābhyarcane'pi vā || 124 ||
viphalaṃ tasya tattīrthaṃ yāvattaddhanamaśnute
gaṅgāyamunayormadhye yastu kanyāṃ prayacchati || 125 ||
na sa paśyati ghoraṃ tu narakaṃ tena karmaṇā
uttarāṃstu kurūn gatvā modate kālamakṣayam || 126 ||
putrāndārāṃśca labhate dhārmikān rūpasaṃyutān
adhaḥśirāstato dhūmamūrddhvabāhuḥ pibennaraḥ || 127 ||
śataṃ varṣasahasrāṇāṃ svargaloke mahīyate
paribhraṣṭastataḥ svargādagnihotrī bhavennaraḥ || 128 ||
bhuktvā tu vipulānbhogāṃstattīrthaṃ labhate punaḥ
ā prayāgātpratiṣṭhānānmatpuro vāsukerhradāt || 129 ||
kambalāśvatarau nāgau nāgādabahumūlakāt
etatprajāpateḥ kṣetraṃ triṣu lokeṣu viśrutam || 130 ||
tatra snātvā divaṃ yānti ye mṛtāste'punarbhavāḥ
na vedavacanāccaiva na lokavacanādapi || 131 ||
matirutkramaṇīyā hi prayāgamaraṇaṃ prati
daśatīrthasahasrāṇi ṣaṣṭikoṭyastathā parāḥ || 132 ||
tatraiva teṣāṃ sānnidhyaṃ kīrtitaṃ vidhinandini
yā gatiryogayuktasya satpathasthasya dhīmataḥ || 133 ||
sā gatistyajataḥ prāṇān gaṅgāyamunasaṅgame
bādhito yadi vā dīnaḥ kruddho vāpi bhavennaraḥ || 134 ||
gaṅgāyamunamāsādya yastu prāṇānparityajet
dīptakāñcanavarṇābhairvimānaiḥ sūryakāntibhiḥ || 135 ||
gandharvāpsarasāṃ madhye svarge modati mānavaḥ
īpsitām̐ llabhate kāmānvadantīti munīśvarāḥ || 136 ||
gītavāditranirghoṣaiḥ prasuptaḥ pratibudhyate
yāvanna smarate janma tāvatsvarge mahīyate || 137 ||
tataḥ svargātparibhraṣṭaḥ kṣīṇakarmātra cāgataḥ
hiraṇyaratnasampūrṇe samṛddhe jāyate kule || 138 ||
tadeva saṃsmaraṃstatra viṣṇulokaṃ sa gacchati
vaṭamūlaṃ samāsādya yastu prāṇānparityajet || 139 ||
sarvalokānatikramya rudra lokaṃ sa gacchati
tatra te dvādaśādityāstapante rudra māśritāḥ || 140 ||
nirgacchati jagatsarvaṃ vaṭamūle sa dahyate
hariśca bhagavāṃstatra prajāpatipuraskṛtaḥ || 141 ||
āste tatra puṭe devi pādāṅguṣṭhaṃ dhayañchiśuḥ
urvaśīpuline ramye vipule haṃsapāṇḍure || 142 ||
parityajati yaḥ prāṇāñchṛṇu tasyāpi yatphalam
ṣaṣṭivarṣasahasrāṇi ṣaṣṭivarṣaśatāni ca || 143 ||
vasetsa pitṛbhiḥ sārddhaṃ svargaloke viriñcije
urvaśīṃ ca yadā paśyeddevaloke sulocane || 144 ||
pūjyate satataṃ devairṛṣigandharvakinnaraiḥ
tataḥ svargātparibhraṣṭaḥ kṣīṇakarmā tvihāgataḥ || 145 ||
urvaśīsadṛśīnāṃ tu kāntānāṃ labhate śatam
madhye nārīsahasrāṇāṃ bahūnāṃ ca patirbhavet || 146 ||
daśagrāmasahasrāṇāṃ bhoktā śāstā ca mohini
kāñcīnūpuraśabdena supto'sau pratibudhyate || 147 ||
bhuktvā tu vipulānbhogāṃstattīrthaṃ labhate punaḥ
śuklāmbaradharo nityaṃ niyataḥ sa jitendri yaḥ || 148 ||
ekakālaṃ tu bhuñjāno māsaṃ yogapatirbhavet
suvarṇālaṅkṛtānāṃ tu nārīṇāṃ labhate śatam || 149 ||
pṛthivyāmāsamudrā yāṃ mahābhogapatirbhavet
dhanadhānyasamāyukto dātā bhavati nityaśaḥ || 150 ||
sa bhuktvā vipulānbhogāṃstattīrthaṃ smarate punaḥ
koṭitīrthaṃ samāsādya yastu prāṇānparityajet || 151 ||
koṭivarṣasahasrāntaṃ svargaloke mahīyate
tataḥ svārgādihāgatya kṣīṇakarmā narottamaḥ || 152 ||
suvarṇamaṇimuktāgre kule jāyeta rūpavān
akāmo vā sakāmo vā gaṅgāyāṃ yo vipadyate || 153 ||
śakrasya labhate svargaṃ narakaṃ tu na paśyati
haṃsasārasayuktena vimānena sa gacchati || 154 ||
apsarogaṇasaṅkīrṇe supto'sau pratibudhyate
tataḥ svargādihāyātaḥ kṣīṇakarmā virañcije || 155 ||
yogināṃ śrīmatāṃ cāpi svecchayā labhate janim
gaṅgāyamunayormadhye karīṣāgniṃ tu dhārayet || 156 ||
ahīnāṅgo hyarogaśca pañcendri yasamanvitaḥ
yāvanti lobhakūpāni tasya gātre tu dhīmataḥ || 157 ||
tāvadvarṣasahasrāṇi svargaloke mahīyate
tataḥ svargātparibhraṣṭo jambūdvīpapatirbhavet || 158 ||
bhuktvā tu vipulānbhogāṃstattīrthaṃ labhate punaḥ
yastu dehaṃ nikṛtya svaṃ śakunibhyaḥ prayacchati || 159 ||
sa varṣaśatasāhasraṃ somaloke mahīyate
tatastasmādihāgatya rājā bhavati dhārmikaḥ || 160 ||
guṇavān rūpasampanno vidyāvānpriyavākchuciḥ
bhuktvā tu vipulānbhogāṃstattīrthaṃ punarāvrajet || 161 ||
pañcayojanavistīrṇe prayāgasya tu maṇḍale
vipanno yatra kutrāpyanāśakaṃ vratamāsthitaḥ || 162 ||
vyatītānpuruṣānsapta bhāvinastu caturdaśa
narastārayate sarvānātmānaṃ ca samuddharet || 163 ||
agnitīrthamiti khyātaṃ dakṣiṇe yamunātaṭe
paścime dharmarājasya tīrthaṃ tu narakaṃ smṛtam || 164 ||
tatra snātvā divaṃ yānti ye mṛtāste'punarbhavāḥ
yamunottarakāle tu pāpaghnāni bahūnyapi || 165 ||
tīrthāni santi vidhije saṃvitāni munīśvaraiḥ
teṣu snātā divaṃ yānti ye mṛtāste'punarbhavāḥ || 166 ||
gaṅgā ca yamunā caiva ubhe tulyaphale smṛte
kevalaṃ jyeṣṭhabhāvena gaṅgā sarvatra pūjyate || 167 ||
yastu sarvāṇi ratnāni brāhmaṇebhyaḥ prayacchati
tena dattena deveśi yogo labhyeta vā na vā || 168 ||
prayāge tu mṛtasyedaṃ sarvaṃ bhavati nānyathā
deśastho yadi vāraṇye videśe yadi vā gṛhe || 169 ||
prayāgaṃ smaramāṇo'pi yastu prāṇānparityajet
brahmalokamavāpnoti mahī yatra hiraṇmayī || 170 ||
sarvakāmaphalā vṛkṣāstiṣṭhanti ṛṣayo gatāḥ
strīsahasrākule ramye mandākinyāstaṭe śubhe || 171 ||
krīḍyate siddhagandharvaiḥ pūjyate tridaśaistathā
tataḥ punarihāyāto jambūdvīpapatirbhavet || 172 ||
dharmātmā guṇasampannastattīrthaṃ labhate punaḥ
etatte sarvamākhyātaṃ māhātmyaṃ ca prayāgajam || 173 ||
sukhadaṃ mokṣadaṃ sāraṃ kimanyacchrotumicchasi || 174 ||
iti śrībṛhannāradīyapurāṇottarabhāge bṛhadupākhyāne vasumohinīsaṃvāde prayāgamāhātmye triṣaṣṭitamo'dhyāyaḥ || 63 ||

iti prayāgamāhātmyaṃ samāptam

atha kurukṣetramāhātmyaṃ prārabhyate

<2.64>

atha catuṣṣaṣṭitamo'dhyāyaḥ
mohinyuvāca -
vaso kṛpālo dharmajña tvayā bahuvidā mama
tīrtharājasya māhātmyaṃ prayāgasya nirūpitam || 1 ||
yatsarvatīrthamukhyeṣu kurukṣetraṃ śubhāvaham
pāvanaṃ sarvalokānāṃ tanmamācakṣva sāmpratam || 2 ||
vasuruvāca -
śṛṇu mohini vakṣyāmi kurukṣetraṃ supuṇyadam
yatra gatvā naraḥ snātvā sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate || 3 ||
tatra tīrthānyanekāni sevitāni munīśvaraiḥ
tānyahaṃ te'bhidhāsyāmi śṛṇvatāṃ muktidāni ca || 4 ||
brahmajñānaṃ gayāśrāddhaṃ gograhe maraṇaṃ tathā
vāsaḥ puṃsāṃ kurukṣetre maktiruktā caturvidhā || 5 ||
sarasvatīdṛṣadvatyordevanadyoryadantaram
taṃ devasevitaṃ deśaṃ brahmāvartaṃ pracakṣate || 6 ||
dūrastho'pi kurukṣetre gacchāmi ca vasāmyaham
evaṃ yaḥ satataṃ brūyātso'pi pāpaiḥ pramucyate || 7 ||
tatra vai yo vaseddhīraḥ sarasvatyāstaṭe sthitaḥ
tasya jñānaṃ brahmamayaṃ bhaviṣyati na saṃśayaḥ || 8 ||
devatā ṛṣayaḥ siddhāḥ saivante kurujāṅgalam
tasya saṃsevanāddevi brahma cātmani paśyati || 9 ||
mohinyuvāca -
kurukṣetraṃ dvijaśreṣṭha sarvatīrthādhikaṃ katham
tanme vistarato brūhi tvāmahaṃ śaraṇaṃ gatā || 10 ||
vasuruvāca -
śṛṇu bhadre pravakṣyāmi kurukṣetraṃ mahāphalam
yathā jātaṃ nṛṇāṃ pāpadahanaṃ brahmaṇaḥ priyam || 11 ||
ādyaṃ brahmasaraḥ puṇyaṃ tatra sthāne samudgatam
tato rāmahrado jātaḥ kurukṣetraṃ tataḥ param || 12 ||
saraḥ saṃnihitaṃ tacca brahmaṇā nirmitaṃ purā
athaiṣā brahmaṇo vedī diśamantarataḥ sthitā || 13 ||
brahmaṇātra tapastaptaṃ sṛṣṭikāmena mohini
sthitikāmena hariṇā tapastaptaṃ ca cakriṇā || 14 ||
saraḥ praveśātsamprāptaṃ sthāṇutvaṃ śambhunāpi ca
piturvadhācca taptena parśurāmeṇa bhāmini || 15 ||
abrahmaṇyakṣatravadhādye ca raktahradāḥ kṛtāḥ
tadra ktena tu santarpya kṛtavāṃstatra vai tapaḥ || 16 ||
rāmatīrthaṃ tataḥ khyātaṃ sañjātaṃ pāpanāśanam
mārkaṇḍeyena muninā santaptaṃ paramaṃ tapaḥ || 17 ||
yatra tatra samāyātā plakṣajātā sarasvatī
sā sabhājya stutā tena muninā dhārmikeṇa ha || 18 ||
saraḥ sannihitaṃ plāvya paścimāṃ prasthitā diśam
kuruṇā tu tataḥ kṛṣṭaṃ yāvatkṣetraṃ samantataḥ || 19 ||
pañcayojanavistāraṃ dayāsatyakṣamodgamam
syamantapañcakaṃ tāvatkurukṣetramudāhṛtam || 20 ||
atra snātā narā devi labhante puṇyamakṣayam
mṛtā vimānamāruhya brahmalokaṃ vrajanti ca || 21 ||
upavāsaśca dānaṃ ca homo japyaṃ surārcanam
akṣayatvaṃ prayāntyeva nātra kāryā vicāraṇā || 22 ||
brahmavedyāṃ kurukṣetre ye mṛtāste'punarbhavāḥ
grahanakṣatratārāṇāṃ kālena patanādbhayam || 23 ||
kurukṣetre mṛtānāṃ tu na bhūyaḥ patanaṃ bhavet
devarṣisiddhagandharvāstatsaraḥsevanotsukāḥ || 24 ||
yatra nityaṃ sthitā devi rantukaṃ nāmatastataḥ
tasya kṣetrasya rakṣārthaṃ viṣṇunā sthāpitāḥ purā || 25 ||
yakṣaḥ sucandraḥ sūryaśca vāsukiḥ śambukarṇakaḥ
vidyādharaḥ sukeśī ca rākṣasāḥ sthāpitāḥ śubhe || 26 ||
sabhṛtyaiste'ṣṭasāhasrairddhanurbāṇadharaiḥ sadā
rakṣaṃnti ca kurukṣetraṃ vārayanti ca pāpinaḥ || 27 ||
rantukaṃ tu samāsādya kṣāmayitvā punaḥ punaḥ
tataḥ snātvā sarasvatyāṃ yakṣaṃ dṛṣṭvā praṇamya ca || 28 ||
puṣpaṃ dhūpaṃ ca naivedyaṃ kṛtvaitadvākyamuccaret
tava prasādādyakṣendra vanāni saritastathā || 29 ||
bhramato mama tīrthāni mā vighnaṃ jāyatāṃ namaḥ
iti prasādya yakṣeśaṃ yātrāṃ samyak samācaret || 30 ||
vanānāṃ cāpi tīrthānāṃ saritāmapi mohini
yo naraḥ kurute yātrāṃ kurukṣetrasya puṇyadām || 31 ||
na tasya nyūnatā kācidiha loke paratra ca || 32 ||
iti śrībṛhannāradīyapurāṇottarabhāge bṛhadupākhyāne vasumohinisaṃvāde kurukṣetramāhātmye kṣetrapramāṇādinirūpaṇaṃ nāma catuṣṣaṣṭitamo'dhyāyaḥ || 64 ||

<2.65>

atha pañcaṣaṣṭitamo'dhyāyaḥ
mohinyuvāca -
vanāni kāni viprendra tatra santi śubhāvahāḥ
saritaśca kramādyātrāṃ vada me sarvasiddhidām || 1 ||
yāni tīrthāni santyatra kurukṣetre supuṇyade
tāni sarvāṇi me brūhi gatidastvaṃ gururyataḥ || 2 ||
vasuruvāca -
śṛṇu mohini vakṣyāmi kurukṣetrasya puṇyadam
yātrāvidhānaṃ yatkṛtvā labhate gatimuttamām || 3 ||
vanāni sapta santīha kurukṣetrasya madhyataḥ
teṣāṃ nāmāni vakṣyāmi puṇyadānāṃ nṛṇāmiha || 4 ||
kāmyakaṃ ca vanaṃ puṇyaṃ tathāditivanaṃ mahat
vyāsasya ca vanaṃ puṇyaṃ phalakīvanameva ca || 5 ||
tathā sūryavanaṃ cātra puṇyaṃ madhuvanaṃ ca vai
sītāvanaṃ tathā khyātaṃ nṛṇāṃ kalmaṣanāśanam || 6 ||
vanānyetāni saptātra teṣu tīrthānyanekaśaḥ
sarasvatī nadī puṇyā tathā vaitaraṇī nadī || 7 ||
gaṅgā mandākinī puṇyā tathaivānyā madhusravā
dṛṣadvatī kauśikī ca puṇyā hairaṇvatī nadī || 8 ||
varṣakālavahāścaitā varjayitvā sarasvatīm
etāsāmudakaṃ puṇyaṃ sparśe pāne samāplutau || 9 ||
rajasvalātvaṃ naitāsāṃ puṇyakṣetraprabhāvataḥ
rantukaṃ tu purāsādya dvārapālaṃ mahābalam || 10 ||
yakṣaṃ samabhivādyātha tatra yātrāṃ samārabhet
tato gacchennaraḥ puṇyaṃ bhadre 'ditivanaṃ mahat || 11 ||
adityā yatra putrārthaṃ samyak cīrṇaṃ mahattapaḥ
tatra snātvā samabhyarcya devamātaramaṅganā || 12 ||
sūte putraṃ mahāśūraṃ sarvalakṣaṇasaṃyutam
tato gacchedvarārohe viṣṇoḥ sthānamanuttamam || 13 ||
vimalaṃ nāma vikhyātaṃ yatra sannihito hariḥ
vimale tu naraḥ snātvā dṛṣṭvā ca vimaleśvaram || 14 ||
vimalaḥ sa labhellokaṃ devadevasya cakriṇaḥ
hariṃ ca baladevaṃ ca dṛṣṭvaikāsanamāsthitau || 15 ||
mucyate kilbiṣātsadyo mohinyatra na saṃśayaḥ
tataḥ pāriplavaṃ gacchettīrthaṃ lokeṣu viśrutam || 16 ||
tatra snātvā ca pītvā yo brāhmaṇaṃ vedapāragam
santoṣya dakṣiṇādyena brāhmayajñaphalaṃ labhet || 17 ||
yatrāsti saṅgamo bhadre kauśikyāḥ pāpanāśanaḥ
tatra snātvā naro bhaktyā prāpnoti priyasaṅgamam || 18 ||
tatastu pṛthivītīrthamāsādya kṣāntimānnaraḥ
snāto bhaktyā mahābhāge prāpnoti gatimuttamām || 19 ||
dharaṇyāmaparādhā ye kṛtāḥ syuḥ puruṣeṇa vai
tānsarvānkṣamate devī tatra snātasya dehinaḥ || 20 ||
tato dakṣāśrame puṇye dṛṣṭvā dakṣeśvaraṃ śivam
aśvamedhasya yajñasya phalaṃ prāpnoti mānavaḥ || 21 ||
tataḥ śālakinīṃ gacchettatra snātvā samarcayet
hariṃ hareṇa saṃyuktaṃ vāñchitārthasya labdhaye || 22 ||
nāgatīrthaṃ tataḥ prāpya snātvā tatra vidhānavit
sarpiścāsya dadhi prāśya nāgebhyo hyabhayaṃ labhet || 23 ||
tataḥ sāyamupāvṛtya rantukaṃ dvārapālakam
ekarātroṣitastatra pūjayettaṃ pare'hani || 24 ||
gandhādyairupacāraistu brāhmaṇaṃ prārcya bhojayet
tataḥ pañcanadaṃ gacchettīrthaṃ trailokyaviśrutam || 25 ||
pañca nādāḥ kṛtā yatra hareṇāsurabhīṣaṇāḥ
tena pañcanadaṃ nāma sarvapātakanāśanam || 26 ||
yatra snānena dānena nirbhayo jāyate naraḥ
koṭitīrthaṃ tato gacchedyatra rudre ṇa mohini || 27 ||
koṭitīrthānyupāhṛtya sthāpitāni mahātmanā
tatra tīrthe naraḥ snātvā dṛṣṭvā koṭīśvaraṃ haram || 28 ||
pañcayajñabhavaṃ puṇyaṃ tatprabhṛtyāpnuyātsadā
tatraiva vāmano devaḥ sarvairdevaiḥ pratiṣṭhitaḥ || 29 ||
tasmāttaṃ tatra sampūjya agniṣṭomaphalaṃ labhet
tato'śvitīrthamāsādya śraddhāvānvijitendri yaḥ || 30 ||
snātvā tatra yaśasvī ca rūpavāṃśca naro bhavet
tato vārāhatīrthaṃ ca prāpya viṣṇuprakalpitam || 31 ||
āplutya śraddhayā tatra naraḥ sadgatimāpnuyāt
tato vrajetsomatīrthaṃ yatra somo varānane || 32 ||
tapastaptvā hyarogo'bhūttatra snānaṃ samācaret
datvā ca tatra gāmekāṃ rājasūyaphalaṃ labhet || 33 ||
bhūteśvaraṃ ca tatraiva jvālāmāleśvaraṃ tathā
tāṇḍaliṅgaṃ samabhyarcya na bhūyo bhavamāpnuyāt || 34 ||
ekahaṃse naraḥ snātvā go sahasraphalaṃ labhet
kṛtaśauce naraḥ snātvā puṇḍarīkaphalaṃ labhet || 35 ||
tato muñjavaṭaṃ nāma prāpya devasya śūlinaḥ
samuṣya ca niśāmekāṃ prārcyeśaṃ gaṇapo bhavet || 36 ||
prasādya yakṣiṇīṃ tatra dvārasthāmupavāsakṛt
snātvābhyarcyāśayedviprānmahāpātakaśāntaye || 37 ||
pradakṣiṇamupāvṛtya puṣkaraṃ ca tato vrajet
tatra snātvā pitṝnprārcya kṛtakṛtyo naro bhavet || 38 ||
kanyādānaṃ ca yastatra kārtikyāṃ vai samācaret
prasannā devatāstasya yacchantyabhimataṃ phalam || 39 ||
kapilaśca mahāyakṣo dvārapālo'tra saṃsthitaḥ
vighnaṃ karoti pāpānāṃ sukṛtaṃ ca prayacchati || 40 ||
patnī tasya mahābhāgā nāmnolūkhalamekhalā
āhatya dundubhiṃ sā tu bhramate nityameva hi || 41 ||
vārayetpāpinaḥ snānāttathā sukṛtino nayet
tato rāmahradaṃ gacchetsnātvā tatra vidhānataḥ || 42 ||
devānpitṝnṛṣīniṣṭvā bhuktiṃ muktiṃ ca vindati
rāmamabhyarcya sacchraddhaḥ svarṇaṃ dattvā dhanī bhavet || 43 ||
vaṃśamūlaṃ samāsādya snātvā svaṃ vaṃśamuddharet
kāyaśodhanake snātvā śuddhadeho hariṃ viśet || 44 ||
lokoddhāraṃ tataḥ prāpya snātvābhyarcya janārdanam
prāpnoti śāśvataṃ lokaṃ yatra viṣṇuḥ sanātanaḥ || 45 ||
śrītīrthaṃ ca tataḥ prāpya śālagrāmamanuttamam
snātvābhyarcya hariṃ nityaṃ paśyati svāntike sthitam || 46 ||
kapilāhradamāsādya snātvābhyarcya surānpitṝn
sahasrakapilāpuṇyaṃ labhate nātra saṃśayaḥ || 47 ||
kapilaṃ tatra viśveśaṃ samabhyarcya vidhānataḥ
devaiśca satkṛto bhadre sākṣācchivapadaṃ labhet || 48 ||
sūryatīrthe tato bhānuṃ sopavāsaḥ samarcayet
agniṣṭomasya yajñasya phalaṃ labdhvā vrajeddivam || 49 ||
pṛthivīvivaradvāri sthito gaṇapatiḥ svayam
taṃ dṛṣṭvātha samabhyarcya yajñasya phalamāpnuyāt || 50 ||
devyāstīrthe naraḥ snātvā labhate rūpamuttamam
brahmāvarte naraḥ snātvā brahmajñānamavāpnuyāt || 51 ||
sutīrthake naraḥ snātvā devarṣipitṛmānavān
samabhyarcyāśvamedhasya yajñasya phalamāpnuyāt || 52 ||
kāmeśvarasya tīrthe tu snātvā śraddhāsamanvitaḥ
sarvavyādhivinirmukto brahma prāpnoti śāśvatam || 53 ||
snātasya mātṛtīrthe tu śraddhayābhyarcakasya tu
āsaptamaṃ kulaṃ devi varddhate śrīranuttamā || 54 ||
tataḥ sītāvanaṃ gacchettatra tīrthaṃ mahacchubhe
punātidarśanādeva puruṣānekaviṃśatim || 55 ||
keśānprakṣipya vai tatra pūto bhavati pāpataḥ
daśāśvamedhikaṃ tatra tīrthaṃ trailokyaviśrutam || 56 ||
darśanāttasya tīrthasya mukto bhavati kilbiṣaiḥ
mānuṣāhvaṃ tatastīrthaṃ prāpya snānaṃ samācaret || 57 ||
yadīcchenmānuṣaṃ janma punaśca vidhinandini
mānuṣācca tatastīrthātkrośamātre mahānadī || 58 ||
apagā nāma vikhyātā tatra snātvā vidhānataḥ
śyāmākaṃ payasā siddhaṃ bhojayed dvijasattamān || 59 ||
tasya pāpaṃ kṣayaṃ yāti pitṝṇāṃ śrāddhato gatiḥ
nabhasye māsi kṛṣṇe tu pitṛpakṣe mahālaye || 60 ||
caturdaśyāṃ tu madhyāhne piṇḍado muktimāpnuyāt
brāhmodumbarakaṃ gacchedbrahmaṇaḥ sthānakaṃ tataḥ || 61 ||
tatra brahmarṣikuṇḍeṣu snātaḥ somaphalaṃ labhet
vṛddhakedārake tīrthe sthāṇuṃ daṇḍisamanvitam || 62 ||
samarcya yatra cāpnoti naro'ntarddhānamicchayā
kalaśyāṃ ca tato gacchedyatra devī svayaṃ sthitā || 63 ||
snātvāsyāmambikāṃ prārcya taretsaṃsārasāgaram
sarake kṛṣṇabhūtāyāṃ dṛṣṭvā devaṃ maheśvaram || 64 ||
śaivaṃ padamavāpnoti naraḥ śraddhāsamanvitaḥ
tisraḥ koṭyastu tīrthānāṃ sarake santi bhāmini || 65 ||
rudra koṭistathā kūpe saromadhye vyavasthitā
tasminsarasi yaḥ snātvā rudra koṭiṃ smarennaraḥ || 66 ||
pūjitā rudra koṭistu tena syānnātra saṃśayaḥ
īhāspadaṃ ca tatraiva tīrthaṃ pāpapraṇāśanam || 67 ||
yasminmuktimavāpnoti darśanādeva mānavaḥ
tatrasthānarcayitvā ca devānpitṛgaṇānapi || 68 ||
na durgatimavāpnoti manasā cintitaṃ labhet
kedāraṃ ca mahātīrthaṃ sarvakalmaṣanāśanam || 69 ||
tatra snātvā ca puruṣaḥ sarvadānaphalaṃ labhet
anyajanmeti vikhyātaṃ sarakasya tu pūrvataḥ || 70 ||
saro mahatsvacchajalaṃ devau hariharau yataḥ
viṣṇuścaturbhujastatra liṅgākāraḥ śivaḥ sthitaḥ || 71 ||
tatra snātvā ca tau dṛṣṭvā stutvā mokṣaṃ labhennaraḥ
nāgahrade tato gatvā snātvā caitre sitāntake || 72 ||
śrāddhado muktimāpnoti yamalokaṃ na paśyati
tatastriviṣṭapaṃ gacchettīrthaṃ devaniṣevitam || 73 ||
yatra vaitaraṇī puṇyā nadī pāpapramocinī
tatra snātvārcayitvā ca śūlapāṇiṃ bṛṣadhvajam || 74 ||
sarvapāpaviśuddhātmā gacchatyeva parāṃ gatim
rasāvarte naraḥ snātvā siddhimāpnotyanuttamām || 75 ||
caitrasya sitabhūtāyāṃ snānaṃ kṛtvā vilepake
pūjayitvā śivaṃ bhaktyā sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate || 76 ||
tato gacchennaro devi phalakīvanamuttamam
yatra devāḥ sagandharvāstapyante paramaṃ tapaḥ || 77 ||
tatra nadyāṃ dṛṣadvatyāṃ naraḥ snātvā vidhānataḥ
devānpitṝṃstarpayitvā hyagniṣṭomātirātrabhāk || 78 ||
darśe tathā vidhudine tatra śrāddhaṃ karoti yaḥ
gayāśrāddhasamaṃ tatra labhate phalamuttamam || 79 ||
śrāddhe phalamaraṇyasya smaraṇaṃ pitṛtṛptidam
pāṇighāte tatastīrthe pitṝnsantarpya mānavaḥ || 80 ||
rājasūyaphalaṃ prāpya sāṅkhyaṃ yogaṃ ca vindati
tatastu miśrake tīrthe snātvā martyo vidhānataḥ || 81 ||
sarvatīrthaphalaṃ prāpya labhate gatimuttamām
tato vyāsavane gatvā snātvā tīrthe manojave || 82 ||
manīṣiṇaṃ vibhuṃ dṛṣṭvā manasā cintitaṃ labhet
gatvā madhuvanaṃ caiva devyāstīrthe naraḥ śuciḥ || 83 ||
snātvā devānṛṣīniṣṭvā labhate siddhimuttamām
kauśikīsaṅgame tīrthe dṛṣadvatyāṃ naraḥ plutaḥ || 84 ||
niyato niyatāhāraḥ sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate
tato vyāsasthaladyaṃ gacchedyatra vyāsena dhīmatā || 85 ||
putraśokābhibhūtena dehatyāgo viniścitaḥ
punarutthāpito devaistatra gatvā na śokabhāk || 86 ||
kinduśūkūpamāsādya tilaprasthaṃ pradāpya ca
gaccheddhi paramāṃ siddhiṃ mṛto muktivāpnuyāt || 87 ||
āhnaṃ ca muditaṃ caiva dvai tīrthe bhuvi viśrute
tayoḥ snātvā viśuddhātmā sūryalokamavāpnuyāt || 88 ||
mṛgamucyaṃ tato gatvā gaṅgāyāṃ praṇataḥ sthitaḥ
arcayitvā mahādevamaśvamedhaphalaṃ labhet || 89 ||
koṭitīrthaṃ tato gatvā snātvā koṭīśvaraṃ śivam
dṛṣṭvā stutvā śraddadhānaḥ koṭiyajñaphalaṃ labhet || 90 ||
tato vāmanakaṃ gacchettriṣu lokeṣu viśrutam
yatra vāmanajanmābhūdbaleryajñajihīrṣayā || 91 ||
tatra viṣṇupade snātvā pūjayitvā ca vāmanam
sarvapāpaviśuddhātmā viṣṇuloke mahīyate || 92 ||
jyeṣṭhāśramaṃ ca tatraiva sarvapātakanāśanam
jyeṣṭhasya śuklaikādaśyāṃ sopavāsaḥ pare'hani || 93 ||
snātvā tatra vidhānena śreṣṭhatvaṃ labhate nṛṣu
śrāddhaṃ tatra kṛtaṃ devi pitṝṇāmatituṣṭidam || 94 ||
tatraiva koṭitīrthaṃ ca triṣu lokeṣu viśrutam
tasmiṃstīrthe naraḥ snātvā koṭiyajñaphalaṃ labhet || 95 ||
tatra koṭīśvaraṃ nāma devedavaṃ maheśvaram
samabhyarcya vidhānena gāṇapatyamavāpnuyāt || 96 ||
sūryatīrthaṃ ca tatraiva snātvātra ravilokabhāk
kulottāraṇake tīrthe gatvā snānaṃ samācaran || 97 ||
uddhṛtya svakulaṃ svarge kalpāntaṃ nivasettataḥ
pavanasya hrade snātvā dṛṣṭvā devaṃ maheśvaram || 98 ||
vimuktaḥ sarvapāpebhyaḥ śaivaṃ padamavāpnuyāt
snātvā ca hanumattīrthe naro muktimavāpnuyāt || 99 ||
śālahotrasya rājarṣestīrthe snātvāghavarjitaḥ
śrīkumbhākhye sarasvatyāstīrthe snātvātha yajñavāk || 100 ||
snātaśca naimiṣe kuṇḍe naimiṣasnānapuṇyabhāk
snātvā vedavatītīrthe strī satītvamavāpnuyāt || 101 ||
brahmatīrthe naraḥ snātvā brāhmaṇyaṃ labhate naraḥ
brahmaṇaḥ paramaṃ sthānaṃ yatra gatvā na śocati || 102 ||
somatīrthe naraḥ snātvā svargatiṃ samavāpnuyāt
saptasārasvataṃ tīrthaṃ prāpya snātvā ca muktibhāk || 103 ||
yatra sapta sarasvatyaḥ samyagaikyaṃ samāgatāḥ
suprabhā kāñcanākṣī ca viśālā ca manoharī || 104 ||
sunandā ca suveṇuśca saptamī vimalodakā
tathaivauśanase tīrthe snātvā mucyeta pātakaiḥ || 105 ||
kapālamocane snātvā brahmahāpi viśudhyati
vaiśvāmitre naraḥ snāto brāhmaṇyaṃ samavāpnuyāt || 106 ||
tataḥ pṛthūdake snātvā mucyate bhavabandhanāt
avakīrṇe naraḥ snātvā brahmacaryaphalaṃ labhet || 107 ||
madhusrāve'thaprayātaḥ snāto mucyeta pātakaiḥ
snātvā tīrthe ca vāsiṣṭhe vāsiṣṭhaṃ lokamāpnuyāt || 108 ||
aruṇāsaṅgame snātvā trirātropoṣito naraḥ
snātvā muktimavāpnoti nātra kāryā vicāraṇā || 109 ||
samudrā statra catvārasteṣu snāto naraḥ śubhe
gosahasraphalaṃ labdhvā svargaloke mahīyate || 110 ||
somatīrthaṃ ca tatrānyattasminsnātvā ca mohini
caitre ṣaṣṭhyāṃ ca śuklāyāṃ śrāddhaṃ kṛtvoddharetpitṝn || 111 ||
atha pañcavaṭe snātvā yogamūrtidharaṃ śivam
samabhyarcya vidhānena daivataiḥ saha modate || 112 ||
kurutīrthe tataḥ snātaḥ sarvasiddhimavāpnuyāt
svargadvāre pluto martyaḥ svargaloke mahīyate || 113 ||
snāto hyanarake tīrthe mucyate sarvakilbiṣaiḥ
tato gacchennaro devi kāmyakaṃ vanamuttamam || 114 ||
yasminpraviṣṭamātrastu mucyate sarvasañcayaiḥ
athādityavanaṃ prāpya darśanādeva muktibhāk || 115 ||
snānaṃ ravidine kṛtvā tatra vāñchitamāpnuyāt
yajñopavītike snātvā svadharmaphalabhāgbhavet || 116 ||
tataścatuḥpravāhākhye tīrthe snātvā narottamaḥ
sarvatīrthaphalaṃ prāpya modate divi devavat || 117 ||
snātastīrthe vihāre tu sarvasaukhyamavāpnuyāt
durgātīrthe naraḥ snātvā na durgatimavāpnuyāt || 118 ||
tataḥ sarasvatīkūpe pitṛtīrthāparāhvaye
snātvā santarpya devādīṃllabhate gatimuttamām || 119 ||
snātvā prācīsarasvatyāṃ śrāddhaṃ kṛtvā vidhānataḥ
durlabhaṃ prāpnuyātkāmaṃ dehānte svargatiṃ labhet || 120 ||
śukratīrthe naraḥ snātvā śrāddhadaḥ proddharetpitṝn
aṣṭamyāṃ vā caturdaśyāṃ caitre kṛṣṇe viśeṣataḥ || 121 ||
sopavāso brahmatīrthe muktibhāṅnātra saṃśayaḥ
sthāṇutīrthe tataḥ snātvā dṛṣṭvā sthāṇuvaṭaṃ naraḥ || 122 ||
mucyate pātakairghorairiti prāha pitāmahaḥ
darśanātsthāṇuliṅgasya yātrā pūrṇā prajāyate || 123 ||
kurukṣetrasya deveśi satyaṃ satyaṃ mayoditam
kurukṣetrasamaṃ tīrthaṃ na bhūtaṃ na bhaviṣyati || 124 ||
tatra dvādaśa yātrāstu kṛtvā bhūyo na janmabhāk
pūrtamiṣṭaṃ tapastaptaṃ hutaṃ dattaṃ vidhānataḥ || 125 ||
tatra syādakṣayaṃ sarvamiti vedavido viduḥ
manvādau ca yugādau ca grahaṇe candra sūryayoḥ || 126 ||
mahāpāte ca saṅkrānto puṇye cāpyanyavāsare
snātastatraḥ kurukṣetre phalānantyamavāpnuyāt || 127 ||
kalijānāṃ tu pāpānāṃ pāvanāya mahātmanām
brahmaṇā kalpitaṃ tīrthaṃ kurukṣetraṃ sukhāvaham || 128 ||
ya imāṃ kīrtayetpuṇyāṃ kathāṃ pāpapraṇāśinīm
śṛṇuyādvā naro bhaktyā so'pi pāpaiḥ pramucyate || 129 ||
yadyaddadāti yastatra kurukṣetre ravigrahe
tattadeva sadāpnoti naro janmani janmani || 130 ||
atha kiṃ bahunoktena vidhije śṛṇu niścitam
sevetaiva kurukṣetraṃ yadīcchedbhavamokṣaṇam || 131 ||
etadeva mahatpuṇyametadeva mahattamaḥ
etadeva mahajjñānaṃ yadvrajetsthāṇutīrthakam || 132 ||
kurukṣetrasamaṃ tīrthaṃ nānyadbhuvi śubhāvaham
sācāro vāpyanācāro yatra muktimavāpnuyāt || 133 ||
etatte sarvamākhyātaṃ yatpṛṣṭo'ha tvayānaghe
kurukṣetrasya māhātmyaṃ sarvapāpanikṛntanam || 134 ||
puṇyadaṃ mokṣadaṃ caiva kimanyacchrotumicchasi || 135 ||
iti śrībṛhannāradīyapurāṇe bṛhadupākhyāne uttarabhāge vasumohinīsaṃvāde kurukṣetramāhātmye tīrthayātrāvarṇanaṃ nāma pañcaṣaṣṭitamo'dhyāyaḥ || 65 ||

iti kurukṣetramāhātmyaṃ samāptam

atha gaṅgādvāramāhātmyaṃ prārabhyate

<2.66>

atha ṣaṭṣaṣṭitamo'dhyāyaḥ
mohinyuvāca -
kurukṣetrasya māhātmyaṃ śrutaṃ pāpāpahaṃ mahat
tvatto dvijavaraśreṣṭha sarvasiddhipradaṃ nṛṇām || 1 ||
gaṅgādvāreti yatkhyātaṃ tīrthaṃ puṇyāvahaṃ guro
tatsamākhyāhi bhadraṃ te śrotuṃ vāñchāsti me hṛdi || 2 ||
vasuruvāca -
śṛṇu bhadre pravakṣyāmi māhātmyaṃ pāpanāśanam
gaṅgādvārasya te puṇyaṃ śṛṇvatāṃ paṭhatāṃ śubham || 3 ||
yatra bhūmimanuprāptā bhagīratharathānugā
śrīgaṅgālakanandākhyā nagānbhittvā sahasraśaḥ || 4 ||
yatrāyajata yajñeśaṃ purā dakṣaḥ prajāpatiḥ
tatkṣetraṃ puṇyadaṃ nṝṇāṃ sarvapātakanāśanam || 5 ||
yasminyajñe samāhūtā devā indra purogamāḥ
svaiḥ svairgaṇaiḥ samāyātā yajñabhāgajighṛkṣayā || 6 ||
yatra devarṣayaḥ prāptāstathā brahmarṣayo'malāḥ
śiṣyapraśiṣyaiḥ sahitāstathā rājarṣayaḥ śubhe || 7 ||
sarve nimantritāstena brahmaputreṇa dhīmatā
gandharvāpsaraso yakṣāḥ siddhavidyādharoragāḥ || 8 ||
samprāptā yajñasadanamṛte śarvaṃ pinākinam
tatastu gacchatāṃ teṣāṃ sapriyāṇāṃ vimāninām || 9 ||
dakṣayajñotsavaṃ prītyānyonyaṃ varṇayatāṃ satī
śrutvā sotkā mahādevaṃ prārthayāmāsa bhāminī || 10 ||
tacchrutvā bhagavānāha na śreyo gamanaṃ tataḥ
atha devamanādṛtya bhāvino'thasya gauravāt || 11 ||
jagāmaikākinī bhadre dra ṣṭuṃ pitṛmakhotsavam
tataḥ sā tatra samprāptā na kenāpi sabhājitā || 12 ||
prāṇāṃstatyāja tanvaṅgī tajjātaṃ kṣetramuttamam
tasmiṃstīrthe tu ye snātvā tarpayanti surānpitṝn || 13 ||
te syurdevyāḥ priyatamā bhogamokṣaikabhāginaḥ
ye'nye'pi tatra svānprāṇāṃstyajantyanaśanādibhiḥ || 14 ||
te'pi sākṣācchivaṃ prāpya nāpnuvanti punarjanim
atha tannāradācchrutvā bhagavānnīlalohitaḥ || 15 ||
maraṇaṃ svapriyāyāstu vīrabhadraṃ vinirmame
sa sarvaiḥ pramathairyuktastaṃ yajñaṃ samanāśayat || 16 ||
punarvidheḥ prārthanayā mīḍhvānsadyaḥ prasāditaḥ
saṃdadhe ca punaryajñaṃ vikṛtaṃ prakṛtisthitam || 17 ||
tatastattīrthamatulaṃ sarvapātakanāśanam
jātaṃ yatrāplutaḥ somo mukto yakṣmagrahādabhūt || 18 ||
tatra yo vidhivatsnātvā yaṃ yaṃ kāmaṃ vicintayet
taṃ tamāpnoti vidhije nātra kāryā vicāraṇā || 19 ||
yatra yajñeśvaraḥ sākṣādbhagavānviṣṇuravyayaḥ
stuto dakṣeṇa devaiśca tattīrthaṃ harisaṃjñitam || 20 ||
tatra yo vidhivanmartyaḥ srāyāddharipade sati
sa viṣṇorvallabho bhūyādbhuktimuktyekabhājanam || 21 ||
ataḥ pūrvadiśi kṣetraṃ trigaṅgaṃ nāma viśrutam
yatra tripathagā sākṣāddṛśyate sakalairjanaiḥ || 22 ||
tatra snātvātha santarpya devarṣipitṛmānavān
samyakchraddhāyuto martyo modate divi devavat || 23 ||
tatra yastyajati prāṇānpravāhe patitaḥ sati
sa vrajedvaiṣṇavaṃ dhāma devaiḥ samyaksabhājitaḥ || 24 ||
tataḥ kanakhale tīrthe dakṣiṇīṃ diśamāśrite
trirātropoṣitaḥ snātvā mucyate sarvakilbiṣaiḥ || 25 ||
atha yastatragāṃ dadyādbrahmaṇe vedapārage
sa kadācinna paśyettu devi vaitaraṇīṃ yamam || 26 ||
atra japtaṃ hutaṃ taptaṃ dattamānantyamaśnute
atraiva jahnutīrthe ca yatra vai jahnunā purā || 27 ||
rājarṣiṇā nipītābhūdgaṇḍūṣīkṛtya sā nadī
prasāditena sā tena muktā karṇādvinirgatā || 28 ||
tatra snātvā mahābhāge yo naraḥ śraddhayānvitaḥ
sopavāsaḥ samabhyarcedbrāhmaṇaṃ vedapāragam || 29 ||
bhojayetparamānnena svarge kalpaṃ vasetsa tu
atha paścāddiśi gataṃ koṭitīrthaṃ sumadhyame || 30 ||
yatra koṭiguṇaṃ puṇyaṃ bhavetkoṭīśadarśanāt
oṣyaikāṃ rajanīṃ tatra puṇḍarīkamavāpnuyāt || 31 ||
tathaivottaradigbhāge saptagaṅgeti viśrutam
tīrthaṃ paramakaṃ devi sarvapātakanāśanam || 32 ||
yatrāśramāśca puṇyā vai saptarṣīṇāṃ mahāmate
teṣu sarveṣu tu pṛthak snātvā santarpya devatāḥ || 33 ||
pitṝṃśca labhate martya ṛṣilokaṃ sanātanam
bhagīrathena vai rājñā yadānītā surāpagā || 34 ||
tadā sā prītaye teṣāṃ saptadhārāgatābhavat
saptagaṅgaṃ tatastīrthaṃ bhuvi vikhyātimāgatam || 35 ||
sa āvartaṃ tataḥ prāpya santarpyāmarapūrvakān
snātvā devendra bhavane modate yugameva ca || 36 ||
tato bhadre samāsādya kapilāhradamuttamam
dhenuṃ dattvā dvijāgryāya gosahasraphalaṃ labhet || 37 ||
atraiva nāgarājasya tīrthaṃ paramapāvanam
atrābhiṣekaṃ yaḥ kuryātso'bhayaṃ sarpato labhet || 38 ||
tato lalitakaṃ prāpya śantanostīrthamuttamam
snātvā santarpya vidhivatsurādīm̐ llabhate gatim || 39 ||
yatra śantanunālabdhā gaṅgā mānuṣyamāgatā
tatraiva tatyaje dehaṃ vasūnsūtvānuvatsaram || 40 ||
taddeho nyapatattatra tatrābhūdṛkṣajanma ca
tatra yaḥ snāti manujo bhakṣayedoṣadhīṃ ca tām || 41 ||
sa na durgatimāpnoti gaṅgādevīprasādataḥ
bhīmasthalaṃ tataḥ prāpya yaḥ snāyātsukṛtī naraḥ || 42 ||
bhogānbhuktveha dehānte svargatiṃ samavāpnuyāt
etānyuddeśato devi tīrthāni gaditāni te || 43 ||
anyāni vai mahābhāge santi tatra sahasraśaḥ
yo'sminkṣetre naraḥ snāyātkumbhagejye'jage ravau || 44 ||
sa tu syādvākpatiḥ sākṣātprabhākara ivāparaḥ
atha yāte prayāgādipuṇyatīrthe pṛthūke || 45 ||
atha yo vāruṇe yoge mahāvāruṇake tathā
mahāmahāvāruṇe ca srāyāttatra vidhānataḥ || 46 ||
sampūjya brāhmaṇān bhaktyā sa labhedbrahmaṇaḥ padam
saṅkrāntau vāpyamāyāṃ vā vyatīpāte yugādike || 47 ||
puṇye'hani tathānyadvai yatiṃñciddānamācaret
tattu koṭiguṇaṃ bhūyātsatyametanmayoditam || 48 ||
gaṅgādvāraṃ smaredyo vai dūrasaṃstho'pi mānavaḥ
sadgatiṃ sa samāpnoti smarannante yathā harim || 49 ||
yaṃ yaṃ devi haridvāre pūjayetprayato naraḥ
sa sa devaḥ suprasannaḥ pūrayettanmanorathān || 50 ||
etadeva tapaḥsthānametadeva japasthalam
etadeva hutasthānaṃ yatra gaṅgā bhuvaṃ gatā || 51 ||
yastatra niyato martyo gaṅgānāmasahasrakam
trikālaṃ paṭhati snātvā so'kṣayāṃ santatiṃ labhet || 52 ||
gaṅgādvāre purāṇaṃ tu śṛṇuyādyaśca bhaktitaḥ
niyamena mahābhāge sa yāti padamavyayam || 53 ||
haridvārasya māhātmyaṃ yaḥ śṛṇoti narottamaḥ
paṭhedvā bhaktisaṃyuktaḥ so'pi snānaphalaṃ labhet || 54 ||
devi tiṣṭhati yadgehe māhātmyaṃ likhitantvidam
tadgṛhe sarpacaurāgnigraharājabhayaṃ nahi || 55 ||
varddhaṃte sampadaḥ sarvā viṣṇudevaprasādataḥ || 56 ||
iti śrībṛhannāradīyapurāṇe bṛhadupākhyāne uttarabhāge vasumohinīsaṃvāde haridbāramāhātmyaṃ nāma ṣaṭṣaṣṭitamo'dhyāyaḥ || 66 ||

iti gaṅgādvāraharidvāramāhātmyaṃ samāptam

atha badarīmāhātmyaṃ prārabhyate

<2.67>

atha saptaṣaṣṭitamo'dhyāyaḥ
mohinyuvāca -
aho dvijavarākhyātaṃ gaṅgādvārasamudbhavam
māhātmyamadhunā brūhi badaryāḥ pāpanāśanam || 1 ||
vasuruvāca -
śṛṇu bhadre pravakṣyāmi māhātmyaṃ badarībhavam
yacchrutvā mucyate janturjanmasaṃsārabandhanāt || 2 ||
badaryākhyaṃ hareḥ kṣetraṃ sarvapātakanāśanam
muktidaṃ bhavabhītānāṃ kalidoṣaharaṃ nṛṇām || 3 ||
yatra nārāyaṇo devo naraśca bhagavānṛṣiḥ
dharmānmūrtyāṃ labdhajanī yayaturgandhamādanam || 4 ||
yatrāsti badarīvṛkṣo bahugandhaphalānvitaḥ
tasminsthāne mahābhāga ākalpādāsthitau tapaḥ || 5 ||
nāradādyairmunivaraiḥ kalāpagrāmavāsibhiḥ
siddhasaṅghaiḥ parivṛtau lokānāṃ sthitaye sthitau || 6 ||
yatrāgnitīrthaṃ vikhyātaṃ vartate sarvasiddhidam
mahāpātakinastatra snātvā śudhyanti pātakāt || 7 ||
durvarṇaṃ hāṭakaṃ yadvadagnau dhmātaṃ viśuddhyti
tathāgnitīrthaṃ āplutya dehī pāpairvimucyate || 8 ||
cāndrā yaṇasahasraistu kṛcchraiḥ koṭibhireva ca
yatphalaṃ labhate martyastatsnānādvahnitīrthataḥ || 9 ||
śilāḥ pañcāpi tattīrthe santi yanmadhyataḥ sthitam
agnitīrthaṃ vidhisute darśanāttadaghāpaham || 10 ||
nārado yatra bhagavāṃstapastepe sudāruṇam
sā śilā nāradī nāma darśanādeva muktidā || 11 ||
nityadā yatra sānnidhyaṃ harerasti sulocane
tatra nāradakuṇḍaṃ ca yatra snāto naraḥ śuciḥ || 12 ||
bhuktiṃ muktiṃ harerbhaktiṃ yadyadvāñchettu tallabhet
etasyāṃ yo naro bhaktyā snānaṃ dānaṃ surārcanam || 13 ||
homaṃ japaṃ tathānyadvā yatkaroti tadakṣayam
vainateyaśilā cānyā tasmin kṣetre śubhāvahā || 14 ||
yatra taptaṃ tapastīvaṃ garuḍena mahātmanā
triṃśadvarṣasahasrāṇi haridarśanakāmyayā || 15 ||
tataḥ prasanno bhagavāndadau tasmai varaṃ śubhe
ajeyo daityasaṅghānāṃ nāgānāṃ ca vibhīṣaṇaḥ || 16 ||
vāhanaṃ bhava me vatsa prasanno'ha tavopari
tvannāmneyaṃśilākhyātiṃ gamiṣyati mahītale || 17 ||
darśanātpuṇyadā nṝṇāṃ yatra taptaṃ tvayā tapaḥ
atra mukhyatame gaṅgā tīrthe matprītikāmyayā || 18 ||
āvirastu mahābhāga puṇyadā snānakāriṇām
pañcagaṅge tu yaḥ snātvā devādīṃstarpayiṣyati || 19 ||
na tasya punarāvṛttirbrahmalokātsanātanāt
evaṃ datvā varaṃ viṣṇurbabhūvāntarhitastadā || 20 ||
garuḍo'pyājñayā viṣṇorvāhanatvamupāgataḥ
tataḥ prabhṛti tattīrthaṃ jātaṃ pāpavināśanam || 21 ||
puṇyadaṃ vai smareccāpi vainateyagatipradam
athānyā tu śilā tatra vārāhīti śubhāvahā || 22 ||
yatroddhṛtya mahīṃ devo hiraṇyākṣaṃ nipātya ca
śilārūpeṇa cākramya sthitaḥ pāpavināśanaḥ || 23 ||
tatra yo manujo gatvā gaṅgāmbhasyamale plutaḥ
pūjayettāṃ śilāṃ bhaktyā sa na durgatimāpnuyāt || 24 ||
athānyā nārasiṃhākhyā śilā tatra sureśvarī
hiraṇkaśipuṃ hatvā sthito yatra babhūva ha || 25 ||
tataḥ surarṣibhiḥ sarvaiḥ krodhastasya nivāritaḥ
prārthitaśca viśālāyāṃ sthātuṃ tatra sadaiva hi || 26 ||
caturbhujastathā tatra śilārūpamupāgataḥ
jalakrīḍāparo nityaṃ vartate toyamadhyagaḥ || 27 ||
tatra yaḥ snāti manujo nṛhareḥ pūjayecchilām
sa labhedvaiṣṇavaṃ dhāma punarāvṛttidurlabham || 28 ||
pañcamīṃ tu śilāṃ devi vahnikuṇḍataṭasthitām
naranārāyaṇākhyāṃ ca vakṣyāmi śṛṇu sāmpratam || 29 ||
kṛte yuge tu sarveṣāṃ naranārāyaṇo hariḥ
pratyakṣaṃ vasate tatra bhuktimuktipradāyakaḥ || 30 ||
tretāyāṃ munibhirdevairyogibhirdṛśyate śubhe
nānyaiḥ samāsthito yogaṃ lokasthitividhāyakaḥ || 31 ||
dvāpare samanuprāpte jñānayogena dṛśyate
nānyopāyena kenāpi tiṣye darśanatāṃ gataḥ || 32 ||
tato brahmādayo devā ṛṣayaśca tapodhanāḥ
stutvā vāgbhirvicitrābhirdevaṃ prāsādayanharim || 33 ||
tato'śarīriṇī vāṇī prāha tānvidhipūrvakān
kalau na darśanaṃ yāmi sarvadharmavivarjite || 34 ||
yadi vo darśane śraddhā maṇḍapasya sureśvarāḥ
gṛhīdhvaṃ māmakīṃ mūrtiṃ śailadyaṃ nāradakuṇḍagām || 35 ||
tatastāṃ giramākarṇya brahmādyā hṛṣṭamānasāḥ
niṣkāsya śailadyaṃ tāṃ divyāṃ mūrtiṃ nāradakuṃḍagām || 36 ||
sthāpayāmāsurabhyarcya svaṃ svaṃ dhāma yayustataḥ
vaiśākhe māsi te devā gacchanti nijamandiram || 37 ||
kārtike tu samāgatya punararcāṃ caranti ca
tato vaiśākhamārabhya mānavā himasaṃkṣayāt || 38 ||
labhante darśanaṃ puṇyāḥ pāpakarmavivarjitāḥ
ṣaṇmāsaṃ daivataiḥ pūjyā ṣaṇmāsaṃ mānavaistathā || 39 ||
evaṃ vyavasthayā mūrtistatprabhṛtyāvirāsa sā
yaḥ śailadyaṃ pratimāṃ viṣṇoḥ pūjayedbhaktibhāvataḥ || 40 ||
naivedyaṃ bhakṣayeccāpi sa muktiṃ labhate dhruvam
etāḥ pañca śilāḥ puṇyā viśālāyāṃ vyavasthitāḥ || 41 ||
āsāṃ madhye tu naivedyaṃ devānāṃ durlabhaṃ hareḥ
kiṃ punarmānuṣādīnāṃ bhakṣitaṃ mokṣasādhanam || 42 ||
badaryā viṣṇunaivedyaṃ sikthamātraṃ ca bhakṣitam
śodhayeddehagaṃ pāpaṃ dīptāgniriva kāñcanam || 43 ||
kapālamocanaṃ hyetattīrthaṃ pāpaviśodhanam
yanmadhye tu śilāḥ pañca santi pāpavimocikāḥ || 44 ||
athāparaṃ mahattīrthamatraiva śṛṇu mohini
yatra snāto naro bhaktyā vedānāṃ pārago bhavet || 45 ||
suptasya brahmaṇo vaktrānnirgatānasuro'harat
vedānhayaśirā nāma devādīnāṃ bhayāvahaḥ || 46 ||
tatastu brahmaṇā viṣṇuḥ prārthitaḥ prakaṭo'bhavat
matsyarūpeṇa taṃ hatvā vedānpratyarpayadvidheḥ || 47 ||
tacca tīrthaṃ mahatpuṇyaṃ sarvavidyāprakāśakam
taimiṅgilaṃ mahābhāge darśanātpāpanāśanam || 48 ||
hayagrīvasvarūpeṇa bhagavānviṣṇuravyayaḥ
punaśca hatvā mattau dvāvasurau madhukaiṭabhau || 49 ||
vedāpahāriṇau bhūyo vedānvai brahmaṇe hyadāt
tattīrthaṃ sarvapāpaghnaṃ snānamātreṇa vaidhasi || 50 ||
mātsye cāpi hayagrīve vedāste dra varūpiṇaḥ
vartaṃte sarvadā bhadre tajjalaṃ pāpanāśanam || 51 ||
tīrthamindra padaṃ tatra vikhyātaṃ vahnikoṇagam
tatra snātvā naro devi padamaindra mavāpnuyāt || 52 ||
mānasodbhedakaṃ cānyattatra tīrthaṃ manoramam
bhinatti hṛdayagranthiṃ chinattyakhilasaṃśayam || 53 ||
haratyaṃhaśca sakalaṃ mānasodbhedakaṃ tataḥ
kāmākāmābhidhaṃ cānyattīrthaṃ tatra varānane || 54 ||
kāmapradaṃ kāmavatāmakāmānāṃ tu mokṣadam
tataḥ paścimato bhadre vasudhāreti tīrthakam || 55 ||
tatra snātvā naro bhaktyā labhate vāñchitaṃ phalam
atra puṇyavato yānti dṛśyate jalamadhyagam || 56 ||
yaddṛṣṭvā na punarjanturgarbhavāsaṃ prapadyate
tato nairṛtidigbhāge pañca dhārāḥ patantyadhaḥ || 57 ||
prabhāsapuṣkaragayānaimiṣāraṇyasaṃjñakāḥ
tāsu snātvā pṛthaṅmartyastattattīrthaphalaṃ labhet || 58 ||
tato'nyadvimalaṃ tīrthaṃ somakuṇḍāparāhvayam
yatra taptvā tapastīvraṃ somaḥ kheṭādyadhīśvaraḥ || 59 ||
tatra snātvā naro bhadre gatadoṣaḥ prajāyate
tatrānyaddvādaśādityaṃ tīrthaṃ pāpaharaṃ param || 60 ||
snātvā yatra naro bhūtvā tejasā bhāskaropamaḥ
catuḥsroto'paraṃ tatra tīrthaṃ tatrāpluto naraḥ || 61 ||
dharmārthakāmamokṣāṃśca labhate yaṃ yamicchati
atha saptapadaṃ nāma tīrthaṃ tatra manoharam || 62 ||
darśanādyasya tīrthasya pātakāni mahāntyapi
naśyanti niyataṃ tasya kiṃ punaḥ snānataḥ sati || 63 ||
triṣu lokeṣu kuṇḍasya brahmaviṣṇumaheśvarāḥ
āsthitāstatra maraṇānnaraḥ satyapadaṃ labhet || 64 ||
naranārāyaṇāvāse tīrthamastyaparaṃ śubhe
urvaśīkuṇḍanāmātra snāto rūpamanoharaḥ || 65 ||
nārāyaṇapriyo'tyarthaṃ bhavedviśvavaśaṅkaraḥ
tato dakṣiṇadigbhāge tīrthamastrābhidhaṃ param || 66 ||
naranārāyaṇau yatra śastraṃ nyasya tapaḥsthitau
āyudhāni tu divyāni śaṅkhacakrādikāni ca || 67 ||
mūrtimanti mahābhāge dṛśyante kṛtibhiryataḥ
tatra snātvā naro bhaktyā na śatrorbhayamāpnuyāt || 68 ||
merutīrthaṃ ca tatrāsti yatra dṛṣṭvā dhanurddharam
snātvā ca labhate so'pi śubhe sarvānmanorathān || 69 ||
lokapālāhvayaṃ nāma tatrānyattīrthamuttamam
lokapālaistapastaptaṃ yatra tatrāpluto naraḥ || 70 ||
sarvatīrthāplutiphalaṃ labhate devi mānavaḥ
daṇḍenāhatya hariṇā yatastīrthaṃ vinirmitam || 71 ||
daṇḍapuṣkariṇītyetattato lokaprasaukhyadam
bhāgīrathī yatra yogaṃ prāptā hyalakanandayā || 72 ||
tattīrthaṃ sarvataḥ śreṣṭhaṃ puṇye badarikāśrame
tatra snātvā pitṝndevānsantarpyābhyarcya bhaktitaḥ || 73 ||
labhate vaiṣṇavaṃ dhāma sarvadevanamaskṛtaḥ
saṅgamāddakṣiṇe bhāge dharmakṣetraṃ śubhānane || 74 ||
tatkṣetraṃ pāvanaṃ manye sarvatīrthottamottamam
tatra snātvā naro bhadre sādhyasannidhibhāgbhavet || 75 ||
urvaśīsaṅgamaṃ tīrthaṃ sarvapāpaharaṃ nṛṇām
karmoddharāhvayaṃ cānyaddharibhaktyekasādhanam || 76 ||
brahmāvartāhvayaṃ tīrthaṃ brahmalokaikakāraṇam
gaṅgāśritāni caitāni tīrthāni kathitāni te || 77 ||
brahmāpi kārtsnyato vaktuṃ tatrasthāni na vai prabhuḥ
ya idaṃ śṛṇuyānnityaṃ śrāvayedvā samāhitaḥ || 78 ||
sarvapāpavinirmuktaḥ so'pi viṣṇupadaṃ labhet
māsamātraṃ naro bhaktyā yo'tra tiṣṭheddhṛtavrataḥ || 79 ||
sa sākṣādeva paśyettu naranārāyaṇaṃ harim
yatraitallikhitaṃ devi māhātmyaṃ badarībhavam || 80 ||
nālpamṛtyurbhavettatra hyādhivyādhyahibhīstathā
kalyāṇāni sadā tatra prasādātsanti vai hareḥ || 81 ||
varddhante sampadassarvāstathā viṣṇuprasādataḥ || 82 ||
iti śrībṛhannāradīyapurāṇe bṛhadupākhyāne uttarabhāge vasumohinīsaṃvāde badarikāśramamāhātmyaṃ nāma saptaṣaṣṭitamo'dhyāyaḥ || 67 ||

iti badarikāśramamahātmyaṃ samāptam

atha kāmodāmāhātmyam

<2.68>

athāṣṭaṣaṣṭitamo'dhyāyaḥ
mohinyuvāca -
kāmodāyāstu māhātmtyaṃ brūhi me dvijasattama
yacchrutvāhaṃ tava mukhātprasannā syāṃ kṛtārthavat || 1 ||
vasuruvāca -
śṛṇu devi pravakṣyāmi kāmodākhyānakaṃ śubham
yacchrutvā sarvapāpebhyo mucyate nātra saṃśayaḥ || 2 ||
kāmodākhyaṃ puraṃ devi gaṅgātīre vyavasthitam
kāmodā yatra vartaṃte sārddhaṃ devairharipriyāḥ || 3 ||
yadā surāsurairdaivi mathitaḥ kṣīrasāgaraḥ
kāmodā sā tadotpannā kanyāratnacatuṣṭaye || 4 ||
kanyā ramākhyā prathamā dvitīyā vāruṇī smṛtā
kāmodākhyā tṛtīyā tu caturthī tu varābhidhā || 5 ||
tatra kanyātrayaṃ prāptaṃ viṣṇunā prabhaviṣṇunā
vāruṇī tvasurairnītā viṣṇudevājñayā sati || 6 ||
tataḥ prabhṛti lakṣmīstu viṣṇorvakṣaḥsthale sthitā
babhūva viṣṇupatnī sā sapatnīrahitā śubhe || 7 ||
bhaviṣyakāryaṃ vijñāya devā viṣṇusamājñayā
kāmodākhye pure devīṃ kāmodāṃ pūjayanti hi || 8 ||
sā tatra vartate nityaṃ viṣṇusaṃyogakāmyayā
bhāryātvaṃ bhāvataḥ prāptā viṣṇudhyānaparāyaṇā || 9 ||
sa tatra bhāvagamyo vai viṣṇuḥ sarvagato mahān
anayāpi tayā nityaṃ vartate tatsamīpataḥ || 10 ||
sa devairvāsurairdevi munibhirmānavaistathā
alakṣyadeho viśvātmā vartate dhyānagocaraḥ || 11 ||
dhyānenaiva prapaśyanti devāśca munayo vibhum
kāmodā sā mahābhāgā yadā hasati mohini || 12 ||
harṣeṇa tu samādiṣṭā tadāśrūṇi patanti ca
ānandāśrūṇi gaṅgāyāṃ patitāni sureśvari || 13 ||
kāmodākhyāni padmāni tāni tatra bhavanti ca
pītāni ca sugandhīni mahāmodapradāni ca || 14 ||
yastu bhāgyavaśāllabdhvā tāni taiḥ pūjayecchivam
sa labhedvāñchitānkāmānityājñā pārameśvarī || 15 ||
duḥkhajāni tathāśrūṇi kadācitprapatanti hi
tebhyaśca tāni padmāni vigandhīnyudbhavanti ca || 16 ||
taistu yaḥ pūjayeddevaṃ śaṅkaraṃ lokaśaṅkaram
sa yujyetākhilairdukhaiḥ pūrvapāpairvimohitaḥ || 17 ||
gaṅgādvārādupari ca daśayojanake sthitam
kāmodaṃ tatra varṣaikaṃ yo japeddvādaśākṣaram || 18 ||
varṣāṃte caitramāsasya dvādaśyāṃ vidhinandini
vāsantīṃ ca śriyaṃ dṛṣṭvā sā haseddharṣataḥ sadā || 19 ||
tāni padmāni sa labhennānyadā ko'pi karhicit
tatra yaḥ snāti manuje viṣṇubhaktiparāyaṇaḥ || 20 ||
dhyātvā puraṃ ca kāmodaṃ sa bhavedviṣṇuvallabhaḥ
devātānāṃ pitṝṇāṃ ca vallabho nātra saṃśayaḥ || 21 ||
yo dvādaśa samāstatra tiṣṭhejjapaparāyaṇaḥ
salabheddarśanaṃ sākṣātkāmodāyāḥ śubhānane || 22 ||
yaṃ yaṃ cintayate kāmaṃ tatra tīrthe naraḥ śuciḥ
snānamātreṇa labhate taṃ tamaihikamaṅgane || 23 ||
etaddhi paramaṃ tīrthaṃ labhyaṃ bhāgyavaśādbhavet
himātyayādage bhadre durgamaṃ vikaṭasthalam || 24 ||
etatte sarvamākhyātaṃ kāmodākhyānakaṃ śubham
yaḥ śṛṇoti naro bhaktyā so'pi pāpaiḥ pramucyate || 25 ||
iti śrībṛhannāradīyapurāṇe bṛhadupākhyāne uttarabhāge vasumohinīsaṃvāde kāmodākhyānaṃ nāmāṣṭaṣaṣṭitamo'dhyāyaḥ || 68 ||

iti kāmodāmāhātmyaṃ samāptam

atha siddhanāthacaritrasahitaṃ kāmākṣāmāhātmyaṃ prārabhyate

<2.69>

athaikonasaptatitamo'dhyāyaḥ
mohinyuvāca -
śrutaṃ kāmodakākhyānaṃ pāpaghnaṃ puṇyadaṃ nṛṇām
sāmprataṃ śrotumicchāmi kāmākṣāyāḥ phalaṃ dvija || 1 ||
vasuruvāca -
kāmākṣā paramā devī pūrvasyāṃ diśi saṃsthitā
sāgarānūpataṭagā kalau siddhipradā nṛṇām || 2 ||
yastatra gatvā kāmākṣāṃ sampūjya niyatāśanaḥ
tiṣṭhedekāṃ niśāṃ bhadre sa paśyettāṃ dṛḍhāsanaḥ || 3 ||
sā devī bhīmarūpeṇa yāti sandarśanaṃ nṛṇām
tāṃ dṛṣṭvā na caledyo vai sa siddhiṃ vāñchitāṃ labhet || 4 ||
yastu dṛṣṭvā sureśānīṃ kāmākṣāṃ bhīmarūpiṇīm
āsanāccalitaḥ sadyaḥ sa vikṣipto bhaveddhruvam || 5 ||
tatrāste pārvatīputraḥ siddhanātho varānane
ugre tapasi lokaiḥ sa prekṣyate na kadācana || 6 ||
kṛtatretādvāpareṣu pratyakṣaṃ dṛśyate'khilaiḥ
kalāvantarhitastiṣṭhedyāvatpādaḥ kalervrajet || 7 ||
kaleḥ pāde gate caikasminghore ca dharātale
sa vai pratyakṣatāṃ prāpya sādhayedakhilaṃ janam || 8 ||
mohanādyairupāyaistu mlecchaprāyāñjanāṃstadā
kṛtvā vaśe mahābhāge gamayettripadaṃ kaleḥ || 9 ||
yastatra gatvā siddheśaṃ bhaktibhāvasamanvitaḥ
cintayedvarṣamātraṃ tu kāmākṣāṃ nityadārcayan || 10 ||
sa labheddarśanaṃ svapne darśanānte samāhitaḥ
sūcitāṃ tena siddhiṃ sa labdhvā siddho bhavedbhuvi || 11 ||
vicaretsarvalokānāṃ kāmanāḥ pūrayañśubhe
trilokyāṃ yāni vastūni tāni saṅkarṣayedvarāt || 12 ||
sa matsyanāthaḥ kila tatra saṃstho vijñānapāraṅgama eva bhadre
cacāra lokābhimataṃ vitanvaṃstapo'tighoraṃ na ca yāti dṛṣṭim || 13 ||
yugānyanekāni purā bhramitvā lokānsamagrānahateṣṭagatyā
tapasthito'dyāsti mahānubhāvo na kālavegena śubhe'bhibhūtaḥ || 14 ||
gaṇḍāntajātastu purābhave'bhūddvijasya kasyāpi sutaḥ subhadre
sa jātamātraḥ kila puṣkarākhye dvīpe'sya pitrā hyudadhau visṛṣṭaḥ || 15 ||
prakṣiptamātraṃ kila tatra bālaṃ matsyo'grasītko'pi vidherniyogāt
tatra sthito'nekayugāni so'bhūtkālasya gatyā hyajarāmarāṅgaḥ || 16 ||
tataḥ kadācitpriyayā pradiṣṭo maheśvaraḥ sārddhamagaprasūtyā
tattvopadeśāya jagāma bhadre sa lokalokācalamaprameyaḥ || 17 ||
tatsaumyaśṛṅge maṇibhiḥ pradīpte sthitvā kṣaṇārddhaṃ harimagnacetāḥ
devīmumāṃ sampratibodhya śaktyā tālatrayeṇāpyabhibhūya sattvān || 18 ||
uvāca tattvaṃ surahasyabhūtaṃ yaddvādaśārṇārthanijasvarūpam
tatastu sā śailasutā maheśaṃ mārāntakaṃ yāvadabhipraṇamya || 19 ||
ajñāya tattvaṃ samavasthitā'bhūttāvatsa matsyastu mahārṇavasthaḥ
drutaṃ samutplutya jagāma śṛṅgaṃ yo viprabālo hyudare sthito'sya
sa tattvasiddho'khilabandhamuktaḥ || 20 ||
nirgamya matsyodarataḥ śubhāsye namaḥ pracakre bhavayoḥ purastāt
vijñātatattvo'pi maheśvarastaṃ papraccha tadgarbhagaternidānam || 21 ||
sa varṇayāmāsa yathārthameva tayoḥ puraḥ sarvamapi pravṛttam
ākarṇya tadvṛttamanuprasannā somā maheśānumatiṃ ca kṛtvā || 22 ||
taṃ kalpayāmāsa sutaṃ śubhāṅge sotsaṅga āsthāpya cucumba vaktram
suto mamāyaṃ kila matsyanātho vijñātatattvo'khilasiddhanāthaḥ || 23 ||
nijecchayā samprati yātu lokānkīrtiṃ vitanvansukhamāvayośca
tataḥ prabhṛtyeṣa suto'mbikāyā lokānsamagrānpravihṛtya kāmam || 24 ||
tatsiddhapīṭhaṃ samavāpya tatra tapasyupādiṣṭa ivāsthito'sti
taṃ siddhanāthaṃ manasā vicintya naro bhavetsiddhasamastakāmaḥ || 25 ||
saṃprāpya vidyāṃ nijavākyavāhe nimajjayetpaṇḍitavargajātam
etāṃ kathāṃ tasya jagatpavitrāṃ śṛṇoti yaḥ karṇapathaprayātām || 26 ||
sa cābhikāmaṃ samavāpya bhūmau svargaṃ prayātyeva surārcitāṅighraḥ
etanmayā te kathitaṃ sunetre śrīsiddhanāthasya caritrayuktam
kāmākṣamāhātmyamaghaghnamādyaṃ bhūyo'pi kiṃ te pravadāmi bhadre || 27 ||
iti śrībṛhannāradīyapurāṇe bṛhadupākhyāne uttarabhāge vasumohinīsaṃvāde siddhanāthacaritrayuktaṃ kāmākṣāmāhātmyaṃ nāmaikonasaptatitamo'dhyāyaḥ || 69 ||

iti siddhanāthacaritrasahitaṃ kāmākṣāmāhātmyaṃ samāptam

atha prabhāsamāhātmyaṃ prārabhyate

<2.70>

atha saptatitamo'dhyāyaḥ
mohinyuvāca -
prabhāsasya tu māhātmyaṃ vada me dvijasattama
yacchrutvāhaṃ prasannātmā dhanyā syāṃ tvatprasādataḥ || 1 ||
vasuruvāca -
śṛṇu devi pravakṣyāmi prabhāsākhyaṃ supuṇyadam
tīrthaṃ pāpaharaṃ nṝṇāṃ bhuktimuktipradāyakam || 2 ||
yasminnasaṅkhyatīrthāni vidyante vidhinandini
someśo yatra viśveśo bhagavān girijāpatiḥ || 3 ||
snātvā prabhāsake tīrthe somanāthaṃ prapūjya ca
naro muktimavāpnoti satyametanmayoditam || 4 ||
yojanānāṃ daśa dve ca prabhāsaparimaṇḍalam
madhye'sya pīṭhikā proktā pañcayojanavistṛtā || 5 ||
gocarmamātraṃ tanmadhye tīrthaṃ kailāsato'dhikam
arkasthalaṃ tatra puṇyaṃ tīrthamanyatsuśobhanam || 6 ||
siddheśvarādiliṅgāni yatra santi sahasraśaḥ
yatra snātvā naro bhaktyā santarpya pitṛdevatāḥ || 7 ||
liṅgāni pūjayitvā ca yāti rudra salokatām
agnitīrthaṃ tathānyacca sāgarasya taṭe sthitam || 8 ||
tatra snātvā naro devi vahnilokamavāpnuyāt
tatra devaṃ kaparddīśaṃ sopavāsaḥ prapūjya ca || 9 ||
śivalokamavāpnoti bhuktvā bhogānihepsitān
kedāreśaṃ tato gatvā samabhyarcya vidhānataḥ || 10 ||
svargatiṃ samavāpnoti vimānena surārcitaḥ
bhīmeśaṃ bhairaveśaṃ ca caṇḍīśaṃ bhāskareśvaram || 11 ||
aṅgāreśaṃ ca gurvīśaṃ someśaṃ bhṛgujeśvaram
śanirāhuśikhīśāṃśca kramādgaccheccaturdaśa || 12 ||
bhaktyā pṛthak pṛthak teṣāṃ pūjāṃ kṛtvā vidhānavit
śivasālokyamāpnoti nigrahānugrahe kṣamaḥ || 13 ||
siddheśvarādipañcānyaliṅgāni vidhinandini
samarcya labhate siddhimaihikāmuṣmikīṃ naraḥ || 14 ||
varārohāmajāpālāṃ maṅgalāṃ laliteśvarīm
sampūjya kramataścaitā vipāpo jāyate naraḥ || 15 ||
lakṣmīśvaraṃ bāḍaveśamarghyeśaṃ kāmakeśvaram
samabhyarcya naro bhaktyā sākṣāllokeśatāṃ vrajet || 16 ||
gaurītapovanaṃ prāpya gaurīśavaruṇeśvarau
uṣeśvaraṃ ca sampūjya naraḥ svargatimāpnuyāt || 17 ||
gaṇeśaṃ ca kumāreśaṃ svākakeśakuleśvarau
uttaṅkeśaṃ ca vahnīśaṃ gautamaṃ daityasūdanam || 18 ||
samabhyarcya vidhānena na naro durgatiṃ vrajet
cakratīrthaṃ tataḥ prāpya tatra snātvā vidhānataḥ || 19 ||
gaurīdevīṃ samabhyarcya naro'bhilaṣitaṃ labhet
sannihatyāhvayaṃ tīrthaṃ prāpya tatra varānane || 20 ||
snātvā santarpya devādīnsannihatyāphalaṃ labhet
athaikādaśa liṅgāni bhūteśādīni yo'cayet || 21 ||
sa labdhveha varānbhogānante rudra padaṃ vrajet
ādinārāyaṇaṃ devaṃ samabhyarcya narottamaḥ || 22 ||
mokṣabhāgī bhaveddevi nātra kāryā vicāraṇā
tataścakradharaṃ prāpya pūjayedyo vidhānataḥ || 23 ||
sa tu śatruṃ vinirjitya bhogānuccāvacām̐ llabhet
sāmbādityaṃ tataḥ prāpya snātvā niyamapūrvakam || 24 ||
nīrogo dhanadhānyāḍhyo jāyate mānavo bhuvi
tatastu manujaḥ prāpya devīṃ kaṇṭakaśodhinīm || 25 ||
mahiṣaghnīṃ ca sampūjya nirbhayo jāyate naraḥ
kapālīśaṃ ca koṭīśaṃ samabhyarcya narottamaḥ || 26 ||
susaubhāgyo bhavedevaṃ madhyayātrāṃ samāpayet
bālabrahmābhidhaṃ paścātprāpya martyo nareśvari || 27 ||
jāyate bhuktimuktīśaḥ sarvadevaprapūjitaḥ
narakeśaṃ tataḥ prāpya saṃvarteśaṃ nidhīśvaram || 28 ||
balabhadre śvaraṃ prārcya jāyate bhuktimuktimān
gaṅgāgaṇapatiṃ prāpya samabhyarcya vidhānataḥ || 29 ||
labhate vāñchitānkāmāniha loke paratra ca
tato jāmbavatīṃ prāpya nadīṃ bhaktyā samāhitaḥ || 30 ||
snātvā surādīnabhyarcya kṛtakṛtyo bhavennaraḥ
pāṇḍukūpe tataḥ snātvā pāṇḍaveśvaramarcayet || 31 ||
sa naraḥ svargamāyāti krīḍate nandanādiṣu
śatamedhaṃ lakṣamedhaṃ koṭimedhamanukramāt || 32 ||
liṅgatrayaṃ samabhyarcya modate divi devavat
durvāsādityakaṃ dṛṣṭvā sampūjya ca vidhānataḥ || 33 ||
aśvamedhasya yajñasya phalaṃ prāpnotyasaṃśayam
yādavasthalamāsādya varṣeśaṃ prārcya mānavaḥ || 34 ||
labhate vāñchitāṃ siddhiṃ devarājena satkṛtaḥ
hiraṇyāsaṅgame snātvā dadyāddhemarathaṃ dvije || 35 ||
śivamuddiśya yo bhaktyā sa lokānakṣayām̐ llabhet
nagarārkaṃ tataḥ prārcya sūryalokamavāpnuyāt || 36 ||
nagarādityapārśve tu balakṛṣṇau subhadri kām
dṛṣṭvā sampūjya vidhinā kṛṣṇasāyujyamāpnuyāt || 37 ||
kumārikāṃ tataḥ prāpya samabhyarcya vidhānataḥ
labhate vāṃchitānkāmāñjayecchakraṃ na saṃśayaḥ || 38 ||
kṣetrapālaṃ tato'bhyarcya sarvānkāmānavāpnuyāt
brahmeśvaraṃ ca saṃpūjya sarasvatyāstaṭe sthitam || 39 ||
sarvapāpavinirmukto brahmaloke mahīyate
piṅgalākhyāṃ nadīṃ prāpya snātvā tatra surādikān || 40 ||
santarpya śrāddhakṛnmartyo neha bhūyo'bhijāyate
saṅgameśaṃ samabhyarcya na naro durgatiṃ vrajet || 41 ||
samprārcya śaṅkarādityaṃ ghaṭeśaṃ ca maheśvaram
mānavaḥ sakalānkāmānprāpnuyānnātra saṃśayaḥ || 42 ||
ṛṣitīrthaṃ tataḥ prāpya snātvā niyatamānasaḥ
ṛṣīṃstatra samabhyarcya sarvatīrthaphalaṃ labhet || 43 ||
nandādityaṃ tataḥ prārcya mucyate sarvarogataḥ
tritakūpaṃ tataḥ prāpya snātvā yāti divaṃ naraḥ || 44 ||
śaśopāne naraḥ snātvā devānpaśyati mohini
vāñchitāṃśca labhetkāmānsatyaṃ satyaṃ mayoditam || 45 ||
parṇādityaṃ naro dṛṣṭvā nīrogo bhogavānbhavet
tato nyaṅkumatīṃ prāpya snātvā tatra vidhānataḥ || 46 ||
siddheśvaraṃ samarcyātra aṇimādikasiddhibhāk
vārāhasvāminaṃ dṛṣṭvā mucyate bhavasāgarāt || 47 ||
chāyāliṅgaṃ samabhyarcya mucyate sarvapātakaiḥ
gulphaṃ dṛṣṭvā naro'bhyarcya cāndrā yaṇaphalaṃ labhet || 48 ||
devīṃ kanakanandāṃ ca samabhyarcya naraḥ sati
sarvānkāmānavāpnoti dehānte svargatiṃ labhet || 49 ||
kuntīśvaraṃ samabhyarcya mucyate sarvapātakaiḥ
gaṅgeśvaraṃ samabhyarcya gaṅgāyāṃ manujaḥ plutaḥ || 50 ||
trividhebhyo'pi pāpebhyo mucyate nātra saṃśayaḥ
camasodbhedake snātvā piṇḍadānaṃ karoti yaḥ || 51 ||
gayākoṭiguṇaṃ puṇyaṃ sa labhennātra saṃśayaḥ
tatastu vidhije gatvā vidurāśramamuttamam || 52 ||
trigaṃ tribhuvaneśaṃ ca sampūjyātra sukhī bhavet
maṅkaṇeśvaramabhyarcya labhate sadgatiṃ naraḥ || 53 ||
traipuraṃ ca triliṅgaṃ tu prārcya pāpaiḥ pramucyate
ṣaṇḍatīrthaṃ tataḥ prāpya snātvā svarṇaprado naraḥ || 54 ||
sarvapāpaviśuddhātmā śaivaṃ padamavāpnuyāt
sūryaprācyāṃ naraḥ snātvā vipāpmā bhogāvānbhavet || 55 ||
trilocane naraḥ snātvā rudra lokamavāpnuyāt
devikāyāmumānāthaṃ samarcya manujottamaḥ || 56 ||
labhate vāñchitānkāmāndehānte svargamāpnuyāt
bhūdvāraṃ tu samabhyarcya labhate vāñchitaṃ phalam || 57 ||
śūlasthāne tu vālmīkiṃ namaskṛtya kavirbhavet
cyavanārkaṃ tataḥ prārcya sarvakāmasamṛddhimān || 58 ||
cyavaneśārcanānmartyaḥ śivasyānucaro bhavet
pajāpāleśamabhyarcya dhanadhānyānvito bhavet || 59 ||
bālārkapūjako martyo vidyāvāndhanavān bhavet
kuberasthānake snātvā nidhiṃ prāpnoti niścitam || 60 ||
ṛṣitoyanadīṃ prāpya snātvā tatra naraḥ śuciḥ
datvā suvarṇaṃ viprāya mucyate sarvapātakaiḥ || 61 ||
saṅgāleśvaramabhyarcya rudra loke mahīyate
nāradādityamabhyarcya trikālajñānavānbhavet || 62 ||
tato nārāyaṇaṃ prārcya muktibhāgī naro bhaveta
taptakuṇḍodake snātvā mūlacaṇḍīśamarcayet || 63 ||
sarvapāpavinirmukto vāñchitārthaṃ labhennaraḥ
vināyakaṃ caturvaktramabhyarcyāpnoti kāmitam || 64 ||
kalambeśvaramabhyarcya dhanadhānyasamṛddhimān
gopālasvāmipūjāto gomānvai dhanavānkaviḥ || 65 ||
bakulasvāmino'bhyarcā nṛṇāṃ svargatidāyinī
sampūjya mārutāṃ devīṃ sarvakāmaphalaṃ labhet || 66 ||
kṣemādityārcanānmartyaḥ kṣemī siddhārthasatyabhāk
unnatākhyaṃ vighnarājaṃ prārcya vighnairna hanyate || 67 ||
jalasvāmī kālameghaḥ pūjitau sarvasiddhidau
rukmiṇī pūjitā devī vāñchitārthapradā nṛṇām || 68 ||
durvāseśaṃ ca piṅgeśaṃ prārcya pāpairvimucyate
bhadrā yāḥ saṅgame snātvā naro bhadrā ṇi paśyati || 69 ||
śaṅkhāvarte naraḥ snātvā sarvasiddhīśvaro bhavet
mokṣatīrthe naraḥ snātvā bhavenmukto bhavārṇavāt || 70 ||
goṣpadasnānamātreṇa sarvasaukhyamavāpnuyāt
nārāyaṇagṛhe gatvā naro bhūyo na śocati || 71 ||
jāleśvarārcanātpuṃsāṃ siddhayaḥ syurabhīpsitāḥ
snāto huṅkārakūpe tu garbhavāsaṃ na cāpnuyāt || 72 ||
tathā caṇḍīśamabhyarcya sarvatīrthaphalaṃ labhet
vighneśamāśāpuragaṃ prārcya vighnaṃ na cāpnuyāt || 73 ||
kalākuṇḍāpluto martyo muktibhāgī na saṃśayaḥ
kapileśaṃ samabhyarcya kapilāyūthamāpnuyāt || 74 ||
jaradgaveśvaraṃ prārcya jarasā nābhibhūyate
naleśvarārcako bhogī karkkoṭeśārcako dhanī || 75 ||
hāṭakeśvarapūjātaḥ pūryante sarvakāmanāḥ
nāradeśārcako bhaktiṃ labhedviṣṇau ca śaṅkare || 76 ||
svargārho jāyate'bhyarcya devīṃ mantravibhūṣaṇām
durgakūṭaṃ gaṇapatiṃ pūjayitvā sukhī bhavet || 77 ||
dhanadhānyayuto bhūyātpūjayankauraveśvarīm
suparṇelāṃ bhairavīṃ ca pūjayitvā sukhī bhavet || 78 ||
mallatīrthe naraḥ snātvā mucyate sarvakilbiṣaiḥ
kardamāle naraḥ snātvā pātakairviprayujyate || 79 ||
guptasomeśvaraṃ dṛṣṭvā na bhūyo'hati śocitum
bahusvarṇeśvaraṃ dṛṣṭvā svargatiṃ samavāpnuyāt || 80 ||
śṛṅgeśvarārcako martyo na duḥkhairabhibhūyate
tīrthe nārāyaṇe snātvā muktimāpnoti mānavaḥ || 81 ||
mārkaṇḍeśvaramabhyarcya dīrghāyurjāyate naraḥ
tathā koṭīhrade snātvābhyarcya koṭīśvaraṃ sukhī || 82 ||
siddhasthāne punaḥ snātvā tatra liṅgāni pūjayet
asaṅkhyātāni yo martyaḥ sa siddho jāyate bhuvi || 83 ||
dāmodaragṛhaṃ dṛṣṭvā sukhamāpnotyanuttamam
vastrāpathaṃ prabhāsasya nābhisthāne sthitaṃ śubhe || 84 ||
tatrābhyarcya bhavaṃ sākṣādbhavedbhavasamaḥ svayam
dāmodare svarṇarekhā brahmakuṇḍaṃ ca raivate || 85 ||
kuntīśa ujjayante tu bhīmeśaśca mahāprabhaḥ
mṛgīkuṇḍaṃ ca sarvasvaṃ kṣetre vastrāpathe smṛtam || 86 ||
eteṣu kramaśaḥ snātvā devānabhyarcya yatnataḥ
pitṝnsantarpya toyena sarvatīrthaphalaṃ labhet || 87 ||
dunnābile naraḥ snātvā bhuktabhogo divaṃ vrajet
gaṅgeśvaraṃ tato'bhyarcya gaṅgāsnānaphalaṃ labhet || 88 ||
girau raivatake devi santi tīrthānyanekaśaḥ
teṣu snātvā naro bhaktyā brahmaviṣṇumaheśvarān || 89 ||
indrā dilokapānprārcya bhuktiṃ muktiṃ ca vindati
etānyuddeśatastīrthānyuktāni tava sundari || 90 ||
avāntarāṇyanantāni tāni vaktuṃ na śakyate
ekaikasyāpi tīrthasya santi vistarataḥ kathāḥ || 91 ||
ataḥ saṃkṣipya gaditaṃ mayā puṇyaṃ prabhāsajam
na prabhāsasamaṃ tīrthaṃ triṣu lokeṣu mohini || 92 ||
yatra snāto'pi manujaḥ svargiṇā sparddhate śubhe
māhātmyaṃ ca prabhāsasya likhitaṃ vartate gṛhe || 93 ||
yatra tatra na bhītiḥ syādbhūtacaurāhiśatrujā
yaḥ śṛṇoti naro bhaktyā śrāvayedvā samāhitaḥ || 94 ||
prabhāsatīrthamāhātmyaṃ so'pi sadgatimāpnuyāt || 95 ||
iti śrībṛhannāradīyapurāṇe bṛhadupākhyāne uttarabhāge vasumohinīsaṃvāde prabhāsatīrthamāhātmyaṃ nāma saptatitamo'dhyāyaḥ || 70 ||

iti prabhāsakṣetramāhātmyaṃ samāptam

atha puṣkaramāhātmyaṃ prārabhyate

<2.71>

athaikasaptatitamo'yāyaḥ
mohinyuvāca -
śrutaṃ prabhāsamāhātmyaṃ dvijaśreṣṭhātipuṇyadam
adhunā śrotumicchāmi māhātmyaṃ puṣkarodbhavam || 1 ||
yadādyaṃ sarvatīrthānāṃ pavitraṃ pāpanāśanam
matpituryajñasadanaṃ tanmamākhyāhi vistarāt || 2 ||
vasuruvāca -
śṛṇu bhadre pravakṣyāmi narāṇāṃ kāmadaṃ sadā
puṇyaṃ puṣkaramāhātmyaṃ bahutīrthasamanvitam || 3 ||
viṣṇunā sahitā devā yatrendrā dyā vyavasthitāḥ
gajavaktraḥ kumāraśca raivataśca divākaraḥ || 4 ||
śivadūtī tathā devī kṣetrakṣemakarī sadā
jyeṣṭhe tu puṣkarāraṇye yastiṣṭhati nirudyamī || 5 ||
aṣṭāṅgayogajaṃ puṇyaṃ sa labhennātra saṃśayaḥ
nātaḥ parataraṃ kiñcitkṣetramastīha bhūtale || 6 ||
tasmātsarvaprayatnena sevyametannarottamaiḥ
brāhmaṇāḥ kṣattriyā vaiśyāḥ śūdrā vā kṣetravāsinaḥ || 7 ||
sarvātmanā brahmabhaktā bhūtānugrahakāriṇaḥ
te yānti brahmaṇo lokaṃ yatra gacchanti yoginaḥ || 8 ||
yo naraḥ sarvatīrtheṣu yatphalaṃ labhate plutaḥ
tatsarvaṃ sa labhenmartyo jyeṣṭhakuṇḍe sakṛtplutaḥ || 9 ||
puṣkarāraṇyamāsādya yatra prācī sarasvatī
matiḥ smṛtirdayā prajñā medhā buddhisamāhvayā || 10 ||
tatrasthaṃ tajjalaṃ ye'pi prapaśyanti taṭe sthitāḥ
te'pyaśvamedhayajñasya phalaṃ prāpya vrajanti kam || 11 ||
trīṇi śṛṅgāṇi śubhrāṇi trīṇi prasravaṇāni ca
jyeṣṭhamadhyakaniṣṭhāni trīṇi tatra sarāṃsi ca || 12 ||
tatrānyatsumahattīrthaṃ nandā nāma sarasvatī
yatra paścimadiśyāste saraso yojane sati || 13 ||
tatra snātvā vidhānena gāṃ ca dattvā payasvinīm
viprāya vedaviduṣe vrajedbrahmakṣayaṃ naraḥ || 14 ||
koṭitīrthaṃ tathātrāsti yatrarṣikoṭirāgatā
tatra snātvā dvijānprārcya mucyate sarvapātakaiḥ || 15 ||
agastyāśramamāsādya snātvā sampūjya kumbhajam
dīrghāyurjāyate bhogī dehānte svargatiṃ labhet || 16 ||
saptarṣīṇāṃ tathā prāpya tatrāśramamananyadhīḥ
snātvā sampūjya bhaktyā tām̐ llabhate tatsalokatām || 17 ||
manūnāmāśrame snātvā pūjāmāpnoti sarvataḥ
gaṅgāvinirgame snātvā gaṅgāsnānaphalaṃ labhet || 18 ||
jyeṣṭhe tu puṣkare snātvā gāṃ ca dattvā dvijātaye
bhuktveha bhogānakhilānbrahmaloke mahīyate || 19 ||
madhyame puṣkare snātvā brāhmaṇāya mahīṃ dadat
viṣṇulokamavāpnoti vimānavaramāsthitaḥ || 20 ||
kaniṣṭhe tu naraḥ snātvā dattvā viprāya kāñcanam
sarvānkāmānavāpyānte rudra loke mahīyate || 21 ||
tato viṣṇupade snātvā dattvā kiñciddvijātaye
labhate sakalānkāmān viṣṇoścaiva prasādataḥ || 22 ||
nāgatīrthe tataḥ snātvā nāgānabhyarcya mānavāḥ
dattvā dānaṃ dvijātibhyo modate tridive yugam || 23 ||
piśācatīrthake snātvā dattvā viprāya bhojanam
na kadācitpiśācatvaṃ prāpnoti tridivaṃ vrajet || 24 ||
śivadūtīhrade snātvā tatra śambhuṃ samarcya ca
viprānsambhojya miṣṭānnaṃ svargaloke mahīyate || 25 ||
ākāśapuṣkare snātvā mantrayogaphalaprade
naro muktimavāpnoti satyaṃ satyaṃ tavoditam || 26 ||
āpohiṣṭhādibhirmantrairākāśasthaṃ vicintya tat
snāyādyaḥ puṣkarāraṇye sa labhecchāśvataṃ padam || 27 ||
āgneyaṃ tu yadā ṛkṣaṃ kārtikyāṃ bhavati kvacit
mahatī sā tithistatra snānamākāśapuṣkare || 28 ||
yāmyarkṣayoge kārtikyāṃ madhyame snānakṛnnaraḥ
tatphalaṃ samavāpnoti yannabhaḥpuṣkarodbhavam || 29 ||
prājāpatyarkṣayuktāyāṃ kārtikyāṃ tu kaniṣṭhake
snātastatphalamāpnoti yadviyatpuṣkarodbhavam || 30 ||
nandā cārke gurau some yāmye vahnau vidhau kramāt
yadā nabhaḥ puṣkarotthaṃ tadā syātsakalaṃ phalam || 31 ||
viśākhāyāṃ yadā bhānuḥ kṛttikāyāṃ ca candra māḥ
tadā nabhaḥpuṣkarākhye yoge snāto divaṃ vrajet || 32 ||
antarikṣāvatīrṇe'smistīrthe paitāmahe śubhe
kurvanti ye narāḥ snānaṃ teṣāṃ lokā mahodayāḥ || 33 ||
pañcasrotaḥsarasvatyāṃ puṣkarāraṇyake sati
kṛtāni munibhiḥ siddhaistīrthānyāyatanāni ca || 34 ||
teṣu sarveṣu vijñeyā dharmahetuḥ sarasvatī
hāṭakakṣitigaurīṇāṃ dānaṃ teṣu mahāphalam || 35 ||
dhānyānyapi tilāṃścāpi yo'tra dadyāddvijottame
sa naro labhate bhogānihāmutra parāṃ gatim || 36 ||
saṅge gaṅgāsarasvatyoryaḥ snātvā pūjayeddvijān
sa prāpnoti gatiṃ cāgryāṃ bhuktvā bhogānihepsitān || 37 ||
aviyogābhidhāyāṃ tu vāpyāṃ snātvā naraḥ sati
piṇḍaṃ dattvā vidhānena pitṝnnayati vai divam || 38 ||
tathā saubhāgyakūpe tu snātvā santarpya pūrvajān
sadgatiṃ labhate martyo saubhāgyaṃ cātulaṃ bhuvi || 39 ||
maryādā parvatau dvau tu snātvā spṛṣṭvā samarcya ca
vāñchitām̐ llabhate lokānsāṅgayātrāphalaṃ tathā || 40 ||
ajagandhaṃ śivaṃ prāpya samabhyarcya vidhānataḥ
labhate vāñchitānkāmāniha loke paratra ca || 41 ||
saraso dakṣiṇe bhāge sāvitrīṃ parvatopari
saṃsthitāṃ yaḥ samabhyarcetso'pi vedasya tattvavit || 42 ||
vārāhasya nṛsiṃhasya brahmaṇaśca harestathā
śivasya cāpi sūryasya somasya ca guhasya ca || 43 ||
pārvatyā jvalanasyāpi tatra tīrthāni mohini
pṛthak teṣu mahābhāge naraḥ snātvā samāhitaḥ || 44 ||
dānaṃ ca dattvā viprebhyo labhate gatimuttamām
puṣkare durlabhaṃ snānaṃ puṣkare durlabhaṃ tapaḥ || 45 ||
puṣkare durlabhaṃ dānaṃ puṣkare durlabhā sthitiḥ
śatayojanasaṃstho'pi snānakāle tu yo naraḥ || 46 ||
puṣkaraṃ cintayedbhaktyā sa tatsnānaphalaṃ labhet
maṅkaṇādyāstapaḥsiddhāḥ sevante puṣkaraṃ mudā || 47 ||
yatra tatra gato bhāgyātkāmitāṃ siddhimāpnuyāt
puṣkareti ca nāmāpi yenoktaṃ vidhinandini || 48 ||
puṣkarasnānajaṃ puṇyaṃ labhetso'pi na saṃśayaḥ
yaḥ śṛṇoti naro bhaktyā māhātmyaṃ puṣkarasya ca || 49 ||
so'pi snānaphalaṃ prāpya modate divi devavat || 50 ||
iti śrībṛhannāradīyapurāṇottarabhāge vasumohinīsaṃvāde puṣkaramāhātmyaṃ nāmaikasaptatitamo'dhyāyaḥ || 71 ||

iti puṣkaramāhātmyaṃ samāptam

atha gautamāśramamāhātmyaṃ prārabhyate

<2.72>

atha dvisaptatitamo'dhyāyaḥ
mohinyuvāca -
śrutaṃ puṣkaramāhātmyaṃ vaso pāpapraṇāśanam
gautamāśramamāhātmyamadhunā kīrtaya prabho || 1 ||
vasuruvāca -
śṛṇu devi pravakṣyāmi gautamāśramamuttamam
yatra gatvā naro bhaktyā na bhūyo'hati yātanāḥ || 2 ||
gautamasyāśramaṃ puṇyaṃ devarṣigaṇasevitam
sarvapāpapraśamanaṃ sarvopadra vaśāntidam || 3 ||
sevate dvādaśābdaṃ yo bhaktibhāvasamanvitaḥ
sa śaivaṃ labhate dhāma yatra gatvā na śocati || 4 ||
māyādevīsuto yatra tapasyugre samāsthitaḥ
tatra godāvarī gaṅgā sarvapātakanāśinī || 5 ||
tapasyato munestasya dvādaśābdamavarṣaṇam
babhūva ghoraṃ vidhije sarvasattvakṣayaṅkaram || 6 ||
tasminnugre tu durbhikṣe kṣutkṣāmā munayo'khilāḥ
nānādeśebhya āyātā gautamasyāśramaṃ śubhe || 7 ||
cakrurvijñāpanaṃ tasya gautamasya tapasyataḥ
dehi no bhojanaṃ yena prāṇāstiṣṭhanti varṣmasu || 8 ||
evaṃ vijñāpitastaistu munibhirgautamo muniḥ
jātānukampastānāha viśvastāṃstapaso balāt || 9 ||
gautama uvāca -
tiṣṭhadhvaṃ munayaḥ sarve mamāśramasamīpataḥ
bhojanaṃ vaḥ pradāsyāmi yāvaddurbhikṣamādṛtaḥ || 10 ||
viśvāsyaivamṛṣīnsarvāngautamastapaso balāt
dadhyau prasannamanasā gaṅgāṃ sarvārthasādhinīm || 11 ||
smṛtamātrā tu sā devī tatrodbhūtā dharātalāt
tāṃ tu dṛṣṭvā munirgaṃgāṃ samplāvitadharātalām || 12 ||
prātaruptvā kṣitau śālīnpakvānmadhyāhnake'lunāt
śālyannena tatastena tānṛṣīnsamabhojayat || 13 ||
tataste munayaḥ prītā bhuktvānnaṃ tṛptimāgatāḥ
nivāsaṃ cakrire tatra gautamāśramake mudā || 14 ||
evaṃ pratidinaṃ bhadre śālibhiḥ pakvatāṃ gataiḥ
ātithyaṃ vidadhe teṣāṃ bhaktibhāvasamanvitaḥ || 15 ||
tatastasya munīndra sya dvijānbhojayato'nvaham
vyatīyāya ca durbhikṣaṃ dvādaśābdāntakālataḥ || 16 ||
tataste munayaḥ sarve subhikṣe kāla āgate
vijñāpya taṃ muniśreṣṭhaṃ jagmurdeśānsvakānpunaḥ || 17 ||
evaṃprabhāvaḥ sa munirgautamastatra mohini
tapastepe bahutithaṃ kālaṃ niyamitendri yaḥ || 18 ||
tatastattapasā tuṣṭo bhagavānambikāpatiḥ
sagaṇo darśanaṃ yāto varaṃ brūhītyuvāca ha || 19 ||
tato munivaro dṛṣṭvā devadevamupāpatim
tryambakaṃ sa namaścakre nipatya bhuvi tatpuraḥ || 20 ||
tata utthāya sahasā kṛtāñjalirupasthitaḥ
provāca dehi me bhaktiṃ pādayostava nityadā || 21 ||
mamāśramasamīpe'tra parvatopari śaṅkara
tvāmevaṃ saṃsthitaṃ paśyāmyeṣa eva varo mama || 22 ||
ityuktaḥ pārvatīnātho bhaktānāṃ vāñchitapradaḥ
tatra dattvā svasānnidhyaṃ sadyaḥ prītaṃ cakāra tam || 23 ||
tena rūpeṇa tatraiva nyavasat tryambakaḥ sati
sa giristryambakākhyastu tataḥ prabhṛti kīrtyate || 24 ||
ye tu godāvarīṃ gaṅgāṃ prāpya bhaktiyutā narāḥ
snānaṃ kurvanti subhage te syurmuktā bhavārṇavāt || 25 ||
snātvā godāvarītoye tryambakaṃ ye giristhitam
upacāraiḥ pūjayanti te syuḥ sākṣānmaheśvarāḥ || 26 ||
tryambakasya tu māhātmyaṃ saṃkṣepādvarṇitaṃ mayā
brahmāpi vistarādvaktuṃ tava tātaḥ kṣamo nahi || 27 ||
tato godāvarī yāvatsākṣāddarśanatāṃ gatā
tāvadapyāśramāḥ puṇyāstatra santi hyanekaśaḥ || 28 ||
teṣu snātvā vidhānena santarpya pitṛdevatāḥ
naro'bhilaṣitānkāmānprāpnuyānnātra saṃśayaḥ || 29 ||
prakāśā tu kvacidbhadre kvacidguptā tataḥ param
plāvayāmāsa dharaṇīṃ puṇyā godāvarī nadī || 30 ||
yatra prakaṭatāṃ yātā nṛṇāṃ bhaktyā maheśvarī
tatra tīrthaṃ mahatpuṇyaṃ snānamātrādaghāpaham || 31 ||
tataḥ pañcavaṭīṃ prāpya sā devī niyatavratā
suprakāśamanuprāptā lokānāṃ gatidāyinī || 32 ||
godāvaryāṃ pañcavaṭyāṃ yaḥ snāyānniyatavrataḥ
sa naraḥ prāpnuyātkāmānabhīṣṭānvidhinandini || 33 ||
yadā tretāyuge rāmaḥ pañcavaṭyāmupāgataḥ
sabhāryaḥ sānujastatra vasanpuṇyatarāṃ vyadhāt || 34 ||
ityetsarvamākhyātaṃ gautamāśramajaṃ śubhe
śṛṇvatāṃ paṭhatāṃ puṇyaṃ pāpaghnaṃ vāñchitapradam || 35 ||
iti śrībṛhannāradīyapurāṇottarabhāge bṛhadupākhyāne vasumohinīsaṃvāde gautamāśramamāhātmye gautamatapaścaryāprabhāvavarṇanaṃ nāma dvāsaptatitamo'dhyāyaḥ || 72 ||

iti gautamāśramamāhātmyaṃ

atha tryambakamāhātmyaṃ prārabhyate

<2.73>

atha trisaptatitamo'dhyāyaḥ
mohinyuvāca -
śrutaṃ guro tvayā proktaṃ puṇyākhyānaṃ ca gautamam
tryambakasya ca māhātmyaṃ godāpañcavaṭībhavam || 1 ||
adhunā śrotumicchāmi puṇḍarīkapurodbhavam
yathā tatra mahādevastāṇḍavaṃ nṛtyamācarat || 2 ||
tathā vada mahīdeva puṇyātpuṇyataraṃ mama
vasuruvāca -
bhaktavaśyo mahādevaḥ sadyo varada eva ca || 3 ||
prākaṭyaṃ yāti bhaktānāṃ karoti ca tadicchayā
ekadā jaiminirnāmnā vyāsaśiṣyo munīśvaraḥ || 4 ||
agniveśādibhiḥ śiṣyaiḥ sārddhaṃ tīrthānyaṭannagāt
puṇḍarīkapuraṃ sākṣāddevarājapuropamam || 5 ||
sarvato'laṅkṛtaṃ śrīmatsarvartukusumadrumaiḥ
śobhitaṃ śītalacchāyaiḥ śubhravārijalāśayaiḥ || 6 ||
nānāvayaḥkulākīrṇaiḥ suramyaiḥ kamalākaraiḥ
nārībhirapsarobhiśca nṛbhirvidyādharadyubhiḥ || 7 ||
vilasadbhavanaiḥ śubhrairvimānamiva rājate
dṛṣṭvā śobhāṃ munistasya purasya prītimānbhṛśam || 8 ||
babhūva ca manaścakre snātuṃ tatra sarovare
snigdhacchāyairdrumairyukte nānāpuṣpasugandhite || 9 ||
viśramya taruchāyāḍhye taṭe tasya kṣaṇaṃ muniḥ
snātvā nityāḥ kriyāścakre devarṣipitṛtarpaṇam || 10 ||
nirmāya pārthivaṃ liṅgaṃ vividhairupacārakaiḥ
pūjayāmāsa vidhivatpādyārghyādyairanākulaḥ || 11 ||
gandhairdhūpaistathā dīpairnaivedyairvividhairapi
puṣpaiḥ sugandhibhiḥ prārcya sthito yāvadabhūnmuniḥ || 12 ||
tāvatprasanno bhagavānsākṣātkāramupāgataḥ
tasminneva ca mṛlliṅge nānāratnaprabhodgame || 13 ||
tataḥ sa jaiminirdṛṣṭvā sākṣāddevamumāpatim
papāta daṇḍavadbhūmau punarutthāya sāñjali || 14 ||
prāha prapannārtiharaṃ harārddhāṅgaṃ hariṃ vibhum
jaiminiruvāca -
dhanyo'smi kṛtakṛtyo'smi devadeva jagatpate || 15 ||
yastvaṃ brahmādibhirdhyeyaḥ sākṣānmaddṛṣṭigocaraḥ
tataḥ prasannaḥ sa vibhurgirīśo'sya nijaṃ karam || 16 ||
śirasyādhāyāgadattaṃ brūhi putra kimicchasi
tacchrutvā vacanaṃ śambhorjaiminiḥ pratyuvāca ha || 17 ||
devaṃ sāmbaṃ savighneśaṃ sakumāraṃ vilokaye
tataḥ sāmbaḥ saputro'sya śaṅkaro darśanaṃ gataḥ || 18 ||
punaḥ prāha prasannātmā brūhi putra kimicchasi
athāsya jaiminirvīkṣya kṛpālutvaṃ jagadguroḥ || 19 ||
smayannāha samīkṣe tvāṃ tāṇḍavaṃ naṭanaṃ gatam
atha tadvāñchitaṃ kartuṃ bhagavānambikāpatiḥ || 20 ||
sasmāra pramathānsarvānnānākrīḍāviśāradān
smṛtamātrāstu te sarve nandibhṛṅgipurogamāḥ || 21 ||
samājagmuḥ prajalpantaḥ kautūhalasamanvitāḥ
savighneśakumārāmbaṃ te namaskṛtya vāgyatāḥ
tasthuḥ prāñjalayo devadevasyājñāsamīkṣakāḥ || 22 ||
tato haro harājñayā vidhāya rūpamadbhutaṃ pranartumudyato babhau vicitraveṣabhūṣaṇaḥ
vilolanāgavallarīddhakakṣaīṣadutsitānano lalāṭaśobhitendulekha ūrdhvadordhvajaḥ || 23 ||
sudṛgviliptabhasmadeharugjitenducandri ko jaṭākalāpanissaratsurāpagārdra vigrahaḥ
lalāṭalocanojjvalatkṛśānutaptaśītaguḥ sravatsudhānujīvitaiṇabhūpakṛttihuṅkṛtaḥ || 24 ||
kumāravāhakekicañcukṛṣṭanāgaphuṅkṛto galatsudhānujīvadabjayonituṇḍatuṅkṛtaḥ
phaṇiprabhītavighnarājavāhanākhucuṅkṛto mṛgendra nādabhīṣitākṣatanmahokṣabhāṅkṛtaḥ || 25 ||
muhuḥ padāmbujaprapātakampitāvanītalaḥ prakṛṣṭavādyahṛṣṭagātraromarājikaṇṭakaḥ
surāsurendra mauliratnabhāsitāṅighrapaṅkajo gaṇeśakārtikeyaśailaputrivīkṣitānanaḥ || 26 ||
pravṛddhaharṣabhaktavṛndasamyaguktasañjayaḥ pravṛttatāṇḍavairvibhurbabhau diśo'vabhāsayan || 27 ||
athānandārṇave magno dṛṣṭvā nṛtyaṃ maheśituḥ
jaiminirvedapādena stavenāstautsamāhitaḥ || 28 ||
viriñciviṣṇugiriśapraṇatāṅighrasaroruhe
jagatsūte namastubhyaṃ devi kāmpilyavāsini || 29 ||
vighneśavidhimārtaṇḍacandre ndro pendra vandita
namo gaṇapate tubhyaṃ brahmaṇāṃ brahmaṇaspate || 30 ||
umākomalahastābjasambhāvitalalāṭakam
hiraṇyakuṇḍalaṃ vaṃde kumāraṃ puṣkarasrajam || 31 ||
śivaṃ brahmādidurdarśaṃ naraḥ kaḥ stotumarhati
darśanātte stutirme sā cābhrādvṛṣṭirivājani || 32 ||
namaḥ śivāya sāmbāya namaḥ śarvāya śambhave
namo naṭāya rudrā ya sadasaspataye namaḥ || 33 ||
pādabhinnāya lokāya maulibhinnāṇḍabhittaye
bhujabhrāntadigantāya bhūtānāṃ pataye namaḥ || 34 ||
kvaṇannūpurayugmāya vilasatkṛttivāsase
phaṇīndra mekhalāyāstu paśūnāṃ pataye namaḥ || 35 ||
kālakālāya somāya yogine śūlapāṇaye
asthibhūṣāya śuddhāya jagatāṃ pataye namaḥ || 36 ||
pātre sarvasya jagato netre sarvadivaukasām
gotrāṇāṃ pataye tubhyaṃ kṣetrāṇāṃ pataye namaḥ || 37 ||
śaṅkarāya namastubhyaṃ maṅgalāya namonamaḥ
dhanānāṃ pataye tubhyamannānāṃ pataye namaḥ
ātmādhipataye tubhyaṃ namo mīḍhuṣṭamāya ca || 38 ||
aṣṭāṅgāyātihṛdyāya kliṣṭabhakteṣṭadāyine
iṣṭighnāya sutuṣṭāya puṣṭānāṃ pataye namaḥ || 39 ||
pañcabhūtādhipataye kālādhipataye namaḥ
nama ātmādhipataye diśāṃ ca pataye namaḥ || 40 ||
viśvakatre maheśāya viśvabhartre pinākine
viśvahantre'gninetrāya viśvarūpāya vai namaḥ || 41 ||
īśāna te tatpuruṣa namo ghoraya te sadā
vāmadeva namaste'stu sadyojātāya vai namaḥ || 42 ||
bhūtibhūṣāya bhaktānāṃ bhītibhaṅgapradāyine
namo bhavāya bhargāya namo rudrā ya mīḍhuṣe || 43 ||
sahasrāmbāya sāmbāya sahasrābhīṣave namaḥ
sahasrabāhave tubhyaṃ sahasrākṣāya mīḍhuṣe || 44 ||
sukapolāya somāya sulalāṭāya subhruve
sudehāya namastubhyaṃ sumṛḍīkāya mīḍhuṣe || 45 ||
bhavakleśanimittorubhayacchedakṛte sadā
namastubhyamaṣāḍhāya sahamānāya mīḍhuṣe || 46 ||
vaṃde'ha devamānandasandohaṃ lāsyasundaram
samastajagatāṃ nāthaṃ sadasaspatimadbhutam || 47 ||
sujaṅghaṃ sundaraṃ kṣaumaṃ rakṣobhūtakṣatikṣamam
yakṣeśeṣṭaṃ namāmīśamakṣaraṃ paramaṃ prabhum || 48 ||
arddhālakamavastrārddhamasthyutpaladalasrajam
arddhapuṃlakṣaṇaṃ vaṃde puruṣaṃ kṛṣṇapiṅgalam || 49 ||
sakṛtpraṇatasaṃsāramahāsāgaratārakam
praṇamāmi tamīśānaṃ jagatastasthuṣaspatim || 50 ||
dhātāraṃ jagatāmīśaṃ dātāraṃ sarvasampadām
netāraṃ marutāṃ vaṃde jetāramaparājitam || 51 ||
taṃ tvāmantakanetāraṃ vaṃde mandākinīdharam
dadhāti vidadhadyo māmimāni trīṇi viṣṭapā || 52 ||
sarvajñaṃ sarvagaṃ sarvaṃ kaviṃ vaṃde tamīśvaram
yataśca yajuṣā sārddhamṛcaḥ sāmāni jajñire || 53 ||
bhavantaṃ sudṛśaṃ vaṃde bhūtabhavyabhavanti ca
tyajantītarakarmāṇi yo viśvābhirvipaśyati || 54 ||
haraṃ suraniyantāraṃ harantamahamānataḥ
yadājñayā jagatsarvaṃ vyāpya nārāyaṇaḥ sthitaḥ || 55 ||
taṃ namāmi mahādevaṃ yanniyogādidaṃ jagat
kalpādau bhagavāndhātā yathāpūrvamakalpayat || 56 ||
īśvaraṃ tamahaṃ devaṃ yasya liṅgamaharniśam
yajante saha bhāryābhirindra jyeṣṭhā marudgaṇāḥ || 57 ||
namāmi tamimaṃ rudraṃ yamabhyarcya sakṛtpurā
avāpuḥ svaṃ svamaiśvaryaṃ devāsaḥ pūṣarātayaḥ || 58 ||
taṃ vaṃde devamīśānaṃ yaṃ śivaṃ hṛdayāṃbuje
satataṃ yatayaḥ śāntāḥ sañjānānā upāsate || 59 ||
