tadasmai satataṃ kurmo namaḥ kamalakāntaye
umākucapaṭoraska yā te rudra śivā tanūḥ || 60 ||
namaste rudra bhāvāya namaste rudra kelaye
namaste rudra śāntyai ca namaste rudra manyave || 61 ||
vedāśvarathaniṣṭhābhyāṃ pādābhyāṃ tripurāntaka
bāṇakārmukayuktābhyāṃ bāhubhyāmuta te namaḥ || 62 ||
namaste vāsukijyāya viṣkirāya ca śaṅkara
mahate merurūpāya namaste astu dhanvane || 63 ||
namaḥ paraśave te'stu śūlāyātularociṣe
hayagrīvātmane tubhyamutota iṣave namaḥ || 64 ||
suretaravadhūhārahārīṇi hara yāni te
anyānyastrāṇyahaṃ tūṇa idaṃ tebhyo'karaṃ namaḥ || 65 ||
dharādharasutālīlāsarojāhatabāhave
tasmai tubhyamaghorāya namo asmā śravasya ca || 66 ||
rakṣa māmajñamakṣīṇamaśikṣitamananyagam
anāthaṃ dīnamāpannaṃ daridraṃ nīlalohitam || 67 ||
durmukhaṃ duṣkriyaṃ duṣṭaṃ rakṣa māmīśa durdaśam
ādṛśā tamahaṃ na tvadanyaṃ vindāmi rādhase || 68 ||
bhavākhyenāgninā śambho rāgadveṣamadārciṣā
dayālo dahyamānānāmasmākamavitā bhava || 69 ||
paradāraṃ parāvāsaṃ paravastraṃ parāśrayam
hara pāhi parānnaṃ māṃ puruṇāmanpuruṣṭuta || 70 ||
laukikairyatkṛtaṃ duṣṭairnāvamānaṃ sahāmahe
deveśa tava dāsebhyo bhūridā bhūri dehi naḥ || 71 ||
kulokānāmayatnānāṃ garviṇāmīśa paśyatām
asmabhyaṃ kṣetramāyuśca vasu spārhaṃ tadābhara || 72 ||
yāñcāto mahatīṃ lajjāmasmadīyāṃ ghṛṇānidhe
tvameva vetsi nastūrṇamidaṃ stotṛbhya ābhara || 73 ||
jāyā mātā pitā cānye māṃ dviṣantyamitaṃ kṛśam
dehi me mahatīṃ vidyāṃ rāyā viśvapuṣā saha || 74 ||
adṛṣṭārtheṣu sarveṣu dṛṣṭārtheṣvapi karmasu
merudhanvannaśaktebhyo balaṃ dhehi tanūṣu naḥ || 75 ||
labdhāniṣṭasahasrasya nityamiṣṭaviyoginaḥ
hṛdro gaṃ mama deveśa harimāṇaṃ ca nāśaya || 76 ||
ye ye rogāḥ piśācā vā narā devāśca māmiha
bādhante devatāḥ sarvā nibādhasva mahānasi || 77 ||
tvameva rakṣitāsmākamanyaḥ kaścinna vidyate
tasmātsvīkṛtya deveśa rakṣā ṇo brahmaṇaspate || 78 ||
tvamevomāpate mātā tvaṃ pitā tvaṃ pitāmahaḥ
tvamāyustvaṃ matistvaṃ śrīruta bhrātota naḥ sakhā || 79 ||
yatastvameva deveśa kartā sarvasya karmaṇaḥ
tataḥ kṣamasva tatsarvaṃ yanmayā duṣkṛtaṃ kṛtam || 80 ||
tvatsamo na prabhutvena phalgutvena ca matsamaḥ
tato deva mahādeva tvamasmākaṃ tavasmasi || 81 ||
susmitaṃ bhasmagaurāṅgaṃ taruṇādityavigraham
prasannavadanaṃ saumyaṃ gāye tvā namasā girā || 82 ||
eṣa eva varo'smākaṃ nṛtyantaṃ tvāṃ sabhāpatim
lokayantamumākāntaṃ paśyema śaradaḥ śatam || 83 ||
arogiṇo mahābhāgā vidvāṃsaśca bahuśrutāḥ
bhagavaṃstvatprasādena jīvema śaradaḥ śatam || 84 ||
sadārā bandhubhiḥ sārddhaṃ tvadīyaṃ tāṇḍavāmṛtam
pibantaḥ kāmamīśānaṃ nandāma śaradaḥ śatam || 85 ||
devadeva mahādeva tvadīyāṅighrasaroruham
kāmaṃ madhumayaṃ pītvā modāma śaradaḥ śatam || 86 ||
kīṭā nāgāḥ piśācā vā ye vā ke vā bhave bhave
tava dāsā mahādeva bhavāma śaradaḥ śatam || 87 ||
sabhāyāmīśa te deva nṛtyavādyagalasvanam
śravaṇābhyāṃ mahādeva śṛṇuyāma śaradaḥ śatam || 88 ||
smṛtimātreṇa saṃsāravināśanakarāṇi te
nāmāni khalu divyāni prabravāma śaradaḥ śatam || 89 ||
iṣusandhānamātreṇa dagdhatripura dhūrjaṭe
ādhibhirvyādhibhirnityamajitāḥ syāma śaradaḥ śatam || 90 ||
cārucāmīkarābhāsaṃ gaurīkucapaṭorasam
kadā nu lokayiṣyāmi yuvānaṃ viśpatiṃ kavim || 91 ||
pramathendrā vṛtaṃ prītavadanaṃ priyavāsasam
seviṣye'ha kadā sāmbaṃ subhāsaṃ śukraśociṣam || 92 ||
bahvenasaṃ māmakṛtapuṇyaleśaṃ ca durmatim
svīkariṣyati kiṃ nvīśo nīlagrīvo vilohitaḥ || 93 ||
kālaśūlānalāsaktaṃ bhītaṃ vyākulamānasam
kadā nu dra kṣyatīśo māṃ tuvigrīvo anānataḥ || 94 ||
gāyakā yūyamāyāta yadi rāgādilipsavaḥ
dhanadasya sakhāyaṃ tamupāsmai gāyatā naraḥ || 95 ||
svastyastu sakhi te jihve vidyādāturumāpateḥ
stavamuccataraṃ brūhi jayatāmiva dundubhiḥ || 96 ||
cetayājana dha śāntastvaṃ nedaṃ vedākhilaṃ jagat
asya tṛptyākhilaṃ śambhorgauro na tṛṣitaḥ piba || 97 ||
sugandhiṃ ca sukhasparśaṃ kāmadaṃ somabhūṣaṇam
gāḍhamāliṅga maccittaṃ yoṣā jāramiva priyam || 98 ||
mahāmayūkhāya mahābhujāya mahāśarīrāya mahāmbarāya
mahākirīṭāya maheśvarāya maho mahīṃ suṣṭutimīrayāmi || 99 ||
yathākathañcidra citābhirīṣatprasannataścārubhirādareṇa
prapūjayāmi stutibhirmaheśamaṣāḍhamugraṃ sahamānamābhiḥ || 100 ||
namaḥ śivāya tripurāntakāya jagattrayeśāya digambarāya
namo'stu mukhyāya harāya bhūyo namo jaghanyāya ca budhniyāya || 101 ||
namo vikārāya vikāriṇe te namo bhavāyāstu bhavodbhavāya
bahuprajātyantavicitrarūpa yataḥ prasūtā jagataḥ prasūtiḥ || 102 ||
tasmai sureśorukirīṭanānāratnāvṛtāṣṭāpadaviṣṭarāya
bhasmāṅgarāgāya namaḥ parasmai yasmātparaṃ nāparamasti kiñcit || 103 ||
sarpādhirājauṣadhināthayuddhakṣubhyajjaṭāmaṇḍalagahvarāya
tubhyaṃ namaḥ sundaratāṇḍavāya yasminnidaṃ sañcavicaiti sarvam || 104 ||
murārinetrārcitapādapadmamumāṅighralākṣāpariraktapāṇim
namāmi devaṃ vṛṣanīlakaṇṭhaṃ hiraṇyadantaṃ śucivarṇamārāt || 105 ||
anantamavyaktamacintyamekaṃ harantamāśāmbaramambarāṅgam
ajaṃ purāṇaṃ praṇamāmi yūpamaṇoraṇīyānmahato mahīyān || 106 ||
antaḥsthamātmānamajaṃ na dṛṣṭvā bhramanti mūḍhā girigahvareṣu
paścādudagdakṣiṇataḥ purastādadhaḥ svidāsī3dupari svidāsī3t || 107 ||
imaṃ namāmīśvaramindumaule śivaṃ mahānandamaśokaduḥkham
hṛdaṃbuje tiṣṭhati yaḥ parātmā yataśca sarvāḥ pradiśo diśaśca || 108 ||
rāgādikāpaṭyasamudbhavena bhāntaṃ bhavākhyena mahāmayena
vilokya māṃ pālaya candra maule bhiṣaktamaṃ tvā bhiṣajāṃ śṛṇomi || 109 ||
duḥkhāmburāśiṃ sukhaleśahīnamaspṛṣṭapuṇyaṃ bahupātakaṃ mām
mṛtyoḥ karasthaṃ bhava rakṣa bhītaṃ paścātpurastādadharādudaktāt || 110 ||
girīndra jācārumukhāvalokasuśītayā deva tavaiva dṛṣṭyā
vayaṃ dayāpūritayaiva pūrṇamapo na nāvā duritā tarema || 111 ||
apārasaṃsārasamudra madhye nimagnamutkrośamanalparāgam
māmakṣamaṃ pāhi maheśajuṣṭamojiṣṭhayā dakṣiṇayeva rātim || 112 ||
smaranpurā sañcitapātakāni kharaṃ yamasyāpi mukhaṃ yamāre
bibhemi me dehi yatheṣṭamāyuryadi kṣitāyuryadi vā paretaḥ || 113 ||
sugandhibhiḥ sundarabhasmagaurairanantabhogairmṛdulairaghoraiḥ
imaṃ kadāliṅgati māṃ pinākī sthirebhiraṅgaiḥ pururūpa ugraḥ || 114 ||
krośantamīśaṃ patitaṃ bhavābdhau nākusthamaṇḍūkamivātibhītam
kadā nu māṃ rakṣati devadevo hiraṇyarūpaḥ sa hiraṇyasandṛk || 115 ||
cārusmitaṃ candra kalāvataṃsaṃ gaurīkaṭākṣāruhayugmanetram
ālokayiṣyāmi kadā nu sāmbamādityavarṇaṃ tamasaḥ parastāt || 116 ||
āgacchatānādimumukṣavo ye yūyaṃ śivaṃ cintayatāntare'bje
dhyāyanti muktyarthamamuṃ hi nityaṃ vedāntavijñānasuniścitārthāḥ || 117 ||
āyāta yūyaṃ bhavatādhipatye kāmaṃ gaheśaṃ giriśaṃ yajadhvam
evaṃ purābhyarcya hiraṇyagarbho bhūtasya jātaḥ patireka āsīt || 118 ||
ekāyanaṃ te vipulāṃ śriyaṃ te śrīkaṇṭhamenaṃ sukṛtā namantām
śrīmānayaṃ śrīpativandyapādaḥ śrīṇāmudāro dharuṇo rayīṇām || 119 ||
suputrakāmā api ye manuṣyā yuvānamenaṃ giriśaṃ yajantām
yataḥ svayambhūrjagato vidhātā hiraṇyagarbhaḥ samavartatāgre || 120 ||
alaṃ kimuktairbahubhiḥ samīhitaṃ samastamasyāśrayaṇena sidhyati
purainamāśritya hi kumbhasambhavo divā na naktaṃ palito yuvājani || 121 ||
anyatparityajya mamākṣibhṛṅgāḥ sarvaṃ sadainaṃ śivamāśrayadhvam
āmodavāneṣa mṛduḥ śivāya svāduḥṣkilāyaṃ madhumām̐ utāyam || 122 ||
bhaviṣyasi tvaṃ pratimānahīno vinirjjitāśeṣanarāmaraśca
namaśca te vāṇi maheśamenaṃ stuhi śrutaṃ gartasadaṃ yuvānam || 123 ||
yadyanmanaścintayasi tvamiṣṭaṃ tattadbhaviṣyatyakhilaṃ dhruvaṃ te
duḥkhena vṛttiṃ viṣaye kadācidyakṣvāmahe saumanasāya rudra m || 124 ||
ajñānayogādapacārakarma yatpūrvamasmābhiranuṣṭhitaṃ te
tadeva soḍhvā sakalaṃ dayālo piteva putrānpati no juṣasva || 125 ||
saṃsārākhyakruddhasarpeṇa tīvrai rāgadveṣonmādalobhādidantaiḥ
daṣṭaṃ dṛṣṭvā māṃ dayāluḥ pinākī devastrātā trāyatāmaprayucchan || 126 ||
ityuktvānte ye samādhernamante rudra tvāṃ te yānti janmāhidaṣṭāḥ
santo nīlagrīvasūtrātmanāhaṃ tattvā yāmi brahmaṇā vandamānaḥ || 127 ||
bhavādhibhīṣaṇajvareṇa pīḍitānmahāmayānaśeṣapātakālayānadūrakālalocanān
anāthanātha te kareṇa bheṣajena kālahā
udūṣuṇo vaso mahe mṛśasva śūra rādhase || 128 ||
jayema yena sarvametadiṣṭamaṣṭadiggaṇaṃ
bhuvaḥ sthalaṃ divaḥ sthalaṃ nabhaḥsthalaṃ ca tadgatam
ya eṣa sarvadevadānavaprabhuḥ sabhāpatiḥ
sa no dadātu taṃ rayiṃ puruṃ piśaṅgasaṅdṛśam || 129 ||
namo bhavāya te harāya bhūtibhāsitorase
namo bhavābhibhūtibhītisaṅgine pinākine
namaḥ śivāya viśvapāya śāśvatāya helaye
na yasya hanyate sakhā na jīyate kadācana || 130 ||
surapatipataye namo namaḥ prajāpatipataye namo namaḥ
kṣitipatipataye namo namo'mbikāpataya umāpataye namo namaḥ || 131 ||
vināyakaṃ vandanamastakāhatasvanādyasaṅghṛṣṭakirīṭamastakam
namāmi nityaṃ praṇatārtināśanaṃ kaviṃ kavīnāmupamaśravastamam || 132 ||
devā yuddhe yāge viprāstrapyāhvayaṃ dha dvayaṃ vindanti skandam
vaṃde subrahmaṇyaoṃ subrahmaṇyaoṃ subrahmaṇyom || 133 ||
namaḥ śivāyai jagadambikāyai śivapriyāyai śivavigrahāyai
samudbabhūvādri pateḥ sutā yā catuṣkapardā yuvatiḥ supeśāḥ || 134 ||
hiraṇyavarṇāṃ maṇinūpurāṃghriṃ prasannavaktrāṃ śukapadmahastām
viśālanetrāṃ praṇamāmi gaurīṃ vacovidaṃ vācamudīrayantīm || 135 ||
namāmi menātanayāmameyāmimāmumāṃ kāntimatīmameyām
karoti yā bhūtisitau stanau dvau priyaṃ sakhāyaṃ pariṣasvajānā || 136 ||
kāntāmumāṃ kāntanibhāṅgakāntiṃ bhāntāmupāttānataharyajendrā m
nato'smi yāste giriśasya pārśve viśvāni devī bhuvanāni caṣṭe || 137 ||
vaṃde gaurīṃ tuṅgapīnastanīṃ tāṃ candrā cūḍāṃ kliṣṭasarvāṅgarāgām
yaiṣā duḥkhiprāṇināmātmakāntiṃ devīṃ devīṃ rādhase codayantīm || 138 ||
enāṃ vaṃde dīnarakṣāvinodāṃ menākanyāṃ mānadānandadātrīm
yā vidyānāṃ maṅgalānāṃ ca vācāmeṣā netrī rādhasaḥ sūnṛtānām || 139 ||
saṃsāratāporubhayāpahantrī bhavāni bhojyābharaṇaikabhoge
dhiyaṃ varāṃ dehi śive nirargalāṃ yayāti viśvā duritā tarema140 ||
śive kathaṃ tvatsamatā kva dīyate jagatkṛtiḥ kelirayaṃ śivaḥ patiḥ
haristu dāso'nucarīndirā śacī sarasvatī vā subhagādadirvasu || 141 ||
vasuruvāca -
ityanena staveneśaṃ stutyetthaṃ sa mahāmuniḥ
snehāśrupūrṇanayanaḥ praṇanāma sabhāpatim || 142 ||
muhurmuhuḥ pibannīśaṃ tāṇḍavāmṛtamaṅgalam
sarvānkāmānavāpyānte gāṇapatyamavāpa ha || 143 ||
imaṃ stavaṃ jaimininā vacoditaṃ dvijottamo yaḥ paṭhatīha bhaktitaḥ
tamiṣṭavāksiddhimatidyutiśriyaḥ pariṣvajante janayo yathā patim || 144 ||
mahīpatiryastu yuyutsurādarādamuṃ paṭhatyasya tathaiva sādarāt
prayānti śīghraṃ pramadāntakāntikaṃ bhiyaṃ dadhānā hṛdayeṣu śatravaḥ || 145 ||
traivarṇikeṣvanyatamo ya enaṃ nityaṃ kadācitpaṭhatīśabhaktitaḥ
kalevarānte śivapārśvavartī nirañjanaḥ sāmyamupaiti divyam || 146 ||
labhante paṭhanto matiṃ buddhikāmā labhante tathaiva śriyaṃ puṣṭikāmāḥ
labhante hi dhānyaṃ narā dhānyakāmā labhante ha putrānnarāḥ putrakāmāḥ || 147 ||
pādaṃ vāpyarddhapādaṃ vā ślokaṃ ślokārddhameva vā
yastu dhārayate nityaṃ śivalokaṃ sa gacchati || 148 ||
yatra nṛttaṃ śivaścakre tāṇḍavaṃ tatsthalaṃ śubhe
puṇyātpuṇyataraṃ tīrthaṃ tatra snātvā vimucyate || 149 ||
yastatra kurute śrāddhaṃ pitṝṇāṃ manujottama
pūrvajānsa nayetsvargaṃ nātra kāryā vicāraṇā || 150 ||
gāṃ suvarṇaṃ dharāṃ śayyāṃ vastramātapavāraṇam
pānamannaṃ dvije dadyāttatra tatsarvamakṣayam || 151 ||
etadākhyānakaṃ puṇyaṃ puṇḍarīka purodbhavam
śṛṇuyācchrāvayedvāpi so'pi rudra priyo bhavet || 152 ||
iti śrī bṛhannāradīyapurāṇe bṛhadupākhyāne uttarabhāge vasumohinīsaṃvāde tryambakeśvaramāhātmye vedapādastavo nāma trisaptatitamo'dhyāyaḥ || 73 ||

iti tryambakeśvarajyotirliṅgamāhātmyaṃ sampūrṇam

atha gokarṇamāhātmyamārabhyate

<2.74>

atha catuḥsaptatitamo'dhyāyaḥ
mohinyuvāca -
puṇḍarīkapurākhyānaṃ tvayā proktaṃ śrutaṃ guro
gokarṇasyādya tīrthasya māhātmyaṃ me samādiśa || 1 ||
vasuruvāca -
śṛṇu mohini vakṣyāmi tīrthaṃ puṇyapradaṃ nṛṇām
gokarṇākhyaṃ harakṣetraṃ sarvapātakanāśanam || 2 ||
paścimasthasamudra sya tīre'sti varavarṇini
sārddhayojanavistāraṃ darśanādapi muktidam || 3 ||
sagarasyātmajairdevi khanite bhūtale kramāt
sāgaro varddhitastvārātplāvayāmāsa medinīm || 4 ||
triṃśadyojanavistārāṃ satīrthakṣetrakānanām
tatastannilayāḥ sarve sadevāsuramānavāḥ || 5 ||
tatsthānaṃ samparityajya sahyādigiriṣu sthitāḥ
tato guhyaṃ paraṃ tīrthaṃ gokarṇākhyaṃ samudra gam || 6 ||
cintayanto munivarāstaduddhāre matiṃ dadhuḥ
tataḥ sammantrya te sarve parvatopatyakāsthitāḥ || 7 ||
mahendrā calasaṃsthānaṃ parśurāmaṃ didṛkṣavaḥ
jagmurmunivarā devi gokarṇoddhārakāṃkṣayā || 8 ||
samāruhya tu taṃ śailaṃ dadṛśustasya cāśramam
praśāntakrūrasatvāḍhyaṃ sarvartuṣu sukhāvaham || 9 ||
phalitaiḥ puṣpitairvṛkṣairgahanaṃ tattapovanam
snigdhacchāyamanaupapyaṃ svāmodisukhamārutam || 10 ||
taṃ tadāśramamāsādya brahmaghoṣanināditam
viviśurthṛṣṭamanaso yathāvṛddhapuraḥsaram || 11 ||
brahmāsane sukhāsīnaṃ mṛdukṛṣṇājinottare
śiṣyaiḥ parivṛtaṃ śāntaṃ dadṛśustaṃ tapodhanam || 12 ||
kālāgnimiva lokāṃstrīndagdhvā śāntaṃ tapaḥ sthitam
te sametya bhṛguśreṣṭhaṃ vinayena vavandire || 13 ||
tatastānāgatāndṛṣṭvā munīnbhṛgukulodvahaḥ
arghyapādyādibhiḥ samyakpūjayāmāsa sādaram || 14 ||
tānāsīnānkṛtātithyānuvāca bhṛgunandanaḥ
svāgataṃ vo mahābhāgā yadarthamiha cāgatāḥ || 15 ||
tadvadadhvaṃ suviśvastāḥ karaṇīyaṃ mayāsti yat
tato'bruvanmuniśreṣṭhā yadarthaṃ rāmamāgatāḥ || 16 ||
avehyasmān bhṛguśreṣṭha gokarṇanilayānmunīn
khanadbhiḥ sāgarairbhūmiṃ tasmāttīrthādvivāsitān || 17 ||
sa tvamātmaprabhāveṇa kṣetrapravaramadya naḥ
dātumarhasi viprendra samutsāryārṇavodakam || 18 ||
tacchrutvā vacanaṃ teṣāṃ nyastaśastro vyacintayat
tato vicintya bhagavāndharmyaṃ sādhvabhirakṣaṇam || 19 ||
pragṛhya svadhanurbāṇānsampratasthe sa taiḥ samam
so'varuhya mahendrā dre rdiśaṃ dakṣiṇapaścimām || 20 ||
samuddiśya yayau śīghraṃ sa svamullaṅghya parvatam
samprāptaḥ sāgarataṭaṃ sārddhaṃ gokarṇāvāsibhiḥ || 21 ||
muhūrttaṃ tatra viśramya varuṇaṃ yādasāṃ patim
meghagabhīrayā vācā provāca vadatāṃ varaḥ || 22 ||
rāmo'ha bhārgavaḥ prāpto munibhiḥ saha kāryavān
praceto darśanaṃ dehi kāryamātyāyikaṃ tvayā || 23 ||
evaṃ rāmasamāhūto yādaḥpatirahantayā
śrutvāpi tasya tadvākyaṃ nāyāto rāmasannidhau || 24 ||
evaṃ punaḥ punastena samāhūto'pi nāgataḥ
yadā tadābhisaṃkruddho dhanurjagrāha bhārgavaḥ || 25 ||
tasminsandhāya viśikhaṃ vahnidevaṃ tu bhārgavam
astraṃ saṃyojayāmāsa śoṣaṇāya saritpateḥ || 26 ||
tasminsaṃyojite'stre tu bhārgaveṇa mahātmanā
saṃkṣubdhaḥ sāgaro bhadre yādogaṇasamākulaḥ || 27 ||
varuṇo'strābhisantapto rāmasya bhayasamplutaḥ
svarūpeṇa samāgatya rāmapādau samagrahīt || 28 ||
tato'stra sa vinirvartya varuṇaṃ prāha satvaram
gokarṇo dṛśyatāṃ deva utsarpaya jalaṃ kila || 29 ||
tato rāmājñayā so'pi gokarṇodakamāharat
rāmo'pi taṃ samabhyarcya gokarṇaṃ nāma śaṅkaram || 30 ||
prāptaḥ punarmahendrā drau tasthustatraiva te dvijāḥ
yatra sarve tapastaptvā munayaḥ śaṃsitavratāḥ || 31 ||
nirvāṇaṃ paramaṃ prāptāḥ punarāvṛttivarjitam
tatkṣetrasya prabhāveṇa prītyā bhūtagaṇaiḥ saha || 32 ||
devyā ca sakalairdevairnityaṃ vasati śaṅkaraḥ
enāṃsi darśanāttasya gokarṇasya maheśituḥ || 33 ||
sadyo viyujya gacchanti pravāte śuṣkaparṇavat
tatkṣetrasevanaratirnṛṇāṃ jātu na jāyate || 34 ||
nirbandhena tu ye tatra prāṇinaḥ sthirajaṅgamāḥ
mriyante devi sadyaste svargaṃ yānti sanātanam || 35 ||
smṛtyāpi sakalaiḥ pāpairyasya mucyeta mānavaḥ
tadgokarṇābhidhaṃ kṣetraṃ sarvatīrthaniketanam || 36 ||
snātvā kṣetreṣu sarveṣu yajantaśca sadāśivam
labhante yatphalaṃ martyāstatsarvaṃ tatra darśanāt || 37 ||
kāmakrodhādibhirhīnā ye tatra nivasanti vai
acireṇaiva kālena te siddhiṃ prāpnuvanti hi || 38 ||
japahomaratāḥ śāntā niyatā brahmacāriṇaḥ
vasanti tasminye te hi siddhiṃ prāpsyantyabhīpsitām || 39 ||
dānahomajapādyaṃ ca pitṛdevadvijārcanam
anyasmātkoṭiguṇitaṃ bhavettasminphalaṃ sati || 40 ||
ityetatkathitaṃ bhadre gokarṇakṣetrasambhavam
māhātmyaṃ sarvapāpaghnaṃ paṭhatāṃ śṛṇvatāmapi || 41 ||
iti śrībṛhannāradīyapurāṇe bṛhadupākhyāne uttarabhāge vasumohinīsaṃvāde gokarṇamāhātmyaṃ nāma catuḥsaptatitamo'dhyāyaḥ || 74 ||

iti gokarṇamāhātmyaṃ samāptam

atha lakṣmaṇācalamāhātmyamārabhyate

<2.75>

atha pañcasaptatitamo'dhyāyaḥ
mohinyuvāca -
śrutaṃ gokarṇamāhātmyaṃ vaso pāpavināśanam
lakṣmaṇasyāpi māhātmyaṃ vaktumarhasi sāmpratam || 1 ||
vasuruvāca -
śṛṇu devi pravakṣyāmi māhātmyaṃ lakṣmaṇasya ca
yaṃ dṛṣṭvā manujo devaṃ mucyate sarvapātakaiḥ || 2 ||
caturvyūhāvatāre yo devaḥ sakarṣaṇaḥ svayay
sarvabhūmaṇḍalaṃ hyetatsahasravadanaḥ svarāṭ || 3 ||
ekasmiñchirasi nyastaṃ nāvaitsiddhārthakopamam
devo nārāyaṇaḥ sākṣādrā mo brahmādivanditaḥ || 4 ||
pradyumno bharato bhadre śatrughno hyaniruddhakaḥ
lakṣmaṇastu mahābhāge svayaṃ saṅkarṣaṇaḥ śivaḥ || 5 ||
brahmādyaiḥ prārthitaḥ pūrvaṃ sākṣāddevo ramāpatiḥ
rāmādināmabhirjajñe caturddhā diggrathānnṛpāt || 6 ||
tataḥ kālāntare devi viśvāmitro munīśvaraḥ
yajñarakṣārthamāgatya prārthayadrā malakṣmaṇau || 7 ||
tato rājā daśarathaḥ prāṇebhyo'pi priyau sutau
muneḥ śāpabhayādbhīto dadau tau rāmalakṣmaṇau || 8 ||
gatvā yajñaṃ munīndra sya gādhipasya rarakṣatuḥ
satāḍakaṃ subāhuṃ tu hatvā prakṣipya dūrataḥ || 9 ||
mārīcaṃ mānavāstreṇa viśvāmitramatoṣayat
tataḥ prītānmuniśreṣṭhādastragrāmamavāpya ca || 10 ||
uvāca sa kiyatkālaṃ sānujastena satkṛtaḥ
vaidehanagaraṃ nīto viśvāmitreṇa tatparam || 11 ||
tatastu rājā janako viśvāmitraṃ susatkṛtam
papraccha bālakāvetau kasya kṣatrakuleśituḥ || 12 ||
tatastasmai munivaro rājño daśarathasya tau
putrau nivedayāmāsa bhrātarau rāmalakṣmaṇau || 13 ||
tato videhaḥ saprīto dṛṣṭvā rāmaṃ ca lakṣmaṇam
sītormilākhyayoḥ putryoścetasākalpayatpatī || 14 ||
trikālajñastu sa munirjñātvā tasya manogatam
modamāno'tha janakaṃ prāha darśaya taddhanuḥ || 15 ||
sītāsvayaṃvare nyastaṃ nyāsabhūtaṃ maheśituḥ
rājā śrutvā tu tadvākyaṃ viśvāmitrasya satvaram || 16 ||
bhṛtyatriśatyānāyyāsmai darśayāmāsa sādaram
rāmaścaṇḍīśacāpaṃ tadvāmadoṣṇoddharan kṣaṇāt || 17 ||
sajyaṃ vikṛṣya sahasā babhañjekṣumivebharāṭ
tato'ti mithilaḥ prītaḥ sve kanye rāmalakṣmaṇau || 18 ||
samabhyarcyārpayāmāsa tābhyāṃ te vidhipūrvakam
jñātvā munivarādanyau rājño daśarathasya tu || 19 ||
tābhyāṃ saha tamāhūya bhrātṛkanye adāpayat
tataḥ sa kṛtadāraistu caturbhistanayaiḥ saha || 20 ||
samarcito videhenāyodhyāṃ munyājñayā yayau
mārge bhṛgupaterdarpaṃ śamayitvā sa rāghavaḥ || 21 ||
pitṛbhrātṛyutaḥ śrīmānmumude bahuvatsarān
paṇḍitaistu vasiṣṭhādyairbodhito'sau nijaṃ mahaḥ || 22 ||
brahmākhyaṃ bubudhe rāmo mānuṣatvaṃ viḍambayan
tato daśaratho rājā jñātajñeyaṃ nijaṃ sutam || 23 ||
rāmaṃ samudyato hṛṣṭo yauvarājye'bhiṣecitum
yajjñātvā kaikayī devī rājñaḥ preṣṭhā kanīyasī || 24 ||
sannivārya haṭhāttasya putrasya tadarocata
tato rāmo mude tasyāḥ pitrānanumato yayau || 25 ||
sabhāryaḥ saḥ sasaumitriścitrakūṭaṃ giriṃ śubhe
kiyatkālamuvāsāsau tatraiva muniveṣadhṛk || 26 ||
mātāmahagṛhāttacca śrutvā'yātaḥ piturvadham
sa vijñāya mṛtaṃ tātaṃ hā rāmeti virāviṇam || 27 ||
dhikkṛtya kaikayīṃ yāto rāmaṃ sa vinivartitum
tataḥ svapāduke datvā bharataṃ vinivartya ca || 28 ||
rāmo'treścāpyagastyasya sutīkṣṇasyāśrameṣvagāt
teṣu dvādaśa varṣāṇi gamayitvā raghūdvahaḥ || 29 ||
bhāryānujānvitaḥ śrīmāṃstataḥ pañcavaṭīmagāt
tatrāvasajjanasthāne triśiraḥkharadūṣaṇān || 30 ||
śūrpaṇakhyā vikṛtayā preritānsa vyanāśayat
tato rakṣaḥsahasraiśca caturddaśabhirāgatān || 31 ||
vicitravājairnārācairyamakṣayamanīnayat
yacchrutvā rakṣasāṃ rājā mārīcaṃ kāñcanaṃ mṛgam || 32 ||
darśayitvāpavāhyaitau sītāṃ hṛtvā jaṭāyuṣam
rundhānaṃ mārgamāhatya laṅkāyāṃ samupānayat || 33 ||
āgatya tau hṛtāṃ sītāṃ mārgamāṇau samantataḥ
dṛṣṭvā jaṭāyuṣaṃ śāntaṃ dagdhvā hatvā kabandhakam || 34 ||
śabarīmanukampyātha ṛṣyamūkamupāgatau
tatastu hanumadvākyātsvasakhyuḥ plavageśituḥ || 35 ||
vidviṣaṃ vālinaṃ hatvā sugrīvamakaronnṛpam
tadājñaptāstu te kīśāḥ sarvataḥ samupāgatāḥ || 36 ||
hanumatpramukhāḥ sītāṃ mārganto dakṣiṇodadhim
prāpya sampātivacanāllaṅkāyāṃ niścayaṃ gatāḥ || 37 ||
tatastu hanumānekaḥ prāpya laṅkāṃ purīṃ kapiḥ
samudra sya pare pāre'paśyadrā mapriyāṃ satīm || 38 ||
dattvā rāmāṅgulīratnaṃ viśvāsamupapādya tām
tayoḥ kuśalamāśrāvya labdhvā cūḍāmaṇiṃ tataḥ || 39 ||
bhaṅktvā cāśokavanikāṃ hatvā cākṣaṃ sasainyakam
indra jidbandhanātprāpya sambhāṣyāpi ca rāvaṇam || 40 ||
dagdhvā laṅkāṃ purīṃ kṛtsnāṃ punardṛṣṭvā tu maithilīm
labdhājño'ṇavamullaṅghya rāmāyaināṃ nyavedayat || 41 ||
śrutvā rāmo'pi tāṃ sītāṃ rākṣasasya nivāsagām
sārddhaṃ sa kapisainyena samprāpto makarālayam || 42 ||
sāgarānumatenāsau setuṃ baddhvā mahodadhau
adri kūṭeḥ paraṃ tīraṃ prāpya senāṃ nyaveśayat || 43 ||
tato'sau rāvaṇo bhrātrā bodhito'pi kanīyasā
pradānaṃ tatra maithilyāstadbhartre na tvarocayat || 44 ||
padā hatastatastena rāvaṇena vibhīṣaṇaḥ
samprāptaḥ śaraṇaṃ rāmaṃ rāmo laṅkāmupāruṇat || 45 ||
tatastu mantriṇo'mātyāḥ putrā bhṛtyāḥ pracoditāḥ
yuddhāya te kṣayaṃ nītāstābhyāṃ saṃkhye kapīśvaraiḥ || 46 ||
lakṣmaṇaḥ śakrajetāraṃ jaghnivānniśitaiḥ śaraiḥ
rāmo'pi kumbhaśravaṇaṃ rāvaṇaṃ cāpyajīghanat || 47 ||
vibhīṣaṇena tatkṛtyaṃ kārayitvā nijāṃ priyām
vahnau saṃśodhya datvāsmai rāmo rakṣogaṇeśatām || 48 ||
laṅkāmāyuśca kalpāntaṃ yayau cīrṇavrataḥ purīm
puṣpakeṇa vimānena sasugrīvavibhīṣaṇaḥ || 49 ||
nandigrāmasthabharataṃ nītvāyodhyāṃ samāviśat
mātṝḥ praṇamya tāḥ sarvā bhrātaraste purodhasā || 50 ||
vasiṣṭhenānuvijñāpya rāmaṃ rājye'bhyaṣecayan
tato rāmo'pi bhagavānprajāḥ śāsannivaurasān || 51 ||
lokāpavādātsantrastaḥ sītāṃ tatyāja dharmavit
sā tu samprāpya vālmīkerāśramaṃ nyavasatsukham || 52 ||
putrau ca suṣuve tatra nāmnā khyātau kuśīlavau
vālmīkistu tayoḥ kṛtvā yathā samuditāḥ kriyāḥ || 53 ||
rāmāyaṇaṃ viracyaitāvadhyāpayadudāradhīḥ
tau gāyamānau satreṣu munīnāṃ khyātimāgatau || 54 ||
yajñe rāmasya samprāptau vājimedhe pravartite
tatra tābhyāṃ tu tadgītaṃ svacaritraṃ prasannadhīḥ || 55 ||
munimākārayāmāsa sasītaṃ tatra saṃsadi
sā tu rāmāya tau putrau nivedya jagatījaniḥ || 56 ||
jagatyā vivaraṃ bhūyo viveśāsīttadadbhutam
tataḥ paraṃ brahmacaryaṃ yajñameva trayodaśa || 57 ||
sahasrābdānprakuvārṇastasthau bhuvi raghūttamaḥ
tatastu kāle durvāsāḥ samprāpto rāghavaṃ prati || 58 ||
brahmaṇā preṣito bhadre vaikuṇṭhagamanāya ca
sa ekāntagato rāmaṃ prāha ko'pīha nā'vrajet || 59 ||
āgato vadhyatāṃ yātu rāmastatpratijajñivān
sa lakṣmaṇaṃ samāhūya provāca raghunandanaḥ || 60 ||
dvāri tiṣṭhātra nirviṣṭo vadhyatāṃ me prayāsyati
sa tatheti pratijñāya rāmasyājñāṃ samācaran || 61 ||
praveśanaṃ na kasyāpi pradadau rāmasannidhau
evamekāntagaṃ rāmaṃ kālasaṃvidamāsthitam || 62 ||
jñātvātha dvāri durvāsā lakṣmaṇaṃ samupāgamat
tamāgataṃ tu saṃprekṣya saumitriḥ praṇipatya ca || 63 ||
muhūrtaṃ pālayetyāha mantravyagro'sti rāghavaḥ
durvāsāstadvacaḥ śrutvā kālasyārthavidhāyakaḥ || 64 ||
kruddhaḥ provāca saumitriṃ dehi me'ntapraveśanam
no cettvāṃ bhasmasātsadyaḥ kariṣyāmi vicāraya || 65 ||
vaco durvāsasaḥ śrutvā lakṣmaṇo jātasambhramaḥ
munerbhīto viveśāntarvijñāpayitumagrajam || 66 ||
dṛṣṭvā tu lakṣmaṇaṃ kāla utthāya kṛtamantrakaḥ
pratijñāṃ pālayetyuktvā yayau rāmavisarjjitaḥ || 67 ||
tato niṣkramya bhagavān rāmo dharmabhṛtāṃ varaḥ
pratoṣya taṃ muniṃ prīto durvāsasamabhojayat || 68 ||
bhojayitvā praṇamyainaṃ visṛjya prāha lakṣmaṇam
bhrātarlakṣmaṇa samprāptaṃ saṅkaṭaṃ dharmakāraṇāt || 69 ||
yattvaṃ me vadhyatāṃ prāpto daivaṃ hi balavattaram
mayā tyaktastato vīra yathecchaṃ gaccha sāmpratam || 70 ||
tataḥ praṇamya taṃ rāmaṃ satyadharme vyavasthitam
dakṣiṇāṃ diśamāśritya tapaścakre nagopari || 71 ||
tato rāmo'pi bhagavānbrahmaprārthanayā punaḥ
svadhāmāviśadavyagraḥ sasāketaḥ sakośalaḥ || 72 ||
gopratāre sarayvāṃ ye rāmaṃ sañcintya samplutāḥ
te rāmadhāma viviśurdivyāṅgā yogidurlabham || 73 ||
lakṣmaṇastu kiyatkālaṃ tapoyogabalānvitaḥ
rāmānugamanenaiva svadhāmāviśadavyayam || 74 ||
sānnidhyaṃ parvate tasmindattvā saumitriranvaham
cakraṃ nijādhikāraṃ sa tatastatkṣetramuttamam || 75 ||
ye paśyanti narā bhaktyā lakṣmaṇaṃ lakṣmaṇācale
te kṛtārthā na sandeho gacchanti harimandiram || 76 ||
tatra dānaṃ praśaṃsanti svarṇagobhūmivājinām
dattaṃ tatrākṣayaṃ sarvaṃ hutaṃ japtaṃ kṛtaṃ tathā || 77 ||
bahunā kimihoktena darśanaṃ tasya durlabham
agastyājñāntarā devi dṛṣṭe muktirna saṃśayaḥ || 78 ||
etadrā macaritraṃ tu lakṣmaṇākhyānaṃsayutam
śrāvayedyo'pi śṛṇuyātsyātāṃ tau rāmavallabhau || 79 ||
iti śrībṛhannāradīyapurāṇe bṛhadupākhyāne uttarabhāge vasumohinīsaṃvāde rāmalakṣmaṇacaritrasahitalakṣmaṇācalamāhātmyaṃ nāma pañcasaptatitamo'dhyāyaḥ || 75 ||

iti lakṣmaṇācalamāhātmyaṃ samāptam

atha setumāhātmyamārabhyate

<2.76>

atha ṣaṭsaptatitamo'dhyāyaḥ
mohinyuvāca -
sādhu sādhu dvijaśreṣṭha yanme rāmāyaṇaṃ tvayā
śrāvitaṃ sarvapāpaghnaṃ nṛṇāṃ puṇyavivarddhanam || 1 ||
adhunā śrotumicchāmi setumāhātmyamuttamam
vasuruvāca -
śṛṇu devi pravakṣyāmi setumāhātmyamuttamam || 2 ||
yaṃ dṛṣṭvā manujo devi mucyate bhavasāgarāt
setoḥ sandarśanaṃ puṇyaṃ yatra rāmeśvaro vibhuḥ || 3 ||
darśanādeva martyānāmamaratvaṃ prayacchati
rāmeśvaraṃ tu sampūjya naro niyatamānasaḥ || 4 ||
sarvāḥ samaśnute bhūtīrnātra kāryā vicāraṇā
cakratīrthamihānyacca vartate pāpanāśanam || 5 ||
snānaṃ dānaṃ japo homastatrānantyaṃ vigāhate
tālatīrthaṃ tu samprāpya yaḥ snāyāttatra mānavaḥ || 6 ||
sa uttamāṃ janiṃ labdhvā modate devavadbhuvi
tataḥ samprāpya subhage tīrthaṃ pāpavināśanam || 7 ||
snātvā nirdhūtapāpo'sau naraḥ svarge mahīyate
sītākuṇḍe tataḥ prāpya snānaṃ samyagvidhāya ca || 8 ||
tarpayitvā pitṝndevānsarvānkāmānavāpnuyāt
maṅgalaṃ tīrthamāsādya snātvā mucyeta pātakān || 9 ||
snātvaivāmṛtavāpyāṃ tu mānavo'maratāṃ labhet
brahmakuṃḍe naraḥ snātvā brahmalokamavāpnuyāt || 10 ||
snātvā lakṣmaṇatīrthe vai naro yogagatiṃ vrajet
jaṭātīrthe naraḥ snātvā nīrogī jāyate bhuvi || 11 ||
hanumatkuṇḍake snātvā durjayo jāyate'ribhiḥ
agastyatīrtha āplutya putravāndhanavānbhavet || 12 ||
rāmakuṇḍe pluto martyo rāmasālokyamāpnuyāt
lakṣmītīrthābhiṣekeṇa lakṣmīvān rūpavānbhavet || 13 ||
agnitīrthe naraḥ snātvā mucyate sarvakilbiṣaiḥ
snānena śivatīrthe tu śivalokagatirbhavet || 14 ||
śaṅkhatīrthe tu saṃsnāto na naro durgatiṃ vrajet
yamunādiṣu tīrtheṣu naraḥ snātvā divaṃ vrajet || 15 ||
koṭitīrthe tu samplutya sarvatīrthaphalaṃ labhet
sādhyāmṛte plute yāti naraḥ sādhyasalokatām || 16 ||
sarvatīrthe naraḥ snātvā labhetkāmānabhīpsitān
dhanuṣkoṭyāṃ tu vidhivatsnāto mucyeta bandhanāt || 17 ||
kṣīrakuṃḍāpluto martyo bhogānuccāvacām̐ llabhet
kapitīrthe naraḥ snātvā na viyoniṃ samālabhet || 18 ||
gāyatryāṃ ca sarasvatyāṃ snāto mucyeta kilbiṣāt
ṛṇamocanatīrthādau snātvā mukto bhavedṛṇāt || 19 ||
ityetatsetutīrthānāṃ māhātmyaṃ gaditaṃ śubhe
paṭhatāṃ śṛṇvatāṃ caiva sarvapātakanāśanam || 20 ||
iti śrībṛhannāradīyapurāṇe bṛhadupākhyāne uttarabhāge vasumohinīsaṃvāde setumāhātmyaṃ nāma ṣaṭsaptatitamo'dhyāyaḥ || 76 ||

atha narmadātīrthamāhātmyamārabhyate

<2.77>

atha saptasaptatitamo'dhyāyaḥ
mohinyuvāca -
śrutaṃ mayā dvijaśreṣṭha setumāhātmyamuttamam
adhunā śrotumicchāmi narmadātīrthasaṅgraham || 1 ||
vasuruvāca -
śṛṇu mohini vakṣyāmi narmadobhayatīragam
catuḥśataṃ mukhyatamaṃ proktaṃ tīrthakadambakam || 2 ||
ekādaśottare tīre dakṣiṇe ca triviṃśatiḥ
pañcatriṃśattamaḥ prokto revāsāgarasaṅgamaḥ || 3 ||
oṃkāratīrthaṃ parito nagādamarakaṇṭakāt
krośadvaye sarvadikṣu sārddhakoṭitrayī sthitā || 4 ||
koṭirekā tu tīrthānāṃ kapilāsaṅgame sthitā
aśokavanikāyāṃ ca tīrthalakṣaṃ pratiṣṭhitam || 5 ||
śatamaṅgāragartāyāḥ kubjāyā ayutaṃ tathā
sahasraṃ vāyusaṅge tu sarasvatyāḥ śataṃ sthitam || 6 ||
śatadvayaṃ śuklatīrthe sahasraṃ viṣṇutīrthake
māhiṣmatyāṃ ca sāhasraṃ śūlabhede'yutaṃ viduḥ || 7 ||
devagrāme sahasraṃ colūke saptaśatī sthitā
tīrthānyaṣṭottaraśataṃ maṇinadyāśca saṅgame || 8 ||
vaidyanāthe ca tāvanti tāvantyeva ghaṭeśvare
sārddhalakṣaṃ ca tīrthānāṃ sthitaṃ revābdhisaṅgame || 9 ||
aṣṭāśītisahasrāṇi vyāse dvīpaśatāni ca
saṅgame tu karañjāyāḥ sthitamaṣṭottarāyutam || 10 ||
eraṇḍīsaṅgame tadvattīrthānyaṣṭādhikaṃ śatam
dhūtapāpe'ṣṭaṣaṣṭiśca sārddhakoṭiśca kokile || 11 ||
sahasraṃ romakeśe ca dvādaśārke sahasrakam
lakṣāṣṭake sahasre dve śuklatīrthe nareśvari || 12 ||
saṅgameṣu tu sarveṣu śatamaṣṭādhikaṃ viduḥ
kāveryāḥ saṅgame nande tīrthapañcaśatī sthitā || 13 ||
bhṛgoḥ kṣetre ca tīrthānāṃ koṭirekā vyavasthitā
bhārabhūtyāṃ ca tīrthānāṃ śatamaṣṭottaraṃ sthitam || 14 ||
akrūreśe sārddhaśataṃ vimaleśe daśāyutam
sā sārddhakoṭirityeṣā tīrthasaṃjñā ca nārmade || 15 ||
daśādityasya nava ca kapilasyāṣṭa vai vidhoḥ
nandinaḥ koṭisaṃjñāni tathaivāṣṭa śubhānane || 16 ||
nāgāgnisiddhāvartāni saptasaṃkhyāni mohini
kedārendri yavārīśanaṃdidaivāni pañca vai || 17 ||
yameśā vaidyanāthāśca vāmanāṅgārakeśvarāḥ
sārasvatā munīśāśca dārukeśāśca gautamāḥ || 18 ||
catvāra eva gaditāstrayo vai vimaleśvarāḥ
sahasrayajñabhīṣmeśāssvarṇatīrthāni cāpi hi || 19 ||
dhautapāpakarañjeśaṛṇamuktiguhāhvayam
daśāśvamedhanandākhyaṃ manmatheśākhyabhārgavam || 20 ||
parāśarāyonisaṃjñaṃ vyāsākhyapitṛnandikam
gopeśamāruteśākhyaṃ jaṅgaleśākhyaśuklakam || 21 ||
akṣareśaṃ pippaleśaṃ māṇḍavyadīpakeśvaram
uttareśamaśokeśaṃ yodhaneśaṃ ca rauhiṇam || 22 ||
lukeśaṃ ca dvisaṅkhyākaṃ pratyekaṃ gaditaṃ śubhe
saikonaviṃśatiśataṃ tīrthānyekaikaśaḥ śubhe || 23 ||
stabakeṣu ca tīrthāni dviśataṃ ca caturddaśam
śaivānyetāni tīrthāni vaiṣṇavāni dviviṃśatiḥ || 24 ||
brāhmāṇi sarvatīrthāni śāktānyaṣṭau ca viṃśatiḥ
teṣu sapta ca mātṝṇāṃ trīṇi brāhmyāḥ śubhānane || 25 ||
vaiṣṇavyā dve tathā bhadre raudrī śeṣeṣu saṃsthitā
tathaikaṃ kṣetrapālasya tīrthamuktaṃ śubhānane || 26 ||
avāntarāṇi guhyāni prakaṭāni ca mohini
sārddhatrikoṭitīrthāni gaditānīha vāyunā || 27 ||
divi bhuvyantarikṣe ca revāyāṃ tāni santi ca
yastveteṣu mahābhāge yatra kutrāpi mānavaḥ || 28 ||
snānaṃ karoti śuddhātmā sa labheduttamāṃ gatim
snānaṃ dānaṃ japo homo vedādhyayanamarcanam || 29 ||
sarvamakṣayatāṃ yāti narmadāyāstaṭe kṛtam
tryahātsārasvataṃ toyaṃ saptāhādyāmunaṃ sati || 30 ||
gāṅgaṃ sakṛtplavātpuṇyaṃ darśanādeva nārmadam
ityeṣa kathito devi narmadātīrthasaṅgrahaḥ || 31 ||
smaratāmapi martyānāṃ mahāpātakaśāntidaḥ
ya imaṃ śṛṇuyānmartyo narmadātīrthasaṅgraham || 32 ||
śrāvayedvā paṭhedbhadre so'pi pāpaiḥ pramucyate
yadgṛhe likhitaṃ caitanmāhātmyaṃ narmadābhavam || 33 ||
vidyate'bhyarcitaṃ tatra na mārī nāgnijaṃ bhayam
na rājacauraśatrubhyo bhayaṃ rogabhayaṃ na ca || 34 ||
tadgṛhaṃ pūryyate lakṣmyā dhanairddhānyairnirantaram
putrapautrādikānāṃ ca vivāhādyaiḥ sumaṅgalam || 35 ||
iti śrībṛhannāradīyapurāṇe bṛhadupākhyāne uttarabhāge vasumohinīsaṃvāde narmadātīrthamāhātmyaṃ nāma saptasaptatitamo'dhyāyaḥ || 77 ||

iti narmadātīrthamāhātmyaṃ samāptam

athāvantīmāhātmyamārabhyate

<2.78>

athāṣṭasaptatitamo'dhyāyaḥ
mohinyuvāca -
atyadbhutamidaṃ vipra māhāmtyaṃ narmadābhavam
śrutaṃ tvayā nigaditaṃ nṛṇāṃ pāpavināśanam || 1 ||
adhunā tu mahābhāga brūhi me'vantisambhavam
māhātmyaṃ devavandyasya mahākālasya ca prabho || 2 ||
vasuruvāca -
śṛṇu bhadre pravakṣyāmi hyavantyāḥ puṇyadaṃ nṛṇām
māhātmyaṃ sarvapāpaghnaṃ yathāvatparikīrtitam || 3 ||
mahākālavanaṃ puṇyaṃ tapaḥsthānamanuttamam
yatra devo mahākālaḥ sthitastapasi nityadā || 4 ||
mahākālavanātkṣetraṃ nāparaṃ vidyate bhuvi
yatra gatvā naro devi sparddhate daivataiḥ saha || 5 ||
kapālamocanaṃ nāma yatra tīrthaṃ sulocane
tatra snātvā naro bhaktyā brahmahāpi viśudhyati || 6 ||
tathā kalakaleśākhyaṃ devamabhyarcya mānavaḥ
vivāde jayamāpnoti kāryasiddhiṃ ca santatam || 7 ||
atrānyadapsaraḥkuṇḍaṃ tīrthaṃ tatrāpluto naraḥ
subhago bhogavānbhūyātsākṣātkandarppasannibhaḥ || 8 ||
mahiṣākhye tathā kuṇḍe snātaḥ śatrūñjayedra ṇe
snātastu rudra sarasi rudra loke mahīyate || 9 ||
kuṇḍaveśvaramāsādya samabhyarcya vidhānataḥ
vyāpāre lābhamāpnoti jāyate ca śivapriyaḥ || 10 ||
vidyādharāhvaye tīrthe nara snātvā viśudhyati
mārkaṇḍeśvaramabhyarcya dīrghāyuśca dhanī bhavet || 11 ||
sampūjya śītalāṃ devīṃ naraḥ kālavane sthitām
visphoṭakabhayaṃ naiva kadācittasya jāyate || 12 ||
svargadvāraṃ samāsādya snātvābhyarcya sadāśivam
naro na durgatiṃ yāti svargaloke mahīyate || 13 ||
rājasthalaṃ naraḥ prāpya tataḥ sāmudri ke plutaḥ
snānasya sarvatīrthānāṃ labhate phalamuttamam || 14 ||
śaṅkarasya tathā vāpyāṃ snātvā niyamavānnaraḥ
prāpyeha vāñchitān bhogānante rudra puraṃ vrajet || 15 ||
śaṅkarādityamabhyarcya naraḥ syādduṣpradharṣaṇaḥ
snātastu nīlagaṅgāyāṃ devīṃ gandhavatīṃ naraḥ || 16 ||
sampūjya bhaktibhāvena sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate
daśāśvamedhike snātvā vājimedhaphalaṃ labhet || 17 ||
atha martyaḥ samāsādya ekānaṃśāṃ sureśvarīm
sampūjya gandhapuṣpādyaiḥ sarvānkāmānavāpnuyāt || 18 ||
harasiddhiṃ naro'bhyarcya sarvasiddhīśvaro bhavet
piśācakādikānmartyaḥ samabhyarcya caturddaśa || 19 ||
sarvānkāmānavāpnoti nātra kāryā vicāraṇā
hanumatkeśvaraṃ prārcetsnātvā rudra sarovare || 20 ||
yo naraḥ śraddhayā yuktaḥ sa labhetsampado'khilāḥ
vālmīkeśvaramabhyarcya sarvavidyānidhirbhavet || 21 ||
śukreśvarādiliṅgāni yo'cayecchraddhayā naraḥ
sa syādakhilabhogāḍhyaḥ sarvarogavivarjitaḥ || 22 ||
pañceśānaṃ samabhyarcya syānnaraḥ sarvasaddhibhāk
kuśasthaladyaṃ parikramya vāñchitaṃ labhate phalam || 23 ||
akrūreśaṃ tu sampūjya krūrebhyo'pyabhayaṃ labhet
mandākinyāṃ samāplutya gaṅgāsnānaphalaṃ labhet || 24 ||
aṅkapādaṃ naro'bhyarcya śivasyānucaro bhavet
candrā dityaṃ prapūjyātha bhogānnānāvidhām̐ llabhet || 25 ||
karabheśvaramabhyarcya yānasaukhyamavāpnuyāt
laḍḍukapriyavighneśaṃ samabhyarcya sukhī bhavet || 26 ||
kusumeśādikānprārcya sarvānbhogānsamaśnute
yajñavāpyāṃ naraḥ snātvā mārkaṇḍeśaṃ samarcya ca || 27 ||
sarvayajñaphalaṃ labdhvā yugamekaṃ vaseddivi
somavatyāṃ naraḥ snātvābhyarcya someśvaraṃ sati || 28 ||
vāñchitām̐ llabhate kāmānihāmutra ca mohini
yātanākalane snātvā yātanāṃ naiva paśyati || 29 ||
narakeśaṃ samabhyarcya svargalokagatiṃ labhet
kedāreśaṃ tataḥ prārcya rāmeśvaramathāpi vā || 30 ||
saubhāgyeśaṃ narādityaṃ labhate vāñchitaṃ phalam
keśavārkaṃ tu sampūjya naraḥ syātkeśavapriyaḥ || 31 ||
śaktibhede tataḥ snātvā mucyate'tyugrasaṅkaṭāt
svarṇakṣurabrahmavāpyāṃ snātvābhyarcyābhayeśvaram || 32 ||
agastyeśaṃ ca vidhije sampadāmayanaṃ bhavet
oṃkāreśādiliṅgāni yo naraḥ samyagarcayet || 33 ||
sa labhedakhilānkāmānmaheśasya prasādataḥ
mahākālavane devi liṅgasaṅkhyā na vidyate || 34 ||
yatra tatra sthitaṃ liṅgaṃ sampūjya syācchivapriyaḥ
tathā kanakaśṛṅgāhvā kuśasthalyapyavantikā || 35 ||
tathā padmāvatī devī kumudvatyujjayinyapi
pratikalpābhidhā bhinnā viśālākhyāmarāvatī || 36 ||
śiprāyāṃ vai naraḥ snātvā yo maheśaṃ samarcayet
sa labhetsakalānkāmandevayostu prasādataḥ || 37 ||
snātvā tu gomatīkuṇḍe svargatiṃ labhate naraḥ
kuṇḍe tu vāmane snātvā stauti nāmasahasrataḥ || 38 ||
śrīdharaṃ sarvadeveśaṃ yaḥ sa sākṣāddharirbhuvi
snātvā vīreśasarasi yo'cayetkālabhairavam || 39 ||
sa sarvāḥ sampado bhuktvā śivalokamavāpnuyāt
yaḥ kuṭumbeśvaraṃ prāpya pūjayedupacārakaiḥ || 40 ||
samprāpya vividhānkāmānante svargagatiṃ labhet
devaprayāgasarasi yo'cayeddevamādhavam || 41 ||
sa bhaktiṃ mādhave prāpya padaṃ viṣṇoḥ samāpnuyāt
kakarājasya tīrthe tu snātvā prayatamānasaḥ || 42 ||
sarvarogavinirmukto dhanī bhogī bhavetsati
antargṛhasya yātrāyāṃ vighneśaṃ bhairavaṃ hyumām || 43 ||
rudrā dityānsurānanyānyo'cayecchraddhayā naraḥ
yathālabdhopacārādyaiḥ sa bhavetsvargalokabhāk || 44 ||
rudra saraḥprabhṛtiṣu tīrthānyanyāni bhāmini
bahūni teṣu cābhyarcya śaṅkaraṃ syātsukhī naraḥ || 45 ||
aṣṭatīrthyāṃ naraḥ snātvā sāṅgaṃ yātrāphalaṃ labhet
kālāraṇyasya vidhije satyaṃ satyaṃ mayoditam || 46 ||
etatte sarvamākhyātaṃ māhātmyaṃ pāpanāśanam
avantyā yannaraḥ śrutvā sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate || 47 ||
iti śrībṛhannāradīyapurāṇe bṛhadupākhyāne uttarabhāge vasumohinīsaṃvāde avantikāmāhātyaṃ nāmāṣṭasaptatitamo'dhyāyaḥ || 78 ||

ity avantīmāhātmyaṃ samāptam

atha mathurāmāhātmyamārabhyate

<2.79>

athaikonāśītitamo'dhyāyaḥ
mohinyuvāca -
śrutaṃ hyavantyā māhātmyaṃ vaso pāpaharaṃ nṛṇām
adhunā śrotumicchāmi māhātmyaṃ mathurābhavam || 1 ||
vasuruvāca -
śṛṇu mohini vakṣyāmi mathurāyāḥ śubhāvaham
vaibhavaṃ yatra bhagavāñjātaḥ padmabhuvārthitaḥ || 2 ||
āvirbhūya vibhustatra samprāpto nandagokulam
tatra sthitvākhilāḥ krīḍāścakāra saha gopakaiḥ || 3 ||
hatvā ca kaṃsaprahitānpūtanādīnniśācarān
vijahāra sa gopībhirvaneṣu dvādaśasvapi || 4 ||
vaneṣu yāni tīrthāni santi yāni ca māthure
tāni te'ha pravakṣyāmi śṛṇu mohini sāmpratam || 5 ||
ādyaṃ madhuvanaṃ nāma snāto yatra narottamaḥ
santarpya devarṣipitṝnviṣṇuloke mahīyate || 6 ||
atha tālāhvayaṃ devi dvitīyaṃ vanamuttamam
yatra snāto naro bhaktyā kṛtakṛtyaḥ prajāyate || 7 ||
kumudākhyaṃ tṛtīyaṃ tu yatra snātvā sulocane
labhate vāñchitānkāmānihāmutra ca modate || 8 ||
tataḥ kāmyavanaṃ nāma caturthaṃ parikīrtitam
bahutīrthānvitaṃ yatra gatvā syādviṣṇulokabhāk || 9 ||
yattatra vimalaṃ kuṇḍaṃ sarvatīrthottamottamam
tatra snāto naro bhadre labhate vaiṣṇavaṃ padam || 10 ||
pañcamaṃ bahulākhyaṃ tu vanaṃ pāpavināśanam
yatra snātastu manujaḥ sarvānkāmānavāpnuyāt || 11 ||
asti bhadra vanaṃ nāma ṣaṣṭhaṃ snāto'tra mānavaḥ
kṛṣṇadevaprasādena sarvabhadrā ṇi paśyati || 12 ||
khādiraṃ tu vanaṃ devi saptamaṃ yatra mānavaḥ
snānamātreṇa labhate tadviṣṇoḥ paramaṃ padam || 13 ||
mahāvanaṃ cāṣṭamaṃ te sadaiva harivallabham
taddṛṣṭvā manujo bhaktyā śakraloke mahīyate || 14 ||
lohajaṅghaṃ tu navamaṃ vanaṃ yatrāpluto naraḥ
mahāviṣṇuprasādena bhuktiṃ muktiṃ ca vindati || 15 ||
bilvāraṇyaṃ tu daśamaṃ yatra snātaḥ sumadhyame
śaivaṃ vā vaiṣṇavaṃ vāpi yāti lokaṃ nijecchayā || 16 ||
ekādaśaṃ tu bhāṇḍīraṃ yogināmativallabham
yatra snāto naro bhaktyā sarvapāpairvimucyate || 17 ||
vṛndāvanaṃ dvādaśaṃ tu sarvapāpanikṛntanam
yatsamaṃ na dharāpṛṣṭhe vanamastyaparaṃ sati || 18 ||
yatra snātastu manujo devarṣipitṛtarpaṇam
kṛtvā ṛṇatrayānmukto viṣṇuloke mahīyate || 19 ||
viṃśatiryojanānāṃ tu māthuraṃ parimaṇḍalam
yatra kutrāplutastatra viṣṇubhaktimavāpnuyāt || 20 ||
tanmadhye mathurā nāma purī sarvottamottamā
yasyā darśanamātreṇa bhaktiṃ vindati mādhave || 21 ||
viśrāntisaṃjñakaṃ yatra tīrtharatnaṃ nareśvari
tatra snāto naro bhaktyā vaiṣṇavaṃ labhate padam || 22 ||
viśrānternikaṭe dakṣe vimuktaṃ tīrthamuttam
tatra snāto naro bhaktyā muktimāpnoti niścitam || 23 ||
tato'pi dakṣiṇe bhāge rāmatīrthaṃ janeśvari
yatra snāto naro'jñānabandhānmukto bhaveddhruvam || 24 ||
saṃsāramokṣaṇaṃ tasmāddakṣiṇe tīrthamuttamam
tatra snātvā tu manujo viṣṇuloke mahīyate || 25 ||
prayāgākhyaṃ tato dakṣe tīrthaṃ tridaśadurlabham
snātastatra naro devi agniṣṭomaphalaṃ labhet || 26 ||
tataḥ kanakhalaṃ tīrthaṃ yatra snāto naraḥ sati
svargamāsādya dehānte modate nandanādiṣu || 27 ||
taddakṣe tindukaṃ tīrthaṃ yasminsnāto narottamaḥ
rājasūyaphalaṃ prāpya modate divi devavat || 28 ||
tataḥ paraṃ paṭusvāmitīrthaṃ bhāskaravallabham
snātvā yatra raviṃ dṛṣṭvā bhuktabhogo divaṃ vrajet || 29 ||
tasmāddakṣiṇato bhadre dhruvatīrthamanuttamam
yatra snāto dhruvaṃ dṛṣṭvā labhate vaiṣṇavaṃ padam || 30 ||
dhruvatīrthāttato dakṣe tīrthaṃ saptarṣisevitam
tatra snātvā munīndṛṣṭvā ṛṣiloke pramodate || 31 ||
dakṣiṇe ṛṣitīrthasya mokṣatīrthamanuttamam
yatra vai snānamātreṇa mucyate sarvapāpataḥ || 32 ||
taddakṣe bodhinītīrthaṃ snātvā yatra tu mānavaḥ
datvā piṇḍaṃ pitṛbhyaśca nayettāṃstridaśālayam || 33 ||
taddakṣe koṭitīrthaṃ vai yatra snānena mānavaḥ
sarvapāpavinirmukto viṣṇulokamavāpnuyāt || 34 ||
viśrānteruttare bhāge tīrthamastyasikuṇḍakam
yatra snāto naro devi vaiṣṇavaṃ labhate padam || 35 ||
asikuṇḍasya saumye tu navatīrthaṃ sulocane
yatra vai snānato martyaḥ svargaloke mahīyate || 36 ||
tīrthaṃ saṃyamanaṃ nāma tata uttarataḥ sthitam
tatra snānena dānena yamalokaṃ na paśyati || 37 ||
taduttare paraṃ tīrthaṃ dhārāyatanasaṃjñakam
tatra snātvā tu manujaḥ pitṛbhiḥ saha modate || 38 ||
taduttare nāgatīrthaṃ yatra snānena mohini
sarpebhyo hyabhayaṃ labdhvā svarge loke mahīyate || 39 ||
taduttare brahmalokaṃ ghaṇṭābharaṇakāhvayam
snānātpāpāpahaṃ tīrthaṃ brahmalokagatipradam || 40 ||
asmātsaumye paraṃ tīrthe somākhyaṃ yatra samplutaḥ
somalokamavāpnoti vipāpo manujottamaḥ || 41 ||
snātvā prācīsarasvatyāṃ tasmāduttarataḥ śubhe
yatra vai snānamātreṇa naro vāgīśvaro bhavet || 42 ||
taduttare cakratīrthaṃ yatra snātastu mānavaḥ
jitvā śatrugaṇaṃ svarge modate yugasaptakam || 43 ||
daśāśvamedhikaṃ tīrthaṃ tasmāduttarataḥ sthitam
yatra snānena subhage vājimedhaphalaṃ labhet || 44 ||
gokarṇākhyaṃ śivaṃ tatra sampūjya vidhivannaraḥ
sarvānkāmānavāpyānte śivaloke mahīyate || 45 ||
vighnarājāhvayaṃ tīrthaṃ tasmāduttarataḥ sthitam
yatra snātvā hyavighnena sarvakarmaphalaṃ labhet || 46 ||
taduttare hyanantākhyaṃ tīrthaṃ tatrāpluto naraḥ
caturviṃśatitīrthānāṃ māthurāṇāṃ phalaṃ labhet || 47 ||
mathurāyāṃ mahābhāge sākṣāddevo hariḥ sthitaḥ
caturvyūhasvarūpeṇa māthurāṇāṃ vimuktidaḥ || 48 ||
ekā vārāhamūrtiśca parā nārāyaṇāhvayā
vāmanākhyā tṛtīyā ca caturthī haladhāriṇī || 49 ||
caturvyūhadharaṃ dṛṣṭvā samabhyarcya vidhānataḥ
naro muktimavāpnoti nātra kāryā vicāraṇā || 50 ||
raṅgeśaṃ cāpi bhūteśaṃ mahāvidyāṃ ca bhairavam
dṛṣṭvā sampūjya vidhivattīrthayātrāphalaṃ labhet || 51 ||
catuḥsāmudri ke kūpe kubjāyāśca gaṇeśituḥ
tathā kṛṣṇākhyagaṅgāyāṃ snātvā mucyeta pātakāt || 52 ||
sarvasya māthurākhyasya maṇḍalasya śubhānane
ādhipatye sthito devaḥ keśavaḥ kleśanāśanaḥ || 53 ||
adṛṣṭvā keśavaṃ bhadre māthure puṇyamaṇḍale
janurnirarthakaṃ tasya saṃsaredbhavasāgare || 54 ||
anyānyasaṅkhyatīrthāni tatra santi śubhānane
snātvā teṣvapi dattvā ca kiñcittatra sthitāya ca || 55 ||
naro na durgatiṃ yāti satyaṃ tubhyaṃ mayoditam
mathurāyāśca māhātmyaṃ śrāvayedyaḥ śṛṇoti ca
so'pi bhaktiṃ harau labdhvā sarvānkāmānavāpnuyāt || 56 ||
iti śrībṛhannāradīyapurāṇe bṛhadupākhyāne uttarabhāge vasumohinīsaṃvāde mathurāmāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāmaikonāśītitamo'dhyāyaḥ || 79 ||

iti mathurāmāhātmyaṃ samāptam

atha vṛndāvanamāhātmyamārabhyate

<2.80>

athāśītitamo'dhyāyaḥ
mohinyuvāca -
mathurāyāstu māhātmyaṃ vanānāṃ cāpi mānada
śrutaṃ vṛndāvanasyāpi rahasyaṃ kiñcidīraya || 1 ||
vṛndāraṇyaṃ bhuvo brahmankīrtirūpaṃ rahogatam
tacchrotuṃ mama vāñchāsti tannirūpaya vistarāt || 2 ||
vasuruvāca -
śṛṇu devi rahasyaṃ me vṛndāraṇyasamudbhavam
yanna kasmaicidākhyātaṃ mayā prāpya gurūttamāt || 3 ||
gurave kathitaṃ bhadre nāradena mahātmanā
vṛndayā nāradāyoktaṃ rahasyaṃ gopikāpateḥ || 4 ||
tatte'ha sampravakṣyāmi jagaduddhārakāraṇam
ekadā nārado lokānparyaṭanbhagavatpriyaḥ || 5 ||
vṛndāraṇyaṃ samāsādya tasthau puṣpasarastaṭe
paścimottarato devi māthure maṇḍale sthitam || 6 ||
vṛndāraṇyaṃ turīyāṃśaṃ gopikeśarahaḥsthalam
govarddhano yatra giriḥ sakhisthalasamīpataḥ || 7 ||
vṛndāyāstattapo'raṇyaṃ nandigrāmānuyāmunam
taṭe tu yāmune ramye ramyaṃ vṛndāvanaṃ sati || 8 ||
puṇyaṃ tatrāpi subhage supuṇyaṃ kausumaṃ saraḥ
vṛndāyāstu taṭe ramye āśramo'tisukhāvahaḥ || 9 ||
nityaṃ viśramate yatra madhyāhne sakhibhirhariḥ
muhūrtaṃ sa tu viśramya snigdhacchāyatarostale || 10 ||
śītalaṃ puṣpasaraso vāryupaspṛśya nāradaḥ
kṛtvā mādhyāhnikaṃ karma tasthau tatra sarastaṭe || 11 ||
tatra vṛndāśrame ramye gopyo gopāśca mohini
āyānti vargaśo yānti nāradasya vipaśyataḥ || 12 ||
athaivaṃ yāmamekaṃ tu tatra sthitvā tu nāradaḥ
praharārddhāvaśiṣṭe'hni viveśāśramamadbhutam || 13 ||
yatra vṛndā sthitā devī kṛṣṇabhaktiparāyaṇā
samāgatānāṃ satkāraṃ vidadhānā phalādibhiḥ || 14 ||
tāṃ dṛṣṭvā tāpasīṃ bhadre nāradaḥ sādhusammataḥ
namaskṛtya vinamrāṅgo niṣasāda dharātale || 15 ||
tataḥ sā dhyānayogānte samunmīlya vilocane
āsanaṃ sandideśātho'tithaye nāradāya vai || 16 ||
tataḥ sa nāradastatra satkṛto vṛndayāvasat
rahasyaṃ gopikeśasya tasyā jijñāsurādarāt || 17 ||
tayā kṛtāṃ satkṛtiṃ tu svīkṛtya vidhinandanaḥ
suprasannāntarāṃ vṛndāṃ jñātvā hārdaṃ vyajijñapat || 18 ||
sā tu tadvāñchitaṃ jñātvā dhyānayogena bhāmini
svasakhīṃ mādhavīṃ tatra samāhūyābravīdidam || 19 ||
mādhavi priyametasya nāradasya mahātmanaḥ
sampādaya yathā mahyamāśramasya supuṇyatā || 20 ||
svāśramaṃ hyāgatasyaiva yo na sampādayetpriyam
niṣphalo hyāśramastasya pherurājagṛhopamaḥ || 21 ||
atha sā mādhavī devī nītvā nāradamājñayā
svādhiṣṭhātryāstu vṛndāyāḥ sarasastaṭamuttamam || 22 ||
paścimottaratastasmin snātuṃ taṃ sandideśa ha
tatastadājñayā bhadre nārado devadarśanaḥ || 23 ||
nimamajja jale tasmindhyāyañchrīkṛṣṇasaṅgamam
nimajjamāne sarasi nārade munisattame || 24 ||
yayau vṛndāntike bhadre saṃvidhāya tadīpsitam
athāsau nāradastatra sannimajyodgatastadā || 25 ||
dadarśa nijamātmānaṃ vanitārūpamadbhutam
tatastu parito vīkṣya nāradī sā śucismitā || 26 ||
pūrvottarāyāṃ tiṣṭhantī āhvayantī kareṅgitaiḥ
dadarśa vanitāṃ ramyāṃ bhūṣitāṃ bhūṣaṇottamaiḥ || 27 ||
tatastayā samāhūtā nāradī sā tadantikam
prāptā viśvāsitā svasthā nītā cāpi sthalāntaram || 28 ||
ratnaprākārakhacite bhavane vanitākule
prāpayya tāṃ nivṛttāsau sāpi tābhiḥ susatkṛtā || 29 ||
viśākhādisakhīvṛndairāśvāsyā'lyaikayā tataḥ
prāpitābhyantare devi sāpaśyadgopikeśvaram || 30 ||
dūtyāṃ tasyāṃ nivṛttāyāṃ samāhūtā priyeṇa sā
nāradī praṇipatyeśaṃ lajjā namrāntikaṃ yayau || 31 ||
rasikena samāśliṣya ramayitvā visarjjitā
krameṇaiva tu samprāptā sā punaḥ kausumaṃ saraḥ || 32 ||
sā punastatra mādhavyā majjitā dakṣapaścime
puṃbhāvamabhisamprāpto nārado vismito'bhavat || 33 ||
tato vṛndājñayā tatra sarasaḥ pūrvadakṣiṇe
ekāntaṃ tapa āsthāya tasthau tatprekṣaṇotsukaḥ || 34 ||
evaṃ tapasyatastasya nāradasya mahātmanaḥ
vṛndayā preṣitairvṛttiṃ nijāṃ kalpayataḥ phalaiḥ || 35 ||
ekadā nāradastatra vicarannāśramāntare
śuśrāva saubhagaṃ śabdaṃ kayācitsamudīritam || 36 ||
tacchrutvā kautukāviṣṭo nārado'dhyātmadarśanaḥ
vicinvanvanamāsthāya na dadarśa ca tatpadam || 37 ||
tataḥ sa vismayāviṣṭo vṛndāṃ papraccha sādaram
sāpi tasmai samācakhyau kubjāvṛttāntamāditaḥ || 38 ||
bhūmyantaragṛhasthānā kubjā nārī varā vibhoḥ
kāmamekāntake sveśaṃ samupācarati svayam || 39 ||
na tāṃ ko'pi muniśreṣṭha vijānāti mayā vinā
tataḥ saṃkṣepato vakṣye yāṃ didṛkṣustapo'caraḥ || 40 ||
prātaḥ prabodhito mātrā snātvā bhuktvānugānvitaḥ
gocāraṇāya vipine vṛndāvana upāviśat || 41 ||
sakhibhirgopakaiḥ krīḍāṃ kurvansaṃvārayaṃśca gāḥ
dvitraiḥ priyasakhairatra mamāśrama upāvrajat || 42 ||
mayā prakalpitairvatsabhavane sārvakāmike
phalamūlādibhirbhakṣyaistarpitaḥ priyayā'svapat || 43 ||
susakhyā rādhayā tatra sevyamāno vrajapriyaḥ
sārddhayāmaṃ viharati nikuñjeṣu pṛthak pṛthak || 44 ||
rādhādibhistatra supto vījitaḥ śayanaṃ gataḥ
sārddhayāme svayaṃ buddho nijāḥ saṃmānya tāḥ priyāḥ || 45 ||
gopairgobhirvṛtaḥ sāyaṃ vrajaṃ yāti praharṣitaḥ
sakhyaṃ sakhisthalaṃ prāpya priyāṃ sañcaya rādhikām || 46 ||
tayā saha viśālākṣyaḥ svagehānyānti cānvaham
evaṃ gatāgataṃ kurvallīm̐ lāṃ nityamupāgataḥ || 47 ||
mayaiva dṛśyate vatsa nāpi brahmabhavādibhiḥ
mayāpyalakṣitaṃ vatsa kubjāsaṅketavaibhavam || 48 ||
prītapriyoktyā jānāmi sugopyaṃ pravadāmi te
aṅgarāgārpaṇātpuṇyātprāptā saṅketamuttamam || 49 ||
sadā sā sevanavyagrā saikaikenāpyanekadhā
śatakoṭimitānyevaṃ mithunāni vasanti hi || 50 ||
kubjākṛṣṇānurūpāṇi nānākrīḍāparāṇi ca
sambhūtānyādyamithunātsthāvaraṃ bhāṣayantyapi || 51 ||
gatāgatavihīnāni nityaṃ navanavāni ca
tadagamyaṃ tṛtīyasya dvitīyasyaikatāṃ gatam || 52 ||
rūpaṃ vilakṣaṇaṃ vipra sṛṣṭisthitilayaikalam
ekaivāhaṃ vijānāmi śrutaṃ śrutvā tvamapyatha || 53 ||
dagdhaḥ ṣaṭkarṇago mantra ityuktaṃ samupācara
śrutvaitaddurlabhaṃ so'pi vṛndoktyā nārado muniḥ || 54 ||
ubhayaṃ cintayanprāpto munistatraiva tatparam
etadra hasyaṃ vidhije viṣayaṃ guruśiṣyayoḥ || 55 ||
nva ko'pyaparo vetti dharmaḥ saivāvayorapi
eka eva vijānāti vaktuḥ śrotaikataḥ śubhe || 56 ||
tadekaṃ tattvamevāsti neha nānāsti kiñcana
gaditaṃ te mahābhāge rahasyaṃ gopikeśituḥ || 57 ||
prakāśācaritaṃ cāpi vakṣye samyaṅniśāmaya
yatra sandarśitaṃ tattvaṃ tvatpitre vidhinandini || 58 ||
tadbrahmakuṇḍametaddhi puṇyaṃ vṛndāvane vane
tatra yaḥ snāti manujo mūlaveṣaṃ vibhāvayan || 59 ||
vaibhavaṃ paśyate kiñcidevaṃ nityavihāriṇaḥ
śakreṇa jñātatattvena govindo yatra cintitaḥ || 60 ||
govindakuṇḍaṃ tadbhadre snātvā tatrāpi tallabhet
ekānekasvarūpeṇa yatra kuñjavihāriṇā || 61 ||
vallavībhiḥ samārabdho rāsastadapi tadvidham
yatra nandādayo gopā dadṛśurvaibhavaṃ vibhoḥ || 62 ||
tacca tattvaprakāśākhyaṃ tīrthaṃ śrīyamunājale
darśitaṃ yatra gopānāṃ kāliyasya vimardanam || 63 ||
tacca puṇyaṃ samākhyātaṃ tīrthaṃ pāpāpahaṃ nṛṇām
dāvāgnermocitā yatra sastrībāladhanārbhakāḥ || 64 ||
gopāḥ kṛṣṇena tatpuṇyaṃ tīrthaṃ snānādaghāpaham
yatra keśī hatastena līlayaiva hayākṛtiḥ || 65 ||
tatra snātastu manujo labhate dhāma vaiṣṇavabh
yatra duṣṭo vṛṣastena hatastatrābhavacchuciḥ || 66 ||
ariṣṭakuṇḍaṃ vikhyātaṃ snānamātreṇa muktidam
dhenuko'gho bako vatso vyomo lambāsuro'pi ca || 67 ||
hatāḥ kṛṣṇena līlāsu tatra tīrthāni yānyapi
teṣu snātvā naro bhaktaḥ santarpya pitṛdevatāḥ || 68 ||
labhate vāñchitānkāmān gopālasya prasādataḥ
suptaṃ bhuktaṃ vicaritaṃ śrutaṃ dṛṣṭaṃ vilakṣaṇam || 69 ||
kṛtaṃ yatra ca tatkṣetraṃ snānātsvargagatipradam
śrutaḥ sañcintito dṛṣṭo nataḥ śliṣṭaḥ stuto'thitaḥ || 70 ||
yatra puṇyanarairbhadre tacca tīrthaṃ gatipradam
yatra śrīrādhayā bhadre tapastaptaṃ sudāruṇam || 71 ||
tacchrīkuṇḍaṃ mahatpuṇyaṃ snāne dāne japādike
vatsatīrthe candra sarastathaivāpsarasāṃ saraḥ || 72 ||
rudra kuṇḍaṃ kāmakuṇḍaṃ paramaṃ mandiraṃ hareḥ
viśālālakanandāḍhyā nīpakhaṇḍaṃ manoharam || 73 ||
vimalaṃ dharmakuṇḍaṃ ca bhojanasthalameva ca
balasthānaṃ bṛhatsānuḥ saṅketasthānakaṃ hareḥ || 74 ||
naṃdigrāmaḥ kiśoryāśca kuṇḍaṃ kokilakānanam
śeṣaśāyipayo'bdhiśca krīḍādeśo'kṣayo vaṭaḥ || 75 ||
rāmakuṇḍaṃ cīracauryaṃ bhadra bhāṇḍīrabilvakam
mānāhvaṃ ca saraḥ puṇyaṃ pulinaṃ bhaktabhojanam || 76 ||
akrūraṃ tārkṣyagovindaṃ bahulāraṇyakaṃ śubhe
etadvṛndāvanaṃ nāma samantātpañcayojanam || 77 ||
supuṇyaṃ puṇyakṛjjuṣṭaṃ darśanādeva muktidam
yasya sandarśanaṃ devā vāñchanti ca sudurlabham || 78 ||
līlāmābhyantarīṃ dra ṣṭuṃ tapasāpi na ca kṣamāḥ
sarvatra saṅgamutsṛjya yastu vṛndāvanaṃ śrayet || 79 ||
na tasya durlabhaṃ kiñcittriṣu lokeṣu bhāmini
vṛndāvaneti nāmāpi yaḥ samuccarati priye || 80 ||
tasyāpi bhaktirbhavati satataṃ nandanandane
yatra vṛndāvane puṇye naranārīplavaṅgamāḥ || 81 ||
kṛmikīṭapataṅgādyāḥ khagā vṛkṣā nagā mṛgāḥ
samuccaranti satataṃ rādhākṛṣṇeti mohini || 82 ||
kṛṣṇamāyābhibhūtānāṃ kāmakaśmalacetasām
svapne'pi durlabhaṃ puṃsāṃ manye vṛndāvanekṣaṇam || 83 ||
vṛndāraṇyaṃ tu yairdṛṣṭaṃ naraiḥ sukṛtibhiḥ śubhe
taiḥ kṛtaṃ saphalaṃ janma kṛpāpātrāṇi te hareḥ || 84 ||
kiṃ punarbahunoktena śrutena vidhinandini
sevyaṃ vṛndāvanaṃ puṇyaṃ bhavyaṃ muktimabhīpsubhiḥ || 85 ||
dṛśyaṃ gamyaṃ ca saṃsekhyaṃ dhyeyaṃ vṛndāvanaṃ sadā
nāsti loke samaṃ tasya bhuvi kīrtivivarddhanam || 86 ||
yatra govarddhano nāma dvijaḥ kalpe purātane
viraktaḥ sarvasaṃsārāttaptavānparamaṃ tapaḥ || 87 ||
tadgatvā devi deveśo bhagavānviṣṇuravyayaḥ
krīḍāsthānaṃ nijaṃ prāpto varaṃ dātuṃ dvijanmane || 88 ||
taṃ dṛṣṭvā devadeveśaṃ śaṅkhacakragadādharam
vilasatkaustubhoraskaṃ makarākṛtikuṇḍalam || 89 ||
sukirīṭaṃ sukaṭakaṃ kalanūpurabhūṣitam
vanamālānivītāṅgaṃ śrīvatsāṅkitavakṣasam || 90 ||
pītakauśeyavasanaṃ navāmbudasamaprabham
sunābhiṃ sundaragrīvaṃ sukapolaṃ sunāsikam || 91 ||
sudvijaṃ susmitaṃ suṣṭhujānūrubhujamadhyakam
kṛpārṇavaṃ pramuditaṃ suprasannamukhāmbujam || 92 ||
dṛṣṭvā sa sahasotthāya nanāma bhuvi daṇḍavat
varaṃ brūhīti nirddiṣṭo prāha govarddhano harim || 93 ||
padbhyāmākramya matpṛṣṭhe tiṣṭha caiva varo mama
tacchrutvā bhaktavaśyo vai vicintya ca punaḥ punaḥ || 94 ||
tasthau tatpṛṣṭhamākramya tadā bhūyo dvijo'bravīt
nāhaṃ tvāmutsahe deva nijapṛṣṭhe jagatpate || 95 ||
avatārayituṃ tasmādevameva sthiro bhava
tataḥ prabhṛti viśvātmā tyaktvā govarddhanaṃ dvijam || 96 ||
girirūpadharaṃ yāti nityaṃ yogivanaṃ kvacit
kṛṣṇāvatāre bhagavān jñātvā govarddhanaṃ dvijam || 97 ||
samprāptaṃ nijasārūpyaṃ nandādyaiḥ samabhojayat
annakūṭena dohena tarpayitvācalaṃ dvijam || 98 ||
tṛṭparītaṃ samājñāya navameghānapāyayat
mitraṃ sa vāsudevasya sañjātaṃ tena karmaṇā || 99 ||
taṃ yo bhaktyā naro devi pūjayedupacārakaiḥ
pradakṣiṇaṃ parikrāmenna tasya punarudbhavaḥ || 100 ||
govarddhano giriḥ puṇyo jāto harinivāsataḥ
taṃ dṛṣṭvā darśanenālamanyapuṇyācalasya ca || 101 ||
yāmunaṃ pulinaṃ ramyaṃ kṛṣṇavikrīḍanāñcitam
tvameva brūhi subhage kvānyatra jagatītale || 102 ||
tasmātsarvaprayatnena tyaktvā vananadīgirīn
supuṇyānpuṇyadān nṝṇāṃ sevyaṃ vṛndāvanaṃ sadā || 103 ||
yamī puṇyā nadī yatra puṇyo govarddhano giriḥ
tatkiṃ vṛndāvanātpuṇyamaraṇyaṃ bhuvi vidyate || 104 ||
kalikalmaṣabhītānāṃ viṣayāsaktacetasām
nānyaṃ vṛndāvanātsevyamasti lokeṣvaṣi triṣu || 105 ||
yasminnityaṃ vicarati harirgopagogopikābhirbarhāpīḍī naṭavaravapuḥ karṇikārāvataṃsī
vaṃśīhaṃsīsvanajitaravo vaijayantīvṛtāṅgo nandasyāṅgāddhṛtamaṇigaṇo yaśca haṃso'hamākhyaḥ || 106 ||
yasya dhyānaṃ nagajaniyuto'harniśaṃ vai girīśo
bhaktiklinno rahasi kurute hyarddhanārīśvarākhyaḥ
gāyatrīṃ strīṃ hṛdayakuhare padmayonirvidhatte
netrairindro daśaśatamitairvīkṣate vai śacīṃ tām || 107 ||
aśrotreśo rahasi vanitāṃ rakṣati svāṃ rasajño vaṃśīnādaśravaṇajabhiyā kānyavārtā janānām
chedaṃ śoṣaṃ tadanu dahanaṃ bhedanaṃ prāpya yāsīcchrīgopīśāgharajanisudhāṃ sādaraṃ śīlayantī || 108 ||
yābhirvṛndāvanamanugato nandasūnuḥ kṣapāsu
reme candrā śukalitasamudyotabhadre nikuñje
tāsāṃ diṣṭaṃ kimahamadhunā varṇaye ballavīnāṃ
yāsāṃ sākṣāccaraṇajarajaḥ śrīśavidhyādyalabhyam || 109 ||
yatra prāptāstṛṇamṛgakhagā ye kṛmiprāṇivṛndāvṛndāraṇye vidhihararamābhyarhaṇīyā bhavanti
tatsamprāpyādvayapadarato brahmabhūyaṃ gataḥkau premasnigdho viharati sukhāmbhodhikallolamagnaḥ || 110 ||
yatra krūrāḥ sahajamasubhṛdvrātajātā visṛjya
vairaṃ svairaṃ suhṛda iva tatsaukhyamevāśrayaṃte
tatkiṃ prāpya prabhumiva janaḥ samparityajya gacchan
kvāpyanyatra prabhavati sukhī kṛṣṇamāyākaraṇḍe || 111 ||
vṛndāraṇyaṃ tadakhiladharāpuṇyarūpaṃ śrayanme
svāntaṃ dhvāntaṃ jagadidamadhaḥ kṛtya varvarti śaśvat
gopīnāthaḥ pratipadamapi premasaṅiklannacetā
nīcaṃ voccaṃ na ca gaṇayati proddharatyeva bhaktān || 112 ||
gopān gopīḥ khagamṛganagāgopagobhūrajāṃsi
smṛtvā dṛṣṭvā praṇamati jane premarajjvā nibaddhaḥ
dāsyaṃ bhakte kalayatitarāṃ tatkimanyaṃ vrajeśāt
sevyaṃ devaṃ gaṇaya vidhije'ha tu jānāmi naiva || 113 ||
etatsaṃkṣepataḥ proktaṃ vṛndāraṇyasamudbhavam
māhātmyaṃ vidhije tubhyaṃ vaktavyaṃ nāvaśeṣitam || 114 ||
saṃsārabhītairmanujairetadeva sadānaghaiḥ
śrotavyaṃ kīrtanīyaṃ ca smartavyaṃ dhyeyameva ca || 115 ||
vṛndāraṇyasya māhātmyaṃ yaḥ śṛṇoti naraḥ śuciḥ
kīrtayedvāpi vidhije so'pi viṣṇurna saṃśayaḥ || 116 ||
iti śrībṛhannāradīyapurāṇe bṛhadupākhyāne uttarabhāge vasumohinīsaṃvāde śrīvṛndāvanamāhātmyaṃ nāmāśītitamo'dhyāyaḥ || 80 ||

<2.81>

athaikāśītitamo'dhyāyaḥ
vasuruvāca -
yadetatkīrtitaṃ devi tīrthamāhātmyamuttamam
tallabhasva mahābhāge caritvā tīrthamaṇḍalam || 1 ||
ahaṃ brahmāṇamāmantrya pitaraṃ te nareśvari
vṛndāvanamupāgamya cariṣyāmi mṛgaiḥ saha || 2 ||
sūta uvāca -
ityuktvā mohinīṃ viprā vasustasyāḥ purohitaḥ
satkṛtya pūjito'bhīkṣṇaṃ brahmalokaṃ yayau tathā || 3 ||
sa gatvā tatra dhātāraṃ brahmāṇaṃ jagatāṃ vidhim
praṇamya mohinīvṛttaṃ taṃ kārtsnyena nyavedayat || 4 ||
tacchrutvā vacanaṃ brahmā brāhmaṇasya vasordvijāḥ
prasannaḥ prāha taṃ vatsa sukṛtaṃ hi tvayā kṛtam || 5 ||
yā mayā mohinī vipra devakāryārthamātmajā
niyuktā'kṛtakāryā sā tvayā śaptā kṣayaṃ gatā || 6 ||
bhūyo mamājñayā vatsa tvayā sā jīvitādhunā
nītā kṛtārthatāṃ tasmātko'nyastvatto'dhikaḥ kṛtī || 7 ||
yattvayā mahyamākhyātaṃ mohinīvṛttamuttamam
prasannastena dāsyāmi brūhi tubhyaṃ varaṃ dvija || 8 ||
ityuktaḥ sa dvijastena brahmaṇā lokabhāvinā
praṇamya vavre sa varaṃ vṛndāraṇyanivāsanam || 9 ||
tacchrutvā jagatāṃ dhātā smayamānacaturmukhaḥ
prāha prapannārtiharastathāstviti munīśvarāḥ || 10 ||
sa praṇamya vidhātāraṃ vasurthṛṣṭamanāstataḥ
vṛndāvanamupāvrajya tapaścakre samāhitaḥ || 11 ||
tapatastasya tu vasoḥ saṃvatsaragaṇā dvijāḥ
vyatīyuḥ pañcasāhasrāstatastuṣṭo hariḥ svayam || 12 ||
gopaiḥ priyasakhairdvitrairyuto'bhyāha dvijottamam
brūhi kiṃ vṛṇuṣe vipra tuṣṭo'ha tapasā tava || 13 ||
tataḥ sa vasurutthāya aṣṭāṅgāliṅgitāvaniḥ
prāha vṛndāvane deva vāsamicchāmi sarvadā || 14 ||
atha viṣṇurdvijaśreṣṭhā varaṃ tadvāñchitaṃ dadau
tenābhivandito bhūyo hyantardhānamupāgataḥ || 15 ||
sa dvijastatprabhṛtyevaṃ svecchārūpadharaḥ sthitaḥ
cintayansatataṃ devaṃ vṛndāraṇyakutūhalam || 16 ||
kadācidyamunātīre niviṣṭastaṃ vicintayan
dadarśa nāradaṃ prāptaṃ vṛndāraṇyaṃ vidheḥ sutam || 17 ||
sa taṃ dṛṣṭvā namaskṛtya paramaṃ gurumātmanaḥ
prapaccha vividhāndharmān bhagavadbhaktivarddhanān || 18 ||
sa tenaivaṃ susampṛṣṭo nārado'dhyātmadarśanaḥ
tasmai provāca nikhilaṃ bhaviṣyaccaritaṃ hareḥ || 19 ||
ekadāhaṃ gato vipra devaṃ kailāsavāsinam
dra ṣṭuṃ praṣṭuṃ bhaviṣyacca vṛndāvanarahasyakam || 20 ||
tataḥ praṇamya deveśaṃ tataḥ siddhaiḥ samāvṛtam
maheśaṃ svamahivyāptasarvabrahmāṇḍagolakam || 21 ||
apṛcchamīpsitaṃ bhadraṃ sa māṃ prāha smayanharaḥ
vaidhātra yattvayā pṛṣṭaṃ harervṛttamanāgatam || 22 ||
tatte vrabīmi yatpūrvaṃ śrutaṃ surabhivaktrataḥ
ekadā surabhirdṛṣṭā mayā golokamadhyagā || 23 ||
svasantānasamāyuktā suprītā snigdhamānasā
tataḥ pṛṣṭā bhaviṣyārthe gavāṃ mātā payasvinī || 24 ||
mahyaṃ provāca devarṣe bhaviṣyaccaritaṃ hareḥ
sukhamāste'dhunā devaḥ kṛṣṇo rādhāsamanvitaḥ || 25 ||
goloke'sminmaheśāna gopagopīsukhāvahaḥ
sa kadāciddharāloke māthure maṇḍale śiva || 26 ||
āvirbhūyādbhutāṃ krīḍāṃ vṛndāraṇye kariṣyati
vṛṣabhānusutā rādhā śrīdāmānaṃ hareḥ priyam || 27 ||
sakhāyaṃ virajāgehadvāḥsthaṃ kruddhā śapiṣyati
tataḥ so'pi mahābhāga rādhāṃ prati śapiṣyati || 28 ||
yāhi tvaṃ mānuṣaṃ lokaṃ mithaḥ śāpāddharāṃ tataḥ
prāpsyatyatha hariḥ paścādbrahmaṇā prārthitaḥ kṣitau || 29 ||
bhūbhāraharaṇāyaiva vāsudevo bhaviṣyati
vasudevagṛhe janma prāpya yādavanandanaḥ || 30 ||
kaṃsāsurabhiyā paścādvrajaṃ nandasya yāsyati
tatra yāto hariḥ prāptāṃ pūtanāṃ bālaghātinīm || 31 ||
viyojayiṣyati prāṇaiścakravātaṃ ca dānavam
vatsāsuraṃ mahākāyaṃ haniṣyati surārddanam || 32 ||
damitvā kāliyaṃ nāgaṃ yamyā uccāṭayiṣyati
duḥsahaṃ dhenukaṃ hatvā bakaṃ tadvadaghāsuram || 33 ||
dāvaṃ pradāvaṃ ca tathā pralambaṃ ca haniṣyati
brahmāṇamindraṃ varuṇaṃ pramattau dhanadātmajau || 34 ||
vimadānsa vidhāyeśo haniṣyati vṛṣāsuram
śaṅkhacūḍaṃ keśinaṃ ca vyomaṃ hatvā vraje vasan || 35 ||
ekādaśa samāstatra gopībhiḥ krīḍayiṣyati
tataśca mathurāṃ prāpya rajakaṃ sannihatya ca || 36 ||
kubjāmṛjvīṃ tataḥ kṛtvā dhanurbhaṅktvā gajottamam
hatvā kuvalayāpīḍaṃ mallāṃścāṇūrakādikān || 37 ||
kaṃsaṃ svamātulaṃ kṛṣṇo haniṣyati tataḥ param
vimucya pitarau baddhau yavaneśaṃ nihatya ca || 38 ||
jarāsandhabhayātkṛṣṇo dvārakāyāṃ samuṣyati
rukmiṇīṃ satyabhāmāṃ ca satyāṃ jāmbavatīṃ tathā || 39 ||
kaikeyīṃ lakṣmaṇāṃ mitravindāṃ kālindikāṃ vibhuḥ
dārānṣoḍaśasāhasrānbhaumaṃ hatvodvahiṣyati || 40 ||
pauṇḍrakaṃ śiśupālaṃ ca dantavaktraṃ vidūratham
śālvaṃ ca hatvā dvividaṃ balvalaṃ ghātayiṣyati || 41 ||
vajranābhaṃ sunābhaṃ ca sārddhaṃ vai ṣaṭpurālayaiḥ
triśarīraṃ tato daityaṃ haniṣyati varorjjitam || 42 ||
kauravānpāṇḍavāṃścāpi nimittamitaretaram
kṛtvā haniṣyati śiva bhūbhāraharaṇotsukaḥ || 43 ||
yadūnyadubhiranyonyaṃ saṃhṛtya svakulaṃ hariḥ
punaretannijaṃ dhāma sameṣyati ca sānugaḥ || 44 ||
etatte'bhihitaṃ śambho bhaviṣyaccaritaṃ hareḥ
gaccha dra kṣyasi tatsarvaṃ jagatītalage harau || 45 ||
tacchrutvā surabhervākyaṃ bhṛśaṃ prīto vidhātṛja
svasthānaṃ punarāyātastubhyaṃ cāpi mayoditam || 46 ||
tvaṃ ca dra kṣyasi kālena caritaṃ gokuleśituḥ
tacchrutvā śūlino vākyaṃ vasurthṛṣṭatanūruhaḥ || 47 ||
gāyanmādyan vibhuṃ tantryā ramayāmyāturaṃ jagat
etadbhaviṣyatkathitaṃ mayā tubhyaṃ dvijottama || 48 ||
yathā tu gautamastadvadahaṃ cāpi hite rataḥ
sūta uvāca -
ityuktvā nāradastasmai vasave sa dvijanmane || 49 ||
jagāma vīṇāṃ raṇayaṃścintayanyadunandanam
sa vasustadvacaḥ śrutvā vraje suprītamānasaḥ || 50 ||
uvāsa sarvadā viprāḥ kṛṣṇakrīḍekṣaṇotsukaḥ || 51 ||
iti śrībṛhannāradīyapurāṇe bṛhadupākhyāne uttarabhāge vasumohinīsaṃvāde vasucaritranirūpaṇaṃ nāmaikāśītitamo'dhyāyaḥ || 81 ||

<2.82>

atha dvyaśītitamo'dhyāyaḥ
ṛṣaya ūcuḥ -
sūta sādho tvayākhyātaṃ śrīkṛṣṇacaritāmṛtam
śrutaṃ kṛtārthāstena smo vayaṃ bhavadanugrahāt || 1 ||
gate vasau brahmalokaṃ mohinī vidhinandinī
kiṃ cakāra tataḥ paścāttanno vyākhyātumarhasi || 2 ||
sūta uvāca -
śṛṇudhvamṛṣayaḥ sarve mohinyāścaritaṃ śubham
yaccakāra vasoḥ paścāttīrthānāṃ parisevanam || 3 ||
yathānuśiṣṭā vasunā mohinī sā vidheḥ sutā
jagāma vidhinā tīrthayātrārthaṃ svarṇadītaṭam || 4 ||
tatra gatvā samāplutya gaṅgādīni tu vaidhasī
cacāra vidhivaddhṛṣṭā brāhmaṇaiḥ saha saṅgatā || 5 ||
purohitena vasunā yasya tīrthasya yo vidhiḥ
kathitastatprakāreṇa sevamānā cacāra ha || 6 ||
teṣu tīrtheṣu devāṃśca viṣṇvādīnpūjayantyatha
samarpayantī viprebhyo dānāni vividhāni ca || 7 ||
gayāyāṃ vidhivadbhartuḥ piṇḍadānaṃ cakāra ha
kāśyāṃ viśveśvaraṃ prārcya samprāptā puruṣottamam || 8 ||
tasmin kṣetre tu naivedyaṃ bhuktvā sā jagadīśituḥ
śuddhadehā tataḥ paścātsamprāptā lakṣmaṇācalam || 9 ||
taṃ samabhyarcya vidhivadgatvā setuṃ samarcya ca
rāmeśvaraṃ mahendrā driṃ bhārgavaṃ samavandata || 10 ||
gokarṇaṃ ca śivakṣetraṃ gatvābhyarcya tamīśvaram
prabhāsaṃ prayayau viprāḥ sārddhaṃ tairdvijasattamaiḥ || 11 ||
snātvā santarpya devādīṃstasya yātrāṃ vidhāya ca
dvārakāyāṃ hariṃ dṛṣṭvā kurukṣetraṃ jagāma sā || 12 ||
tatrāpi vidhivadyātrāṃ saṃvidhāya nareśvarī
gaṅgādvāramupeyāya tatra sasnau vidhānataḥ || 13 ||
tatastu dṛṣṭvā kāmodāṃ namaskṛtya mudānvitā
badaryāśramamāsādya naranārāyaṇāvṛṣī || 14 ||
samabhyarcyātha kāmākṣīṃ yayau dra ṣṭuṃ tvarānvitā
siddhanāthaṃ namaskṛtya tato'yodhyāmupāgatā || 15 ||
snātvā sarayvāṃ vidhivatprārcya sītāpatiṃ tataḥ
madhyayātrāmupāśritya yayāvamarakaṇṭakam || 16 ||
maheśaṃ tatra sampūjya pratisrotastunarmadām
saṃsevyoṅkāramīśānaṃ dṛṣṭvā māhiṣmatīṃ yayau || 17 ||
tryambakeśaṃ tataḥ prārcya samprāptā sā tripuṣkaram
puṣkareṣu vidhānena datvā dānānyanekaśaḥ || 18 ||
samprāptā sā tu mathurāṃ sarvatīrthottamottamām
vidhāyābhyantarīṃ yātrāṃ yojanānāṃ tu viṃśatim || 19 ||
parikramya purīṃ paścāccaturvyūhaṃ dadarśa sā
snātvā viṃśatitīrthe tu samāpyātha pradakṣiṇām || 20 ||
dhenūnāmayutaṃ prādānmāthurebhyo hyalaṅkṛtam
sambhojya tānvarānnena bhaktiklinnena cetasā || 21 ||
namaskṛtya visṛjyaitānkālindīṃ samupāviśat
tataḥ praviṣṭā sā devīṃ kālindīmaghanāśinīm || 22 ||
nādyāpi nirgatā bhūyo yamatithyantamāsthitā
smārtānsūryodayaṃ prāpya śrautānapyaruṇodayam || 23 ||
niśīthaṃ vaiṣṇavānviprāḥ prāpya dūṣayate vratān
mohinīvedharahitāmupoṣyaikādaśīṃ naraḥ || 24 ||
dvādaśyāṃ viṣṇumabhyarcya vaikuṇṭhaṃ yātyasaṃśayam
mohinī vidhijā devī viṣṇujaikādaśī dvijāḥ || 25 ||
viṣṇujāsparddhayā dhātrā mohinī sā vinirmitā
rukmāṅgadastu rājarṣirviṣṇubhaktiparāyaṇaḥ || 26 ||
na tu vārayituṃ śaktā sā tamekādaśīvratāt
viṣṇulokaṃ gate tasminsabhārye sasute nṛpe || 27 ||
sparddhantyaikādaśīṃ siddhiṃ yamānte mohinī sthitā
ityetaduktaṃ viprendrā mohinīcaritaṃ mayā || 28 ||
yadarthaṃ nirmitā dhātrā tathā cātra vyavasthitā
nāradīyottaraṃ hyetatproktaṃ vo bhuktimuktidam || 29 ||
atra samyaggharerbhaktiḥ sādhyate'nupadaṃ nṛṇām
nāradīyaṃ purāṇaṃ tu lakṣaṇairddaśabhiryutam || 30 ||
yaḥ śṛṇoti naro bhaktyā sa gacchedvaiṣṇavaṃ padam
dharmārthakāmamokṣāṇāṃ caturṇṇāṃ kāraṇaṃ param || 31 ||
sarveṣāṃ ca purāṇānāmidaṃ bījaṃ sanātanam
pravṛttaṃ ca nivṛttaṃ ca purāṇe'smindvijottamāḥ || 32 ||
vistarāduditaṃ sarvaṃ pārāśaryeṇa dhīmatā
alaukikacaritrāḍhyaṃ purāṇaṃ nāradīyakam || 33 ||
yasmai kasmai na dātavyaṃ mahyaṃ vyāsena kīrtitam
hitvā svaśiṣyānpailādīnmahyaṃ nāradasaṃhitām || 34 ||
yo vyācakre namastasmai vedavyāsāya viṣṇave
purāṇasaṃhitāmetāṃ nāradāya vipaścite || 35 ||
sanakādyā mahābhāgā munayaḥ pracakāśire
haṃsasvarūpī bhagavānyadā taṃ brahma śāśvatam || 36 ||
tadupādiśadetebhyo vijñānena vijṛmbhitam
tadidaṃ bhagavānsākṣānnārado'dhyātmadarśanaḥ || 37 ||
vedavyāsāya munaye rahasyaṃ nirdideśa ha
mayā prakāśitaṃ hyetadra hasyaṃ bhuvi durlabham || 38 ||
caturvargapradaṃ nṝṇāṃ śṛṇvatāṃ paṭhatāṃ sadā
vipro vedanidhirbhūyātkṣatriyo jayate mahīm || 39 ||
vaiśyo dhanasamṛddhaḥ syācchūdro mucyeta duḥkhataḥ
pañcaviṃśatisāhasrī saṃhiteyaṃ prakīrtitā || 40 ||
pañcapādasamāyuktā kṛṣṇadvaipāyanena ha
asyāṃ vai śrūyamāṇāyāṃ sarvasandehabhañjanam || 41 ||
puṃsāṃ sakāmabhaktānāṃ niṣkāmānāṃ vimokṣaṇam
puṇyatīrthaṃ samāsādya naimiṣaṃ puṣkaraṃ gayām || 42 ||
mathurāṃ dvārakāṃ viprā naranārāyaṇāśramam
kurukṣetraṃ narmadāṃ ca kṣetraṃ śrīpuruṣottamam || 43 ||
haviṣyāśī dharāśāyī niḥsaṅgo vijitendri yaḥ
paṭhitvā saṃhitāmenāṃ mucyate bhavasāgarāt || 44 ||
ekādaśī vratānāṃ ca saritāṃ jāhnavī yathā
vṛndāvanamaraṇyānāṃ kṣetrāṇāṃ kauravaṃ yathā || 45 ||
yathā kāśī purīṇāṃ ca tīrthānāṃ mathurā yathā
sarasāṃ puṣkaraṃ viprāḥ purāṇānāmidaṃ tathā || 46 ||
gaṇeśabhaktāḥ saurāśca vaiṣṇavāḥ śāktaśāmbhavāḥ
sarve'dhikāriṇo hyatra sakāmāścāpyakāmakāḥ || 47 ||
yaṃ yaṃ kāmamabhidhyāyannaro nāryathavādarāt
śṛṇoti śrāvayedvāpi taṃ taṃ prāpnoti niścitam || 48 ||
rogārto mucyate rogādbhayārto nirbhayo bhavet
jayakāmo jayecchatrūnnāradīyānuśīlanāt || 49 ||
sṛṣṭyādau rajasā viśvaṃ madhye sattvena pāti yaḥ
tamasāṃ'te grasedetattasmai sarvātmane namaḥ || 50 ||
ṛṣayo manavaḥ siddhā lokapālāḥ prajeśvarāḥ
brahmādyā racitā yena tasmai brahmātmane namaḥ || 51 ||
yato vāco nivartante na mano yatra saṃviśet
tadvidyādātmano rūpaṃ hyarūpasya cidātmanaḥ || 52 ||
yasya satyatayā satyaṃ jagadetadvikāśate
vicitrarūpaṃ vande taṃ nirguṇaṃ tamasaḥ param || 53 ||
ādau madhye cāpyajanaścānte caikākṣaro vibhuḥ
vibhāti nānārūpeṇa taṃ vande'ha nirañjanam || 54 ||
nirañjanātsamutpannaṃ jagadetaccarācaram
tiṣṭhatyapyeti vā yasmiṃstatsatyaṃ jñānamadvayam || 55 ||
śivaṃ śaivā vadantyenaṃ pradhānaṃ sāṅkhyavedinaḥ
yoginaḥ puruṣaṃ viprāḥ karma mīmāṃsakā janāḥ || 56 ||
vibhuṃ vaiśeṣikādyāśca cicchaktiṃ śakticintakāḥ
brahmādvitīyaṃ tadvande nānārūpakriyāspadam || 57 ||
bhaktirbhagavataḥ puṃsāṃ bhagavadrrūpakāriṇī
tāṃ labdhvā cāparaṃ lābhaṃ ko vāñchati vinā paśum || 58 ||
bhagavadvimukhā ye tu narāḥ saṃsāriṇo dvijāḥ
teṣāṃ muktirbhavāṭavyā nāsti satsaṅgamantarā || 59 ||
sādhavaḥ samudācārāḥ sarvalokahitāvahāḥ
dīnānukampino viprāḥ prapannāstārayanti hi || 60 ||
yūyaṃ dhanyatamā loke munayaḥ sādhusammatāḥ
yanmuhurvāsudevasya kīrtiṃ pallavanūtanām || 61 ||
dhanyo'smyanugṛhīto'smi bhavadbhirlokamaṅgalam
yatsmārito hariḥ sākṣātsarvakāraṇakāraṇam || 62 ||
oṃ iti śrībṛhannāradīyapurāṇe bṛhadupākhyāne uttarabhāge mahāpurāṇe etatpurāṇaśravaṇādiphalanirūpaṇaṃ nāma dvyaśītitamo'dhyāyaḥ || 82 ||

samāptamidaṃ nāradīyaṃ mahāpurāṇam
